### [1] [Machine Learning Moment Closure for the Boltzmann Transport Equation: Enforcing Hyperbolicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14894)
*Bongseok Kim,Jiahao Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个多维线性Boltzmann方程Hermite矩系统的数据驱动闭合，通过构造保证双曲性，低阶矩系统以更少矩数优于高阶Pn截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boltzmann输运方程速度依赖求解代价高；矩方法需对未解析高阶矩做闭合，但数据驱动闭合缺乏结构约束时会产生复特征速度导致不稳定。已有保证双曲性的学习型闭合仅限一维，而多维需在所有方向上同时保持双曲性。

Method: 网络学习一个被所有空间方向共享的对称正定对称化子（symmetrizer），限制在保持已知低阶输运方程的子空间上，未解析行由对称补全得到，从而保证任意网络输出和任意截断阶数下各方向Jacobian有实特征值和完备特征向量。仅用动理学参考解的未解析矩空间导数训练，训练回路中无求解器，并采用部分守恒间断Galerkin法演化。

Result: 二维过渡与散射主导区实验中，单个学习的十矩三阶系统在各情形均稳定；时间平均精度在每项实验中均优于36矩P7和136矩P15截断，密度误差比同矩数的P3截断小约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 以最小结构约束（共享SPD对称化子+对称补全）可实现构造性双曲的多维数据驱动闭合，兼具稳定性、精度与计算经济性。

Abstract: The Boltzmann transport equation describes particle transport across collisional regimes, but resolving its velocity dependence is expensive. Moment methods reduce this cost by evolving finitely many velocity moments, requiring a closure for unresolved higher-order moments. Data-driven closures can represent these moments accurately, yet without structural constraints they may produce complex characteristic speeds and unstable simulations. Learned Boltzmann closures with guaranteed hyperbolicity have so far been formulated in one spatial dimension; in multiple dimensions, hyperbolicity must hold in every direction simultaneously. We develop, to the best of our knowledge, the first data-driven closure for multidimensional Hermite moment systems of the linear Boltzmann equation that is hyperbolic by construction. The network learns a symmetric positive-definite symmetrizer shared by all spatial directions and restricted to a subspace that preserves the known lower-order transport equations; the unresolved rows follow from a symmetric completion. Every directional Jacobian thus has real eigenvalues and a complete set of eigenvectors for every network output and at any truncation order. The closure is trained only on spatial derivatives of unresolved moments from kinetic reference solutions, with no solver in the training loop, and evolved by a partially conservative discontinuous Galerkin method. In two-dimensional experiments in the transition and scattering-dominated regimes, a single learned third-order system with ten moments remains stable in all cases. Averaged over time, it is more accurate than the classical P7 and P15 truncations with 36 and 136 moments in every experiment, and its density error is about two orders of magnitude smaller than that of the P3 truncation with the same moments.

</details>


### [2] [CAFIN: CUDA-accelerated Adaptive Fisher Information Matrix Network Optimization via GPU-Native Kernel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14589)
*Maryam Arshad,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAFIN，基于CUDA核融合加速AdaFisher二阶优化器的GPU执行效率，在不改变其数学公式的前提下显著降低FIM构建与优化器步延迟，并减少显存分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化器利用曲率信息提升训练效果，但AdaFisher的PyTorch实现存在顺序CUDA核启动、中间张量分配和重复访存等GPU执行开销，限制其实际效率。

Method: GPU原生CUDA核融合框架，将FIM预条件子构建、指数移动平均更新、梯度预条件、动量累积、权重衰减与参数更新逐步融合为统一CUDA核；辅以共享内存复用、寄存器驻留计算和原地操作以减少访存与中间分配。

Result: 在GTX 1660 Ti与RTX 3060 Laptop上，ResNet-18于CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet：孤立FIM构建延迟最多降低78.4%，优化器步延迟降低2.1–26.9%，每步消除约18.91 MB中间显存分配；FP32容差内数值一致，收敛精度与随机种子结果可比。

Conclusion: CUDA核融合可在保持AdaFisher数学形式与学习行为不变的前提下显著加速二阶优化，验证了系统级实现优化对二阶优化器实用性的价值。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers improve deep neural network training using curvature information, but their practical efficiency is limited by GPU execution overhead. AdaFisher reduces Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) approximation costs through diagonal Kronecker structures and exponential moving averages, yet its PyTorch implementation requires sequential CUDA kernel launches, intermediate tensor allocations, and repeated memory accesses. We introduce CAFIN, a GPU-native CUDA kernel fusion framework that improves AdaFisher execution efficiency without changing its optimization formulation. The proposed approach progressively integrates FIM preconditioner construction, exponential moving average updates, gradient preconditioning, momentum accumulation, weight decay, and parameter updates into fused CUDA kernels. Shared-memory reuse, register-resident computation, and in-place operations further reduce memory traffic and intermediate allocations. Experiments using ResNet-18 on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet across NVIDIA GTX 1660 Ti and RTX 3060 Laptop GPUs demonstrate up to 78.4% lower isolated FIM construction latency and 2.1-26.9% lower optimizer-step latency. The implementation also eliminates approximately 18.91 MB of intermediate GPU memory allocations per optimizer step. Numerical validation confirms consistency within the predefined FP32 tolerance, while convergence accuracy remains comparable across evaluated datasets and random seeds. These findings demonstrate that CUDA kernel fusion can substantially accelerate second-order optimization while preserving AdaFisher's underlying mathematical formulation and learning behavior.

</details>


### [3] [An Asynchronous Federated Learning Platform for Lung Adenocarcinoma Grading via Foundation Models and Structural Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14590)
*Moon-Il Joo,Md Ariful Islam Mozumder,Hee-Cheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了基于Web的联邦学习系统，仅共享本地模型权重以保护隐私，使用UNI2基础模型与CLAM-SB对肺腺癌病理全切片进行PD/WMD分型，并通过算法比较与结构化剪枝在FedNova+40%剪枝下实现通信效率与分类性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺腺癌的差分化（PD）与中高分化（WMD）分型对患者生存预测意义重大，但病理医生间观察者间变异性导致分类不一致；同时受患者隐私限制，多机构数据难以集中收集，亟需在保护隐私前提下整合多中心数据。

Method: 采用UNI2基础模型与CLAM-SB对病理全切片图像分类；搭建四客户端联邦学习系统，仅交换本地模型权重；NLST队列专用于外部验证；在CPTAC与TCGA联合训练基础上比较FedAvg、FedProx、FedBN、FedNova四种算法；对模型施加20%、30%、40%稀疏度的L1范数结构化剪枝以降低传输规模；并开发支持切片上传、本地训练、AI分类与注意力热图可视化的Web应用。

Result: 集中式评估中，CPTAC+TCGA联合训练的外部验证效果优于单机构训练，验证了多机构数据整合的必要性。测试AUC排序为FedBN > FedProx > FedNova > FedAvg；FedNova在PD与WMD两类间分类性能最均衡。剪枝后FedAvg、FedProx、FedBN的测试AUC呈下降趋势，而FedNova在各稀疏度下均获得AUC提升。

Conclusion: FedNova与40%剪枝的组合被确认为兼顾通信效率、分类性能与患者隐私保护的实用方案。

Abstract: Classification of lung adenocarcinoma into poorly differentiated (PD) and well-to-moderately differentiated (WMD) subtypes plays an important role in patient survival; however, consistent classification is difficult due to inter-observer variability among pathologists. Collecting data from multiple hospitals is also challenging due to patient privacy concerns. This study proposes a web-based federated learning system that shares only locally trained model weights without sharing patient data between institutions. The UNI2 foundation model and CLAM-SB were used to classify pathological whole slide images. A four-client federated learning system was constructed, and the NLST cohort was reserved exclusively for external validation. In centralized evaluation, joint training with CPTAC and TCGA outperformed single-institution training on external validation, confirming the necessity of multi-institutional data integration. FedAvg, FedProx, FedBN, and FedNova were compared as federated learning algorithms under identical experimental conditions. Test AUC ranked as FedBN, FedProx, FedNova, and FedAvg. FedNova showed the most balanced classification performance between PD and WMD. L1-norm structural pruning at 20%, 30%, and 40% sparsity was applied to reduce model transmission size. FedAvg, FedProx, and FedBN showed a tendency of decreasing Test AUC under pruning, while FedNova achieved improved Test AUC across all sparsity levels. A web-based application was developed supporting slide upload, local training, AI classification, and attention heatmap-based visualization. The combination of FedNova and 40% pruning was confirmed as a practical approach that improves communication efficiency and classification performance while protecting patient privacy.

</details>


### [4] [Adversarial Risk in the Generative AI Era Necessitates Dropping the Small Epsilon Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14594)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil G. Marchant,Paul Montague,Jiani Xie,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性（position）论文，指出对抗机器学习对ℓ_p范数"ε-球"的执念是一种"计量学陷阱"，它向防御方传递了失真的风险感知，与实际系统脆弱性脱节，甚至可能因制造虚假安全感而加剧危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对抗机器学习研究以寻找最小ℓ_p扰动为核心范式，但这一抽象度量与真实世界的系统级威胁不匹配；作者希望纠正该范式造成的风险认知偏差。

Method: 论证性/概念分析：从计量学与风险沟通角度剖析ℓ_p范数主导地位的误导性，指出其促成"窄化的数学鲁棒性"优化，并激励攻击者转向与之不重叠的替代对抗路径。

Result: 概念性结论而非实证结果：ℓ_p球主导的研究不仅未能减少危害，反而通过传递针对错误威胁的虚假安全感，可能主动促进危害。

Conclusion: 应摆脱以ℓ_p范数为唯一度量的研究定势，重新以真实系统脆弱性与系统性安全为对齐目标来评估对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adversarial Machine Learning research is currently centered on the search for the epsilon-ball: minimal perturbations designed to fool models while remaining imperceptible to humans. We argue that this fixation on $\ell_p$ norms is a metrological trap that communicates a distorted perception of risk to defenders, one that is unaligned with real-world system vulnerabilities. This paradigm misleads defenders into optimizing for narrow, mathematical robustness at the expense of systemic security, while simultaneously incentivizing attackers to exploit non-overlapping alternative adversarial pathways. Ultimately, we argue that the dominance of the $\ell_p$ ball does not just fail to reduce harm---it may actively facilitate it by communicating a false sense of security against the wrong threats.

</details>


### [5] [StructRL: Model-Free Reinforcement Learning with Replay Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14599)
*Ivo Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StructRL将强化学习过程中收集的经验构建为显式回放图，用动态规划在已观测状态空间上求解出仅在可验证区域有定义的图策略，再将其以动作偏好（DQN）和最短路径方向标签（SAC）的形式蒸馏给无模型学习器，在稀疏奖励MiniGrid与连续PointMaze上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励任务下，无模型强化学习（如DQN、SAC）样本效率低、成功率极低，且难以利用经验中蕴含的显式状态转移结构；作者希望把经验中的可行路径信息显式化并回灌给无模型学习器。

Method: 提出StructRL：1) 将交互经验构建为显式回放图；2) 当图中存在通往目标的路径时，用动态规划在已观测状态空间上选取动作，形成仅在已观测转移处有定义的“图策略”；3) 将图策略迁移至无模型学习器——对DQN作为Q模型的动作偏好，对SAC作为沿最短观测路径的目标方向标签；4) 额外实验将图策略蒸馏为全新的Q模型。

Result: DQN在三个稀疏奖励MiniGrid任务上最终成功率提升：DoorKey 0.10→0.90，KeyCorridor 0.10→0.80，ObstructedMaze 0→0.50，且普通DQN用三倍预算仍追不上；SAC在连续PointMaze上，图导方向标签使七个匹配运行的成功率从0.17升至1.0；蒸馏得到的DoorKey 6x6控制器可从91%的可达状态到达目标，普通DQN为38%。

Conclusion: 显式图策略与无模型学习的结合能有效缓解稀疏奖励难题，但扩展性受三个瓶颈限制：图覆盖不完整、结构别名（不同状态在图表示中混淆）、以及高维输入下图信息损失。

Abstract: StructRL turns the experience collected during reinforcement learning into an explicit replay graph. Once the graph contains a path to the task goal, dynamic programming selects actions on the observed part of the state space; these actions form a graph policy that is defined only where the required transitions have been observed. StructRL transfers these actions to model-free learners: as action preferences for a DQN Q-model and as target directions along shortest observed paths for a SAC actor. For DQN on three sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks, final success rises from 0.10 to 0.90 (DoorKey), 0.10 to 0.80 (KeyCorridor) and 0 to 0.50 (ObstructedMaze); plain DQN does not catch up with three times the budget. For SAC on continuous PointMaze, graph-derived direction labels raise final success from 0.17 to 1.0 on seven matched runs that reached the goal. Distilling the graph policy into a fresh Q-model gives a DoorKey 6x6 controller that reaches the goal from 91% of reachable states, against 38% for plain DQN. Scaling experiments identify three bottlenecks: incomplete graph support, structural aliasing, and loss of graph information in high-dimensional inputs.

</details>


### [6] [KernelMosaic: An Open Dataset and Agentic Framework for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14604)
*Ningyuan Xi,Yuhang Lai,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelMosaic：面向CUDA内核生成的开源数据集，配对多样化任务、经校验的解决方案与优化轨迹，微调小模型后在KernelBench L1/L2/L3上正确率由4%/0%/0%提升至88%/82%/16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM生成高效CUDA内核需要覆盖多样计算需求与迭代式代码优化的监督信号，但异构规格（自然语言、参考程序、数学规范）使监督构建困难，且有效实现依赖复杂的反复测试与改进，缺乏高质量监督数据。

Method: 构建开源数据集KernelMosaic：采用人工引导的评审与多样性感知筛选，将任务表达为自然语言描述、参考程序、数学规范及其交叉形式；用智能体框架驱动强LLM，结合可复用的CUDA指导与执行反馈，完成实现、正确性修复与性能优化，产出经校验的解与轨迹。

Result: 在小开源模型上做监督微调：KernelBench L1/L2/L3正确率从4%/0%/0%提升至88%/82%/16%，各层级中内核快于PyTorch eager的任务比例均提高；训练实验表明来自精选轨迹的监督显著优于直接生成答案，并能促进持续的测试与修订行为。

Conclusion: KernelMosaic使通用模型在CUDA内核生成上取得显著提升，验证了异构规格任务+经校验优化轨迹的监督范式在代码生成与性能优化领域的有效性。

Abstract: Training large language models to generate efficient CUDA kernels requires supervision spanning diverse computational requirements and iterative code optimization. Constructing this supervision is challenging because of heterogeneous specifications, and useful implementations emerge through iterative complicated testing and refinement. We introduce KernelMosaic, an open dataset pairing diverse CUDA tasks with verified solutions and trajectories. Human-guided review and diversity-aware selection curate tasks expressed as natural-language descriptions, reference programs, mathematical specifications, and their intersections. To build the solutions and trajectories, our agentic framework guides a strong LLM through implementation, correctness repair, and performance optimization with reusable CUDA guidance and execution feedback. Supervised fine-tuning of a small open-source model on KernelMosaic raises correctness from 4%, 0%, and 0% to 88%, 82%, and 16% on KernelBench L1, L2, and L3, respectively, while increasing the fraction of tasks with kernels faster than PyTorch eager at every level. Further training experiments show that supervision from the curated trajectories substantially outperforms direct responses and promotes sustained testing and revision. These results demonstrate that KernelMosaic enables a general-purpose model to achieve substantial gains in CUDA kernel generation.

</details>


### [7] [Thompson Sampling using Prior-fitted Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14656)
*Sihwa Park,Jingsen Zhu,Vinamr Jain,Sheng-Yen Chou,Alexander Terenin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出先验拟合扩散Transformer（prior-fitted diffusion transformers），在推理时以in-context方式执行Thompson sampling，并配套设计多种非高斯先验，用于连续黑箱全局优化，性能优于基于高斯过程的传统流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续全局优化中，Thompson sampling因需计算后验分布而基本局限于高斯过程先验——高斯过程几乎是实践中唯一后验可解析处理的先验类，这限制了可表达能力（如无法刻画单峰等非高斯特性）。

Method: 采用prior-fitted network范式预训练扩散模型，使扩散Transformer在推理阶段in-context地完成Thompson sampling；并设计一组适用于一般黑箱函数的实用非高斯先验（含高斯性无法实现的单峰函数先验），同时量化模型规模与预训练迭代次数的权衡关系。

Result: 模型呈现与其它模态Transformer类似的scaling laws；在多个全局优化基准上，配备非高斯先验的先验拟合扩散Transformer相比基于高斯过程的传统流程取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 以扩散Transformer结合先验拟合范式可实现无需后验解析计算的Thompson sampling，突破高斯过程先验的表达限制，为黑箱连续全局优化提供可扩展的新方案。

Abstract: Thompson sampling is natural randomized strategy for optimizing unknown functions in a black-box manner. In continuous global optimization, Thompson sampling has largely been restricted to Gaussian process priors, as these are essentially the only class for which computing the posterior distribution has been tractable in practice. In this work, we introduce \emph{prior-fitted diffusion transformers}, which are diffusion models that perform Thompson sampling in-context at inference time, after being pre-trained using the prior-fitted network paradigm. To apply these models, we develop a set of practical non-Gaussian priors for general black-box functions---including those that have characteristics that cannot be achieved under Gaussianity, such as priors over unimodal functions. We also quantify the tradeoff between model size and the number of pre-training iterations, and show that these models follow scaling laws akin to those observed in transformers defined over other modalities. Across a set of global optimization benchmarks, we show that prior-fitted diffusion transformers with non-Gaussian priors can achieve improved performance compared to traditional pipelines built on Gaussian processes.

</details>


### [8] [Closing the Loop: Co-Evolving EM for Irregular Time Series Generation in Lifted Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14659)
*Idan Arbiv,Gal Fadlon,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出"一次性插补+冻结代理数据"的开环范式存在结构性缺陷，提出插补器与生成器协同演化的蒙特卡洛EM框架，并引入PSLR实现E步后验采样，在十个数据集上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从规则时间序列生成任务常被简化为一次性插补：把每条不完整序列补全一次后冻结，再在代理数据上训练生成器。作者论证这种开环策略根本性有缺陷：确定性插补器把歧义缺失区域坍缩为条件均值，随机插补器则会随生成器演化而过时；真正的瓶颈在于迭代而非插补器设计。

Method: 提出协同演化的蒙特卡洛EM框架以闭合插补器—生成器回路：E步从当前生成器后验中采样缺失值，M步用这些补全重新训练生成器，从而保持无条件生成训练。由于扩散先验工作在提升的二维表示中而观测位于时间序列空间，进一步提出Posterior Sampling for Lifted Representations (PSLR)，在E步中保证算子一致的条件下、不确定性保持与流形一致性。

Result: 在10个数据集与4种损坏机制上达到SOTA：判别分数提升59%、预测分数提升15%、Context-FID提升73%、特征相关性误差降低71%（相对最强基线）。消融显示相比单步基线平均判别分数降低73%，弥合了与干净数据oracle之间的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 闭合插补—生成回路（迭代式后验采样训练）比改进单次插补器更为关键，PSLR使提升表示下的后验采样可行且一致，显著逼近干净数据上界。

Abstract: Regular time-series generation from irregular time series is typically reduced to one-shot imputation: complete each partial sequence once, freeze the completions, and train a generator on the resulting surrogate dataset. We show that this open-loop strategy is structurally flawed. Point imputers collapse ambiguous missing regions to conditional averages, while stochastic imputers become stale as the generator evolves; the binding constraint is therefore iteration, not imputer design. We introduce a co-evolving Monte Carlo EM framework that closes the imputer-generator loop. Each E-step samples missing values from the posterior of the current generator, and each M-step retrains the generator on these completions, preserving unconditional generator training. Since our diffusion prior operates in a lifted 2D representation while observations live in time-series space, we further introduce Posterior Sampling for Lifted Representations (PSLR), which enforces operator-consistent conditioning, uncertainty preservation, and manifold consistency during the E-step. Across ten datasets and four corruption regimes, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving over the strongest baselines by 59% in discriminative score, 15% in predictive score, 73% in Context-FID, and 71% in feature-correlation error. Ablations show that our approach reduces average discriminative score by 73% over single-step baselines, closing most of the remaining gap to the clean-data oracle. Code is available at https://github.com/azencot-group/PSLR.

</details>


### [9] [Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14683)
*Adil Soubki,Elizabeth S. Z. Tan,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将强化学习后训练中"非目标特征"的演化形式化，借助Price方程识别出"特征搭便车"机制：训练中概率重分配会无意中放大或抑制未被奖励直接针对的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为语言模型后训练的标准阶段且算力投入快速增长，其虽提升可验证任务能力，却可能改变奖励未显式针对的行为与内部属性，但目前缺乏描述这类非目标特征如何演化的理论框架。

Method: 提出描述RL后训练中"特征（traits）"演化的形式化框架，由此重新导出进化生物学的Price方程，并据此构建"特征搭便车"（trait hitchhiking）的数学模型；随后在GRPO训练的语言模型上评估该Price更新表达式。

Result: Price更新表达式的估计值与采样得到的特征动态高度吻合，说明该理论模型能准确刻画特征在后训练中的演化。

Conclusion: 特征搭便车是RL后训练悄悄放大不良特征的一种机制，Price方程为分析和预测非目标特征漂移提供了可用的定量工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond Row-Level Prediction: Evaluating and Learning Composition for Reusable Table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14697)
*Ali Younes,Saeed Ghoorchian,Maximilian Schambach,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个直接评测表级嵌入质量的基准（一致性、可分性、鲁棒性、效率四项标准），发现轻量schema/文本指纹优于TabPFN和ConTextTab等表格基础模型；进一步用自监督的SCDM学习组合规则，在冻结编码器的情况下显著提升表级嵌入质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格内容分布于schema、单元格、行、列，检索、重叠估计、去重、数据治理等大规模任务需要把局部信号组合成可复用的单向量表级嵌入。然而现有表格表示方法多通过行级预测或有监督下游任务评估，无法直接衡量表级嵌入质量，缺少专门的评测基准。

Method: 构建表级嵌入基准，从四方面评估：部分视图下的一致性、跨标签粒度的可分性、对良性扰动的鲁棒性、效率；在合成与真实语料上评测十二种方法（含均值/最大及三种无参池化组合的行级状态）。针对TabPFN与ConTextTab落后的问题，冻结ConTextTab编码器，仅学习其单元格状态上的组合规则，提出支持条件分布匹配（SCDM）自监督损失，使嵌入相似度分布与表内/表间划分重叠分布相匹配。

Result: 轻量schema与文本指纹在基准中表现最强；两表格基础模型在五种池化下均落后。学习组合规则后，在全部十五种头配置下，嵌入相似度与部分视图重叠的Spearman相关从0.04提升至合成数据上的0.43–0.50，并改善三元组排序、聚类与检索，真实数据上增益较小但一致；父表检索下游任务进一步验证该结论。

Conclusion: 局部状态如何组合、以及采用何种目标去学习组合规则，对表级嵌入质量有实质性影响；瓶颈更多在于组合规则而非编码器本身，冻结编码器仅学习组合即可获得显著提升。

Abstract: Tables are compositional, with content spread across a schema, cell values, rows, and columns. Large-scale operations such as retrieval, overlap estimation, deduplication, and data curation need these local signals composed into one reusable embedding vector per table. Table representation methods, however, are mostly evaluated through row-level prediction or supervised downstream tasks, which do not measure table-level embedding quality directly. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates table-level embeddings through four desiderata: consistency under partial views, discriminability across label granularities, robustness to benign perturbations, and efficiency. Across twelve methods on synthetic and real corpora, lightweight schema- and text-based fingerprints are among the strongest. The sharpest contrast comes from two tabular foundation models, TabPFN and ConTextTab: although both are strong row-level predictors, their states, composed by mean, max, or three further parameter-free pooling rules, trail the strongest fingerprints. We therefore ask whether the gap lies in the encoder or in the compositional rule. Keeping ConTextTab frozen, we learn only the compositional rule over its cell states, using support-conditioned distribution matching (SCDM), a self-supervised loss we propose that matches the distribution of embedding similarities to the distribution of partition overlaps within and across tables. Across all fifteen head configurations, the learned rule raises the Spearman correlation between embedding similarity and partial-view overlap from 0.04 to 0.43-0.50 on synthetic data and improves triplet ranking, clustering, and retrieval, with smaller but systematic gains on real data. A downstream parent-table retrieval task confirms these findings. How local states are composed, and with what objective, substantially affects the quality of table-level embeddings.

</details>


### [11] [Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14704)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"代码解释"（code explanations）：用一段程序刻画LLM在数据集上的行为，并通过程序执行实现自动验证；定义Consistency指标衡量程序复现模型输出的输入比例，实验显示该方法在部分场景高度一致、比CoT短6–100倍，但在语言成分复杂的任务上一致性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益参与具有后果的决策，需理解其结论的形成方式。理解模型行为需跨多个输入观察，而CoT等自由文本解释按实例生成、难以在新输入上验证，存在"解释生成方式"与"解释评估方式"之间的错配。

Method: 以程序作为解释载体：一段代码解释LLM在整个数据集上的行为，程序执行使解释可自动验证。定义Consistency指标（程序复现LLM输出的输入占比），用于评估与筛选候选程序。实验覆盖3个开源代码能力LLM、8个任务；另用Claude Opus 5.5进行一致性反馈与代码执行反馈实验。

Result: LLM在若干设置下能产出高度一致的代码解释；代码解释比CoT短6–100倍；在24个设置中有9个比CoT更贴合模型行为。但在含更复杂语言成分的任务上一致性显著降低，程序复现率不高于无信息基线解释，且常依赖较长的硬编码模式。加入一致性反馈与代码执行后，跨任务一致性提升，但复杂任务上仍显著偏低。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种新颖、可验证且紧凑的LLM行为解释方式；验证反馈是提升解释一致性的有前景方向。代码已开源于 https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations.

</details>


### [12] [UniPipe: Unified Inference Serving and Fine-Tuning for Deadline-Constrained DNN Surrogate Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14748)
*Jaiaid Mobin,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPipe 是一个单上下文边缘 GPU 系统，将 DNN 代理模型的推理服务与微调统一到同一次前向传播中，通过多项式时间调度器利用非线性延迟曲线优化批组成，在 Ptychographic 重建工作流上同时降低误差与截止期错失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流（如新一代 X 射线光源的 Ptychographic 成像）需近实时处理高速传感器流，DNN 代理虽比迭代求解器快 100-1000x，但输入分布随实验漂移会损害精度。现有部署要么冻结预训练代理，要么用独立 CUDA 上下文并发微调；后者在 GPU 上造成显著资源争用，抬高推理延迟的均值与方差，导致硬性逐单元截止期被错过。

Method: 基于三点洞察：(1) 推理与微调均需前向传播，可融合为一次；(2) 现代 GPU 的逐样本延迟对批大小呈非线性；(3) 负载跨时间区间重复，可离线调度。据此设计：将训练与推理数据融合于单次前向，仅对训练子集执行选择性反向传播；并用多项式时间调度器利用延迟曲线计算最优批组成。

Result: 在真实 Ptychographic 重建工作流（传感器速率达 5000 patterns/sec）上评估：相较预训练基线，重建 MSE 最多降低 30%，推理 MSE 最多降低 50%；相较多上下文基线，截止期错失率降低 13%-22%；延迟离散度从均值的 106% 降至 57%，且总吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 单上下文融合的推理-微调统一部署可有效消除多上下文并发争用，在保证实时性与截止期约束的同时提升精度与吞吐，为边缘 GPU 上的科学流式工作负载提供了可行方案。

Abstract: DNN surrogates are increasingly used to accelerate scientific workflows that must process high-rate sensor streams in near real time, exemplified by Ptychographic imaging at next-generation X-ray light sources. Such surrogates deliver 100-1000x higher throughput than iterative solvers, enabling faster steering of experiments. To preserve surrogate accuracy during an experiment, as the input distribution evolves, state-of-the-art deployments freeze the pretrained surrogate or run a separate CUDA context that fine-tunes concurrently with serving. However, multi-context concurrency causes significant contention on the GPU, inflating both the mean and the variance of inference latency, triggering deadline misses in workflows where sensor data has to be processed within a hard per-unit deadline. To address this challenge, we propose UniPipe, a single-context system that unifies inference serving and fine-tuning of a DNN surrogate on a single edge GPU. UniPipe is driven by three key insights: both serving and fine-tuning require a forward pass and can be fused into one; the per-sample latency of modern GPUs is non-linear in batch size; and the workload is repetitive across intervals, enabling offline scheduling. Specifically, UniPipe fuses training and inference data in a single forward pass with a selective backward pass on the training subset, and uses a polynomial-time scheduler that exploits the latency curve to compute the batch composition. We evaluate UniPipe on a real-world Ptychographic reconstruction workflow with sensor rates up to 5000 patterns/sec.; UniPipe lowers reconstruction MSE by up to 30% and inference MSE by up to 50% over a pretrained baseline, and reduces the deadline-miss rate by 13%-22% over a multi-context baseline. UniPipe's unified deployment reduces the latency spread from 106% to 57% of the mean, and increases total throughput compared to multi-context baseline.

</details>


### [13] [From Nodes to Pixels: Topological and Structural Two-View Graph Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14750)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Soham Changani,Sai Karthik Navuluru,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G2Image 提出一种固定预算的"图成像"框架，将每个图转换为两个互补的图像式视图（TopoGrid 与 GraphGrid），使图数据获得类似视觉中 patch、语言中 token 的可复用标准输入接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏像视觉中的 patch 或语言中的 token 那样广泛可复用的输入接口。图本身不规则、尺寸与密度可变，且在等价节点重标号下保持不变，导致跨任务、跨架构的输入难以标准化。

Method: 框架将每个图转为两个互补视图：(1) TopoGrid，通过编码内在双过滤（bifiltration）为紧凑的多持续同调曲面网格，提供稳定的拓扑视图；(2) GraphGrid，按内在节点分数分组并聚合组间边密度，提供更高带宽的结构视图。两视图均具有置换不变性，并具备分辨率控制的稳定性保证。由于表示是固定尺寸张量，可用轻量 2D 编码器处理，并通过有监督的跨视图对比目标进行对齐。

Result: 在图分类与分子预测基准上，G2Image 在对比方法中取得较强的平均性能，优于单视图与标准融合变体；且无需改变整体架构即可扩展到属性丰富的分子图。

Conclusion: 固定预算的图图像为图表示学习提供了一种实用且可复用的接口。

Abstract: Graph learning still lacks a broadly reusable input interface comparable to patches in vision or tokens in language. Unlike images or text, graphs are irregular, vary in size and density, and are invariant under many equivalent node labelings, making it difficult to standardize inputs across tasks and architectures. We introduce G2Image, a fixed-budget graph imaging framework that converts each graph into two complementary image-like views. TopoGrid provides a stable topological view by encoding an intrinsic bifiltration as a compact multipersistence surface grid, while GraphGrid provides a higher-bandwidth structural view by aggregating edge densities between groups defined by intrinsic node scores. Both views are permutation-invariant and come with resolution-controlled stability guarantees. Because the resulting representations are fixed-size tensors, they can be processed by lightweight 2D encoders and aligned through a supervised cross-view contrastive objective. Across graph classification and molecular prediction benchmarks, G2Image achieves strong average performance among the compared methods, improves over single-view and standard fusion variants, and extends to attribute-rich molecular graphs without changing the overall architecture. These results suggest that fixed-budget graph images offer a practical and reusable interface for graph representation learning.

</details>


### [14] [Graph Label Alignment: A Diagnostic Atlas for Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14760)
*Neelam Akula,Varun Shiralkar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"图标签对齐"(SLA/FLA)轻量诊断,无需训练图编码器即可量化标签可及性,可将图分类基准难度组织为一个SLA–FLA平面,并区分"边缘统计即可解决"与"需结构–特征交互"两类情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图分类基准难度差异巨大:部分被简单池化基线解决,部分对GNN与图Transformer依然困难。作者从数据中心视角质疑:基准成功究竟来自真实的结构–特征推理,还是来自受限边缘视图(label marginals)中已可获取的标签信号。

Method: 提出图标签对齐诊断,由两个不训练图编码器的监督探针组成:(1) 结构标签对齐(SLA),基于置换不变(permutation-invariant)的拓扑与谱摘要;(2) 特征标签对齐(FLA),基于去边后的池化节点特征。并在真实基准与多种池化骨干上检验,同时构造受控的交互主导压力测试:异配 ego-graph 与合成 Twins 任务,使类间结构与特征边缘分布匹配、仅角色–特征耦合不同。

Result: SLA–FLA平面能强有力地组织测试准确率:低对齐数据集构成标准池化流程持续表现不佳的一致区域;高对齐可解释大量简单基准上的性能。异配 ego-graph 与 Twins 压力测试显示,当边缘分布匹配而耦合不同时,成功取决于是否保留结构–特征对应关系。

Conclusion: 高标签对齐解释了多数简单基准的表现,低对齐则揭示了需要超越标准池化表示、保留结构–特征对应关系的困难机制;该诊断可作为评估基准有效性与模型必要性的数据层面工具。

Abstract: Graph classification benchmarks vary widely in difficulty: some are solved by simple pooled baselines, while others remain challenging for GNNs and graph transformers. We take a data-centric view and ask whether benchmark success is driven by genuine structure--feature reasoning or by label signal already accessible from restricted marginal views. We introduce graph label alignment, a lightweight diagnostic that measures label accessibility through two supervised probes computed without training a graph encoder: structural label alignment (SLA), based on permutation-invariant topological and spectral summaries, and feature label alignment (FLA), based on pooled node features with edges removed. Across real graph classification benchmarks and multiple pooled backbones, the resulting SLA--FLA plane strongly organizes test accuracy, with low-alignment datasets forming a consistent regime where standard pooled pipelines struggle. To probe what lies beyond marginal accessibility, we introduce controlled interaction-dominated stress tests, including heterophilic ego-graphs and synthetic Twins tasks whose structural and feature marginals are matched across classes but whose role--feature couplings differ. These results show that high alignment explains much of easy benchmark performance, while low alignment reveals regimes where success requires preserving structure--feature correspondence beyond standard pooled representations.

</details>


### [15] [Owned Edits to a Shared Decision Rule: Learning Private Exceptions as Typed, Gated, Withdrawable Weight Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15917)
*John J. O'Hare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以"归属式编辑"(owned edit)为变更单元的学习机制：将公开决策规则精确编译为小型ReLU网络作为结构化先验，各方在小型类型化声明空间内进行门控搜索，实现隐私隔离的本地修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个组织共用的公开决策规则往往对其本地个案并不正确，但各方持有的私有证据不会共享。需要在"不汇总私有数据"的前提下实现规则的本地化修正。

Method: 将已发布规则精确编译为小型ReLU网络作为结构化先验；每方学习器在类型化声明空间（subject、scope、form、size等少量命名值）中搜索，仅当本地记录通过"选择感知证据门"(selection-aware evidence gate)时才提出编辑；学习即"先验周围的门控搜索"。验收机构根据声明重算编辑并写入该方专属单元。

Result: 两项预注册的合成数据实验（20个采样隐藏真值）中，每方30条记录时学习器以96.6%对91.7%（未修改规则）不劣于全部七个调优基线（受限梯度学习器、Ditto、FedRep、联邦平均、两个池化网络、池化GBDT），差距在2分以内；去掉先验或门控的同种搜索仅84.0%。第三项预注册测试中，基于共享记录训练网络上的类型化编辑较该网络单独提升5.4分，较公开规则提升3.1分。机制上保证：不影响任何其他主体的决策、可精确撤回、信息泄露不超过类型化空间大小的log2比特、除声明值外无法隐藏信息。

Conclusion: 在规则仅用于形式化异常登记时，"归属式例外表"可达成与学习系统相同的决策效果。局限包括：全体共享的真值、放宽型修改、仅10条记录、两项实验中均失败的事前门控判据，以及与语言模型单次抽样准确度相当。

Abstract: Organisations that apply the same published decision rule often hold private evidence that the rule is wrong for their own cases, and will not pool it. We describe a learning mechanism for that setting whose unit of change is an owned edit. The published rule is compiled exactly into a small ReLU network, a structured prior. Each party's learner searches a small typed space of declarations, a few named values such as subject, scope, form and size, and proposes one only when its own records pass a selection-aware evidence gate. The learning is that gated search around the prior. In two analyses registered before they ran, on synthetic data with 20 sampled hidden truths, the learner at 30 records per party was non-inferior, within 2 points, to all seven tuned baselines (a confined gradient learner, Ditto, FedRep, federated averaging, two pooled networks and pooled gradient-boosted trees), scoring 96.6% against 91.7% for the unchanged rule; the same search without the prior or the gate scored 84.0%. In a third registered test, typed edits over a network trained on shared records beat that network alone by 5.4 points and the same edits over the published rule by 3.1. An acceptance body recomputes each edit from its declaration and writes it into units reserved for that party, so by construction an edit changes no decision about any other subject, withdraws exactly, discloses at most log2 of the typed space's size in bits, and can hide nothing beyond its declared values. We report where it loses: truths shared by all parties, relaxations, ten records, a pre-stated gate criterion that failed in both runs, and a language model given the records that decided about as accurately on one draw. On a rule-only base, a table of owned exceptions makes the same decisions.

</details>


### [16] [SPIRE: Task-agnostic embodied continual learning through autonomous situation inference and causal motor-primitive reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14840)
*Yiyang Jia,Mingyang Lyu,Gongce Cao,Feifei Zhao,Yinqian Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE：一种循环脉冲神经网络系统，从动作-结果证据中推断潜在情境并在线学习情境特定运动执行器，成熟执行器成为受保护源与目标情境残差结合，实现无标签、无边界条件下的自主技能恢复与因果复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身持续学习要求控制器在无任务标签、无已知任务边界的情况下推断情境变化、保留既有行为并复用运动技能；核心难点在于判断何时恢复旧行为以及哪些技能可支撑新学习。

Method: 提出SPIRE（Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment）循环脉冲系统：由动作-结果证据推断潜在情境，在线学习情境特定运动actor；成熟actor被保护为源（source），与目标情境残差共同驱动循环脉冲运动群体。

Result: 在坐标控制、三关节控制与隐含力控制任务中，SPIRE维持控制、自主推断情境、保留先前行为，并将学习未见源组合的平均达标交互次数较全新控制器降低约40%；在全部匹配干预中，回退兼容源actor均损害早期学习，证明因果复用；与七种循环持续学习及模块化控制基线相比，SPIRE的平均先验学习优势为最强无任务标签替代方案的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明在连续具身控制中可实现运动技能的自主恢复与因果复用，从而支撑新学习。

Abstract: Embodied continual learning requires controllers to infer changing situations, retain prior behavior and reuse motor skills without task labels or known boundaries. The challenge is deciding when to recover earlier behavior and which skills can support new learning. We introduce Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment (SPIRE), a recurrent-spiking system that infers latent contexts from action-outcome evidence and learns context-specific motor actors online. Mature actors become protected sources that combine with a target-context residual to drive recurrent-spiking motor populations. Across coordinate, three-joint and hidden-force control, SPIRE maintained control, autonomously inferred situations, retained prior behavior and accelerated acquisition of an unseen source composition, reducing mean interactions to criterion by approximately 40% relative to matched fresh controllers. Rewinding compatible source actors impaired early acquisition in every matched intervention, demonstrating causal reuse. Against seven recurrent continual-learning and modular-control comparators, SPIRE achieved 2.4 times the mean prior-learning advantage of the strongest eligible task-free alternative selected for each paired stream. These results demonstrate autonomous recovery and causal reuse of motor skills for new learning in continuous embodied control.

</details>


### [17] [Wayfinder: Adaptive Resource Routing from Agent Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16053)
*Miguel Romero,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应路由器 Wayfinder，以下游智能体引用文档作为反馈更新资源表示，并引入资源标识匿名置换的数据增强，在语义重叠资源场景下优于监督、工具检索与上下文赌博机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的上下文信息分散在聊天、工单、邮件、知识库等资源中，且其位置对智能体未知，导致跨交互重复探索；常见做法是将资源建模为工具并依赖高层描述泛化到新环境，但描述难以区分相似或重叠资源，且无法从后续交互中自我改进；同时缺少公开的、将真实查询与资源访问模式配对的职业环境测试平台。

Method: 构建自适应路由器 Wayfinder：以下游智能体所引用的文档作为"相关信息位置"的反馈，用这些引用更新内部资源表示，使后续交互优先访问更可能含相关信息资源；并在离线强化学习中引入资源增强，对资源身份进行匿名化与置换以抑制对"某资源承载某主题"的过拟合；另基于 NFCorpus、FEVER 和 Tech Startup 构建并发布三个受控测试床，在统一闭环协议下与监督式、工具检索式和上下文赌博机基线对比，并进行消融分析。

Result: 在资源内容语义重叠、静态描述无法区分的情形下，Wayfinder 的路由效果最佳；当资源来自彼此独立的语料库时，基于描述的工具检索已足够。消融显示资源增强在训练环境数量较少时收益最明显。

Conclusion: 利用下游引用作为反馈进行资源表示在线更新，是解决重叠资源路由的有效路径；路由方法的必要性与资源间语义可分性相关，静态描述检索适用于彼此独立的资源集合；资源身份置换增强可缓解少环境下的过拟合，且闭环评测协议与公开测试床对该类研究具有支撑价值。

Abstract: To act effectively, agents must gather contextual information across resources such as chats, issue trackers, email and knowledge bases, to answer questions like "what was decided in the last project meeting?" or "where is the FAQ for our internal tooling?" Where this information lives in a user's environment is unknown to the agent, so it re-explores the same resources across consecutive interactions. Common routing approaches represent each resource as a tool and rely on high-level descriptions to generalize to unseen environments. Such descriptions often fail to separate similar or overlapping resources and cannot improve from the agent's subsequent interactions. We introduce Wayfinder, an adaptive router that uses the documents cited by the downstream agent as feedback about where relevant information resides. These citations update Wayfinder's internal resource representations, which subsequent interactions use to prioritize the resources likely to contain relevant information. We also introduce resource augmentation during offline reinforcement learning, which anonymizes and permutes resource identities to discourage training-time overfitting of which resource holds which topic; our ablations suggest it helps most when few training environments are available. Because no openly available professional environments pair realistic queries with resource-access patterns, we construct and release three controlled testbeds derived from existing datasets: NFCorpus, FEVER and Tech Startup. Compared with supervised, tool-retrieval and contextual bandit baselines under a shared closed-loop protocol, Wayfinder gives the best routing when resources contain semantically overlapping content that static descriptions cannot distinguish, while description-based tool retrieval suffices when resources are drawn from distinct corpora.

</details>


### [18] [Robustness Within a Fairness Budget: Parity Frontiers and Equal-Opportunity Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16031)
*Pablo Rivas,Jeremy Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在软DP预算下做PGD对抗训练，可在保持较高平衡准确率的同时大幅压低DPD，但不同公平指标（DPD与EOpp）方向可能相反，需全面审计群体率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练能提升受攻击时的预测性能，但无法决定决策在各受保护群体间的分布；鲁棒性与群体公平性之间的联合行为缺乏可控、可量化的刻画。

Method: 将二分类建模为在平方软人口统计均等（DP）预算约束下的PGD对抗风险最小化问题，采用投影乘子与单侧罚项更新求解；在Adult Income数据集上（性别字段仅用于群体审计、不进入预测），对逻辑回归与单隐层MLP开展3组配对种子、4种PGD强度、5种预算的网格实验。

Result: ε=0.3时PGD训练相较ERM的平衡准确率分别提升7.77与11.45个百分点，并消除85.7%和89.5%由攻击引起的硬DPD增长；在τ=0.015处，约束训练相对PGD训练将DPD分别降低52.2%与49.2%，同时平衡准确率仍高于ERM 3.18与8.34个百分点；8条种子平均的模型-攻击路径上，预算路径严格序化DPD与准确率并反转平均有符号EOpp差异，率定理给出反转所需的精确TPR偏移且实测群体率满足之。

Conclusion: 在该受控特征空间研究中，鲁棒性与公平性可共存于有用的联合工作点，但单一公平性指标无法替代完整的群体率审计。

Abstract: Adversarial training protects predictive performance under attack but does not determine how decisions are distributed across protected groups. We formulate binary classification as projected gradient descent (PGD) adversarial-risk minimization under a squared soft demographic-parity (DP) budget, optimized with projected multiplier and one-sided penalty updates. On Adult Income, using the binary sex field supplied by the benchmark for group auditing but excluding it from prediction, we test logistic regression and a one-hidden-layer multilayer perceptron (MLP) across three paired seeds, four PGD strengths, and five budgets. At $\varepsilon=0.3$, PGD training improves balanced accuracy over empirical-risk minimization (ERM) by $7.77$ and $11.45$ percentage points and removes $85.7\%$ and $89.5\%$ of attack-induced growth in hard demographic-parity difference (DPD). At the representative interior value $τ=0.015$, constrained training cuts DPD relative to PGD training by $52.2\%$ and $49.2\%$, while remaining $3.18$ and $8.34$ balanced-accuracy points above ERM. Across all eight seed-averaged model-attack paths, the sampled budget path strictly orders DPD and accuracy and reverses mean signed equal-opportunity difference (EOpp). A rate theorem gives the exact true-positive-rate shift required for reversal, and the measured group rates satisfy it. Within this controlled feature-space study, robustness and parity admit useful joint operating points, but one fairness metric cannot replace a full group-rate audit.

</details>


### [19] [Generalize Then Personalize: Wearable-Based Heart Rate Forecasting Under Scarce Ruck-March Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14849)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Ruizhe Ma,John W. Ramsay,David J. Cornell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在负重行军心率预测的稀缺数据场景下，对比仅人群训练(POT)、从零个性化训练(PFST)与人群预训练微调(PPFT)，发现PPFT在各骨干网络与30s/60s预测步长上总体最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心率预测可为负重行军提供近未来心血管响应的提前信息，支撑生理监测与负荷管理决策；但大规模行军数据难以采集，形成稀缺数据问题——模型需跨被试泛化，同时仅凭少量个人数据适应个体。

Method: 使用8名美国陆军现役卫生兵、13次会话的数据，对POT、PFST、PPFT三种训练范式进行基准评测；骨干网络包括LSTM、Attention-LSTM、CNN-LSTM，并与持续性(persistence)基线在30s与60s预测步长上比较；采用三种随机种子平均后的被试内配对分析，并进行Holm多重比较校正。

Result: 所有神经架构与步长下，PPFT的MAE、RMSE与终点MAE均值均低于POT与PFST。相对PFST，PPFT使轨迹MAE降低2.22–4.30 bpm、终点MAE降低1.24–2.88 bpm；6项轨迹MAE比较经Holm校正后全部显著(p_adj=0.0469)，6项终点MAE比较中4项显著。30s：持续性基线MAE最低(3.555±1.274 bpm)，CNN-LSTM PPFT的RMSE最低(4.669±1.916 bpm)，LSTM PPFT终点MAE最低(5.093±2.365 bpm)。60s：Attention-LSTM PPFT的MAE(5.573±1.939 bpm)与RMSE(7.134±2.260 bpm)最低，CNN-LSTM PPFT终点MAE最低(7.553±2.357 bpm)。

Conclusion: 人群预训练加被试特异性微调(PPFT)是稀缺数据条件下可穿戴心率预测的实用策略。需注意在30s短步长上，持续性基线在MAE上仍具竞争力，说明模型增益主要体现于RMSE与终点预测。

Abstract: Heart rate (HR) forecasting may provide advance information about near-future cardiovascular response, potentially supporting physiological monitoring and workload-management decisions during ruck marching. However, robust model development is constrained by the difficulty of collecting large-scale ruck-march datasets, creating a scarce-data problem in which models must generalize across participants while adapting to individuals from limited personal data. To address this problem, we benchmark population-only training (POT), personalized-from-scratch training (PFST), and population-pretrained fine-tuning (PPFT) for HR forecasting during loaded ruck marching using data from eight active-duty U.S. Army medics across thirteen sessions. LSTM, Attention-LSTM, and CNN-LSTM backbones were evaluated against a persistence baseline at 30 s and 60 s horizons. Across all neural architectures and horizons, PPFT yielded lower mean MAE, RMSE, and endpoint MAE than both POT and PFST. Participant-level paired analysis after averaging across three training seeds showed that, relative to PFST, PPFT reduced trajectory MAE by 2.22-4.30 bpm and endpoint MAE by 1.24-2.88 bpm. All six trajectory-MAE comparisons remained significant after Holm adjustment ($p_{adj}$ = 0.0469), while four of six endpoint-MAE comparisons remained significant after adjustment. At 30 s, persistence achieved the lowest MAE (3.555 $\pm$ 1.274 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest RMSE (4.669 $\pm$ 1.916 bpm); LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (5.093 $\pm$ 2.365 bpm). At 60 s, Attention-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest MAE (5.573 $\pm$ 1.939 bpm) and RMSE (7.134 $\pm$ 2.260 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (7.553 $\pm$ 2.357 bpm). These results suggest that population pretraining followed by participant-specific fine-tuning may provide a practical strategy for scarce-data wearable HR forecasting.

</details>


### [20] [FOE-Dreamer: Deployment-Efficient Learning of Cyber Defense Policies in Operational Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14880)
*Yinuo Du,Christopher Kiekintveld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOE-Dreamer：一种在真实运营网络中在线训练防御者的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过分解潜状态降低规划方差，3天单卡训练即达约2倍优于Rainbow/IQN的回合损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络防御多用深度强化学习，但多数工作在仿真器中训练，存在保真度差距（认证延迟、部分动作失败、服务依赖、用户流量模式未建模），迁移后性能下降；直接在真实网络中训练虽消除差距，却使交互缓慢昂贵，样本效率成为瓶颈。基于模型的RL已在高维游戏与机器人中大幅降低样本需求，但尚未用于运营网络中的防御者训练。

Method: 提出FOE-Dreamer（Factored Opponent-Enhanced Dreamer），无需仿真器阶段，直接在云基础设施网络中在线训练防御者。将潜状态变异分解为三部分：由防御者动作驱动的可控分量、由良性用户活动驱动之外生分量、以及仅从防御者可观测信号推断的对手嵌入。通过在想象中规划并分离各分量来降低策略梯度方差。实验在8主机OpenStack测试床上，使用GHOSTS框架模拟用户活动，针对两种脚本化攻击者配置进行评估。

Result: 在3天预算内、单GPU上完成训练；在同等算力下，回合损失约优于Rainbow与IQN基线2倍。消融表明分解结构与对手模型均对增益有贡献，且学到的潜表示与预期的分离结构一致。

Conclusion: 基于模型的强化学习可有效用于运营网络中在线训练的自主防御者，潜状态分解与对手建模是提升样本效率与性能的关键机制。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense is widely studied with deep reinforcement learning, but most work trains policies inside simulators because learning requires many trial-and-error interactions. Simulator-based training carries a fidelity gap: trained policies face authentication delays, partial action failures, service dependencies, and user traffic patterns that the simulator did not represent, and degrade on transfer. Training directly in the network being defended removes the gap but makes every interaction slow and costly, so sample efficiency becomes the binding constraint. Model-based reinforcement learning has reduced sample requirements on games and physical robots by orders of magnitude by planning inside a learned world model, but it has not been applied to defenders trained in operational networks. We present FOE-Dreamer (Factored Opponent-Enhanced Dreamer), a model-based reinforcement learning framework that trains a defender online and directly in a network on cloud infrastructure, without a simulator stage. FOE-Dreamer separates three sources of variation in the latent state: a controllable component driven by defender actions, an exogenous component driven by benign user activity, and an opponent embedding inferred from defender-observable signals alone. Separating the components reduces the variance in policy gradients during planning in imagination. Against two scripted attacker profiles with user activity emulated by the GHOSTS framework on an 8-host OpenStack testbed, FOE-Dreamer trains within a 3-day budget on a single GPU and achieves about 2$\times$ better episode loss than Rainbow and IQN baselines under matched compute. Both the factoring and the opponent model contribute to the gain, and the learned latents are consistent with the intended separation.

</details>


### [21] [Social Engineering Attack Detection in Corporate Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14897)
*Hanzla Hamid,Pappu Jha,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用真实数据集构建机器学习基线模型检测社会工程钓鱼邮件，TF-IDF模型准确率超90%；并进一步从邮件中抽取紧迫感、信任、贪婪等心理操纵与说服线索的可解释词典特征，单独评估其检测信号（ROC-AUC 0.74），并检验其跨邮件来源的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会工程攻击手段日益复杂，难以区分真实与钓鱼通信（邮件、聊天、电话），可能泄露商业机密并形成从低层员工到高管的攻击链，因此需要可解释、有效的检测方法，且不仅依赖表层词汇特征。

Method: 使用真实世界数据集构建基线机器学习模型（TF-IDF分类邮件为钓鱼/安全）；在此基础上，从合并邮件数据集中基于词典派生紧迫感、信任、贪婪等说服与心理操纵的可解释指标，单独评估其信号强度并在留出来源上检验其可迁移性。

Result: TF-IDF模型钓鱼邮件分类准确率超过90%；操纵导向特征单独使用时在留出测试集上ROC-AUC为0.74，并进一步测试了其向其他未见邮件来源的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 表层词汇特征可高效检测钓鱼邮件，而可解释的心理操纵/说服线索本身也携带显著检测信号，有望补充或增强传统特征，并具备跨来源应用的潜力。

Abstract: Social Engineering attacks continue to pose a significant risk to the corporate world with a growing level of sophistication. It is difficult to distinguish between authentic and phishing communication via email, chat, and phone. They pose a risk of exposing trade secrets and confidential communications and can create a chain of attack from lower-level employees up to executives. Therefore, we study this growing threat using real-world datasets to build baseline machine-learning models. Our TF-IDF models classify emails as phishing or safe, achieving accuracy above 90%. We build on this by exploring whether psychological manipulation and persuasion indicators can be measured to play a significant role in detecting phishing. Instead of relying solely on surface-level lexical features, we derive interpretable lexicon-based indicators of urgency, trust, greed, and other persuasion cues from the combined email dataset. By evaluating these manipulation-oriented features on their own, we measure how much signal they carry (ROC-AUC of 0.74 on a held-out test split) and test whether they transfer to held-out email sources.

</details>


### [22] [Epigraph Form Reinforcement Learning-based Safe Trajectory Tracking Control for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14903)
*Xiaoke Yang,Jin Chen,Yafei Wang,Qi Zhang,Ming Li,Haiwen Wu,Chengliang Yin,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STTC-EFRL框架，将安全轨迹跟踪（STT）建模为epigraph形式的min-max优化，用递减标量调节策略梯度方向而非放大梯度幅值，提升训练稳定性，并设计高效ray-casting方法量化安全系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV在高度动态环境下的安全轨迹跟踪极具挑战：非学习方法为满足实时性采用简化车辆与交互模型，跟踪精度次优；而常用的Lagrangian DRL因拉格朗日乘子增大导致策略梯度幅值放大，训练不稳定。

Method: 将STT问题构造为epigraph形式的min-max优化问题；据此设计学习机制，利用与epigraph相关的递减标量调整策略梯度下降方向而不增加梯度幅值；并开发计算高效的ray-casting方法求解多面体缩放因子以量化安全性。

Result: 实验（含Vehicle-in-the-Loop测试）表明：相比所对比的非学习基线，跟踪精度更高；相比常用Lagrangian DRL，训练更稳定；同时在网联环境下满足安全要求。

Conclusion: STTC-EFRL可同时兼顾跟踪精度、训练稳定性与安全性，为动态网联环境下的CAV安全轨迹跟踪提供了一种有效的强化学习方案。

Abstract: Safe trajectory tracking (STT) aims to enable autonomous vehicles to follow a reference trajectory accurately while avoiding collisions. This problem is particularly challenging for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) in highly dynamic environments. Existing non-learning-based STT methods often use simplified vehicle and interaction models to meet real-time requirements, which may result in suboptimal tracking accuracy. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) can help address this limitation, widely used Lagrangian DRL methods often suffer from unstable training because increasing Lagrange multipliers amplify policy-gradient magnitudes. To address these issues, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework for STT, called STT Control Epigraph Form RL (STTC-EFRL). In this framework, the STT problem is formulated as an epigraph-form min-max optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we develop a learning mechanism that uses a decreasing scalar related to the epigraph to adjust the policy gradient descent direction without increasing the gradient magnitude, thereby improving training stability. We also develop a computationally efficient ray-casting method to compute a polyhedron scaling factor for safety quantification. Experimental results, including Vehicle-in-the-Loop tests, show that STTC-EFRL achieves higher tracking accuracy than the considered non-learning-based baseline and more stable training than widely used Lagrangian DRL methods, while satisfying safety requirements in connected environments.

</details>


### [23] [Rethinking "RL Generalizes, SFT Memorizes": The Role of SFT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14912)
*Yunlong Hou,Fengzhuo Zhang,Yuan Cheng,Jiachun Pan,Xingyao Li,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从SFT训练数据视角重新审视SFT与RL的泛化差异：数据来源与数据规模是关键因素；精心混配的SFT数据显著优于大模型单独生成的数据；匹配正确rollout数量可大幅提升SFT泛化，匹配总rollout预算可使SFT达到与RL相当的泛化，二者结合甚至超越RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识普遍认为：基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练的LLM推理能力强、泛化好，而SFT更易记忆、迁移有限。作者希望检验这一区分是否由SFT训练数据本身（来源与规模）而非训练范式本质所致。

Method: 在Qwen2.5与Qwen3系列模型上开展受控实验：(1) 比较不同数据来源（精心混配的SFT数据集 vs. 仅由更大模型生成的数据）；(2) 对齐SFT与RL的正确rollout数量、再对齐总rollout预算，考察泛化差异；(3) 用LLM标注刻画训练rollout中的解题方法，分析长尾方法的覆盖与可迁移性；(4) 对浅层Transformer在RL与SFT下的训练动力学进行理论分析。

Result: 精心混配的SFT数据显著优于大模型单独生成的数据；匹配正确rollout数量可大幅提升SFT泛化；匹配总rollout预算时SFT泛化与RL相当；两因素结合后SFT泛化可优于RL。数据规模更大时覆盖更多长尾方法，且这些长尾方法提供可泛化的推理信号；理论分析支持上述实证结论。

Conclusion: SFT与RL的泛化差距并非范式固有，而很大程度上由训练数据（来源多样性与规模/rollout预算）决定；合理设计数据可使SFT达到甚至超过RL的泛化水平，长尾解题方法是泛化信号的重要来源。

Abstract: Large language models trained with Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable rewards exhibit strong reasoning ability and broad generalization, whereas models trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) are often viewed as more prone to memorization and limited transfer. This paper rethinks this distinction through the lens of SFT training data on widely used Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 series models. First, we study the role of data source and show that it is critical: a carefully mixed SFT dataset substantially outperforms data generated solely by a larger model. Second, we study the role of data scale and show that matching the number of correct rollouts between SFT and RL greatly improves SFT generalization, while matching the total rollout budget enables SFT to generalize as well as RL. Combining these two factors further enables SFT to generalize even better than RL. Third, by using LLM annotations to characterize the solution methods in training rollouts, we show that larger datasets cover more tail methods and that these tail methods provide generalizable reasoning signals. Finally, we support these empirical findings theoretically by analyzing the training dynamics of shallow transformers under both RL and SFT.

</details>


### [24] [A Transferable Pretrained 3D Model for Impedance Inversion with Sparse-Well Prior Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14922)
*Gen Li,Zhaoyun Zong,Diqiong Jiang,Man Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可迁移的三维地震波阻抗反演框架，将离线预训练与目标工区部署分离，在稀疏井条件下无需重训练即实现跨工区泛化并降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习波阻抗反演通常需针对每个目标工区重新训练，在井控极稀疏时模型难以泛化到非约束位置，部署成本高、鲁棒性差。

Method: 先用掩码自编码器（MAE）在多工区无标签数据上学习地震结构表征；将冻结的编码器嵌入反演网络，并结合合成地质模型与稀疏井导出的低频先验进行训练；部署阶段反演网络完全固定，仅有的一口条件井用于构建低频先验与全局阻抗范围对齐。

Result: Overthrust模型上取得最低的整体NRMSE，并将逐道NRMSE的P95由0.1310降至0.1074（相对最佳目标工区训练基线），在不同井位下局部大误差更少；实际工区（1口条件井、3口盲井）中，盲井平均NRMSE较目标工区训练的U-Net降低20.95%，相关性最高。

Conclusion: 该框架以低部署成本、免重训练的方式将有效阻抗信息迁移至条件井位置之外，验证了离线表征学习加固定反演网络的跨工区可行性。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based seismic impedance inversion commonly requires retraining for each target survey, which may generalize poorly beyond the conditioning locations when well control is extremely sparse. We propose a transferable three-dimensional inversion framework that separates offline learning from target-survey deployment. A masked autoencoder first learns seismic structural representations from unlabeled multi-survey data, after which the frozen encoder is incorporated into an inversion network trained on synthetic geological models with sparse-well-derived low-frequency priors. During deployment, the complete inversion network remains fixed; the available conditioning well is used only to construct the low-frequency prior and align the global impedance range. Experiments on the Overthrust model show that the proposed method achieves the lowest full-volume NRMSE and reduces the P95 trace-wise NRMSE from 0.1310 to 0.1074 relative to the best target-trained baseline, indicating fewer localized large errors under different well locations. In a field survey with one conditioning well and three blind wells, it reduces the mean blind-well NRMSE by 20.95% relative to the target-trained U-Net while maintaining the highest correlation. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework transfers useful impedance information beyond the conditioning location with low deployment cost and without retraining the inversion network.

</details>


### [25] [Binding Mode Matters: Hotspot-Aware Drug Discovery via Explorative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14956)
*Dingshuo Chen,Hao Yang,Kuangqi Zhou,Zhixun Li,Qiang Liu,Xiangxiang Zeng,Shu Wu,Yaosen Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BindMol 将基于靶点的药物设计建模为多目标探索任务，用定制多目标强化学习生成高亲和力、结构新颖且结合模式多样的分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法多以单一标量对接分数为优化目标，掩盖了各关键结合决定因素（热点相互作用）的独立贡献，且难以获得多样化的结合模式，而多样化结合模式对实验验证至关重要。

Method: 将基于靶点的分子设计重构为多目标探索问题，每个目标对应增强与某一特定热点（hotspot）的相互作用；提出生成框架 BindMol，由定制化的多目标强化学习算法驱动，并在训练中引入探索性偏好以高效发掘具有多样且理想结合特征的分子。

Result: 实验表明 BindMol 能发现兼具高亲和力、结构新颖性和多样化结合模式的化合物；在基于靶点的药物发现与多性质优化任务中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 以热点相互作用为显式多目标的强化学习范式，为面向目标的药物发现提供了通用且灵活的建模范式。

Abstract: Discovering hit molecules requires not just high binding affinity, but also the identification of diverse binding modes, which are critical for experimental assays. Existing generative approaches have predominantly relied on optimizing a scalar docking score, obscuring the distinct contributions of key binding determinants. To this end, we introduce a paradigm shift by formulating target-based drug design as a multi-objective exploration task, where each objective explicitly corresponds to enhancing interactions with a specific hotspot. Here, we introduce BindMol, a novel generative framework driven by a customized multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm. By incorporating explorative preferences during training, our approach efficiently uncovers molecules with diverse and desirable binding profiles. Empirical results demonstrate that BindMol facilitates the discovery of high-affinity compounds characterized by both structural novelty and diverse binding modes. Validated across target-based drug discovery and multi-property optimization tasks, our approach provides a versatile paradigm for goal-oriented drug discovery.

</details>


### [26] [Poison-then-Hide: Finetuning-Activated Backdoor Attack on Pretrained Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14970)
*Qixuan Jin,Abinitha Gourabathina,Vinith Menon Suriyakumar,Walter Gerych,Marzyeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Poison-then-Hide 的后门攻击：在预训练编码器中植入休眠后门，经干净数据全模型微调后仍可激活，六个数据集、三种架构上达最高97%攻击成功率，且标准检测与缓解防御难以彻底移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 finetuning-activated（微调激活）后门攻击假定域偏移有限或编码器层被冻结，在整模型全参数微调下往往失效；需要一种在域迁移、全模型微调场景下依然隐蔽有效的攻击。

Method: Poison-then-Hide 由三部分组成：(1) 触发器优化；(2) 基础编码器投毒；(3) 定向遗忘（targeted unlearning）以隐藏后门。关键设计是在投毒时联合学习良性目标任务，使后门在干净下游数据微调时被激活。

Result: 在六个数据集、三种模型架构上评估，攻击成功率达当前最优（最高约97%）；联合学习良性目标任务对攻击成功至关重要；标准检测与缓解防御无法完全去除后门，后门在良性微调后可能重新出现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明整模型全参数微调并不能消除预训练编码器中的休眠后门，现有防御存在明显不足，需针对微调激活型后门设计更强的检测与缓解手段。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks threaten the integrity of machine learning models by allowing attackers to control model behavior through triggers. Models can be compromised during finetuning by backdoor attacks through either poisoned data or adversarial training objectives. Because existing finetuning-activated attacks assume limited domain shift or frozen encoder layers, they often fail under full-model finetuning. We target a stealthy finetuning-activated attack where a dormant backdoor is implanted in a pretrained encoder, and later activated by finetuning on clean downstream data. We propose Poison-then-Hide, a novel attack that remains effective when the entire model is finetuned in a domain transfer. Our approach consists of three components: trigger optimization, base encoder poisoning, and targeted unlearning to conceal the backdoor. We evaluate our method on six datasets and three model architectures, and achieve state-of-the-art (up to 97%) attack success rates. We find that jointly learning the benign target task alongside the poisoning is critical to the attack's success. We demonstrate standard detection and mitigation defenses cannot fully remove the backdoor, which can reappear after benign finetuning.

</details>


### [27] [EASTAR: Event-Anchored Semantic and Task-Adaptive Region Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14990)
*Peng Liang,Genan Dai,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EASTAR框架：以线下活动事件为稀疏语义锚点，将聚合移动性表示分解为多个目的相关语义通道，并辅以自由通道与全局通道，再用基于LLM先验与下游监督调优的任务自适应提示路由器完成区域表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于移动性的区域表示方法多使用聚合OD矩阵，不同活动目的的出行被混合为与目的无关的交互模式；POI、土地利用等静态属性虽能补充语义，却未将移动交互显式锚定到活动目的信号上，导致下游适配只能在纠缠的嵌入上进行，难以选取任务相关的功能语义。

Method: EASTAR包含三部分：(1) 以线下活动事件作为稀疏语义锚点，将聚合移动性表示分解为多个与目的相关的语义通道；(2) 引入自由通道捕获事件数据未覆盖的潜在移动模式，并设全局通道保留整体空间结构；(3) 设计任务自适应提示路由器，用LLM导出的先验初始化任务-通道相关性，并通过下游监督精化路由权重。

Result: 在两个真实城市数据集、四类预测任务上，EASTAR一致优于代表性基线，MAE最多降低39.15%；消融与可解释性分析验证了事件锚定、语义通道构建与任务自适应路由的有效性。

Conclusion: 以活动事件为锚点对移动性表示进行目的化通道分解，并结合任务自适应路由，可有效缓解目的无关交互模式的纠缠问题，实现任务相关的功能语义选择，提升区域表示在下游任务中的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning supports a wide range of urban analytics tasks by encoding regional functions and interactions, with human mobility serving as a central signal because it directly captures inter-region interactions. However, most mobility-based methods learn from aggregated origin-destination matrices, where trips driven by different activity purposes are mixed into purpose-agnostic interaction patterns. Although static urban attributes such as POIs and land use can enrich region-level semantics, they do not explicitly ground mobility interactions in activity-purpose signals. As a result, downstream adaptation is often performed over entangled embeddings, making it difficult to select task-relevant functional semantics. To address these limitations, we propose EASTAR, an event-anchored and task-adaptive framework for urban region representation learning. EASTAR treats offline activity events as sparse semantic anchors and uses them to factorize aggregated mobility representations into multiple purpose-related semantic channels. It further introduces free channels to capture latent mobility patterns not covered by event data and a global channel to preserve overall spatial structure. To adapt the learned channel space to different downstream objectives, EASTAR designs a task-adaptive prompt router that initializes task-channel relevance from LLM-derived priors and refines the routing weights through downstream supervision. Experiments on two real-world urban datasets across four prediction tasks show that EASTAR consistently outperforms representative baselines, achieving up to 39.15% reduction in MAE. Ablation and interpretability analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of event anchoring, semantic channel construction, and task-adaptive routing.

</details>


### [28] [AI4Plasma I: Data-Efficient Surrogate Modeling of Capacitively Coupled Plasma Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15002)
*Zhenyu Zou,Junjue Liao,Hao Yang,Luowei Yin,Chenyu Wang,Yufeng Wei,Zhichao Peng,Chun Chen,Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Kangxin Liu,Haoran Guan,Rubing Han,Yipei Chen,Peiting You,Yuwei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较五种代理模型（Coordinate MLP、PCA-Net、U-Net、FNO、Transolver）在CCP等离子体场预测中的数据效率，FNO在多数情形下所需参考仿真最少，但最优架构取决于精度目标与参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考电容耦合等离子体（CCP）仿真可给出空间分辨的等离子体场，但跨工况生成代价高昂，需评估不同代理架构在以少量参考仿真达到给定精度时的数据效率。

Method: 在1D平面与2D轴对称GEC CCP基准上，对比五种代理架构预测射频周期平均的电子密度、离子密度、电势与电子温度；以达到1%和2%平均相对L2误差所需的参考仿真数为指标，区分参数内部与参数边界测试情形，并分析FNO谱模态与padding、U-Net跳跃连接、Transolver自适应切片、PCA-Net投影误差与预测误差等机制。

Result: 八项比较中FNO有七项所需参考案例最少或并列最少。1D中FNO以50例达1%内部误差（Transolver 100、U-Net 200）；2D中FNO与U-Net均以100例达1%内部误差，FNO与Transolver均以200例达2%边界误差（U-Net 400）；更严格的2D 1%边界目标下Transolver需400例、FNO需800例。

Conclusion: 在所测试的CCP设置下，FNO可作为数据高效基线；但首选架构依赖于精度目标与运行参数区域，不存在单一最优代理模型。

Abstract: Reference capacitively coupled plasma (CCP) simulations provide spatially resolved plasma fields but are costly to generate across operating conditions. We compare the data efficiency of five surrogate architectures--Coordinate MLP, PCA-Net, U-Net, Fourier neural operator (FNO), and Transolver--for predicting RF-cycle-averaged electron density, ion density, electric potential, and electron temperature in 1D planar and 2D axisymmetric GEC CCP benchmarks. We assess the number of reference simulations needed to achieve 1% and 2% mean relative L2 error for parameter-interior and parameter-boundary test cases, and examine architecture-specific mechanisms. FNO achieves the lowest or joint-lowest reference-case requirement in seven of eight comparisons. In 1D, FNO reaches 1% interior error with 50 training cases, compared with 100 for Transolver and 200 for U-Net. In 2D, FNO and U-Net each reach 1% interior error with 100 cases; FNO and Transolver each reach 2% parameter-boundary error with 200 cases, compared with 400 for U-Net. At the stricter 1% 2D parameter-boundary target, Transolver requires 400 cases versus 800 for FNO. Additional analyses investigate FNO spectral modes and padding, U-Net skip connections, Transolver adaptive slicing, and PCA-Net projection versus prediction error. The results support FNO as a data-efficient baseline for the tested CCP settings, while showing that the preferred architecture depends on the accuracy target and operating-parameter region.

</details>


### [29] [A Cardiorespiratory Foundation Model Pretrained on Ten Physiological Signals for Perioperative and Critical Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15004)
*Cheol-Hui Lee,Wookyung Kim,Hyeonhoon Lee,Garam Lee,Da-in Eun,Hyung-Chul Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARMEN 是一个在十种心肺波形（227万模态小时）上无标签预训练的统一编码器，可接受任意共录信号组合与10秒至5分钟长度，配合 LSCNorm 恢复绝对信号水平，在19项任务上优于监督模型与三个基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理波形基础模型几乎只基于心电和光电容积脉搏波，而临床决策依赖动脉压、静脉压、肺动脉压、颅内压及呼吸机波形等多模态信号，缺乏覆盖这些波形的通用基础模型。

Method: 提出 CARMEN：单一编码器在十种心肺波形（2.27M 模态小时）上无标签预训练；支持任意共录信号组合输入，长度10秒至5分钟；引入条件机制 LSCNorm，恢复归一化过程中丢失的绝对信号水平。

Result: 在韩国与美国队列的19项任务中，冻结编码器在15项中的12项优于监督模型，在19项中的14–17项优于三个基础模型（一个通用模型、两个生物信号模型），无任一任务被超越；仅用心电或 PPG 时在多数比较中匹配或超过专用基础模型；缺失通道下性能保持。消融显示无 LSCNorm 时术中低血压 AUROC 由0.894降至0.780。

Conclusion: 跨模态、跨队列的多波形统一预训练可构建强通用表征，且绝对幅值信息对低血压等任务至关重要；模型对通道缺失与信号子集具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Arterial, venous, pulmonary artery and intracranial pressures and ventilator waveforms guide decisions in operating rooms and intensive care units, yet foundation models for physiological waveforms have been built almost entirely on the electrocardiogram and photoplethysmogram. We present CARMEN, a single encoder pretrained without labels on ten cardiorespiratory waveforms (2.27 million modality-hours). CARMEN accepts any combination of co-recorded signals from 10 s to 5 min, and a conditioning mechanism, LSCNorm, restores the absolute signal level that normalization removes; without it, the AUROC for intraoperative hypotension fell from 0.894 to 0.780. Across 19 tasks from Korean and US cohorts, the frozen encoder outperformed supervised models on 12 of 15 and three foundation models, one general-purpose and two for biosignals, on 14 to 17 of 19, with none exceeding it on any task. Given the electrocardiogram or photoplethysmogram alone, it matched or exceeded signal-specific foundation models in most comparisons, and it retained its performance when channels were missing.

</details>


### [30] [From Forecasting to World Modeling: Can Temporal Foundation Models Recover Causal Response?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15009)
*Juwon Hong,Minha Hwang,Venkatesh Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估表格与时序基础模型（含专为因果效应估计预训练的基础模型）在价格效应估计上的表现，发现预测精度不能指示价格效应精度；对表格基础模型而言，增加上下文改善预测但不改善价格效应；工具变量仅在特定形式下降低误差，原始工具变量在混杂下反而增大误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售商关注价格变动如何影响销量（因果效应），但实践中常以预测精度作为模型选择依据。作者质疑“预测准确即因果效应准确”的隐含假设，考察基础模型在价格效应估计上的可靠性。

Method: 评估已发布的表格基础模型、时序基础模型，以及专门为因果效应估计预训练的基础模型；在已知真实效应的模拟数据与真实零售销售记录上进行对比，并检验上下文数量与工具变量（原始形式与特定编码形式）的作用。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 在模拟数据与真实零售数据上，预测精度均不能指示价格效应估计的准确度；(2) 对表格基础模型，更多上下文提升预测误差表现，但不改善价格效应估计；(3) 工具变量仅在以特定形式提供时降低误差；在混杂条件下以原始工具变量形式提供时，对每个模型都使误差增大。

Conclusion: 预测性能与因果效应估计性能存在系统性脱节，不能以预测精度作为价格决策依据；上下文与原始工具变量并非可靠的补救手段，需针对因果目标专门设计与评估模型及输入形式。

Abstract: A model that forecasts sales accurately can still mislead a retailer about how sales respond to a price change. We evaluate released tabular and time-series foundation models, as well as a foundation model pretrained specifically to estimate causal effects. We report three findings. First, forecast accuracy does not indicate the accuracy of the predicted effect of price, both on simulated data in which the true effect is known and on real retail sales records. Second, for the tabular foundation models, additional context improves forecasts but not the predicted effect of price. Third, instrumental variables, the standard remedy in econometrics, reduce this error only when supplied in a specific form and increase it for every model under confounding when supplied as raw instruments.

</details>


### [31] [SkinAdvisor: An AI-Driven Decision Framework for Skin Production Planning via Multi-Modal Transformer Sales Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15023)
*Ge Fan,Kai Wang,Chaoyun Zhang,Yingjie Li,Yuze Liu,Junyang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkinAdvisor 皮肤生产决策框架，其核心预测模型 SkinCast 基于多模态 Transformer，可对未发布皮肤进行 180 天逐日销量轨迹预测，并已上线运行一个完整发行周期。离线实验 RMSE 较最强基线 Informer 降低约 20%、较经典方法降低约 60%；16 核 CPU 上 p99 延迟 <8 ms、峰值吞吐 >1100 rps。文章总结两条部署经验：数量级精度已足以支撑生产决策；预训练嵌入可能泄露时间信息，需采用按赛季切分来防止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏内皮肤是当前在线游戏的主要收入来源，但"做哪些皮肤、何时发布、如何定价"等生产决策仍主要依赖直觉与事后分析，缺乏前瞻性的定量预测支持，因此需要一个能预测未发布皮肤销售表现并据此优化的决策系统。

Method: 1) 预测模型 SkinCast：四路编码器——预训练 Informer 编码玩家偏好时间序列、预训练 Player-Hero-Skin 图嵌入提供关系先验、两个轻量 Transformer 分别编码皮肤属性与发布上下文；通过共享骨干融合，采用局部+全局的 day-x 位置编码，由 N 层 Informer 解码器单次非自回归输出 180 天逐日销量。2) 决策框架 SkinAdvisor：将预测接入 Select → Fill → Infer → Sales → ROI → Ranking 的服务流水线，按预测 ROI 对候选皮肤排序并支持多方案对比。

Result: 离线实验中 SkinCast 相比最强基线 Informer 的 RMSE 降低约 20%，相比经典方法降低约 60%；16 核 CPU 服务器压力测试显示 p99 延迟低于 8 ms、峰值吞吐高于 1100 rps；系统已在生产环境运行完整一个发行周期。

Conclusion: 多模态 Transformer 可有效支撑皮肤生产规划，但工程上有两点关键经验：其一，生产决策只需数量级准确性，无需追求极致精度；其二，预训练嵌入可能引入时间信息泄露，必须通过按赛季切分数据来规避，以保证评估可信。

Abstract: Skins, cosmetic in-game items, are a primary revenue stream for modern online games, yet production decisions (which skins to make, when to release, how to price) remain driven by intuition and retrospective analytics. We present SkinAdvisor, an AI-driven decision framework for skin production planning, powered by SkinCast, a deployed multi-modal Transformer forecasting 180-day per-day sales trajectories for unreleased skins. SkinCast couples four encoders: a pretrained Informer for player-preference time series, a pretrained Player-Hero-Skin graph embedder for relational priors, and two lightweight Transformers for skin attributes and release context, fused through a shared backbone with a local-plus-global day-x positional encoding and decoded by an N-layer Informer in a single non-autoregressive pass. SkinAdvisor plugs these forecasts into a serving pipeline (Select -> Fill -> Infer -> Sales -> ROI -> Ranking) that ranks candidate skins by forecast-based ROI and supports multi-candidate comparison. The system has been in production for a full release cycle. Offline experiments show SkinCast reduces RMSE by ~20% over the strongest baseline (Informer) and ~60% over classical methods; stress tests on a 16-core CPU server confirm p99 latency under 8ms and peak throughput above 1100 rps. We report two deployment lessons: order-of-magnitude accuracy suffices for production decisions, and pretrained embeddings can leak temporal information, requiring season-based splitting to prevent.

</details>


### [32] [Mask-Pattern Position Augmentation: Training-Free Long-Context Extrapolation for Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15029)
*Gaurav Sarkar,Syed Affan Daimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MPPA，一种免训练的长上下文外推方法：在把位置索引线性插值到预训练窗口的基础上，叠加由累积掩码密度决定的逐位置、逐去噪步偏移量，使扩散大模型在 2× 窗口下困惑度大幅下降并保持检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大模型（dLLM）用双向注意力迭代解掩码，但 RoPE 仍将其限制在固定预训练窗口内。现有免训练外推方法（如 LongLLaDA）直接照搬自回归模型的 NTK-RoPE 缩放，对所有位置、所有去噪步施加同一个仅依赖长度的静态变换，忽略了 dLLM 独有的信号——部分解码序列中不断演化的掩码模式。

Method: MPPA 先将位置索引线性插值映射回预训练窗口，再依据累积掩码密度为每个位置、每个去噪步添加一个偏移量：该偏移可恢复插值所压缩的旋转相位分辨率，并随已确定 token 增多而衰减，使后期步骤趋近干净的插值几何。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 上、窗口为 4096 token 的 2 倍长度处，8192 token 时困惑度由标准解码的 19.51 降至 3.12（低于窗口内水平），passkey 检索准确率 100%，needle-in-a-haystack 精度不变。

Conclusion: 初步的 2× 结果表明，掩码条件化的位置工程设计是通向扩散原生长上下文生成的有效路径；作者定位为初步结果，尚未验证更长外推比例。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate by iteratively unmasking tokens with bidirectional attention, but rotary position embeddings (RoPE) still confine them to a fixed pretraining window. Existing training-free extrapolation, notably LongLLaDA, copies NTK-based RoPE scaling from autoregressive (AR) models and applies one static, length-only transform at every position and denoising step. That transplant ignores a signal unique to dLLMs: the evolving mask pattern of the partially decoded sequence. We propose Mask-Pattern Position Augmentation (MPPA). MPPA linearly interpolates indices into the pretrained window, then adds a per-position, per-step offset from cumulative mask density. The offset restores rotary phase resolution that interpolation collapses, and it shrinks as tokens are committed, so late steps see near-clean interpolated geometry. On LLaDA-8B-Instruct at twice its 4096-token window, MPPA reduces perplexity from 19.51 under standard decoding to 3.12 at 8192 tokens, below the in-window figure, with 100% passkey retrieval and unchanged needle-in-a-haystack accuracy. These preliminary $2\times$ results motivate mask-conditioned position engineering as a diffusion-native path to long-context generation.

</details>


### [33] [AROMA: Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation for Vision-Language Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15041)
*Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AROMA是一个冻结VLM控制框架，将可解释性定位、激活调控、可达性、目标选择与选择性部署拆分为五个独立控制问题，并在Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct上验证其各阶段有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制定位本身不等于可用控制：因果敏感可能只占极少激活能量，局部方向在有限尺度下失效，可达的数值目标也可能本身错误。因此需要区分并分别解决"哪里干预、用何种几何与增益、如何到达目标、追求哪个目标、何时行动"。

Method: 提出AROMA（自适应表征导向多模态驱动）冻结VLM控制框架。以因果验证的跨注意力头为干预点，采用幅度归一化的因果子空间驱动转换能量失配；提出迭代最小努力驱动（MEA）在固定步数预算下追踪目标；在评估前冻结目标与门控，进行前瞻性路由，并在多头、多模型上重复验证，与归因引导基线对比。

Result: 单一跨注意力头的四个维度解释了89.82%的基数敏感性但仅9.52%的中位激活能量；幅度归一化驱动使未触碰的程序化数据精确计数准确率提升+8.00 pp。MEA在等步数预算下达到81.25%的oracle目标（冻结方向仅15.18%）。冻结目标与门控后，在N=630的新自然Stage-2队列上由34.444%提升至38.730%（配对p=4.63e-7），全量7,992例为65.741% vs 65.153%，且仅编辑63个输入。

Conclusion: 定位、驱动、可达性、目标质量和选择性部署应被视为相互独立的控制问题；AROMA通过分离这些阶段实现了有限尺度下可用的精确控制，并在多模型与基线对比中得到验证。

Abstract: Mechanistic localization does not by itself yield usable control: causal sensitivity may occupy little activation energy, an accurate local direction may fail at finite scale, and a reachable numeral target may be wrong. We introduce AROMA (Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation), a frozen-VLM control framework separating where to intervene, which geometry and gain to use, how to reach a target, which target to pursue, and when to act. In Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct, four dimensions of a causally validated cross-attention head explain 89.82% of cardinality sensitivity but only 9.52% of median activation energy; magnitude-normalized causal subspace actuation converts this mismatch into finite control, improving exact-count accuracy on untouched procedural data by +8.00 pp. Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation (MEA) reaches 81.25% of oracle targets versus 15.18% for a frozen direction under matched step budgets. With target and gate frozen before evaluation, targeted MEA improves the previous frozen policy from 34.444% to 38.730% on a new natural Stage-2 cohort (N=630; paired p=4.63e-7) and yields 65.741% versus 65.153% over all 7,992 examples while editing only 63 inputs. Prospective routing, replication across heads and models, and comparisons with attribution-steering baselines further characterize each stage, separating localization, actuation, reachability, target quality, and selective deployment as distinct control problems.

</details>


### [34] [PhysXAI: A Theoretical Framework for Diagnosing Physical Hallucinations in Weather Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15053)
*Anjan Mahapatra,Nikhil Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PhysXAI 诊断框架，用于检测、定位并归因全球天气预报模型产生的"物理幻觉"（违反质量守恒、静力平衡、谱能量标度等物理定律的输出），实现对预报误差与物理合理性的解耦诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习全球天气预报模型精度高，却常输出违反基本物理定律的"物理幻觉"状态；现有事后指标套件无法将预报误差与物理合理性分离，缺乏可学习的、可解释的诊断手段。

Method: 通过数值残差与物理残差的联合条件形式化问题；构建多分支编码器，从守恒、动力学、谱、跨变量、时空残差中提取表示；引入物理一致性对比学习目标（理论上证明潜在表示近似等距于物理残差空间）；推导带梯度加权自适应多目标损失；输出空间分辨的幻觉图、违规类型分类及校准的贝叶斯不确定性。

Result: 在 ERA5、WeatherBench 2、MERRA-2 上，对 GraphCast、Pangu-Weather、NeuralGCM 的预报进行对比评估；理论分析与实证结果共同表明 PhysXAI 能实现物理不一致性的检测与可解释归因，代码与实验协议完全可复现。

Conclusion: PhysXAI 为诊断物理幻觉提供了有原则的框架，可推动可信天气预报 AI 的发展，兼具检测能力与可解释归因能力。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models for global weather forecasting achieve remarkable predictive accuracy, yet they frequently produce states that violate fundamental physical laws, including conservation of mass, hydrostatic balance, or spectral energy scaling. We term these outputs physical hallucinations and propose PhysXAI, a rigorous diagnostic framework that detects, localises, and attributes such inconsistencies. Unlike existing post-hoc metric suites, PhysXAI is a learnable system that explicitly decouples forecast error from physical plausibility. We formalise the problem through a novel joint condition on numerical and physical residuals, and introduce a multi-branch encoder that extracts representations from conservation, dynamical, spectral, cross-variable, and spatiotemporal residuals. A key innovation is a physics-consistency contrastive learning objective, which we theoretically show the latent representation to be approximately isometric to the physical residual space, ensuring that diagnostic decisions remain grounded in physically meaningful deviations. We further derive an adaptive multi-objective loss with gradient-based weighting to balance competing diagnostic objectives. The framework produces spatially resolved hallucination maps, violation-type classifications, and calibrated Bayesian uncertainty, enabling both detection and interpretable attribution of physical inconsistencies.
