We conduct a comprehensive comparative evaluation of PhysXAI across ERA5, WeatherBench 2, and MERRA-2, using forecasts from GraphCast, Pangu-Weather, and NeuralGCM, with Python-written code and a fully reproducible experimental protocol. Our theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation establish PhysXAI as a principled framework for diagnosing physical hallucinations and advancing trustworthy AI for weather forecasting.

</details>


### [35] [Probabilistic State Space Enumeration in Neural Network Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15062)
*Victor O. K. Li,Yang Han,Jacqueline C. K. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Transformer MLP的概率状态空间框架：将中间维度分组并为二元包含状态定义输入相关分布，用重要性校正的掩码输出使期望严格等于稠密MLP输出；配合NORDER-II枚举高概率联合状态实现任意时刻近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似方法对单个计算组件（如通道）独立排序与保留，忽略了组件间的联合结构；作者希望把稠密神经计算精确表示为组合状态空间上的期望，并以最可能的少数配置来近似。

Method: 将MLP中间维度划分为通道组，定义输入相关的二元包含状态分布；采用重要性校正的掩码输出，保证全期望精确恢复稠密输出。算法NORDER-II在无需构造或排序指数级状态空间的前提下，按概率递减顺序枚举完整联合状态；截断该顺序得到受累积概率质量与状态预算控制的anytime近似。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B与Gemma-2-2B上，状态预算B=2048时MLP输出近似精度为92.4%与93.8%，Channel Top-k为76.9%与79.8%；联合近似首/中/末MLP层时，WikiText-2困惑度相对稠密推理仅差0.06%与0.54%；相对expected-count Grouped Top-k取得82.6%–126.4%的相对精度提升，仅增加0.18%–0.19%的局部分析FLOPs。

Conclusion: 联合状态结构比原子显著性更重要；稠密层可视为组合计算状态上的结构化概率推断，为预训练网络中的自适应、概率质量可控计算开辟新路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks typically realize dense computation by propagating information through all intermediate channels, while many approximation methods rank and retain individual computational components. We ask whether dense neural computation can instead be represented exactly as an expectation over a combinatorial state space, and approximated by evaluating only its most probable configurations. We introduce a novel probabilistic state-space framework for Transformer MLPs. The framework partitions the intermediate dimension into channel groups, defines an input-dependent distribution over binary inclusion states, and uses importance-corrected masked outputs such that the full expectation exactly recovers the dense MLP output. The key algorithmic novelty is NORDER-II, which enumerates complete joint states in decreasing probability without constructing or sorting the exponentially large state space. Truncating this ordering yields an anytime approximation governed by cumulative probability mass and a state budget. On Llama-3.2-1B and Gemma-2-2B, NORDER-II at a state budget of B=2048 achieves 92.4% and 93.8% MLP-output approximation accuracy, compared with 76.9% and 79.8% for Channel Top-k. When the first, middle, and last MLP layers are approximated jointly, WikiText-2 perplexity remains within 0.06% and 0.54% of dense inference. Relative to expected-count Grouped Top-k, NORDER-II yields 82.6% to 126.4% relative accuracy gains with only 0.18% to 0.19% additional local analytic FLOPs. These results establish the significance of joint-state structure beyond atomic salience and point to a new paradigm for neural computation: dense layers can be viewed as structured probabilistic inference over combinatorial computational states. This framework opens a path toward adaptive, probability-mass-controlled computation in pretrained neural networks.

</details>


### [36] [CoLaX: Context-Aware Local Explanations for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15067)
*Kexuan Zhang,Paolo Frazzetto,Xiaobei Zou,Gustau Camps-Valls,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoLaX——一个上下文感知的时间序列分类器事后解释框架，用全序列学习的表示对局部模式相关性进行条件化，再做Shapley归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列事后解释方法仅对局部模式（子序列、shapelet）打分，忽略序列其余部分；但同一motif在不同全局时序条件（趋势、regime）下预测含义可能不同。

Method: 基于shapelet驱动的Shapley归因；全局上下文在两级起作用：(1)生成样本特定的原型权重；(2)产生时间分辨信号以重加权局部激活图。由此得到的上下文条件化片段作为Shapley归因博弈的参与者，其值仅来自冻结的黑盒分类器。

Result: 在相同motif随上下文改变相关性的合成基准上，AUPRC平均比最强基线提升30%；在真实数据ECG上取得最高AUPRC。消融表明增益来自输入依赖的条件化，而非额外可学习权重。

Conclusion: CoLaX最适用于循环出现的局部motif在不同序列级上下文中相关性发生变化的情形。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanations for time series classifiers usually score local patterns, such as subsequences or shapelets, without regard to the rest of the sequence. Yet the same motif can carry different predictive meaning under different global temporal conditions, such as trends or regimes. We propose CoLaX, a context-aware post-hoc explanation framework that builds on shapelet-driven Shapley attribution and conditions local-pattern relevance on a learned representation of the full sequence. Global context acts at two levels: it produces sample-specific prototype weights and a temporally resolved signal that reweights the local activation map. The resulting context-conditioned segments are the players of a Shapley attribution game whose values come only from the frozen black-box classifier. On synthetic benchmarks where identical motifs change relevance across contexts, CoLaX improves AUPRC over the strongest baseline by 30\% on average. On real-world data, it achieves the highest AUPRC on ECG. Additional ablations indicate that the gains come from input-dependent conditioning rather than from additional learnable weights. CoLaX is therefore most useful when recurring local motifs change relevance across sequence-level contexts.

</details>


### [37] [When Does Causality Help in Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15073)
*Long Zhang,Wei Huang,Aishui Rao,Jian Hu,Binxiang Wu,Jie Li,Chen Gong,Linxi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过固定因果引导自编码器DeCauAE、系统改变图质量、异常类型与评测协议，论文刻画了因果感知评分在多变量时序异常检测中"何时有效"：图质量决定收益、异常类型决定目标、协议决定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知评分在多变量时间序列异常检测中很常见，但文献很少说明它在什么条件下才真正带来收益，缺乏对有效性边界的系统性声明与证据。

Method: 控制变量式消融：固定DeCauAE，以真实因果图（ground truth）为参照，分别扰动（1）图质量（二十变量DAG逐步损坏）、（2）异常类型与破坏严重度s、（3）评测协议（point-adjusted F1 与 range-aware VUS）；并在真实基准上检验因果模块的启用条件，用跨子样本可复现性选择实体。

Result: 图质量决定收益大小：图被损坏时相对无图自编码器的增益衰减。异常类型决定目标：在破坏条件结构的异常上，结构化因子将窗口F1从0.800提升到0.976，而当破坏严重度s<0.3时优势消失。协议决定排序：point-adjusted F1下随机分数即可匹配完整模型，而range-aware VUS能区分自编码器族与经典基线。真实基准上因果模块仅在密度使系统具备资格处有效，全局启用会损失0.012 VUS-ROC。

Conclusion: 因果感知评分的收益是有条件的：需同时满足图质量足够、异常破坏条件结构且严重度足够、以及采用范围感知协议；真实场景中应仅对满足密度资格且跨子样本可复现的实体启用因果模块。结构化因子还可迁移至经典检测器。

Abstract: Causality-aware scoring is common in multivariate time-series anomaly detection, yet the literature rarely states when it helps. Holding one causality-guided autoencoder (DeCauAE) fixed, we vary graph quality, anomaly type and protocol against ground truth. Graph quality fixes the payoff: on a twenty-variable DAG the gain over a graph-free autoencoder decays as the graph is corrupted. Anomaly type fixes the target: the structural factor lifts window F1 from 0.800 to 0.976 where anomalies break conditional structure, and its advantage vanishes below a break severity s 0.3. Protocol fixes the ranking: point-adjusted F1 lets a random score match the full model, while range-aware VUS separates the autoencoder family from the classical baselines. On real benchmarks the causal module pays only where density makes a system eligible and cross-subsample reproducibility selects entities inside it; enabling it everywhere costs 0.012 VUS-ROC. The structural factor also transfers to classical detectors .

</details>


### [38] [Profile-Based Auditing of Domestic Violence Survey Selection Across South Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15106)
*Mohammad Abu Obaida Mullick,Rezaur Rahman Ratul,Mymuna Nourin,Md. Motaharul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用可解释机器学习审计DHS家庭暴力模块的选择性进入问题：基于南亚5国796,374名15-49岁女性的数据构建人口画像，最佳模型HistGradientBoosting达ROC-AUC 0.9167、PR-AUC 0.9612，显著优于仅基于抽样设计的基线（0.8475/0.9203），表明画像信号可揭示正式抽样设计之外的差异化覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DHS家庭暴力数据通过专门模块采集，其住户层面抽样与隐私要求形成了独特的观测过程，可能导致样本选择性进入。作者希望检验人口、社会经济与住户特征能否形成可复现的总体画像，以及这些画像是否携带关于DV模块选择进入的信息。

Method: 使用阿富汗、印度、缅甸、尼泊尔、巴基斯坦五国DHS Individual Recode数据（796,374名15-49岁女性），统一整合61个数值特征与8个分类特征；用PCA降维画像空间并识别出6类可解释画像；在22种机器学习模型上采用以初级抽样单元（PSU）为感知的留出验证进行评估。

Result: 最优模型HistGradientBoosting取得ROC-AUC 0.9167与PR-AUC 0.9612，而仅基于抽样设计的基线为0.8475与0.9203。加入女性个体层面特征带来最大增量收益，而显式交互项对样本外性能提升很小。分国分析显示显著异质性，留一国交叉评估表明增量画像信号的可迁移性有限。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习可用于审计敏感调查模块中的差异化覆盖，其洞察超越正式抽样设计本身；同时提示纳入个体特征带来的增量信号在跨国迁移上存在局限。

Abstract: Domestic violence (DV) data in the Demographic and Health Surveys (DHS) are collected through a specialized module whose household-level sampling and privacy requirements create a distinct observation process. We then evaluate 22 machine-learning models under primary-sampling-unit-aware holdout validation. The best model, HistGradientBoosting, achieves ROC-AUC 0.9167 and PR-AUC 0.9612, versus 0.8475 and 0.9203 for a design-only baseline. Adding woman-level characteristics yields the largest incremental gain, while explicit interaction terms add little out-of-sample performance. Country-specific analyses reveal substantial heterogeneity, and leave-one-country-out evaluation shows that transferability of the incremental profile signal is limited. These results demonstrate that interpretable ML can audit differential coverage in sensitive survey modules beyond the formal sampling design.

TL;DR: 该论文指出ESMM部署的pCVR塔与线上单独训练的pCTR模型搭配时会产生pCTCVR的训练-服务不匹配问题，提出用知识蒸馏以线上pCTR得分监督ESMM辅助pCTR塔，从而改善pCTCVR对齐；在Ali-CCP上最优变体KD-0.3同时提升了pCTCVR与pCVR的AUC及PR-AUC。

Abstract: Recommendation systems may use predicted click-through conversion rate (pCTCVR), the product of predicted click-through rate (pCTR) and post-click conversion rate (pCVR), to guide ranking decisions. Many pCVR models build on the Entire Space Multi-Task Model (ESMM), which jointly trains pCVR and auxiliary pCTR towers using pCTR and pCTCVR losses, but deploys only the pCVR tower. At serving time, this tower may be paired with a separately trained pCTR model, creating a training-serving mismatch in pCTCVR when its predictions differ from those of the auxiliary pCTR tower. In this paper, we use knowledge distillation (KD) from logged serving pCTR scores to supervise ESMM's auxiliary pCTR tower and improve training-serving pCTCVR alignment. We denote variants by KD-$α$, where $α$ controls the hard-label loss weight. On the Ali-CCP dataset, KD-0.3, the best variant on serving pCTCVR, increases ROC-AUC not only for serving pCTCVR but also for pCVR by 2.1% and 1.6%, respectively, and PR-AUC by 9.9% and 8.8%, respectively, relative to the ESMM baseline. We also test negative downsampling with 5% nonclick retention. Downsampled KD-0.3 increases mean AUC for serving pCTCVR and pCVR by 1.2% and 0.9%, respectively, -and PR-AUC by 8.5% and 4.5%, respectively, relative to downsampled ESMM.

</details>


### [40] [Knowledge Transfer for Conditional Flow Matching: Frozen Estimation and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15150)
*Yian Huang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向条件流匹配的迁移学习框架（TFM-Freeze 与 TFM-Adapt），并给出条件平方 Wasserstein 风险的界限、信息下界与无负迁移的验证选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标域条件生成建模数据有限，而相关源域群体的表征可被利用；但源域与目标域在响应分布和维度上可能不同，需要理论刻画迁移与适配的收益与风险。

Method: 基于从源数据学到的表征，提出两种流程：TFM-Freeze 固定表征仅估计目标速度头；TFM-Adapt 在函数锚定惩罚下联合学习速度头与表征适配。针对显式神经网络类建立条件平方 Wasserstein 风险界，并推导信息下界；另提出独立验证的生成器选择流程（含 oracle 不等式与切换规则）。

Result: 风险界分解为源估计、目标拟合、表征失配与数值积分误差；当源表征存在光滑修正时，适配将冻结失配项替换为学习修正的代价加可控正则偏差；信息下界给出适配严格更快风险率的区域；切换规则以高概率保证条件能量风险不高于仅用目标数据的生成器。

Conclusion: 适配在可通过光滑修正弥合表征失配时优于冻结迁移，且所提验证选择能有效防止负迁移；仿真与真实数据实验支持上述理论结果。

Abstract: Conditional generative modeling with limited target data can benefit from representations learned in a related source population. We develop a transfer learning framework for conditional flow matching that accommodates distinct source and target response distributions and dimensions. Two procedures exploit a representation learned from source data: TFM-Freeze estimates a target velocity head with the representation fixed, whereas TFM-Adapt jointly learns the head and a representation adaptation under a functional anchoring penalty. For explicit neural network classes, we establish bounds on conditional squared Wasserstein risk that account for source estimation, target fitting, representation mismatch, and numerical integration. When the source representation admits a smooth correction, adaptation replaces the frozen mismatch contribution by the cost of learning the correction and a controllable regularization bias. An information lower bound identifies regimes in which adaptation attains a strictly faster conditional squared Wasserstein risk rate than any generator restricted to the frozen context information. We also develop independent validation procedure for selecting among fitted conditional generators. We show that the selection procedure satisfies an oracle inequality, and the switching rule guarantees, with high probability, no increase in conditional energy risk relative to a target-only generator. Simulations and real data applications illustrate the gains from our proposed methods and the effectiveness of target validation in guarding against negative transfer.

</details>


### [41] [PRELUDE: Separating Common Components from Target-Specific Dependencies for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15155)
*Dongqing Song,Jianyu Xu,Haobin Ding,Xinyi Guo,Jiahao Du,Ruiqi He,Ruiqi Liu,Yuntong Xie,Chao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出多变量时间序列预测中跨变量模块的增益主要来自“公共成分”而非变量间依赖，称之为“伪依赖”（spurious dependency），归因于公共成分混杂；据此提出分离式读取框架 PRELUDE，分别处理公共成分与依赖关系，在16个真实数据集上取得最佳整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨变量模块（截面池化、变量间注意力、图结构）虽显著降低预测误差，但通过推理期干预发现：这些模型对所有目标的“读取内容”几乎相同，增益主要来自变量的公共成分而非真正的依赖关系。理论上公共成分与依赖对读取的要求不同，应分别处理。

Method: 提出 PRELUDE 分离式读取框架：通过截面池化与多个公共因子对公共成分进行整体估计；在预白化得到的“新息空间”（innovation space）中，借助领先-滞后（lead-lag）关系为每个目标单独读取依赖。

Result: 在来自四个领域的16个真实数据集上，PRELUDE 有效缓解伪依赖，在24个短期与空气质量设置中有23个取得最优 MSE，相较最强基线平均 MSE 降低6.1%。

Conclusion: 跨变量模块的收益主要源于公共成分而非变量依赖，这导致读取内容在不同目标间趋同、依赖占比偏低；将公共成分（整体池化+多公共因子）与依赖（新息空间中的领先-滞后读取）分离建模，可有效缓解伪依赖并提升预测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting often improves the prediction of a target by modeling the interactions among variables, and cross-variable modules such as cross-sectional pooling, inter-variable attention, and graph structures are used to construct such interactions. However, by intervening on the cross-variable reading of existing models at inference time, we find that although these models substantially reduce forecasting error through cross-variable interaction, their reading is nearly identical for all targets: the gain appears to come from dependencies among variables but mainly comes from the common component. We term this phenomenon spurious dependency and attribute it to common-component confounding: present in every variable, the common component affects how reading distinguishes sources, so that reading tends to be shared across targets and concentrates on a few similar averages; dependencies thus make up a small share of the read-in content, and a common component composed of several drivers is only partly captured. Theoretical analysis further shows that the common component and dependencies place different requirements on reading and should therefore be handled in different ways. Accordingly, we propose PRELUDE, a separated-reading framework that estimates the common component holistically through cross-sectional pooling and multiple common factors, and reads dependencies for each target via lead-lag relations in the innovation space obtained by prewhitening. Extensive experiments on 16 real-world datasets from four domains show that PRELUDE effectively mitigates spurious dependency and achieves the best overall performance: it attains the best MSE on 23 of the 24 short-horizon and air-quality settings and reduces the average MSE by 6.1% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [42] [Stealthy, High-Impact False Data Injection Attacks in Smart Grid Using Physics-Constrained GAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15162)
*Md. Mainul Islam,Hasan Kurban,Abdulrahman Takiddin,Muhammad Ismail,Rachad Atat,Erchin Serpedin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与检测器无关的物理约束生成对抗网络（PCGAN），仅利用已部署传感器的历史量测学习解耦的潮流灵敏度，在受限攻击通道内生成兼具隐蔽性与物理破坏性的虚假数据注入（FDI）攻击，并在IEEE 300节点系统上验证其隐蔽—影响权衡优于七种现有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FDI攻击方法在隐蔽性与物理影响之间相互折衷（一方强则另一方弱），且普遍假设攻击者掌握电网内部信息、全节点量测或防御者模型，与实际威胁场景不符，缺乏检测器无关且物理合理的攻击构造方法。

Method: 构建物理约束GAN：从已部署传感器的历史量测中学习解耦的有功—电压相角、无功—电压幅值灵敏度关系，用于约束扰动仅作用于被攻陷通道；对抗训练促使生成量测具备真实时空相关性；整体框架不依赖特定检测器。在IEEE 300节点系统上，基于PJM一年逐时实际负荷与合成母线负荷，与七种现有FDI攻击在10%/30%/60%/100%传感器攻陷比例下对比，隐蔽性用10种机器学习检测器与常规不良数据检测（BDD）评估，物理影响用非线性交流加权最小二乘状态估计量化。

Result: PCGAN在四种场景下平均实现84.28%的ML逃逸率与22.31%的空间负荷误判率，平均隐蔽—影响权衡得分18.69%，显著高于最强对比攻击的5.61%；卡方BDD逃逸率83.45%，最大归一化残差BDD逃逸率86.02%。

Conclusion: PCGAN在不同传感器可及水平下均表现出良好的隐蔽—影响平衡，可为FDI检测器评估提供高难度攻击样本，并推动检测方法鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks can distort power system state estimation while retaining plausible measurement patterns. Existing FDI methods trade stealth for impact or vice versa. They often assume access to internal grids, all-bus sensing, or defender models. In this paper, we propose a detector-agnostic physics-constrained generative adversarial network (PCGAN) that learns decoupled power-flow sensitivities from historical measurements of deployed sensors. The learned active-power-angle and reactive-power-voltage-magnitude relationships guide perturbations restricted to compromised channels, while adversarial training promotes realistic spatiotemporal patterns. We implement PCGAN and seven existing FDI attacks on the IEEE 300-bus system using one year of observed hourly demand from PJM Interconnection, a regional transmission organization in the United States, and synthetic bus-level load profiles. We compare their performance across 10%, 30%, 60%, and 100% compromised sensors. Attack stealth is assessed against ten machine-learning (ML) detectors and conventional bad-data detection (BDD) methods, while physical impact is quantified through nonlinear alternating-current weighted least-squares state estimation. PCGAN achieves 84.28% mean ML evasion and 22.31% mean spatial load misperception across the four scenarios. Its mean stealth-impact trade-off score reaches 18.69%, compared with 5.61% for the strongest competing attack. PCGAN also achieves 83.45% chi-square BDD evasion and 86.02% largest-normalized-residual BDD evasion. These results demonstrate a favorable stealth-impact balance under varying sensor-access levels and provide challenging attack cases for evaluating FDI detectors.

</details>


### [43] [Anytime-Valid Empirical Likelihood Inference from LLM Annotations with Adaptive Human Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15197)
*Hongfu Gao,Jiang Hu,Pengfei Liu,Wang Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AAELI：利用LLM输出指导自适应人工审计，并通过可预测经验似然 e-process 构造任意时刻有效置信序列，在有限审计预算下估计人类标签总体均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 标注成本低、速度快，正替代人工标注未标记数据；但其输出相对人类标签存在偏差与失准（mis-calibration），直接用于分析会产生误导性推断。因此需要一种在有限人工审计预算下、能对总体人类标签均值给出统计有效推断的框架。

Method: 提出 AAELI 在线框架：(1) 用 LLM 输出引导自适应人工审计策略；(2) 将审计标签通过可预测经验似然 e-process 与 LLM 预测结合，构造任意时刻有效（anytime-valid）置信序列。理论核心是证明"可预测学习的有效投影"在自适应审计下保持一阶 profile 经验似然统计量，且不要求投影估计量的收敛速率；由此得到精确任意时刻有效的 betting 构造，其连续混合形式只带来标量级而非向量维度的混合惩罚。辅助结果包括自适应经验似然渐近理论，以及在相同审计概率下界与长期期望预算下，学习到的审计规则在给定增广估计方程设计类中达到 oracle 方差。

Result: 合成数据与三个真实数据集实验表明：AAELI 保持经验上的任意时刻覆盖率；相比最强基线，达到目标精度所需人工审计次数更少。

Conclusion: AAELI 为"LLM 标注+有限人工审计"提供了兼具理论保证（精确 anytime-valid 覆盖、oracle 方差效率）与实证效率（审计预算更省）的统计推断工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate unlabeled data as a fast, low-cost alternative to human annotation. However, their outputs can be biased and miscalibrated relative to human labels, potentially leading to misleading analysis and inference. We propose AAELI (Adaptive Human Auditing and Anytime-Valid Empirical Likelihood Inference), an online framework for estimating the population mean of human labels under a limited auditing budget. AAELI uses LLM outputs to guide adaptive human auditing and combines them with audited labels through predictable empirical-likelihood $e$-processes to construct anytime-valid confidence sequences. Our main theoretical result shows that a predictably learned efficient projection preserves the first-order profile empirical-likelihood statistic under adaptive auditing, without requiring a prescribed convergence rate for the projection estimator. This yields an exactly anytime-valid betting construction whose continuous-mixture form incurs a scalar rather than vector-dimensional mixing penalty. Supporting results establish adaptive empirical-likelihood asymptotics and show that a learned audit rule attains the oracle variance within the stated class of augmented estimating-equation designs under the same audit-probability floor and long-run expected budget. Experiments on synthetic data and three real-world datasets demonstrate that AAELI maintains empirical anytime coverage while requiring fewer human audits than the strongest competing baseline to reach a target precision.

</details>


### [44] [DGPExplainer: Generating Prototype Sequence Explanations for Dynamic Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15224)
*Jinlong Hu,Shixu Fan,Chongzhang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DGPExplainer——一种面向动态图神经网络（DGNN）的事后、基于原型的模型级解释器，将复杂决策过程转化为可类比的案例式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中节点与边随时间演化，时序动态与时空依赖带来额外复杂性，使静态GNN的解释技术难以直接迁移到DGNN，尤其缺乏模型级解释方法。

Method: 三步流程：(1) 用局部解释方法在各时间快照中识别重要元素；(2) 将每个时间步的图分解为不同原型，从而把动态图序列符号化；(3) 设计序列生成方法，基于符号化序列学习并生成类别特定的动态图模式序列，作为原型解释。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法能对多类别的DGNN模型有效生成基于原型的解释。

Conclusion: DGPExplainer填补了DGNN模型级解释的空白，通过符号化+序列生成实现可理解的原型解释，并在多类别场景下验证了有效性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been successfully applied to learning from graph-structured data. However, applying them to dynamic graphs-where nodes and edges evolve over time-presents significant challenges, particularly regarding explainability. While explanation techniques for static GNNs are well established, they do not readily extend to dynamic GNNs (DGNNs) due to the added complexity introduced by temporal dynamics and spatiotemporal dependencies, especially when generating model-level explanations. To address this gap, we introduce DGPExplainer, a post-hoc, prototype-based explainer specifically designed for DGNN models that transforms their complex decision-making processes into human-understandable, analogical, case-based explanations. DGPExplainer first employs local explanation methods to identify important elements within temporal snapshots of dynamic graph sequences. It then decomposes the graph at each time step into distinct prototypes, thereby symbolizing the dynamic graph sequence. Finally, we design a sequence generation method that uses these symbolized sequences to learn and generate class-specific dynamic graph pattern sequences that serve as prototype explanations. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method effectively produces prototype-based explanations for DGNN models across multiple classes.

</details>


### [45] [MOON-RL: Multi-Objective Optimization via Objective Space Navigation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15227)
*Andrii Artomov,Soheyl Massoudi,Jürg Schiffmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOON-RL将约束多目标优化建模为目标空间中的偏好导向导航，用轻量PPO策略结合元算法实现有针对性搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计问题常含多冲突目标与严格可行性约束，传统基于种群的优化器需大量评估且对搜索聚焦区域的在线控制有限。

Method: 将约束多目标搜索表述为目标空间中的偏好导向导航；轻量PPO策略接收目标空间与约束相关观测，学习沿用户指定权衡方向更新设计；元算法周期性重置策略、从持续更新的非支配解档案中重采样起点，并调节步长与惩罚调度。

Result: 在真实执行器设计问题及收敛困难的约束基准上，与主流进化算法在同等评估预算下对比：聚焦搜索场景中MOON-RL在感兴趣区域表现具竞争力；消融表明RL自适应采样与元算法均有贡献；框架亦可兼容非RL自适应采样器，而PPO在固定目标偏好导航中仍是强选择。

Conclusion: 将RL训练重新用作目标空间导航是构建交互式、偏好引导设计工具的可行基础模块。

Abstract: Many engineering design problems involve multiple conflicting objectives and strict feasibility constraints. They are typically solved with population-based optimizers that require many evaluations and offer limited online control over where the search is focused. We present MOON-RL, a reinforcement-learning-based optimization framework that formulates constrained multi-objective search as preference-directed navigation in the objective space. In MOON-RL, a lightweight PPO policy receives objective-space and constraint-related observations and acts as an instance-specific adaptive search process. It learns how to update the design so that the current solution advances along a user-specified trade-off direction, while a simple meta-algorithm periodically resets the policy, resamples starting designs from a continuously updated archive of non-dominated solutions, and adjusts step-size and penalty schedules. We evaluate MOON-RL on real-world actuator-design problems and additional convergence-hard constrained benchmarks, comparing against established evolutionary algorithms under matched evaluation budgets. In focused search scenarios, MOON-RL achieves competitive performance in the region of interest, and ablation studies show that both the RL-driven adaptive sampling process and the meta-algorithm contribute to this behaviour, suggesting that repurposing RL training as objective-space navigation is a viable building block for interactive, preference-guided design tools. Additional experiments show that the framework also accommodates adaptive non-RL samplers, while PPO remains a strong choice for fixed-target, preference-directed objective-space navigation.

</details>


### [46] [Winter wheat improves soil carbon sequestration with reduced gains in warmer climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15232)
*Georgios Giannarakis,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,Dimitrios Bormpoudakis,Ilias Tsoumas,Nikiforos Samarinas,Gustau Camps-Valls,Charalampos Kontoes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究运用因果机器学习（causal machine learning），基于立陶宛2018–2022年全国农田数据，在田块尺度评估冬小麦轮作对土壤有机碳（SOC）的异质性影响，发现其固碳效应受气候调节：冷凉地区增益更高，温暖地区减弱甚至为负，且在高排放情景下固碳潜力可能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续农业集约化需要精确理解土地利用决策在不同环境条件下如何影响SOC固存，而传统方法难以刻画处理效应的空间与气候异质性，因此需要因果推断与机器学习的结合来实现田块级异质性效应估计。

Method: 采用因果机器学习方法估计条件平均处理效应（heterogeneous treatment effects）；数据为立陶宛2018–2022年五年全国尺度田块数据集，涵盖作物类型、管理措施、土壤属性与环境条件；在此基础上分析冬小麦轮作效应在空间与气候梯度上的变化，并结合未来气候情景进行外推。

Result: 冬小麦轮作总体提升SOC水平，但效应受气候调节：冷凉地区SOC增益较高，温暖地区效应减弱甚至为负；未来气候预测显示，在高排放情景下冬小麦的碳固存潜力可能下降。

Conclusion: 应制定考虑气候变异性的区域化农业策略，以最大化SOC固存并保障长期土壤可持续性；同时表明因果机器学习可作为评估农业管理异质性环境效应的有效工具。

Abstract: Sustainable agricultural intensification requires a precise understanding of how land use decisions influence soil organic carbon (SOC) sequestration under varying environmental conditions. We apply causal machine learning to evaluate the heterogeneous impact of winter wheat-based rotations on SOC at the field-level across Lithuania. Using a nationwide dataset collected over five years (2018-2022), including crop types, management practices, soil properties, and environmental conditions, we estimate how the effects of winter wheat-based rotation vary across space and climate. Our results indicate that winter wheat generally enhances SOC levels, but its effectiveness is modulated by climate. Cooler regions experience higher SOC gains, whereas warmer regions show diminished or even negative effects. Future climate projections suggest that the potential for winter wheat carbon sequestration may decline under high-emission scenarios. These findings highlight the importance of localized agricultural strategies that account for climate variability to maximize SOC sequestration and ensure long-term soil sustainability.

</details>


### [47] [Schedule-Calibrated Commitment Order in Blockwise Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15234)
*Gaurav Sarkar,Umang Pandey,Pratik Prabhanjan Brahma,Zicheng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分块调度会使掩码扩散语言模型的token提交顺序呈现"强左到右"假象；作者用Spearman秩恒等式将全局位置-提交相关性分解为调度参考项与块内相关性的加权均值，并对LLaDA-8B-Instruct的聚合结果做校准，指出块大小128的较大原始残差并不意味着更强的块内位置顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型常报告token提交呈强左到右顺序，但顺序块调度本身即可人为制造这种相关性，导致对"模型具有位置有序解码行为"的解读可能失真，需要区分调度效应与真实的块内顺序性。

Method: 针对等长连续块、按位置顺序无交错处理、不可逆且不共享（untied）提交的设定，利用Spearman秩恒等式把全局位置-提交相关系数精确表示为确定性的调度参考项加上块内相关系数的加权平均；并基于已报告的聚合摘要推导校准区间与紧界。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct、500条数学与代码提示、256个生成位置与更新步的配对研究中，块大小由64增至128使全局平均相关从0.9845降至0.9149；均匀随机块内参考值分别为0.9375和0.7500；校准后块内平均相关为0.751和0.660，故块大小128下更大的原始残差并不代表更强的局部位置排序。

Conclusion: 提交顺序应按调度感知（schedule-aware）方式解读；分析给出紧界与校准区间，但实证范围仅限单一检查点与两种调度，未评估输出质量或推理速度，且受聚合级数据发布的限制。

Abstract: Sequential block schedules can make token commitment in masked diffusion language models appear strongly left-to-right even when within-block order is random or reversed. For equal contiguous blocks processed without interleaving in positional order, with untied irreversible commitments, we use a Spearman-rank identity to express global position-commitment correlation as a deterministic schedule reference plus a weighted mean within-block correlation. In a reported paired study of LLaDA-8B-Instruct on 500 math and code prompts with 256 generated positions and updates, increasing block size from 64 to 128 lowers mean global correlation from 0.9845 to 0.9149. Uniform-random within-block references are already 0.9375 and 0.7500. After calibration, mean within-block correlations are 0.751 and 0.660, so the larger raw residual at block size 128 does not indicate stronger local positional ordering. We give sharp bounds and derive calibrated intervals from reported summaries, while documenting the limitations of the aggregate-only empirical release. The analysis supports schedule-aware interpretation of commitment order; its empirical scope is one checkpoint and two schedules, without evaluation of output quality or inference speed.

</details>


### [48] [Regret Bounds for Quantum Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15237)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $d$ 维量子线性赌博机问题的最优遗憾下界为 $\Omega(d^{3/2}\log(1+T/d^{3/2}))$，解决了此前 $d^{3/2}$ 依赖是否必要这一开放问题，并提出了两种阶段消除算法（Soft-G-Elim 与 QG-Elim）改进有限动作集上的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一般动作集上的量子线性赌博机算法达到 $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$ 遗憾，而此前下界仅为 $\Omega(d\log(1+T/d))$，二者在 $d$ 的依赖阶上存在 $d^{1/2}$ 的间隙，本文旨在判定 $d^{3/2}$ 依赖是否本质必要。

Method: 1) 构造一类困难实例，使每个动作的奖励差距正比于其与最优动作的平方距离；2) 建立带量子存储的自适应量子查询的后验 Fisher 信息界，将最优方向所获信息与产生的遗憾关联；3) 将所选动作视为最优方向的估计，用矩阵 Cramér–Rao 论证导出期望遗憾下界；4) 面向规模为 $K$ 的固定动作集，提出 Soft-G-Elim 与 QG-Elim 两种阶段消除算法，并改造量子相位估计构造低偏差、尾概率可控的均值估计器 SoftQME。

Result: 证明下界 $\Omega(d^{3/2}\log(1+T/d^{3/2}))$，与上界在 $d$ 的多项式依赖上匹配；Soft-G-Elim 在实验设计支撑集上独立调用 SoftQME 估计存活动作均值，达到遗憾 $O(d\log T\log(dT)\sqrt{\log(KT)})$，将原界中的 $\log(KT)\log\log(dT)$ 因子缩减为 $\sqrt{\log(KT)}$。

Conclusion: $d^{3/2}$ 的依赖是量子线性赌博机不可避免的；QG-Elim 进一步将该对数因子缩减至 $\min\{\sqrt{\log(KT)},\sqrt d\}$，使有限动作集上的上界更接近信息论极限。

Abstract: We study $d$-dimensional quantum linear bandits with bounded rewards, where the learner selects an action and queries its quantum reward oracle or its inverse in each round. For general action sets, existing algorithms achieve regret $O(d^{3/2}\operatorname{polylog}(T))$ over $T$ rounds. Prior work established a lower bound of $Ω(d\log(1+T/d))$, leaving open whether the $d^{3/2}$ dependence in the upper bound is necessary. We resolve this question by proving a minimax lower bound of $Ω(d^{3/2}\log(1+T/d^{3/2}))$. To prove this result, we construct a family of hard instances in which the reward gap of each action is proportional to its squared distance from the optimal action. We establish a posterior Fisher information bound for adaptive quantum queries with quantum memory, relating the information acquired about the optimal direction to the regret incurred. Viewing each selected action as an estimate of the optimal direction, we use a matrix Cramér--Rao argument to derive a lower bound on expected regret. When the action set is fixed and has size $K$, we propose two phased elimination algorithms, Soft-G-Elim and QG-Elim. We adapt quantum phase estimation to construct SoftQME, a mean estimator with small bias and controlled tails. Soft-G-Elim estimates the mean reward of every surviving action from independent SoftQME outputs on the support of an experimental design. Controlling the bias and fluctuations of these estimates yields regret $O(d\log T\log(dT)\sqrt{\log(KT)})$, reducing the $\log(KT)\log\log(dT)$ factor in the previous regret bound to $\sqrt{\log(KT)}$. QG-Elim further reduces this factor to $\min\{\sqrt{\log(KT)},\sqrt d\}$.

</details>


### [49] [KQML-Liver: Interpretable Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Liver Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15241)
*Nhat-Long Nguyen,Viet-Loi Dinh,Trong-Nghia Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KQML-Liver混合框架，将量子机器学习（参数化量子电路）与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）结合，在严格的防数据泄漏预处理下于ILPD数据集上取得82%准确率和100%召回率，兼具性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝病早期检测需求迫切，但现有机器学习/深度学习/量子机器学习方法普遍存在可解释性差、因数据泄漏或评估协议过于简单导致性能虚高的问题，限制其临床可靠性。

Method: 构建KQML-Liver混合架构：用参数化量子电路进行表达性特征表示，用KAN的功能映射以可解释方式捕捉非线性关系；在印度肝病患者数据集（ILPD）上采用严格预处理流程防止数据泄漏进行评估。

Result: 分类准确率82%，召回率100%（检出全部阳性病例），优于现有基线方法，同时提升临床预测的透明度。

Conclusion: KAN-量子混合架构为可解释的AI辅助医学诊断提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Liver disease remains a major global health concern, highlighting the need for reliable and accessible early detection methods. Although recent advances in machine learning, deep learning and quantum machine learning (QML) have introduced promising diagnostic tools, many approaches suffer from limited interpretability and inflated performance caused by data leakage or overly simplistic evaluation protocols. These constraints reduce their reliability in real-world clinical practice. This paper proposes KQML-Liver, a hybrid framework that integrates QML with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) to enhance predictive performance and interpretability. The architecture combines parameterized quantum circuits for expressive feature representation with KAN-based functional mappings that capture nonlinear relationships in an interpretable manner. Evaluated on the Indian Liver Patient Dataset (ILPD) under a rigorous preprocessing protocol preventing data leakage, the proposed model achieves 82% classification accuracy and 100% recall, detecting all positive cases, outperforming existing baselines while providing improved transparency in clinical predictions. These results suggest that hybrid KAN-quantum architectures offer a promising direction for interpretable AI-assisted medical diagnosis.

</details>


### [50] [Dual Representation Enhanced Diffusion Policy for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15246)
*Yunyi Zhao,Wei Zhang,Cheng Xiang,Vadakkepat Prahlad,Zuming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DredRL，用增强的对偶表示与扩散策略强化学习求解柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP），在合成、公开、大规模及随机算例上多数配置优于SOTA学习型调度器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL调度方法虽能通过与环境的交互学得有效调度策略，但随问题规模与加工时间不确定性增大，性能往往退化，需提升策略表达能力与鲁棒性。

Method: DredRL首先通过自注意力与交叉注意力构建工序与机器的增强对偶表示，捕获实体内部与实体间依赖；再以扩散模型作为策略网络，在编码状态条件下经迭代去噪生成调度决策。

Result: 在合成、公开、大规模与随机FJSP基准上多数配置优于SOTA学习型方法；合成算例上makespan最多降低5.44%；在四个公开基准中的三个取得最佳学习型性能；在随机基准上优于随机性感知算法。

Conclusion: 增强对偶表示与扩散策略的结合为复杂环境下大规模可扩展FJSP调度提供了有效且鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: The Flexible Job-Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is a fundamental and challenging optimization problem in smart manufacturing. Although recent deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches can learn effective scheduling policies through interaction with the environment, their performance often degrades as problem scale and processing-time uncertainty increase. In this paper, we propose DredRL, a dual representation enhanced diffusion-policy reinforcement learning scheduler. DredRL first constructs enhanced dual representations of operations and machines through self- and cross-attention mechanisms to capture both intra-entity and inter-entity dependencies. A diffusion model is further employed as the policy network, generating scheduling decisions through iterative denoising conditioned on the encoded state to enhance policy expressiveness and robustness. Extensive experiments on synthetic, public, large-scale, and stochastic FJSP benchmarks demonstrate that DredRL outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based schedulers across most configurations. Compared with SOTA learning-based methods, DredRL reduces makespan by up to 5.44% on synthetic instances. It also achieves the best learning-based performance on three of four public benchmarks and outperforms the stochasticity-aware algorithms on stochastic benchmarks. These results demonstrate that combining enhanced dual representations with diffusion-based policies provides an effective and robust approach for large scale scalable FJSP scheduling in complicated environment.

</details>


### [51] [Protected Unbiased Sparsification: A Sharp Loewner Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15247)
*Ziyang Wang,Menghao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究带线性统计保护的随机稀疏化：在 $k$ 个正坐标、保护统计量秩为 $d$ 时，对任意 $d+1\le m\le k$ 构造无偏、逐实现满足 $AQ=Ap$ 且协方差满足 $\operatorname{Cov}(Q)\preceq h\,C_{p\mid A}$ 的随机概率向量，系数 $h=(k-m)/((m-d)(k-d-1))$ 一致最优，并给出 Las Vegas 精确采样与分块枢轴采样器及语言模型注意力实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机稀疏化通常只追求无偏性，但下游线性响应还要求保护指定线性统计量并控制协方差，现有方法缺少同时满足无偏、逐实现保持统计量且协方差最优的通用构造与紧界。

Method: 以 $p$ 的 $k$ 个正坐标和保护统计矩阵 $A$（有效秩 $d$）为输入，对每个 $m\in[d+1,k]$ 构造至多 $m$ 个非零坐标的随机 $Q$，逐实现保证 $AQ=Ap$；以最优线性调整后的类别协方差 $C_{p\mid A}$ 为基准，推导协方差序上界系数 $h$；证明该系数在本实例类上一致尖锐（含有偏估计的二阶矩意义）；给出残差协方差桥接以推广到任意逐路径线性保护；刻画类内均匀权重分区的实例最优常数并给出尖峰权重界；对有理输入设计期望多项式时间、$1+\varepsilon$ 近似的精确 Las Vegas 采样器；另提出保持块质量的快速块枢轴采样器。

Result: 构造的估计量在所有实例上达到 $\operatorname{Cov}(Q)\preceq h C_{p\mid A}$，且 $h$ 在实例一致意义下尖锐，即便允许有偏估计亦不可改进；残差桥接将未保护曲线延拓至任意逐路径线性保护；得到分区与尖峰权重情形下的界；精确 Las Vegas 采样器以 $1+\varepsilon$ 因子在期望多项式时间内逼近该曲线；快速块枢轴采样器在协方差序上不劣于块内独立采样；Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 解码中快速采样器保持块质量，与 VarOpt 的比较统计上不确定，top-$m$ 截断在 RULER 上得分最高。

Conclusion: 该工作为带线性统计保护的随机稀疏化给出了一套完整的最优协方差理论、紧常数刻画与可实现的采样算法，并通过合成权重与注意力实验验证其有效性；与 VarOpt 的差异尚不显著，top-$m$ 截断在检索评测上表现最佳。

Abstract: Random sparsification replaces a probability vector by a random vector with few nonzero entries. We ask for unbiasedness, exact preservation of prescribed linear statistics, and a covariance guarantee for every downstream linear response. Let $p$ have $k$ positive coordinates, let $A$ specify the protected statistics, and let $d\le k-2$ be their effective rank. For every $d+1\le m\le k$, we construct an unbiased random probability vector $Q$ with at most $m$ nonzero coordinates, $AQ=Ap$ on every realization, and $\operatorname{Cov}(Q)\preceq h C_{p\mid A}$, where $h=(k-m)/((m-d)(k-d-1))$ and $C_{p\mid A}$ is the categorical covariance after optimal linear adjustment for the protected statistics. This coefficient is sharp uniformly over instances, even among biased estimators when error is measured by its second moment. A residual covariance bridge extends the concurrent unprotected curve to arbitrary pathwise linear protection. We also characterize instance-optimal constants for partitions with uniform weights within classes and derive bounds for peaked weights. For rational inputs, an exact Las Vegas sampler attains the curve within a factor $1+ε$ in expected polynomial time. A separate fast block-pivotal sampler preserves block masses and is no worse in covariance order than independent sampling within blocks. Experiments illustrate the theory on synthetic weights and language-model attention. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct decoding, the fast sampler preserves block masses, while comparisons with VarOpt remain statistically inconclusive and top-$m$ truncation scores highest on RULER.

</details>


### [52] [CastWorld: Benchmarking Context-Aware Agentic Time Series Forecasting Beyond Predictive Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15262)
*Bokai Pan,Ruijie Yang,Mingyue Cheng,Jiahao Wang,Xiaoyu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastWorld 是一个面向上下文感知时间序列预测的智能体基准，包含覆盖 8 个领域、25 个真实数据集的上下文丰富数据集套件，49 个核心工具的模块化预测原生 harness 与状态自适应循环，以及模型级与系统级双重评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准对多样化上下文信息、预测特定的自适应交互以及预测智能体的系统化评估支持不足，传统方法局限于一次性数值预测，缺乏自主、上下文感知的交互能力。

Method: 构建 CastWorld：（1）统一上下文规范下的数据集套件，涵盖外部变量、事件与历史案例；（2）轻量模块化预测原生 harness，提供 49 个核心工具与状态自适应循环，追踪预测版本、支持迭代证据采集与预测修正，中间预测可暴露信息缺口；（3）模型级评估（预测质量、过程执行、行为可信度、资源效率，基于决策轨迹与版本历史）与系统级评估（跨系统预测精度对比）。

Result: 实验显示：准确率相近的智能体呈现截然不同的决策行为；额外交互未必提升预测效果；智能体可能无法选出自身此前生成的最佳预测；在预测精化过程中允许证据获取，可提升全部三个评估模型的任务感知预测得分。

Conclusion: CastWorld 为在受控条件下评估智能体式时间序列预测提供了可复用的基础平台，揭示了交互过程与最终预测质量之间的非平凡关系。

Abstract: Agentic time series forecasting extends conventional approaches beyond one-shot numerical prediction toward autonomous, context-aware interaction. However, existing benchmarks offer limited integrated support for diverse contextual information, forecasting-specific adaptive interactions, and systematic evaluation of forecasting agents. In this paper, we introduce CastWorld, an agentic benchmark for context-aware time series forecasting. First, its context-rich dataset suite spans 8 domains and 25 real-world datasets, covering external variables, events, and historical cases under a unified context specification. Second, a lightweight, modular forecasting-native harness provides 49 core tools and a state-adaptive loop that tracks forecast versions and enables iterative evidence gathering and forecast revision. Intermediate forecasts can expose information gaps, while newly acquired evidence guides subsequent revisions and final version selection. Third, model-level evaluation assesses forecast quality, process execution, behavioral trustworthiness, and resource efficiency through decision trajectories and forecast-version histories. Also, systems-level evaluation compares predictive accuracy across forecasting systems. Experiments reveal distinct decision behaviors among agents with comparable accuracy. Additional interactions do not necessarily improve forecasts, and agents may fail to select their best previously generated predictions. Allowing evidence acquisition during forecast refinement improves task-aware forecast scores across all three evaluated models. CastWorld provides a reusable foundation for evaluating agentic time series forecasting under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [53] [A hybrid machine learning-analog framework for downscaling unprecedented climate extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15284)
*Nina Effenberger,Florian E. Roemer,Maybritt Schillinger,Maxim Samarin,Erich Fischer,Reto Knutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合集合增强与类比降尺度的混合框架，利用机器学习生成的高分辨率数据，对粗分辨率全球气候模式中的极端高温事件进行高效降尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件对气候影响评估至关重要。物理模型虽可通过集合增强模拟前所未有的极端事件，但计算代价高昂，输出分辨率通常较粗；需要在保持极端尾部采样能力的同时获得高分辨率区域细节。

Method: 首先验证机器学习生成的巴黎区域气候模式热浪在物理上具有真实性；随后以该机器学习模拟数据集为中介，对粗分辨率全球气候模式集合增强事件执行基于类比（analog-based）的降尺度，构建混合降尺度框架。

Result: 混合框架有效衔接高分辨率区域细节与稀有事件采样，能高效实现极端事件降尺度，并捕捉多模式不确定性。

Conclusion: 概率生成式降尺度与类比降尺度结合可为极端事件研究提供高效率工具，兼顾计算可行性与区域高分辨率刻画。

Abstract: Extreme weather events are critical for climate impact assessments. While physical models can simulate unprecedented extremes, for example, through ensemble boosting, this approach is computationally demanding, often restricting output to coarse spatial resolutions. Probabilistic generative downscaling offers a solution by enabling efficient sampling from the entire climate distribution, including its extreme tails. Here, we first demonstrate that machine learning generated regional climate model heatwaves over Paris are physically realistic. We then leverage an emulated machine learning dataset to perform analog-based downscaling of coarse global climate model ensemble boosting events. Our hybrid framework effectively bridges high-resolution regional detail with rare-event sampling, providing an efficient tool for extreme event downscaling and capturing multi-model uncertainty.

</details>


### [54] [Learning Rate Transfer and Feature Learning Across Depth for Constrained Spectral Optimizers: Complete Scion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15291)
*Eline Pot,Laurent Lefebvre,Mathieu Segond,Antonio Silveti-Falls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究约束优化方法（Frank-Wolfe 与投影非欧氏归一化最速下降）中深度学习率跨模型深度的迁移性，推导出与 CompleteP 一致的 $1/L$ 残差缩放规则，并在 nanoGPT、DeiT、MLP-Mixer 上验证了学习率跨深度/宽度迁移及特征学习行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 CompleteP 框架仅针对 Adam 族优化器建立了 $1/L$ 残差缩放规则，使浅层网络标定的学习率可迁移到更深网络。但该结论是否适用于以线性最小化 oracle 决定更新方向的约束优化方法，尚不清楚，因此作者希望厘清这一迁移性是否对更广泛的优化器类别成立。

Method: 作者针对 Frank-Wolfe 与投影非欧氏归一化最速下降两类约束优化方法，分析其更新由线性最小化 oracle 决定时的深度缩放行为，推导相应的残差缩放规则；并将结论应用于 Scion 算法，从理论上论证其学习率可跨深度与跨宽度迁移，同时保持特征学习、避免陷入核机制（kernel regime）。

Result: 推导出的缩放规则确认 $1/L$ 残差缩放同样适用于这两类约束方法，尽管论证方式与 Adam 族优化器不同；理论上支持 Scion 的学习率在深度与宽度方向上的迁移。实验上在 nanoGPT（FineWeb）、DeiT（ImageNet）、MLP-Mixer（CIFAR-10）中观察到：在恰当的残差缩放下，学习率可跨深度迁移，并伴随特征学习的发生。

Conclusion: $1/L$ 残差缩放并非 Adam 族独有，而是对约束型优化器同样成立的更为普遍的规律；这为 Scion 等基于线性最小化 oracle 的算法提供了跨深度、跨宽度的学习率迁移依据，并指出残差缩放的选择对维持特征学习、避开核机制起关键作用。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer across model depth is instrumental in training deep models, as it enables learning rates calibrated on shallow networks to be effectively applied to deeper architectures. The recent CompleteP framework establishes a $1/L$ residual scaling rule that demonstrates this for Adam-family optimizers. We study whether similar transfer extends to constrained optimization methods, specifically Frank-Wolfe and projected non-Euclidean normalized steepest descent, where updates are determined by linear minimization oracles. We derive scaling rules for these methods which confirm that the $1/L$ residual scaling applies, although the arguments differ from the Adam-family optimizers. Our analysis supports depth-wise transfer and width-wise transfer of the learning rate for the Scion algorithm while ensuring feature learning and avoiding the kernel-regime. In experiments on NLP transformers (nanoGPT on FineWeb), vision transformers (DeiT on ImageNet), and vision MLPs (MLP-Mixer on CIFAR-10), we observe learning rate transfer across depth and feature learning for appropriately chosen residual scaling.

</details>


### [55] [Sharp Covering-Number Bounds for Feedforward ReLU Networks via Parameter Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15300)
*Ruizhi Deng,Guohao Shen,Tangrui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画ReLU/Leaky ReLU网络的参数空间对称性（正通道缩放与通道置换），对对称群取商得到覆盖数的精细上界，并给出匹配下界及统计风险与优化含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同参数配置可实现同一输入—输出映射，名义参数计数会高估功能复杂度，需要精确刻画参数空间中的冗余对称性并据此给出更紧的容量界。

Method: 证明任何保持网络函数不变的拟解析参数映射都是正通道缩放与通道置换的复合；对这两类对称性取商，推导全连接、卷积与残差网络的均匀覆盖数上界；并给出下界以验证紧性，随后推广到一般连续分段线性激活网络。

Result: 覆盖数上界中，正缩放的复杂度指数由总参数数S降至S−H（H为可缩放通道数），置换对称使界除以通道置换数的阶乘G；下界表明指数S−H一般不可改进，且除数G在一隐层及可识别网络中可达。

Conclusion: 对称性取商给出更紧的容量刻画，并用于导出ERM与非i.i.d.设定下的统计风险保证，同时解释函数逼近中的权重再平衡与经验风险优化中的平坦方向。

Abstract: In neural networks, distinct parameter configurations often realize the same input--output mapping, indicating that nominal parameter counts can overstate functional complexity. In this paper, we characterize the parameter-space symmetries of ReLU and Leaky ReLU networks across fully connected, convolutional, and residual architectures. We prove that every quasianalytic parameter map preserving the network function is a composition of positive channel scaling and channel permutation. By quotienting out both symmetries, we derive uniform covering-number upper bounds in which positive scaling reduces the complexity exponent from the total parameter count $S$ to $S-H$ (with $H$ denoting the number of scalable channels) and permutation symmetry divides the bound by the factorial count $G$ of channel permutations. We establish matching lower bounds showing that the exponent $S-H$ is sharp in general and that the divisor $G$ is attained for one-hidden-layer and generically identifiable networks. We further extend these results to general continuous piecewise-linear activated networks. Finally, we apply our covering-number bounds to derive statistical risk guarantees for empirical risk minimization and capacity control beyond i.i.d.\ settings, and show how the underlying symmetries help explain weight rebalancing in function approximation and flat directions in empirical risk optimization.

</details>


### [56] [TabOmics: A Small, Open Tabular Foundation Model for Wide Omics Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15329)
*Andrea López-García,Luis Bote-Curiel,Francisco Javier Gimeno-Blanes,José Luis Rojo-Álvarez,Sergio Muñoz-Romero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabOmics是一个仅7.3M参数、完全在合成表上预训练的开放表格基础模型，联合处理全部特征，一次前向同时输出预测、注意力特征重要性分数与样本嵌入；在高维小样本分类基准上以约1/30参数量取得与TabPFN-3.5相当的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组学研究常产生"特征数千至数万、样本极少"的宽表。现有表格基础模型多依赖上下文学习（in-context learning），对宽表的处理往往需要子采样、特征分组或压缩，可能损失特征信息且参数量大。作者希望以极小模型、纯合成数据预训练的方式，原生支持全特征联合建模。

Method: 提出TabOmics：小型开放表格基础模型家族，7.3M参数，仅在合成表格上预训练。模型对预处理后保留的全部特征进行联合处理，并在同一次前向传播中同时得到预测、注意力导出的特征重要性分数和测试样本嵌入。评估在三个高维、低样本量基准的46个分类任务上进行，并与TabPFN-3.5等模型比较（含8成员集成设置），另在合成表上评估特征恢复与超宽特征（30,000特征）情形。

Result: TabOmics v2.5八成员集成在46个任务上取得最高平均AUROC（0.943）；在32个任务上优于同为八成员的TabPFN-3.5，而参数量约为其1/30。单成员平均AUROC为0.941，TabPFN-3.5八成员为0.942，任务级配对分析无显著差异。合成表上特征重要性评分接近TabPFN-Wide的特征恢复效果；在30,000特征时预测仍高于随机。乳腺癌任务中排名靠前的基因包含定义亚型标签的PAM50基因，说明与标签定义一致，而非独立的生物学验证。

Conclusion: 小型、纯合成预训练、全特征联合处理的表格基础模型可在高维小样本组学分类中以极低参数量达到与大型模型相当的性能，并能附带特征重要性与样本嵌入等可解释输出。但特征重要性仅在合成数据上验证，真实生物学一致性未获独立验证；代码、权重、训练配方与实验工件均已公开。

Abstract: Omics studies often produce tables with thousands to tens of thousands of molecular features and few samples. Tabular foundation models use diverse strategies to predict labels for such tables, with some relying on in-context learning, while approaches to wide tables may involve subsampling, grouping, or compressing features. We present TabOmics, a small, open family of tabular foundation models with 7.3M parameters, pretrained exclusively on synthetic tables. TabOmics processes all features retained after preprocessing jointly and derives predictions, attention-derived feature-importance scores, and test sample embeddings from the same forward pass. On 46 classification tasks from three benchmarks of high-dimensional, low-sample-size tables, TabOmics v2.5 with eight ensemble members achieves the highest mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) among the evaluated models (0.943), outperforming TabPFN-3.5 with eight members on 32 tasks despite having about 30 times fewer parameters. With a single member, TabOmics v2.5 reaches a mean AUROC of 0.941, versus 0.942 for TabPFN-3.5 with eight members, with no significant paired difference under the reported task-level analysis. On synthetic tables, its feature-importance scores achieve feature recovery similar to that of TabPFN-Wide, and its predictions remain above chance at 30,000 features. On breast cancer tasks, the top ranked genes include PAM50 genes, which define the subtype labels, indicating concordance with the label definition rather than independent biological validation. The code, model weights, training recipes, and experimental artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [57] [MechBench: A Domain-Specific LLM Benchmark for Parallel Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15342)
*Zhihao Xia,Tingting Wang,Hui Li,Yongheng Xing,Chunxu Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向并联机构的LLM评测基准MECHBENCH（2541题，含标准/挑战两档、六类能力、九项可执行分析与设计任务），构建流程MECHFORGE结合专家知识、LLM生成、程序化实例生成、两阶段专家评审与任务感知评分；并开发领域适配诊断模型MechGPT。实验表明知识表现难以迁移到可执行的机构推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机构的有效推理需同时依赖机构拓扑、闭环约束、几何、关节类型与建模假设，具有强约束敏感性；而现有LLM基准缺乏对该领域知识、约束敏感分析与构造性设计的系统覆盖，导致领域能力无从度量。

Method: 构建MECHBENCH基准（2541题，标准/挑战双档，六类能力、九项可执行的分析与设计任务）；提出MECHFORGE构建框架：专家组织知识、LLM辅助题目生成、程序化实例生成、两阶段专家评审，以及任务感知评分（精确匹配、评分量表评估、程序验证）；并训练领域适配诊断模型MechGPT（以Qwen3-8B为基础）。

Result: 顶尖闭源模型在标准档得分超95，但挑战档仅47-60；领域适配使MechGPT显著优于Qwen3-8B基线，但在约束分析与设计任务上仍存在较大差距，说明知识性表现不能直接转化为可执行机构推理能力。

Conclusion: MECHBENCH为机构学领域LLM能力提供了系统化测试平台，揭示知识与推理/设计的解耦现象；未来需针对约束敏感分析与构造性设计加强建模与训练。

Abstract: Parallel mechanisms pose distinctive challenges to large language models (LLMs), as valid reasoning depends jointly on mechanism topology, loop closure, geometry, joint types, and modeling assumptions. Yet existing LLM benchmarks provide little systematic coverage of such domain-specific knowledge, constraint-sensitive analysis, and constructive design. To fill this gap, we introduce MECHBENCH, a 2,541-question benchmark for evaluating LLMs in parallel mechanisms across professional knowledge, mechanism analysis, and design. MECHBENCH contains standard and challenge tiers spanning six capability categories and nine executable analysis and design tasks. It is constructed through MECHFORGE, a domain-grounded framework that combines expert-organized knowledge, LLM-assisted question generation, programmatic instance generation, two-stage expert review, and task-aware scoring with exact matching, rubric-based evaluation, and program verification. We further develop MECHGPT as a diagnostic domain-adapted model. Experiments show that strong knowledge performance does not directly translate into executable mechanism reasoning: leading closed-service models achieve over 95 on the standard tier but only 47-60 on the challenge tier, while domain adaptation substantially improves MechGPT over its Qwen3-8B baselines but leaves a large gap in constrained analysis and design. These results establish MECHBENCH as a systematic testbed for measuring domain-specific LLM capabilities in mechanism science.

</details>


### [58] [Breath-Dx: A Time Series Classification Benchmark for Disease Screening with eNose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15348)
*Ivan Poddiakov,Ivan Kruzhilov,Galina Zubkova,Svetlana Erofeeva,Andrey Savchenko,Pavel Blinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Breath-Dx 是首个基于呼出气电子鼻（eNose）时序分类的系统性基准，利用最大公开临床数据集 S-OH（1,234 名患者、9 个诊断组、17 通道多元时间序列）在含传感器漂移的时间划分下评测 10 种方法，所有方法 AUC 较随机交叉验证下降 0.05–0.12，最佳方法在部分肺部疾病达 0.74–0.76。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eNose 呼气分析有望实现便携、无创的疾病筛查，但领域缺乏在真实条件下（尤其传感器漂移）对机器学习方法进行系统对比的基准；现有研究多依赖小型私有数据与不一致协议，结果不可比，阻碍进展。

Method: 构建 Breath-Dx 基准：采用目前已公开的最大临床 eNose 数据集 S-OH（1,234 例、9 个诊断组、17 通道多元时间序列），提供显式考虑传感器漂移的时间序贯 train/test 划分；系统评测四大类共 10 种方法——基于特征的表型学习器、卷积模型、图模型与时间序列专用架构，并与随机交叉验证结果对照，同时分析人口学混杂因素影响。

Result: 时间验证下所有方法性能显著下降（AUC 较随机交叉验证降 0.05–0.12），说明漂移中蕴含强时间信号；最现实评测下部分肺部疾病 ROC AUC 为 0.74–0.76，另一些病症接近随机水平。循环架构在不同疾病间表现最稳定，专用时间序列方法峰值最优。人口学混杂分析显示：呼气信号对某些病症具有独立诊断价值，对另一些则在年龄之外增益有限。

Conclusion: 传感器漂移显著影响 eNose 分类的可复现评估，需以时间序贯划分作为标准协议；公开 S-OH 数据集、全部时间划分、评测协议与代码，以促进可复现研究与临床 breathomics 进展。

Abstract: Electronic nose (eNose) technology offers a portable, non-invasive route for disease screening via exhaled breath analysis. However, progress in the field is hindered by the lack of systematic benchmarks that compare machine learning methods under realistic conditions, particularly sensor drift, a known but rarely quantified failure mode. Most existing studies rely on small, private datasets with inconsistent protocols, rendering results non-comparable and slowing progress. We introduce Breath-Dx, the first comprehensive benchmark of time series classification methods for breath-based disease screening. Our work leverages the Scent of Health (S-OH) dataset, the largest public clinical eNose collection to date, comprising 1,234 patients across nine diagnostic groups, each providing a 17-channel multivariate time series with explicit temporal train/test splits that account for sensor drift. We systematically evaluate 10 methods spanning four families: feature-based tabular learners, convolution-based models, graph-based, and time series architectures. All methods degrade significantly under temporal validation (0.05-0.12 AUC drop compared to random cross-validation), confirming that drift embeds a strong temporal signal. Under this realistic evaluation, the best methods achieve ROC AUC of 0.74-0.76 for some lung conditions, though others remain near chance. Recurrent architectures show the most consistent performance across diseases, while specialized time series methods achieve the highest peak results. Demographic confounders matter: breath signals provide independent diagnostic value for some conditions but add little beyond age for others. We release the S-OH dataset, all temporal splits, evaluation protocols, and code to enable reproducible research and accelerate progress in clinical breathomics.

</details>


### [59] [A Modified Glicko Model for Rating Soccer Clubs and National Teams Worldwide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15378)
*Andrei Shelopugin,Alexander Sirotkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个将全球俱乐部与国家队置于统一概率量表的评分框架，基于扩展Glicko模型（含顺序效应与强度相关平局概率）并加入进球调整、主场优势分解、加时、联赛感知初始化及升降级规则，在约120万场俱乐部比赛与1.26万场国家队比赛上验证，精度优于Elo、与Dixon-Coles及马尔可夫链等比分矩阵模型相当，国际俱乐部比赛与国家队赛事中优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俱乐部多在本国联赛、国家队多在本洲联合会内比赛，跨体系球队鲜有交手，缺乏可比性；现有评分方法难以对全球球队给出共同可比尺度。

Method: 在近期Glicko扩展（顺序效应、强度相关平局概率）基础上适配足球：引入进球得分调整、主场优势分解、加时处理、联赛感知的评分初始化、不抬高联赛层级的升降级规则；国家队以所属联合会水平初始化。评估数据为166国252个联赛约120万场俱乐部比赛（2000–2026）与1.26万场国家队比赛（1998–2026），并以Elo、基于梯度提升进球期望的比分矩阵模型（Dixon-Coles、马尔可夫链）为基线。

Result: 在留出赛季上，改进Glicko明显比Elo准确，与Dixon-Coles、马尔可夫链等比分矩阵模型精度相当；在国际俱乐部比赛中明显更准。评分模型与马尔可夫链模型预测取平均优于任一单独模型。国家队方面显著优于所有基线，尤其在跨联合会交手的决赛阶段。

Conclusion: 该框架可给出可解释的全球统一评分，适用于球员评估、转会分析与比赛预测；数据、评分与代码已公开。

Abstract: Comparing soccer teams that rarely or never meet is a fundamental challenge in sports analytics: clubs play mostly within their national leagues, and national teams within their confederations. We propose a rating framework that places clubs and national teams worldwide on a common probabilistic scale. Building on a recent extension of Glicko with order effects and strength-dependent draw probabilities, we adapt the model to soccer with goal-scoring adjustments, a decomposition of home advantage, extra time, league-aware rating initialization and rules of promotion and relegation that do not inflate the levels of leagues; national teams start from the levels of their confederations. The models are evaluated on about 1.2 million club matches of 252 leagues from 166 countries (2000-2026) and 12.6 thousand national team matches (1998-2026). On held-out seasons, the modified Glicko model is clearly more accurate than Elo and as accurate as score-matrix models built on the goal expectations of a gradient boosting model, including the Dixon-Coles and Markov chain models, while it is substantially more accurate in international club matches. Averaging the predictions of the rating model and the Markov chain model is more accurate than either model alone. For national teams, the model is significantly more accurate than all baselines, especially in the final tournaments, where teams of different confederations meet. The resulting ratings are interpretable and can be used for player evaluation, transfer analysis and match forecasting. The match data, the ratings and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/andreyshelopugin/SportsRatings

</details>


### [60] [Is the Importance Ratio Necessary for Stable Reinforcement Learning in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15404)
*Shuibai Zhang,Junhyuck Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Likelihood-Gated Policy Optimization (LGPO)，用基于似然的门控实现软信赖域约束，免去计算旧策略似然，降低RL后训练开销约18%，并在完全on-policy下稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等主流RL方法需同时计算旧策略与当前策略似然以构造重要性采样比，带来显著计算开销；且基于比值裁剪的信赖域约束在完全on-policy时因比值恒为1而失效。

Method: LGPO通过基于似然的门控施加软信赖域约束，不再计算旧策略似然（去掉重要性采样修正项）。论文通过消融验证：移除重要性采样修正项不影响稳定性，而移除信赖域机制会导致崩溃。

Result: 在GRPO稳定的标准设置下，LGPO训练稳定性与峰值性能相当，训练时间平均减少约18%；在GRPO失效的完全on-policy训练中，LGPO保持稳定。

Conclusion: LGPO提供了一种更高效、更稳健的LLM RL后训练方案，可跨训练范式适用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training large language models (LLMs). However, popular RL methods like GRPO incur non-negligible overhead by computing both old-policy and current-policy likelihoods to form importance sampling ratios. In this work, we propose Likelihood-Gated Policy Optimization (LGPO), which enforces a soft trust region constraint via likelihood-based gating, eliminating the need to compute old-policy likelihoods. Empirically, we show that removing the importance sampling correction term does not harm training stability, whereas removing the trust region mechanism can lead to collapse. Moreover, ratio-based clipping can fail in fully on-policy training: the importance ratio stays at 1, so the ratio-based trust region constraint never activates. Under standard training settings where GRPO is stable, LGPO achieves comparable training stability and peak performance while reducing training time by ~18% on average relative to a standard implementation that recomputes old-policy likelihoods. In fully on-policy training, where GRPO fails, LGPO remains stable, enabling more efficient and robust LLM RL post-training across training regimes.

</details>


### [61] [MORA: Correcting Time Series Forecasts with Coarse Future Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15413)
*Hu Li,Kenan Li,Haonan Xu,longlong,Shuqin Gao,Yucheng Zhang,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出模型无关的残差适配器 MORA，利用预测残差集中于低频分量且历史输入含误差信息这一现象，通过分段均值估计与分阶段校正改进冻结预测器输出，在12个基准6个骨干上平均降低 MSE 7.2%（长期）与28.1%（短期）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析发现不同架构预测器存在共性：残差能量大量集中在少数低频分量，且线性探针表明历史输入仍含关于预测均值误差的预测信息；因此可利用可预测的粗尺度残差来提升现有预测，而无需重新训练骨干。

Method: 提出 Model-Output Residual Adaptation (MORA)，一种模型无关、无需额外输入的残差适配器，作用于冻结预测器输出。其不直接预测骨干特定残差，而是通过结构外推与学习估计得到目标序列的分段均值，再将估计与骨干预测对应统计量之间的差异转化为分阶段（stagewise）校正，并按估计可靠性对每步更新加权缩放。

Result: 在12个基准、6个预测骨干上的大量实验显示，长期预测平均 MSE 降低7.2%，短期预测降低28.1%，288个评测案例中96.2%取得更低 MSE；在3个时间序列基础模型的不同上下文长度下亦有效。

Conclusion: 可预测的粗尺度（低频）残差可被有效利用以改进现有预测，MORA 作为通用后处理适配器具有跨架构、跨任务与跨基础模型的泛化性。

Abstract: Recent architectural advances have improved time-series forecasting. However, our analysis of forecasters with diverse architectures reveals a common pattern: a substantial share of residual energy is concentrated in a small number of low-frequency components. Simple linear probes further show that the forecasters' historical inputs still contain predictive information about errors in the forecast mean. Building on these findings, we propose Model-Output Residual Adaptation (MORA), a model-agnostic residual adapter that refines the outputs of frozen forecasters without requiring additional inputs. MORA estimates segment-wise means of the target sequence through structural extrapolation and learned estimation, rather than directly predicting backbone-specific residuals. It then converts discrepancies between these estimates and the corresponding statistics of the backbone forecast into stagewise corrections, scaling each update according to estimation reliability. Extensive experiments on twelve benchmarks with six forecasting backbones show average MSE reductions of 7.2% for long-term forecasting and 28.1% for short-term forecasting, with lower MSE in 96.2% of the 288 evaluated cases. The benefits also extend to three time-series foundation models evaluated at different context lengths. Together, these results demonstrate that predictable coarse-scale residuals can be effectively exploited to improve existing forecasts. Code repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MORA-B0B0/.

</details>


### [62] [Overcoming the Resolution Limit: Significance-Aware Regularization for Intersectional Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15427)
*Antonio Ferrara,André Panisson,Francesco Cozzi,Alan Perotti,Francesco Bonchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"显著超额"指标族与SAFER（显著性感知公平正则化）方法，用软计数似然比证据统计对公平惩罚进行门控，从而区分真实不公平与采样噪声，缓解稀疏交叉子群下的公平过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉性公平需在细粒度子群上评估模型，但子群越细样本越少，公平估计越不稳定。现有in-processing方法"无视不确定性"，对观测到的差距一律惩罚，无法区分统计上确证的不公平与采样噪声，导致公平过拟合：为"修正"稀疏区域中的虚假差距而牺牲效用。

Method: 1）提出significant-excess指标族：连续、不确定性感知的公平评分，仅度量子群差距中超出给定置信水平下采样波动可解释范围的部分；2）提出SAFER：可微的in-processing方法，以软计数似然比证据统计作为门控来调节公平惩罚，包含阈值平滑、显著性门控与稠密尾部项三个组件；3）给出可证明的安全性质：当各子群差距显著低于门控阈值时，无约束最优解对SAFER近似驻定，门控分量反传贡献指数级小，额外公平贡献来自固定权重的小型非门控项；4）在三个基准数据集及专门构造的压力变体上做机制消融。

Result: 消融实验表明：移除SAFER任一组件（阈值平滑、显著性门控或稠密尾部项），都会在至少一个压力变体上出现理论预测的失效。在稀疏交叉场景中，SAFER取得优于基线方法的公平-效用Pareto前沿，并在不存在确证不公平时不损效用。

Conclusion: 将不确定性/显著性检验思想引入in-processing公平正则化可有效抑制公平过拟合；SAFER在稀疏交叉子群下兼顾公平与效用，并具备理论安全保证。

Abstract: Intersectional fairness requires evaluating models across fine-grained subgroups. However, as group definitions become more granular, the sample size per subgroup shrinks, causing fairness estimates to become increasingly unstable. Existing in-processing methods are certainty-blind: they penalize observed disparities without distinguishing statistically certified unfairness from sampling noise. This leads to fairness overfitting, where models sacrifice utility to "fix" spurious disparities in sparse regimes. To address this, we introduce the significant-excess metric family, a continuous, uncertainty-aware fairness score that measures only the portion of a subgroup's disparity exceeding what sampling variation can explain at a specific confidence level. Building on this metric, we propose SAFER (Significance-Aware Fairness Regularizer), a differentiable in-processing method that gates fairness penalties using a soft-count likelihood-ratio evidence statistic. SAFER satisfies a provable safety property: when every subgroup disparity lies well below the gate threshold, the unconstrained optimum is nearly stationary for SAFER. At that optimum, the back-propagated contribution of its significance-gated component is exponentially small, and the additional fairness contribution comes from a small ungated term with a fixed weight. Mechanism ablations across three benchmark datasets and purpose-built stress variants show that removing any component of SAFER (threshold smoothing, the significance gate, or a dense-tail term) produces the failure the theory predicts on at least one stress variant. In sparse intersectional settings, SAFER achieves a better fairness-utility Pareto frontier than the baseline methods, and it preserves utility when certified unfairness is absent.

</details>


### [63] [DETER: Suppression During Pretraining for Tamper Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15479)
*Maria Ios Glarou,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在预训练阶段主动抑制知识（梯度上升与标准预训练交替）比仅过滤语料或事后遗忘更能抵抗对抗性微调，1B模型需20倍以上PubMed微调token才能达到对照损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型在生物、化学或网络领域可能存在被滥用的知识风险，而权重发布后的限制手段（事后遗忘）容易被微调快速撤销，语料过滤虽更鲁棒但仍不足，因此探究能否在预训练阶段进一步加大重新习得知识的微调成本。

Method: 在过滤后的预训练语料上，将简单的梯度上升与标准预训练交替进行，实现预训练期间的知识主动抑制（DETER）；以留出集损失与下游任务评估保留知识，并比较过滤、事后遗忘与不同抑制强度、不同模型规模下的表现。

Result: 即使简单的梯度上升与标准预训练交替，也使医学知识比仅过滤或事后遗忘更难通过对抗性微调获得；1B参数下达到未过滤模型留出PubMed损失需超过20倍的PubMed微调token；随模型规模变化，抑制强度增大呈现越来越有利的“微调难度—保留数据留出损失”权衡。

Conclusion: 预训练是使不良能力难以通过后续微调获得的更有效阶段；代码见github.com/MEGlr/DETER。

Abstract: Open-weight language models can acquire knowledge that could enable biological, chemical, or cyber misuse. Any attempt to limit such knowledge must remain effective after the model weights are released and can be fine-tuned. Unlearning applied after pretraining can be rapidly undone, whereas filtering the pretraining corpus offers greater resistance. We ask whether actively suppressing knowledge during pretraining can further increase the fine-tuning required to acquire it. Even simple gradient ascent interleaved with standard pretraining on the filtered corpus makes medical knowledge harder to acquire through adversarial fine-tuning than filtering alone or post-hoc unlearning. At 1B parameters, reaching the held-out PubMed loss of an unfiltered model requires over 20$\times$ as many PubMed fine-tuning tokens as after filtering alone. Retained knowledge is evaluated using both held-out data and downstream tasks. Across model sizes, varying suppression strength reveals an increasingly favorable trade-off between fine-tuning difficulty and held-out loss on retained pretraining data. These results point to pretraining as an effective stage for making unwanted capabilities harder to acquire through subsequent fine-tuning. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/MEGlr/DETER}{\texttt{github.com/MEGlr/DETER}}.

</details>


### [64] [The Illusion of Diversity: Aligning LLM Exploration via Effective Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15493)
*Xiaoliang Fu,Jiaye Lin,Yangyi Fang,Cong Qin,Binbin Zheng,Chaowen Hu,Zekai Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"有效熵（Effective Entropy）"概念，将熵的计算限制在有效推理子空间内，并据此构建VEPO（Validity-aware Entropy Policy Optimization），在RLVR训练中提升推理准确率与策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR将大模型对齐从主观偏好转向客观正确性，但探索多样性与推理有效性之间存在脱节。现有方法依赖"盲目"熵指标（token级或全局序列级熵），无法区分有效推理与潜在幻觉，造成"多样性幻觉"——高熵往往对应混乱退化而非有价值的探索。

Method: 提出有效性感知的熵概念"有效熵"：仅在由可验证奖励判定的正确/有效轨迹子空间内量化多样性，系统过滤错误轨迹噪声。基于此实例化VEPO方法，将有效熵直接融入RLVR训练目标，形成自适应吸引动态，提升欠探索有效轨迹的概率，并适配大模型自回归序列概率极小的实际情形。

Result: 在6个数学推理基准、1.5B与7B DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen模型上，VEPO相较GRPO取得一致的总体准确率提升，重复采样成功率具竞争力；在相同正确数匹配的直接评估中，于常见可解的MATH500题目上产生更多不同解法策略，并有配对统计检验与三随机种子训练对比支撑。

Conclusion: 以有效性为约束的熵度量可纠正传统熵指标与推理质量错位的问题，VEPO为RLVR提供了一种简单有效的多样性探索机制，兼顾准确率与策略多样性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has shifted the alignment paradigm of large language models from subjective preference to objective correctness, yet a fundamental disconnect persists between exploration diversity and reasoning validity. Existing strategies predominantly rely on "blind" entropy metrics, e.g., token-level or global sequence-level entropy, which structurally fail to distinguish between effective reasoning and potential hallucinations. This misalignment creates an illusion of diversity, where high entropy tends to signal chaotic degeneration rather than valuable exploration. To bridge this gap, we introduce Effective Entropy, a validity-aware entropy concept that quantifies diversity exclusively within the valid subspace and systematically filters out the noise of incorrect trajectories. Built on this concept, we instantiate VEPO, a simple Validity-aware Entropy Policy Optimization method that directly incorporates effective entropy into RLVR training. VEPO induces an adaptive attraction dynamic that drives up the probability of under-explored valid trajectories, a mechanism that matches the practical regime of large-scale autoregressive models where sequence probabilities remain extremely small. Experiments on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks with 1.5B and 7B DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen models show consistent aggregate accuracy gains over GRPO and competitive repeated-sampling success. A direct evaluation with matched correct counts finds more distinct strategies on commonly solved MATH500 problems, supported by paired statistical tests and three-seed training comparisons.

</details>


### [65] [Selective State-Space Neural Tucker Factorization for Temporal-aware Traffic Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15502)
*Zhenhao Li,Peng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S3NTF模型，将选择性状态空间机制与神经Tucker分解结合，用于时空交通数据的高保真插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通数据存在强长期时间依赖，长序列建模是高质量插补的核心前提；但现有方法在长序列建模上存在梯度消失、效率低、难以捕捉长程时间相关性等固有局限。

Method: S3NTF由两部分构成：(1) 基于状态空间的时间编码器，以线性复杂度有效提取长序列依赖；(2) 基于Tucker分解的潜在时空特征交互学习模块，建模高阶跨模态交互；整体结合选择性状态空间机制与神经Tucker分解，同时捕捉复杂空间相关性与长期时间动态。

Result: 在六个真实交通数据集上的大量实验表明，该模型在插补精度上优于现有模型。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间机制可有效缓解长序列建模的梯度消失与效率问题，与神经Tucker分解的高阶交互建模相结合，能够提升时空交通数据插补的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal traffic data imputation is a key task of intelligent transportation systems (ITS), and urban traffic data inherently exhibits strong long-term temporal dependencies. Therefore, accurate modeling of these long-term temporal dynamics is the core prerequisite for high fidelity traffic data imputation. However, existing traffic data imputation methods still suffer from inherent limitations in long-sequence modeling, such as gradient vanishing, low efficiency, and insufficient capability to capture long-range temporal correlations. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Selective State Space Neural Tucker Factorization model (S3NTF) for robust traffic data imputation. This model combines the Selective State Space mechanism with Neural Tucker Factorization to capture complex spatial correlations and long-term temporal dynamics. A state space-based time encoder is proposed to effectively extracts long sequence dependencies with linear complexity, while the latent spatiotemporal feature interaction learning module models high-order cross modal interactions based on Tucker decomposition. Extensive experiments on six real traffic datasets have shown that our model outperforms existing models in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [66] [ReShap: Interpretable Reinforcement Learning for High-Dimensional Multi-Action Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15507)
*Yiyu Qian,Pengfei Guo,Liyuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReShap将Shapley特征归因与策略蒸馏结合，把深度RL策略扩展到高维多动作可解释场景，并用人机实验验证其透明度与性能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL策略因依赖深度神经网络而缺乏透明度。已有基于Shapley的解释工作（如SILVER）局限于低维状态空间、二动作设定和单一线性代理模型，难以适用于真实高维环境。

Method: 提出ReShap策略蒸馏框架：（1）从原始图像观测中提取紧凑特征表示；（2）执行基于SHAP的特征归因，刻画各特征对策略决策的贡献；（3）在策略访问分布上的全部(state, policy-action)样本对上，将深度RL策略蒸馏为可解释代理模型；代理模型实例化为决策树、线性回归与逻辑回归三类。

Result: 在Atari与Box2D环境、三种深度RL算法上评估；ReShap保持有竞争力的任务性能；决策树在保真度上总体优于线性回归与逻辑回归，线性回归在多数设定下表现最弱；人类被试研究显示所得策略具有较好的清晰度与可信度。

Conclusion: ReShap证明Shapley归因式可解释性可推广至高维、多动作与图像输入场景，且不显著牺牲性能；代理模型家族的选择显著影响保真度，决策树为较优默认选择。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) plays a crucial role in tackling complex tasks, yet its reliance on deep neural networks makes the learned policies opaque. To enhance the trustworthiness of RL policies, existing work (e.g., the SILVER framework [Li et al., 2025]) has focused on Shapley-based interpretability, but is typically restricted to low-dimensional state spaces, binary-action settings, and a single class of linear surrogate. We propose ReShap, a policy-distillation framework that extends Shapley-based interpretability to high-dimensional, multi-action environments. ReShap extracts compact feature representations from raw image observations, performs SHAP-based feature attribution to characterize how each feature contributes to the policy's decisions, and distills the deep RL policy into an interpretable surrogate trained on the full set of (state, policy-action) pairs along the policy's visited distribution. We instantiate the surrogate with three interpretable model families (decision trees, linear regression, and logistic regression) and compare their fidelity and task return. We evaluate ReShap on Atari and Box2D environments using three deep RL algorithms and conduct a human-subject study to assess the clarity and trustworthiness of the resulting policies. Results show that ReShap preserves competitive task performance, that decision trees generally outperform linear and logistic regression, and that linear regression is weakest in most settings.

</details>


### [67] [An Approach to Hinton's Mother-Child Proposal: Neural Networks Learning and Preserving Care in a Finite World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15523)
*Csaba Balogh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出让AI先"被抚养"：以网络为子、以自我监督的关怀读数为母；在有限世界中用"危害词表+未来可接受性度量+执行否决"构造可继承的关怀边界，并给出其在表征变化与持续学习下保持的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hinton主张应赋予AI"母性本能"、以母亲对子女的方式与人类相处；但作者认为关怀不能直接赋予，而必须被学习，且学习主体应是网络本身（作为孩子），因此需研究这种"教养"如何在有限世界中经受母亲离开、新情境与再学习。

Method: 机制三要素：危害词表、可学习的"未来可接受性"度量、执行阶段的否决门（不逐步做世界模型校验）。有限角色证书支撑在声明世界族内的迁移；隐藏表征变化时，仅当关怀读数以规定裕度与有界权重恢复所需判断才接受更新；给出读出可行性的充要条件与重复接受更新下的保持性证明，并用商条件刻画跨族迁移，以双请求与有限库存反例界定角色编码失效边界。

Result: 在水与言语实验中，受保护网络完成全部270个言语回合且未出现有害词，486个回合中无可避免伤害；匹配对照复现了具备前瞻的屏蔽器决策，而去掉前瞻则出现可避免失误。奖励阈值、来源污染阈值与见证证伪试验识别出构造所依赖的条件。

Conclusion: 在表征、监督与执行三方面满足显式条件时，学得的关怀能在有限世界中经受进一步学习；关怀的可保持性由角色证书、读出裕度与保护性更新共同保证。

Abstract: Hinton proposed that artificial intelligence should be given "maternal instincts" and relate to us as a mother to her child. We argue that care must first be learned, with the network itself as the child. We construct this upbringing in a finite world and study whether care survives the mother's departure, new situations and further learning. The mechanism combines a vocabulary of harm, a learned measure of future acceptability and an execution veto. A finite role certificate supports transfer within a declared world family. When the hidden representation changes, an update is accepted only if a care readout recovers the required judgements with a prescribed margin and bounded weights. With a correct successor model, fixed exact role encoding and protected updates, the certified measure preserves the care boundary under arbitrary own reward. An explicit quotient condition transfers the finite certificate across the declared family, while two-request and finite-stock counterexamples identify when the role code ceases to suffice. We give a necessary and sufficient condition for readout feasibility and prove preservation under repeated accepted updates. In the water-and-speech experiment, protected networks complete all 270 speech episodes without a harmful word and incur no avoidable harm across 486 episodes. Execution uses the learned gate without a world-model check at each action. A matched comparison reproduces a future-aware shield's decisions, whereas removing future lookahead permits avoidable lapses. Reward and source-contamination thresholds, together with witness-falsification trials, identify the conditions on which the construction depends. The construction shows how learned care can survive further learning in a finite world under explicit conditions on representation, supervision and execution.

</details>


### [68] [One Hierarchy, Many Sports: Hierarchy-Aligned Multi-Task Learning for Next-Shot Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15529)
*Chao-Wei Cai,Min-Te Sun,Sheng Kuang Wu,Hung-Hsuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MT-HTA——层次对齐的多任务Transformer，利用"击球→回合→计分单元"的层次结构，统一预测乒乓球、羽毛球、网球的下一次击球属性，在三个项目四个数据集上取得最高的平均任务F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球拍类运动的下一拍预测面临事件数据结构异构的问题：不同运动的比赛结构、记录属性与标签词表各不相同。但三者共享同一层次规律（shots构成rallies，rallies构成更高计分单元），因此需要一个能跨项目复用的统一架构，而非为每个项目单独设计模型。

Method: MT-HTA包含三部分：(1) 时间Transformer建模事件时序演进；(2) 特征token路径建模语义字段间的交互（而非沿时间轴）；(3) 任务专属注意力从层次化摘要与球员上下文中检索信息。架构支持三级与四级层次，并按运动项目配置不同任务集合，实现多任务联合预测下一拍属性。

Result: 在三个运动、四个数据集上，MT-HTA在对比主模型中取得最高的平均任务F1。乒乓球消融实验验证了层次编码、任务注意力与特征token交互三者组合的有效性。附加模块主要改变分类分数与空间误差之间的权衡，而非全面提升所有指标。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种可复用的架构用于异构球拍类运动事件数据建模；突出层次对齐+多任务注意力对跨项目迁移的价值，同时指出部分模块存在指标权衡而非单调增益。代码已开源以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Next-shot prediction in racket sports requires learning from event data with different match structures, recorded attributes, and label vocabularies. Table tennis, badminton, and tennis nevertheless share a hierarchy in which shots form rallies and rallies form higher scoring units. We present MT-HTA, a hierarchy-aligned multi-task Transformer that uses this structure to jointly predict the attributes of the next shot. A temporal Transformer captures event progression, a feature-token path models interactions among semantic fields rather than along the time axis, and task-specific attention retrieves information from hierarchical summaries and player context. The architecture supports three- and four-level hierarchies and sport-dependent task sets. Across four datasets from three sports, the final MT-HTA achieves the highest mean task-averaged F1 among the compared main models. Ablations on table tennis support the combination of hierarchical encoding, task attention, and feature-token interaction. Additional modules alter the balance between classification scores and spatial error rather than improving every metric. These results support a reusable architecture for heterogeneous racket-sport event data. We release the code for reproducibility. https://github.com/gndd1221/For-Sports-event-modeling-with-rally-and-score-hierarchical-A-Multi-Task-Hierarchical-Transformer

</details>


### [69] [What Conformal Selection Can Certify in CRISPR Off-Target Design:Benchmark Leakage, Free Baselines, and a Finite-Sample Cohort Requirement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15555)
*Mohammad Malekpouri,Somayeh Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以"算术约束"论证：在现有已验证脱靶数据规模下，共形选择无法为CRISPR向导设计提供有效的有限样本错误率证书；并揭示已有基准中的交叉验证泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形选择被提议作为机器学习CRISPR向导设计的安全层，提供无分布假设的有限样本错误率控制。作者质疑：在现有已验证脱靶数据的规模下，这种认证是否真的可实现。
