Method: 从共形p值的下界1/(n+1)出发做算术分析，推导Benjamini-Hochberg选择需满足的校准队列规模（候选池规模除以容忍错误率）；并审查标准CIRCLE-seq文件的基准协议，指出现有交叉验证按序列列分组导致训练与测试落在同一向导上，提出修正的配对级协议与无训练基线（位置权重评分、近匹配基因组位点计数）。

Result: 公开队列仅提供10条已验证向导，而2000候选池在10%错误率下需20000条校准样本，导致超过96%的划分中接受集为空。修正泄漏后，配对级判别与单向导保真度显著下降；且无训练的位置权重评分与近匹配位点计数基线不被训练模型超越。

Conclusion: 现有数据规模不足以支撑经认证的共形选择流程；作者给出修正协议、新模型必须超越的基线，以及认证流程所需的队列规模。

Abstract: Conformal selection promises distribution-free, finite-sample control of the error rate of a decision, and has been proposed as a safety layer for machine-learning CRISPR guide design. We ask whether such a certificate is attainable from the validated off-target data that exist. The constraint is arithmetic: a conformal p-value cannot fall below one divided by the calibration cohort size plus one, so a Benjamini-Hochberg selection certifies a single candidate only when that cohort is at least the candidate pool size divided by the tolerated error rate. Public cohorts supply ten validated guides, whereas a pool of two thousand at a ten percent error rate needs twenty thousand; the accepted set is consequently empty in over ninety-six percent of splits. Two benchmarking findings explain why this went unnoticed. The standard CIRCLE-seq file lists ten guides under eighty on-target strings, so grouping cross-validation on the sequence column trains and tests on one guide; corrected, pair-level discrimination and per-guide fidelity collapse. With that leakage removed, a training-free position-weight score and a bare count of near-match genomic sites are not beaten by a trained oracle. We give the corrected protocol, the baselines a new model must beat, and the cohort size a certified pipeline needs.

</details>


### [70] [Observations Drift, Structures Remain: Structural Pretraining with Time Alignment for Electromagnetic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15566)
*Wenjin Gui,Junyu Shen,Haibo Xu,Yuchuang Sun,Luqing Luo,Wupeng Xie,Qian Huang,Fengxiang Wang,Yangang Sun,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"观测偏置预训练"问题，并给出时间对齐+结构掩码预测的结构化预训练方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁信号自监督预训练用重构目标在异构I/Q观测上统一预训练，但波形由产生、传播、采集三因素共同塑造，含可复用结构规律与观测特异的表观/采样差异；导致外观失配与时间失配，模型更易重构观测特异表观与采样坐标而非稳定结构，形成"观测捷径"（observation-biased pretraining）。

Method: 1) RoPE-fs：将token位置映射到采样率归一化的物理时间位置，缓解时间失配；2) 以离散结构单元的掩码预测替代波形重构，降低对外观捷径的依赖；单元分为原子结构单元与涌现结构单元，后者可跨固定patch边界捕捉变长组合模式。

Result: 在多个电磁基准上，相比现有电磁预训练基线，全监督与少样本适配性能均提升；表征在波形表观与时间尺度漂移时保持更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: 重构式预训练在异构电磁观测上存在系统性偏置，采用物理时间对齐与结构级掩码预测可学到更鲁棒、可迁移的电磁信号表征。

Abstract: Electromagnetic signal modeling is increasingly moving toward self-supervised pretraining on heterogeneous I/Q observations. However, an observed waveform is jointly shaped by signal generation, propagation environments, and acquisition processes, which mix reusable structural regularities with observation-specific variations. Existing methods usually pursue unified pretraining with reconstruction objectives on mixed observations. This heterogeneity creates two key mismatches for reconstruction pretraining: (1) appearance mismatch, induced by propagation-dependent waveform variations; and (2) temporal mismatch, induced by acquisition-dependent sampling coordinates. These mismatches make observation-specific appearances and sampling coordinates easier to reconstruct than stable signal structures, inducing observational shortcuts. We identify this failure mode as observation-biased pretraining. To address this, we propose Structural Pretraining with Time Alignment for heterogeneous EM signals. It mitigates temporal mismatch with RoPE-fs, which maps token positions to sampling-rate-normalized physical-time positions, and reduces reliance on appearance shortcuts by replacing waveform reconstruction with masked prediction of discrete structural units. These units are organized into atomic structural units and emergent structural units that capture variable-length compositional patterns beyond fixed patch boundaries. Across diverse EM benchmarks, our method improves both full-supervision and few-shot adaptation over existing EM pretraining baselines, showing that the learned representations better preserve structural consistency when observations drift in waveform appearance and temporal scale.

</details>


### [71] [Learning a calibrated decision head from logged bandit feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15572)
*Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究决策头（decision head）在只有日志化 bandit 反馈下的适配问题，基于 Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Base 的 LoRA 实验得出三点结论：Brier/对数分数作为 REINFORCE 奖励不可用；全标签下四种 bandit 方法均未胜过交叉熵加温度缩放；提出的 CAPS 仅用日志反馈即可优于仅用确认答案微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策头部署后通常只能观测到所选选项是否正确（logged bandit feedback），而非完整标签；作者希望检验常见奖励设计与 bandit 方法在此设定下是否可靠，并给出仅依赖反馈的可行适配方案。

Method: 在 Qwen3-0.6B-Base 与 1.7B-Base 上对决策头做 LoRA 适配；对比不同 REINFORCE 奖励（自身概率的 Brier/对数分数、r、r−p）与路径梯度式损失；全标签下比较四种 bandit 方法与交叉熵+单一温度；提出 CAPS（对所选动作概率施加 proper score 的直接法），并与仅用确认答案微调对比，辅以倾向权重、噪声翻转率下的后向校正与基于留存日志结果的早停。

Result: 以采样动作自身概率的 Brier/对数分数作奖励会失效：错误动作奖励不低于正确动作，均值基线使最低概率错误动作优势最大，二选项时信号为零，一次运行中有序量表问题准确率从 .636 降至 .111；路径梯度评分及 r、r−p 奖励避免崩溃。全标签下四种 bandit 方法在四种未见标签模式上的平均 ECE 均未胜过交叉熵+温度。仅用日志反馈时 CAPS 在 Banking77 上准确率高 2.6 点、ECE 低 .053、选择风险更低（3 种子 vs 1 基线种子），在带常错记录器的情绪数据上单种子高 21.7 点；N=5000 时 CAPS 较全标签训练低 1.5 点（0.6B）与 .6 点（1.7B），倾向权重无增益；已知翻转率下 CAPS 置信度平均上跟随噪声信道，后向校正可恢复大部分干净校准，早停在三组运行中均防止记忆噪声抽样。

Conclusion: proper scoring 分数应作为损失而非 REINFORCE 奖励；bandit 方法在该设定下难以超越简单的交叉熵+温度基线；仅凭日志反馈的 CAPS 是可行且鲁棒的适配方案，接近全标签训练水平，代码与结果公开。

Abstract: A decision head reads a state and a list of options and returns a probability per option in one forward pass. Once deployed, it usually observes only whether the option it chose was right: logged bandit feedback. We adapt such a head, LoRA on Qwen3-0.6B-Base and 1.7B-Base, from that feedback and report three results. First, the Brier or log score of the sampled action's own probability is not a usable REINFORCE reward: every wrong action earns at least gold's reward, with a mean baseline the lowest-probability wrong action gets the largest advantage, and at two options the signal is zero; in one run it cut accuracy on ordered-scale questions from .636 to .111. Scoring the probability as a loss by the pathwise gradient avoids the collapse, as do the REINFORCE rewards r and r minus p. Second, with full labels, none of four tested bandit arms beat cross-entropy plus one temperature on mean expected calibration error (ECE) over four unseen label schemas. Third, from logged feedback alone, Chosen-Action Proper Scoring (CAPS), the direct method with a proper score on the chosen action's probability, beats fine-tuning on confirmed answers only, which discards negatives: on Banking77 by 2.6 accuracy points, .053 lower ECE and lower selective risk (three seeds against one baseline seed), and by 21.7 points on one seed on emotion data with an often-wrong logger. At N 5000, CAPS stays 1.5 points below full-label training at 0.6B and .6 points at 1.7B against one full-label seed, and propensity weights add nothing on this log. Under feedback flipped at a known rate, one seed per rate, CAPS's confidence tracks the noisy channel on average, backward correction recovers most of the clean calibration, and early stopping on held-out logged outcomes prevented memorising noisy draws in all three runs. Code, adapters, logs and per-record results are public.

</details>


### [72] [Constructing and Validating Memorisation Audits for EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15578)
*Inigo Stibbe,Joshua Placidi,Padmanabhan Ramnarayan,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了基于MIMIC-IV训练的自回归EHR基础模型（GPT-2类架构）的隐私泄露风险，发现仅存在微弱的队列级记忆，在测试的攻击下未观测到可靠的个体患者级信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归EHR基础模型可能在推理时泄露训练数据，但临床可预测性使得"患者特定记忆"难以与"泛化能力"区分，现有评估方法缺乏对二者进行细致区分的框架，因此需要深入考察记忆与泄露的真实程度。

Method: 在GPT-2类模型上（训练于MIMIC-IV）针对早期、最佳验证、最终三类检查点，评估三条攻击路径：(1) 固定窗口token重构；(2) 敏感属性泄露；(3) 随机患者级canary。并设置匹配模型与1000患者过拟合正对照以验证灵敏度。

Result: 重构精度随上下文增加而提升，但训练集与留出集差异很小；包含有限临床信息的轨迹唯一prompt未对训练患者产生超额敏感属性泄露；随机二值患者级canary下，训练患者标签恢复AUROC由0.512升至0.549，留出患者仍接近随机；过拟合正对照（1000患者）达到近乎完美恢复，证明攻击对强记忆敏感。

Conclusion: 结果表明模型仅存在弱的队列级记忆，在已评估攻击下无可靠的个体患者级泄露。作者主张采用具有临床意义的推理时审计方法，从而在无需以canary重新训练的情况下检测现有模型中的患者特定记忆。

Abstract: Autoregressive electronic health record (EHR) foundation models may disclose training information at inference, yet clinical predictability makes patient-specific memorisation difficult to distinguish from generalisation. We evaluate fixed-window token reconstruction, sensitive-attribute disclosure, and random patient-level canaries in GPT-2-style models trained on MIMIC-IV, across early, best-validation, and final checkpoints. Reconstruction improves with additional context, but training--held-out differences remain small. Trajectory-unique prompts containing limited clinical information produce no excess sensitive disclosure for training patients. In a matched model with random binary patient-level canaries, training-patient label-recovery AUROC increases from 0.512 to 0.549 while held-out recovery remains at chance. An overfit 1,000-patient positive control achieves near-perfect recovery, confirming sensitivity to strong memorisation. These results reveal weak cohort-level memorisation but no reliable patient-level disclosure under the evaluated attacks. They motivate clinically grounded inference-time audits that can detect patient-specific memorisation in existing models without retraining with canaries.

</details>


### [73] [AI4Plasma II: Geometry-Aware Surrogate Modeling for Variable Electrode Gap GEC Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15584)
*Hao Yang,Zhenyu Zou,Wenshuai Jie,Luowei Yin,Yufeng Wei,Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Zhichao Peng,Chun Chen,Kangxin Liu,Rubing Han,Haoran Guan,Yipei Chen,Peiting You,Yuwei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较六种几何感知代理模型在变电极间距工业等离子体过程预测中的数据需求，发现预设映射Geo-FNO与Transolver仅需100个训练样本即可在四个等离子体场上达到2%平均误差；引入电荷分离加权损失后，Geo-FNO在晶圆附近离子密度剖面5%误差目标下的最少训练样本从1000降至100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业等离子体工艺开发需大范围探索操作与几何参数（尤其电极间距），但反复高保真仿真代价高昂（每例平均5.32 CPU h）；几何变化会导致计算域与离散方式改变，使传统代理模型难以直接应用，因此需厘清各类几何感知代理模型的样本效率。

Method: 构建覆盖压强、电压、频率与电极间距的仿真数据集，对比六类几何感知代理模型：预设几何映射、学习式几何映射、扩展域表示、连续坐标表示、几何原生离散（含Geo-FNO与Transolver），以全等离子体场及晶圆附近离子密度剖面的预测精度为指标衡量数据需求；并提出在训练损失中加入电荷分离加权以改善近晶圆区域精度。

Result: 达到四场2%平均误差时，预设映射Geo-FNO与Transolver需100个训练样本，其余模型需250–1000个；晶圆附近离子密度剖面预测更难，模型间差异更大，预设映射Geo-FNO所需样本最少；电荷分离加权使Geo-FNO在5%剖面误差下的最低测试样本需求由1000降至100；训练数据充足时Transolver精度最高。

Conclusion: 在具备合适几何映射的前提下，预设映射Geo-FNO可作为变间距问题的实用起点；若训练数据充足，Transolver可实现最高预测精度。

Abstract: Industrial plasma process development requires broad exploration of operating and geometric parameters. Geometric parameters, particularly electrode spacing, affect discharge structure and processing uniformity. Exploring these parameter spaces through repeated high-fidelity simulations can be computationally expensive. Surrogate models can offer rapid predictions, but geometric variation complicates their application because the computational domain and discretization may change across samples.
  In this work, six geometry-aware surrogate models based on prescribed and learned geometry mappings, extended-domain representation, continuous coordinates, and geometry-native discretizations are compared in terms of their data requirements for accurate predictions of full plasma fields and ion density profiles near the wafer. The simulation dataset spans pressure, voltage, frequency, and electrode gap, with each reference case requiring an average simulation cost of 5.32 CPU h.
  To achieve a 2% mean error across the four plasma fields, prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO and Transolver require 100 training cases, compared with 250-1000 for the other models. Predicting ion density profiles near the wafer is more demanding and reveals larger differences among models, with prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO requiring the fewest training cases for the profile accuracy targets considered. To further reduce the data required for accurate predictions near the wafer, we introduce charge-separation weighting into the training loss, which reduces the prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO's minimum tested training requirement for a 5% profile error from 1000 to 100 cases.
  These results support prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO as a practical starting point for the variable-gap problem studied here when a suitable geometry mapping is available, while Transolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy with sufficient training data.

</details>


### [74] [Imputing Missing Near-Surface Air Temperature using Graph Attention Networks with Distance-Aware Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15598)
*Emma C. Weber,Dominique Guillot,Daniyar Abykhanov,Julien Emile-Geay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种图注意力网络，将每个网格单元作为节点并与k近邻相连，从不完整观测重建近地面气温场，避免经纬度图像方法的极地畸变，在空间RMSE上优于克里金、GraphEM与LaMa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史气温记录可追溯至19世纪中叶但观测覆盖不完整，早期与高纬度地区缺口最大；主流深度学习方法将地球视为平面经纬度图像，导致极地畸变，重建效果受限。

Method: 构建几何图：每格点为节点，连接k近邻，注意力权重由节点特征、距离及海陆过渡共同决定。以CMIP6 CESM2历史模拟为训练数据，并用1850–2014年时变HadCRUT5观测覆盖进行掩膜；在留出实现上与普通克里金、GraphEM和LaMa修复网络对比，并做消融实验。

Result: 图模型取得最低空间RMSE（0.997 K），较LaMa低10.1%，较克里金与GraphEM低约31%；增益集中于北极与南极，LaMa在密集观测的热带略优。随覆盖度提升，图模型误差下降更快，相对LaMa的优势从1850年代到2000年代扩大逾三分之一。消融表明能力主要来自几何图构建而非辅助物理协变量。

Conclusion: 基于几何近邻图与注意力机制的重建框架能有效缓解球面畸变问题，在稀疏与高纬区域优势显著，且随观测覆盖改善持续获益；增益主要源于图结构设计而非协变量，为历史气候场重建提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: We introduce a graph attention network for reconstructing near-surface air temperature fields from incomplete observations. The model represents each grid cell as a node connected to its $k$ nearest neighbors, with attention weights that depend on node features, distance, and land-ocean transitions. This graph representation avoids the polar distortions associated with treating the globe as a flat latitude-longitude image, as most deep learning reconstruction methods do. This is particularly relevant for historical temperature records, which extend back to the mid-nineteenth century but remain incomplete, with the largest gaps early in the record and at high latitudes. We train our model on CMIP6 CESM2 historical simulations masked with the time-varying HadCRUT5 observational coverage from 1850 to 2014. We then compare our approach with ordinary kriging, GraphEM, and the LaMa inpainting network on a held-out realization. The graph model achieves the lowest spatial RMSE (0.997 K), 10.1% lower than LaMa and approximately 31% lower than kriging and GraphEM. The largest gains occur in the Arctic and Antarctic, while LaMa remains slightly more accurate in the densely observed tropics. The graph model's error also falls faster as coverage increases, so its advantage over LaMa grows by more than a third from the 1850s to the 2000s. An ablation study shows that the model's skill comes mainly from the geometric graph construction rather than from auxiliary physical covariates.

</details>


### [75] [Are Scaling Laws of Universal MLIPs Universal?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15603)
*Theo Keane,Anthony Onwuli,Richard Tomsett,Robert M. Forrest,Jonathan J. Bean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究M3GNet、MACE、PET三种机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在固定模型配置下的数据规模律，比较均匀随机采样、三种化学/结构分层策略、迭代DIRECT与基于预训练MLIP嵌入的最远点采样，发现标度指数、误差下限及采样相对效率依赖模型架构、目标量、采样策略与测试分布，且采样策略排序可跨分布反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经标度律可用于预测扩大训练数据集的收益，但MLIP数据集异质、生成昂贵，且相同数量的结构未必包含相同信息量，因此需厘清“结构数量”之外哪些因素决定数据扩展的实际回报。

Method: 在MatPES-MP-ALOE合并训练池子集上固定模型配置训练M3GNet、MACE、PET；子集规模从1万到64万结构并含完整116万训练集；对比均匀随机采样、三种化学与结构信息驱动的分层策略、迭代DIRECT、基于三种预训练MLIP嵌入的最远点采样；在父分布留出测试集与分布偏移的MP-r²SCAN测试集上评估内聚能、力、应力及综合损失。

Result: 随数据增加呈幂律改善，但拟合标度指数、表观误差下限与相对样本效率随模型架构、目标量、采样策略和测试分布变化；采样策略排序可在不同测试分布间反转，化学分层可提升跨数据集性能却损害同分布性能，而嵌入空间多样性策略常不如随机采样；相对损失与测试-训练化学空间散度强相关。

Conclusion: 结构数量是MLIP扩展的必要而非充分变量；第一性原理计算增量的回报不仅取决于新增结构数量，还取决于其化学与构型内容与模型预期用途的匹配程度。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws enable the prediction of returns from larger training datasets, but machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP) datasets are heterogeneous and expensive to generate, and equal numbers of structures need not contain equal amounts of useful information. We study data scaling under fixed model configurations for M3GNet, MACE, and PET trained on subsets of a combined MatPES-MP-ALOE training pool. Across training structure subsets from 10,000 to 640,000, together with the full 1.16-million-structure training set, we compare uniform random sampling, three chemistry- and structure-informed stratification policies, iterative DIRECT, and furthest-point sampling using embeddings from three pretrained MLIPs. Models are evaluated using cohesive-energy, force, stress, and aggregate losses on both a held-out test set from the parent distribution and the distribution shifted MP-r$^2$SCAN test set. We observe power-law scaling improvements with increasing data, but the fitted scaling exponent, apparent error floor, and relative sample efficiency depend on the model architecture, target quantity, data sampling policy, and test set distribution. Sampling-policy rankings can reverse across test distributions: chemical stratification can improve cross-dataset performance while degrading in-distribution performance, whereas the embedding-space diversity policies frequently under-perform random sampling. Relative loss is strongly associated with test-to-train chemical-space divergence. These results show that structure count is a necessary but insufficient scaling variable for MLIPs. For training MLIPs, the return on additional first-principles calculations depends not only on how many additional structures are included, but also on how their chemical and configurational content aligns with the model's intended use.

</details>


### [76] [FADO: Learning Causal Explanations with Prior-Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15607)
*Seth Flaxman,Jess Carr,Annabel Jakob,Marek Masiak,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FADO（Feature-wise Amortized Do-Operators），一种基于先验拟合网络、在合成SCM数据上摊销训练的方法，从纯观测表格数据中直接预测各特征的平均干预效应Δ_i，作为因果可解释性指标，优于预测性归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据的ML解释文献（SHAP、置换重要性等）主要做预测性归因，本质刻画的是特征X_i与结果Y之间的相关性而非因果性；而实际关心的问题是"干预X_i是否会改变Y"，仅凭观测数据估计因果效应通常需要强因果假设，因此需要一种能在弱假设下从观测数据获得干预效应的手段。

Method: 定义特征干预效应Δ_i = E[Y|do(X_i=+1)] − E[Y|do(X_i=−1)]作为目标量；采用摊销推断（amortized inference）与先验拟合网络（prior-fitted networks），在由结构因果模型（SCM）生成的合成表格数据集上训练——训练时数据生成过程已知，可直接计算真实Δ_i作为监督信号，使模型在测试时仅凭纯观测表格数据集预测Δ_i。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法易于训练，并在标准预测性归因失效的多种场景下给出更好的解释，包括代理变量、混杂因子、对撞因子和安慰剂特征；在真实、合成及半合成数据集（含与不含隐藏混杂因子）上，与现有预测性归因方法和先进因果推断方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: FADO将因果解释性问题转化为可摊销的监督学习任务，绕开了测试阶段对强因果假设的依赖，为表格数据的因果可解释性提供了一种实用、易训练且更稳健的替代方案，弥补了预测性归因在因果语义上的不足。

Abstract: In regression and classification tasks with tabular datasets, most of the literature on explanation in ML (e.g. SHAP, permutation importance) has focused on predictive attribution: does feature $X_i$ help predict outcome $Y$? At its heart, predictive attribution captures correlation, not causation. We instead ask a causal question: would manipulating feature $X_i$ change outcome $Y$? We define a natural quantity to answer this question, the feature intervention effect: $Δ_i = E[Y\mid do(X_i=+1)]-E[Y\mid do(X_i=-1)]$. In general, estimating this quantity from observational data alone would entail strong causal assumptions. We propose FADO (Feature-wise Amortized Do-Operators), an amortized inference method using prior-fitted networks trained on synthetic tabular datasets generated by structural causal models. During training, the synthetic data-generating process is known, so we can compute ground-truth intervention effects $Δ_i$, and train a model to predict them at test time from a purely observational tabular dataset. As we demonstrate through extensive experiments, our new causal explainability method is easy to train and provides better explanations under a range of settings for which standard predictive attribution fails, including proxy variables, confounders, colliders, and placebos. We evaluate it on real, synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets, with and without hidden confounders, and compare it to existing predictive attribution methods and state-of-the-art causal inference methods.

</details>


### [77] [GLCR: Global-Local Collaborative Routing for Multi-Task Electromagnetic Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15609)
*Fang Lu,Yao Lu,Zhuangzhi Chen,Sicheng Zhang,Shanqing Yu,Yun Lin,Qi Xuan,Guan Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HeteroSignal-7多任务路由基准与GLCR两阶段路由方法，动态调度电磁信号至异构专家池，实现精度与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁信号深度网络多为固定输入与目标的单任务专家，跨任务迁移能力弱；电磁基础模型覆盖任务广但预训练与推理开销大，需要在精度与效率间折中。

Method: 采用模型路由将未知信号分派至异构专家池；构建HeteroSignal-7基准（7数据集、14万样本、6类任务、7个分类与波形恢复专家、统一评估协议）；提出GLCR两阶段路由：第一阶段轻量1D-CNN配合加性间隔头输出7类路由预测，第二阶段梯度提升树专家用4个物理统计量，对top-2落入验证误差自动识别混淆对的样本进行再判别；全SNR范围训练，推理无需SNR标签与噪声估计。

Result: 优于全部19个基线，路由准确率96.16%、系统得分76.30%，较次优基线分别高1.16%与1.14%；路由延迟最低，1.43 ms/样本；相比执行全部专家，期望系统FLOPs降低5.9倍。

Conclusion: 为电磁信号异构专家路由提供公开评估平台与高效、可复现的基线方法，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for electromagnetic signal processing are typically developed as single-task experts with fixed inputs and objectives, limiting cross-task transferability. Electromagnetic foundation models offer broader task coverage but incur substantial pretraining and inference costs. To balance accuracy and efficiency, we adopt model routing to dynamically dispatch unknown signals to matched experts within a heterogeneous pool. We construct HeteroSignal-7, a multi-task routing benchmark with 140,000 samples from seven datasets covering six task types, paired with seven classification and waveform-recovery experts and a unified evaluation protocol. We then propose GLCR, a two-stage routing method combining global dispatch with local discrimination of similar tasks. First, a lightweight one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) with an additive-margin head predicts one of seven routing classes. Second, a gradient-boosted-tree specialist uses four physical statistics to re-evaluate samples whose top two candidates match a confusion pair automatically identified from validation errors. The pipeline is trained across the full signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range and requires neither SNR labels nor noise estimation at inference time. GLCR outperforms all 19 baselines, achieving 96.16% routing accuracy and a 76.30% system score, exceeding the respective second-best baselines by 1.16% and 1.14%. It achieves the lowest routing latency among the compared methods at 1.43 ms per sample and reduces expected overall system FLOPs by 5.9 times compared with executing all experts. This work provides a public evaluation platform and an efficient, reproducible baseline for heterogeneous expert routing in electromagnetic signal processing.

</details>


### [78] [Data-Free Metrics Don't Work: The Missing Invariance Under Functionally Preserving Reparameterisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15621)
*Gabryel Mason-Williams,Israel Mason-Williams,Fredrik Dahlqvist*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明随机矩阵理论（RMT）类无数据指标与模型性能、训练无关：利用"临界性"现象对鲁棒层做功能保持的重参数化，可使RMT指标任意变化，而其宣称的与泛化性的关联是虚假的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无数据（data-free）方法被用于逐层量化网络层"强""弱"，其中RMT指标被认为可预测预训练模型质量、识别模型病理并指导预训练优化与后训练压缩；作者欲检验这一关系是否真实成立。

Method: 借助神经网络临界性：部分层（robust层）可被重新初始化/随机化而不改变模型函数行为，而critical层不行。通过对robust层重初始化构造功能保持的重参数化，观察RMT指标的变化；在多种架构与规模上验证。

Result: 在功能等价的重参数化下，RMT指标可取任意值，说明其与性能或训练无关；RMT指标之间以及与泛化性之间的关系被证明是虚假相关；多数预训练模型指标落在"良好"区间，部分取决于模型初始化。

Conclusion: RMT等无数据指标不具备所宣称的层wise预测力与泛化指示作用，不应作为预训练优化或压缩的依据；相关结论受初始化影响。

Abstract: Data-free methods for analysing and understanding the layers of neural networks offer many metrics for quantifying notions of 'strong' versus 'weak' layers, with the promise of increased interpretability. In particular, random matrix theory (RMT) offers data-free metrics that claim predictive power over the quality of pre-trained models at a layerwise precision and the ability to identify model pathologies, indicating a unique relationship with generalisation. As a result, metrics from RMT are championed for pre-training optimisation strategies and post-training compression. We establish that RMT-based metrics are unrelated to performance or training by showing that functionally preserving reparametrisations of a pre-trained model can have arbitrary metrics. We show this across a range of architectures and scales. To create functionally preserving reparametrisations, we exploit the well-established phenomenon of criticality: some layers can be re-initialised or re-randomised without affecting the functional behaviour of the model -- they are called robust -- while others cannot -- they are called critical. Re-initialising or re-randomising robust layers provides functionally preserving reparametrisations for which RMT-based metrics are arbitrary. Moreover, we show that relationships between metrics offered by RMT are spuriously related to generalisation. We conclude by showing that if many pre-trained models have data-free metrics in a 'good' range, it is, in part, dependent on model initialisation. Project page available at: https://gmw99.github.io/data-free-metrics-dont-work

</details>


### [79] [A Mechanistic Interpretability Study of an Astronomy Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15653)
*Rachel C. Zhang,Alberto Bietti,Wes Gurnee,Alessandro Favero,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次对天文基础模型AION-1开展因果机制可解释性研究，揭示其多模态红移预测优势主要定位于编码器首个block，且"可线性解码性"与"因果可定位性"脱钩：空间延展信息分布式冗余，尘埃衰减则依赖单一必要注意力头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在科学领域广泛应用，但其内部计算是否对应已知科学操作尚不明确；厘清机制是评估可靠性、识别失效模式并从模型中获取新科学知识的前提。本文以AION-1的多模态增益为切入点。

Method: 对AION-1进行因果机制可解释性分析：比较成像+测光与仅测光的红移预测表现，按星系属性（空间延展、尘埃衰减）分层评估增益来源；结合线性探针、注意力头定位、消融实验，将多模态优势定位到编码器12个block中的首个block，并区分"线性可解码性"与"因果必要性"。

Result: 多模态优势在空间延展、尘埃衰减强的星系上最大，且大部分集中于第一个block。空间延展可被因果使用，单一头主导线性探针，但消融该头对红移预测影响可忽略——信息分布于多头且擦除后下一层可重新解码。尘埃衰减模式相反：全编码器内解码性均弱，但单一头贡献了大部分多模态优势（尤以高尘埃衰减星系为甚），仅保留该头即可维持几乎全部优势。所测试的天体物理动机操作均未能解释该头所计算的内容。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性揭示"可解码≠因果必要"，冗余分布式表示与必要单头计算并存。该头的计算是否对应未知天体物理操作仍未定，这一未决问题是将可解释性用作科学发现工具的先决条件。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used across the sciences, but whether their internal computations correspond to known scientific operations or proceed through different mechanisms remains unclear. Understanding these mechanisms is essential for assessing reliability, identifying failure modes, and learning new science from the models. We present, to our knowledge, the first causal mechanistic interpretability study of an astronomy foundation model, AION-1, focusing on its multimodal advantage in redshift prediction, where combining imaging with photometry outperforms photometry alone. We find that this advantage is largest for spatially extended and dust-attenuated galaxies, and that most of it is localized to the first of the encoder's twelve blocks. Within that block, how well a property is linearly decodable does not predict whether it can be localized. Spatial extent is causally used, and one attention head dominates linear probing for it, yet ablating that head has negligible effect on redshift prediction: the information is distributed across heads and is decodable again one layer after we erase it. Dust attenuation shows the opposite pattern: it is only weakly decodable anywhere in the encoder, yet a single head is necessary for most of the multimodal advantage, particularly for the most dust-attenuated galaxies, and keeping that head as the only active head in its block preserves nearly all of the advantage. None of the astrophysically motivated operations we tested explains what this head computes. Whether this reflects a limitation of current interpretability methods or a computation that does not correspond to any known astrophysical operation remains open, and resolving it is a prerequisite for using interpretability as a tool for scientific discovery.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Reweighting: Certified Improvements from New Transitions in Discrete MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15668)
*Yishan Xi,Tinglan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出转移支撑设计的框架（同时考虑允许的转移与平稳流），给出支撑增广能严格降低渐近方差的对偶充要条件及可计算的原始-对偶证书，并构造单边增广带来Ω(n)自相关时间改善的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定图上仅优化转移概率会受限于结构本身，导致可逆MCMC估计效率存在难以消除的上限，需要刻画并突破这种支撑层面的结构性限制。

Method: 以目标分布、可观测量与节点流预算为给定条件，推导支撑增广可严格降低最优渐近方差的充要对偶条件，给出优于一切原支撑重加权的原始-对偶可计算证书；研究能量启发的边选择与带约束的平稳流优化，并构造最坏情形族。

Result: 构造出一族情形：仅增加一条边即可使积分自相关时间相对最优原支撑加权改善Ω(n)倍；分子与Ising状态图实验显示，在相同增广Ising支撑上全权重优化在24/24条件下给出严格改进证书，而基于度数的Metropolis-Hastings加权均未通过。

Conclusion: 在显式表示的有限状态空间中，支撑设计与平稳流联合优化可原则性地刻画、认证并利用转移支撑改进，且新增转移的潜在价值与实际核的性能需区分评估。

Abstract: Optimizing transition probabilities on a fixed grap may leave substantial limitations on the efficiency of reversible Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). We develop a framework for transition-support design that considers both allowed transitions and their stationary flows. For a fixed target distribution, observable, and node-flow budget, we derive a necessary and sufficient dual condition for support augmentation to strictly reduce the optimal value of the asymptotic variance, together with computable primal--dual certificates that establish improvements over every admissible original-support reweighting. We also construct a family in which adding a single edge reduces integrated autocorrelation time by an $Ω(n)$ factor relative to optimal original-support weighting, demonstrating that structural limitations can impose arbitrarily large relative losses in estimation efficiency. To realize these gains, we investigate energy-informed edge selection and constrained stationary-flow optimization. Experiments on molecular and Ising state graphs distinguish the potential value of new transitions from the performance of kernels constructed on them: on identical augmented Ising supports, full weight optimization certifies strict improvements in 24/24 conditions, whereas degree-based Metropolis--Hastings weighting certifies none. Our results provide a principled approach to characterizing, certifying, and exploiting transition-support improvements in explicitly represented finite state spaces.

</details>


### [81] [The Noise Floor of a Quantization Comparison: Engine, restart, batch and kernel drift, measured against 8-bit and 4-bit effects in two decision models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15671)
*Aleks Blagojevich,Trevor Sunderland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在NVIDIA GB10上对Cloudflare的两个决策模型（Clef-flash 9B、Clef 27B）在2,400条公开决策上，系统测量"非量化来源"的决策翻转量（引擎重启、引擎更换、batch size变化、未请求的kernel选择），并将量化效应表述为超出这些噪声的"超额翻转率"，发现4-bit量化区间的置信下界高于所有非量化来源的最大翻转，且准确率指标会掩盖大量决策层面的抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常用量化模型与BF16原模型的对比来评估量化，但这种对比混杂了引擎、服务器实例、batch策略、甚至因import包不同而选中的kernel等非量化变量，导致量化效应被高估或误读。作者希望剥离这些混淆源，给出以"超额翻转"为单位的、可复现的量化效应度量，并检验事先注册的假设。

Method: 在2,400条公共决策（同一批记录）上，对Clef-flash（9B）与Clef（27B）在NVIDIA GB10上做分源对照：llama.cpp重启、vLLM重启（BF16/FP8/NVFP4，含第三个实例的探索性对比）、BF16下更换引擎、改变batch size、以及transformers因import自动选择的Gated DeltaNet kernel；量化侧覆盖Q8_0、FP8、Q4_K_M、NVFP4，以逐记录决策翻转率度量，并将量化效应表示为相对非量化源的超额量；对5条预注册预测做检验并在27B上重跑；另在31,275条公共问题上评估准确率与净变化，并发布代码、量化配方、逐记录输出与预注册。

Result: 非量化来源的翻转率在0–0.92%之间；llama.cpp重启逐记录比特一致；vLLM重启在Clef-flash的BF16/FP8/NVFP4下分别翻转0、5、20条，Clef为4、0、22条，第三个实例使NVFP4在4–22条间波动（取决于比较的两个实例）；BF16换引擎翻转6–16条，改batch size翻转8–15条，transformers自动选中的Gated DeltaNet kernel翻转10–14条。量化超额翻转：Clef-flash/Clef在Q8_0为0.88%/1.04%，FP8为2.12%/1.62%，Q4_K_M为5.79%/3.50%，NVFP4为5.79%/5.67%；两模型上所有4-bit区间均排除所有非量化来源。5条预注册预测中2条失败（FP8不落在重启噪声内；换引擎翻转数少于8-bit量化），且在27B上重跑时同样两条失败。准确率掩盖效应明显：Clef的Q4_K_M在31,275题上净变化仅6题（-0.02点，[-0.20,+0.16]），但436条决策变差；NVFP4使Clef-flash降0.95点、Clef降0.65点。

Conclusion: 量化评估必须以非量化噪声为基线，量化效应应报告为超额决策翻转率；在此框架下，4-bit量化的影响显著大于引擎、实例、批处理与kernel选择等工程变量，而8-bit（FP8/Q8_0）的量级可与这些噪声相当甚至更小——这解释了预注册中两条假设的失败。聚合准确率会掩盖决策级别的实质变化，因此需同时发布逐记录输出与配方以保证可复现与可比较。

Abstract: A quantized model is usually judged by comparing it with its BF16 original, but that comparison changes more than the weights. It often changes the serving engine, it always runs on a different server instance, it may change how requests are batched, and on some stacks the library selects a different kernel depending only on which packages import. We measure each of these sources on the same 2,400 public decisions for two of Cloudflare's decision models, Clef-flash (9B) and Clef (27B), on an NVIDIA GB10, and state every quantization effect as the excess over them. Sources that are not quantization move between 0 and 0.92% of decisions. llama.cpp restarts are bit-identical on every record for both models. vLLM restarts flip 0, 5 and 20 decisions at BF16, FP8 and NVFP4 on Clef-flash and 4, 0 and 22 on Clef; a third instance of Clef's quantized builds (exploratory) leaves FP8 at 0 and puts NVFP4 at 4 to 22, depending on which two instances are compared. An engine change at BF16 flips 6 to 16, a batch-size change 8 to 15, and the Gated DeltaNet kernel transformers selects unasked 10 to 14. Quantization inside one engine moves more: for Clef-flash and Clef, 0.88% and 1.04% at Q8_0, 2.12% and 1.62% at FP8, 5.79% and 3.50% at Q4_K_M, and 5.79% and 5.67% at NVFP4. On both models, every 4-bit interval excludes every non-quantization source. Two of our five pre-registered predictions failed, and the same two fail when the tests are rerun on the 27B: FP8 is not inside restart noise, and changing engine moves fewer decisions than 8-bit quantization does. Accuracy hides most of this. Over the full 31,275 public questions, Clef's Q4_K_M build moves accuracy by a net six questions (-0.02 points [-0.20, +0.16]) while 436 of its decisions get worse (exploratory), and NVFP4 costs Clef-flash 0.95 points and Clef 0.65. We release the code, quantization recipes, per-record outputs and the pre-registration.

</details>


### [82] [SpaceMCP: Vision Language Model Spatial Reasoning via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15680)
*Ameen Izhac,Nobuko Yoshida,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpaceMCP 通过 MCP 风格的结构化函数调用，让 VLM 实时查询由前馈三维重建与开放词汇分割构建的场景图，无需训练即在多个空间推理基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的原生空间推理能力进展缓慢、仍显不足；而前馈三维重建已能实现精确、实时的几何理解。作者据此提出用几何工具弥补 VLM 空间推理短板。

Method: 构建 SpaceMCP 框架：用前馈三维重建恢复度量几何，结合开放词汇分割识别并定位物体，实时构建场景图；再以 MCP 式结构化函数调用增强 VLM，使其可查询距离、物体位置、相机属性等空间信息，并由 VLM 组合这些原子信息求解复杂空间任务，全程无需训练。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench、MMSI-Bench 和 VSTI-Bench 上取得 SOTA，超越近期经空间后训练的 VLM，并相对其基座 VLM 每个基准提升 15–22 分。

Conclusion: 鉴于原生 VLM 空间推理进展缓慢而前馈三维视觉发展迅速，该"几何工具+语言推理"的解耦范式是提升鲁棒空间推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Despite impressive advances in large language models (LLM), vision-language models (VLM) still exhibit limited spatial reasoning capabilities. Recent progress in feed-forward 3D reconstruction has enabled precise, real-time geometric understanding from visual inputs. In this work, we propose SpaceMCP, a framework that leverages these advances to introduce an MCP-style paradigm for spatial reasoning. Our approach augments a VLM with structured function calls, allowing it to query a scene graph for spatial information such as distances, object positioning, and camera properties. The scene graph is constructed in real time using feed-forward 3D reconstruction to recover metric geometry, combined with open-vocabulary segmentation to identify and localize relevant objects within the scene. Paired with the VLM's strong reasoning capabilities, the VLM is able to combine this primitive information to solve more complex spatial tasks. Without any training, SpaceMCP achieves state-of-the-art results on VSI-Bench, MMSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench, outperforming recent spatially post-trained VLMs and improving its base VLM by 15-22 points per benchmark. We argue that, given the contrast between the relatively slow progress in native VLM spatial reasoning and the rapid advances in feed-forward 3D computer vision, this paradigm offers a promising path forward for enabling robust spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [Characterizing Predictive Multiplicity in Ranking and Its Fairness Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15705)
*Ainhize Barrainkua,Mikel Malagón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）从分类扩展到基于分数的排序问题，给出排序多重性的形式化定义与理论保证，并揭示其与排序公平性的双重关系，最后用合成与真实数据实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测问题中常存在性能相当但输出相互冲突的竞争模型，即预测多重性；该现象在分类任务中已被广泛研究，但在对社会高利害决策（如福利资源与社会救助分配）至关重要的排序框架中尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 提出刻画排序场景下多重性的形式化定义，并给出基础性理论保证；分析预测多重性与排序算法公平性之间的双重关系：一方面考察模型多重性如何成为不公平的潜在来源，另一方面展示如何通过探索该决策空间以有原则的方式发现歧视性更低的排序；并辅以合成与真实数据集上的实证评估。

Result: 理论保证支撑了所提出的排序多重性定义；实验验证了分析结论，表明模型多重性可导致不公平，同时利用多重性决策空间可获得歧视性更低的排序，并展示了其实际影响。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了预测多重性在排序中的现实相关性，并揭示利用该效应实现更公平结果的潜力。

Abstract: Prediction problems often admit competing models that achieve comparable performance yet produce conflicting outputs, a phenomenon known as predictive multiplicity. While this challenge has been extensively studied in classification tasks, its implications remain largely unexplored in ranking frameworks, which are critical for high-stakes societal decisions such as the allocation of welfare resources and social assistance. This paper investigates the phenomenon of predictive multiplicity in score-based ranking problems. We introduce formal definitions to capture multiplicity in ranking settings, supported by foundational theoretical guarantees. Furthermore, we examine the dual relationship between predictive multiplicity and algorithmic fairness in ranking problems: we analyze how model multiplicity can act as a potential source of unfairness, while demonstrating how exploring this decision space provides a principled avenue for discovering less discriminatory rankings. Finally, we complement our theoretical contributions with insightful empirical evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets, validating our analytical findings and illustrating their practical implications. Taken together, this work highlights the real-world relevance of predictive multiplicity in ranking and sheds light on the potential of leveraging this effect to achieve fairer outcomes.

</details>


### [84] [Efficient Knowledge Transfer in Federated Bayesian Optimization through Neural Network Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15707)
*Alexander Gräfe,Max van Gemmeren,Paul Brunzema,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GLOBAFED，一种无需共享原始数据、允许目标函数各异的知识迁移式联邦贝叶斯优化框架：以"共享特征提取骨干 + 智能体专属变分贝叶斯末层预测头"的概率神经网络代理模型实现跨相关目标的迁移，实证上显著加速收敛并取得更优终态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦贝叶斯优化（FedBO）方法依赖强假设，如各智能体目标函数近乎相同或共享高斯过程超参数，这些假设在异构真实场景中常被违背，导致知识迁移受限、适用性不足。作者希望在不同但相关的目标函数之间实现高效、隐私无损的知识迁移。

Method: 核心是一个概率神经网络代理模型：共享的特征提取骨干网络（shared backbone）加各智能体专属预测头，头部的变分贝叶斯末层（variational Bayesian last layers）提供不确定性估计。优化过程中，智能体在共享骨干上做联邦学习以协同构建公共隐特征表示，而专属头各自适配本地目标；同时支持利用已学到的全局特征对优化进行热启动（warm-start）。

Result: 实验表明，相较当前最先进的 FedBO 基线，GLOBAFED 显著加速收敛并达到更好的最终性能，且智能体能够借助先前学到的全局特征实现优化的热启动。

Conclusion: 通过"共享表示 + 个性化头"的解耦设计，GLOBAFED 在放松同质目标与共享 GP 超参数等限制性假设的前提下，实现了隐私保护下跨异构但相关目标的贝叶斯优化知识迁移，为异构联邦优化场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Bayesian Optimization (FedBO) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Bayesian Optimization (BO) in distributed, privacy-sensitive settings, enabling agents to accelerate the optimization of their local objectives by sharing knowledge about their similarities without exposing raw data. However, existing methods rely on restrictive assumptions, such as near-identical objective functions or shared Gaussian process hyperparameters that are often violated in heterogeneous real-world applications. We present GLOBAFED, a novel framework that enables efficient knowledge transfer across different but related objectives without compromising privacy. At its core is a probabilistic neural network surrogate with a shared feature-extracting backbone and agent-specific heads equipped with varia- tional Bayesian last layers to provide uncertainty estimates. During optimization, agents use federated learning on the shared backbone to collaboratively build a common latent feature representation, while agent-specific heads adapt to their individual objectives. Our empirical results demonstrate that GLOBAFED significantly accelerates convergence and reaches better final performance compared to state-of-the-art FedBO baselines, and enables agents to warm-start their optimization from previously learned global features.

</details>


### [85] [Curiosity-0826-4B: Generative Medical Event Sequence Modeling Across Pediatric and Adult Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15710)
*Mani Nagarathnam,Luke Benham,Paul Blazek,John Cashy,Kevin Chung,John Muenning,Hank Voeller,Shane Waxler,Davis White,Alison Callahan,Kevin Dysart,Andrew Loza,Michal A. Mankowski,Mattheus Ramsis,Nigam H. Shah,Karen K. Wong,Rahul Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Curiosity-0826-4B：基于Epic Cosmos训练的生成式医疗事件基础模型升级版，参数量从1B增至4.3B、上下文窗口从8K扩展至16K，在零样本临床预测上总体匹配或超过前代，并在儿科结局与死亡预测任务上实现拓展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前代 Curiosity-0226 系列模型仅覆盖有限人群与临床事件表征，实验室指标预测精度不足，缺乏对儿科队列与死亡等结局的零样本预测能力，因此需要扩大训练人群、丰富事件表征并提升模型容量与上下文长度。

Method: 基于 Epic Cosmos 数据训练生成式医疗事件基础模型，模拟患者时间线以实现零样本临床预测；通过缩放律分析确定计算最优的初始预训练配置，随后进行上下文扩展的继续预训练；显式表征有记录的死亡事件以支持死亡预测；参数量提升至4.3B，上下文窗口16K，训练token减少56%，训练FLOPs为前代的2.2倍。

Result: 实验室值预测MAE较 Curiosity-0226-1B 降低18%–46%；2–17岁儿科队列中新发哮喘诊断AUROC 0.742–0.785、哮喘急性加重0.829–0.852，与成人参照队列相当，镰状细胞危象AUROC 0.820–0.847 低于成人（0.915）；成人死亡预测在临终关怀、住院与门诊场景AUROC达0.821–0.942；在共同评估任务上总体匹配或超过0226系列最大模型。

Conclusion: 通过扩展至儿科人群与更多结局，Curiosity-0826-4B 拓宽了无需训练任务专用模型即可构建临床与运营应用的途径；后续需验证本地校准、不同患者亚组与事件频率下的表现、时间线完整率、新场景泛化性，并开展临床与运营价值的前瞻性评估。

Abstract: We introduce Curiosity-0826-4B, an updated version of the generative medical event foundation model trained on Epic Cosmos that simulates patient timelines to produce zero-shot clinical predictions. Curiosity-0826-4B incorporates a broader patient population and richer clinical event representations, while generally matching or exceeding Curiosity-0226-1B, the largest model in the previous Curiosity-0226 series, on evaluated tasks supported by both models. Laboratory value prediction improved, with MAE 18--46% lower than that of Curiosity-0226-1B. We evaluated prediction of disease events across pediatric cohorts aged 2--17 years. AUROCs for new asthma diagnosis (0.742--0.785) and asthma exacerbation (0.829--0.852) were comparable to those in the adult reference cohort, while AUROCs for sickle cell crisis (0.820--0.847) were lower than in adults (0.915). Explicit representation of documented death enabled new mortality prediction tasks among adults across hospice, inpatient, and ambulatory settings (AUROC 0.821--0.942). Scaling-law analysis guided compute-optimal initial pretraining followed by continued pretraining with context extension. The resulting Curiosity-0826-4B model has more parameters (4.3B versus 1B) and a longer context window (16K versus 8K). It processes 56% fewer training tokens and requires 2.2 times the training floating point operations (FLOPs) compared with its predecessor. By extending a common zero-shot prediction strategy to pediatric patients and additional outcomes, Curiosity-0826-4B broadens opportunities to develop clinical and operational applications without training separate task-specific models. Further validation should assess local calibration, performance across patient subgroups and event frequencies, timeline completion rates, and generalization to new settings, alongside prospective evaluation of clinical and operational utility.

</details>


### [86] [Orbit: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on IT Operations Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15718)
*Sathwik Narkedimilli,Rakshit Verma,Rahul Soni,Ankit Kushwaha,Balaji Ganesan,Kaustabha Ray,Sunil Saumya,Pratibha Moogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORBIT 是一个面向 IT 运维（ITOps）的 LLM 基准测试，包含选择题、开放式问答和故障排查三种题型，每题附参考答案与来源；审计剔除约十分之一有模板缺陷的生成题，并用真实从业者问答线程重建故障排查题。结果显示 LLM 在选择题上表现好、开放式居中、故障排查最差，且模型自评不可靠，需用独立评审；词面重叠可解出过半选择题，故 ORBIT 定位为诊断与监督工具而非部署测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ITOps 基准多只报告模型得分，却很少解释模型为何失败；同时生成式题目存在质量缺陷，模型自评也可能掩盖问题，因此需要一个可诊断、可追溯的基准。

Method: 构建 ORBIT 基准，含选择题、开放式、故障排查三种题型，每题配参考答案与记录来源；通过审计发现约 10% 生成题存在模板缺陷并将其剔除，故障排查题改为从真实从业者讨论线程及被采纳答案重建；在单一独立评审下评测五个 LLM，并对比模型自评与独立评审、考察词面重叠对选择题的影响。

Result: 五个 LLM 在多数选择题上答对，开放式题正确率约三分之一至略高于一半，故障排查题即使采用更宽松的根因评分标准也只有少数正确；模型自评时对缺陷题的答案大量误判为可接受，故统一使用独立评审；与题目的词面重叠可解出超过一半的选择题正确选项。

Conclusion: ORBIT 应作为选择与监督 IT 运维助手的诊断工具，而非模型部署测试；其核心价值在于揭示失败原因并保证评测可信度。

Abstract: LLMs now support IT operations (ITOps), but benchmarks rarely show why a model fails. We present ORBIT, a benchmark of ITOps questions in three formats, namely multiple-choice, open-ended, and troubleshooting, with each question accompanied by a reference answer and a recorded source. An audit found template defects in about a tenth of the generated items, so we drop them and rebuild the troubleshooting format from real practitioner threads with accepted answers. Under one independent judge, five LLMs answer most multiple-choice items correctly, between a third and just over half of the open-ended items, and a minority of the troubleshooting items, even under a more lenient root-cause rubric. When models judged their own answers, they accepted many answers to defective items, so we use one independent judge for every model. Word overlap with the question finds the correct option for more than half of the multiple-choice items. ORBIT is a diagnostic aid for selecting and supervising assistants rather than a deployment test.

</details>


### [87] [Latent Imagination for Parameterized Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15744)
*Atahan Cilan,İhsan Mert Muhacıroğlu,Ümit Can Bekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyLI，一种在世界模型潜在想象中学习混合策略的基于模型智能体，单次前向即可动作，兼顾样本效率与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAMDP需同时选择离散动作与其含义依赖该选择的连续参数。无模型方法动作快但样本效率低；基于模型方法样本高效但每个决策都需规划，导致动作慢。目标是同时获得样本效率与实时动作速度。

Method: HyLI在world model的潜在想象中端到端学习混合策略，执行时仅需一次前向推理。针对PAMDP结构带来的学习问题，引入两项机制：反事实模式评估（counterfactual mode evaluation），为每个离散动作在每个想象状态上提供学习信号；成功窗口回放（success-window replay），在训练中保留稀有成功样本。

Result: 在7个标准PAMDP基准上，全部任务达到或超过最强无模型基线已发表分数的一个标准差范围内；以少2.4倍环境步数达到该基线90%分数（计入其无奖励预训练则为5.0倍）；决策速度比基于模型的规划器DLPA（其已发表规划设置）快67倍。

Conclusion: HyLI是参评方法中唯一同时比最强无模型基线更样本高效、且快到足以用于实时控制的方法，验证了在潜在想象中学习混合策略可化解基于模型方法"规划慢"的瓶颈。

Abstract: Parameterized-action MDPs (PAMDPs) ask an agent to choose a discrete action together with continuous parameters whose meaning depends on that choice. Model-free PAMDP methods act fast but need many interactions; model-based methods are sample-efficient but plan at every decision, which makes them slow to act. We introduce HyLI (Hybrid Latent Imagination), a model-based agent that learns a hybrid policy entirely in the latent imagination of a world model and acts with a single forward pass. To address learning problems that the PAMDP structure creates, HyLI adds counterfactual mode evaluation, which gives every discrete action a learning signal at every imagined state, and success-window replay, which keeps rare successes in training. On seven standard PAMDP benchmarks, HyLI is within one published standard deviation of, or above, the published score of the strongest model-free baseline in our comparison on all 7 tasks, reaches 90% of that score with 2.4x fewer environment steps than that baseline (5.0x when its reward-free pre-training is counted), and decides 67x faster than the model-based planner DLPA at its published planner settings. It is the only method in our comparison that is both more sample-efficient than that baseline and fast enough for real-time control.

</details>


### [88] [Sensitivity-Aware Surrogate Modeling with Random Feature Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15750)
*John E. Darges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出spike-and-slab随机特征展开(SAS-RFE)，用总Sobol'敏感性指数确定输入纳入概率，从敏感性分析中学习特征采样分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征展开(RFE)的精度依赖采样特征的分布，需要一种能自适应学习该分布以更准确刻画模型输入交互结构的方法。

Method: 采用spike-and-slab分布为各输入在随机Fourier特征中的纳入赋予概率，纳入概率由总Sobol'指数估计确定（而非显式选择重要输入或交互项）；用信息论准则证明总Sobol'指数给出的分布近似模型的输入交互结构；设计迭代算法，利用逐次RFE代理模型的敏感性估计更新采样概率，并给出随机Fourier特征展开Sobol'指数的解析公式，从而无需额外目标函数评估即可完成更新。

Result: 在六个基准问题（输入维度最高100）上的数值实验表明，代理近似精度和敏感性估计均有提升。

Conclusion: SAS-RFE可在不额外评估目标函数的条件下自适应学习特征采样分布，同时改善代理建模精度与敏感性估计，适用于高维问题。

Abstract: Random feature expansions (RFEs) provide a flexible approach to surrogate modeling, but their accuracy depends on the distribution used to sample features. We propose spike-and-slab random feature expansions (SAS-RFE) to learn the feature sampling distribution from sensitivity analysis. The method employs a spike-and-slab distribution that assigns probabilities to the inclusion of individual inputs in randomly sampled Fourier features. These inclusion probabilities are determined by estimates of total Sobol' sensitivity indices, rather than by explicitly selecting important inputs or interaction terms. An information theoretic criterion shows that the total Sobol' indices yield a distribution that approximates the input interaction structure of the model. An iterative algorithm updates the sampling probabilities using sensitivity estimates from successive RFE surrogates. Analytical formulas for the Sobol' indices of random Fourier feature expansions enable these updates without additional evaluations of the target function. Numerical experiments on six benchmark problems, with up to 100 input dimensions, demonstrate improvements in surrogate approximation and sensitivity estimation.

</details>


### [89] [SAME: Semantic Watermark Embedding for Open-Source Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15771)
*Xinyao Song,Jialiang Shen,Hui Kang,Huajie Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Jiyang Zheng,Huaxi Huang,Zhenliang Lu,Chris Russell,Runqi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAME方法，用语义化私有词表嵌入水印，实现跨下游任务可迁移且抗微调的水印，用于开源大模型知识产权保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型易被未授权复制和修改，现有基于触发-响应模式的水印需针对任务数据定制，跨下游任务迁移性差，且难以抵抗后续良性微调。

Method: 引入固定的私有词表，为每个原生token分配私有嵌入对应物，并在模型表示空间中使两套词表的语义表达能力对齐；据此可将私有token灵活组合成语义触发，产生有意义的响应，构造超越预定义触发-响应对的自适应水印；由于私有词表根植于原生语义，水印与模型语义能力耦合，可在微调中持续保持。

Result: 在多种规模和架构的开源预训练模型上，SAME均能提供可靠且鲁棒的水印，支持有效的所有权验证。

Conclusion: SAME通过语义化私有词表解决了传统触发式水印迁移性差与抗微调弱的问题，为开源大模型的知识产权保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Open-source large pretrained models (LPMs) have become increasingly valuable assets for model development and downstream applications, but their accessibility also facilitates unauthorised copying and modification. To protect their intellectual property (IP), existing approaches typically embed tailored trigger-response patterns as watermarks for ownership verification. However, such trigger designs need to be tailored to task-specific data that constrain their transferability across downstream tasks, and are not durable under subsequent benign fine-tuning. To address these limitations, we propose SemAntic waterMark Embedding (SAME), which introduces a semantically meaningful private vocabulary that enables flexible composition of adaptive and persistent watermarks. Specifically, SAME introduces a fixed private vocabulary that assigns each native token a private embedding counterpart, while aligning the semantic expressiveness of the two vocabularies within the model's representation space. This alignment allows private tokens to be flexibly composed into semantic triggers that elicit meaningful responses, enabling adaptive watermarks beyond predefined trigger-response pairs. By grounding the private vocabulary in the model's native semantics, SAME couples the watermark with its evolving semantic capabilities, allowing this connection to be preserved throughout downstream fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAME provides reliable and robust watermarks across LPMs of varying scales and architectures, enabling effective ownership verification for IP protection.

</details>


### [90] [From Link Prediction to Linear Community Detection: A Finite-Sample Guarantee for Graph Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15778)
*Ying Chen,Zhangyang Wang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在有限样本随机块模型(SBM)下，为"链接预测预训练为何能产生线性可分节点嵌入"给出首个定量理论：对任意有界GNN编码器加对称双线性解码器，证明小的总体留出链接预测超额风险蕴含小的社团分类误差，并把误差分解为优化残差、架构近似残差和显式统计项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链接预测(LP)是GNN的标准预训练目标并取得经验成功，但缺乏定量理论解释为何边级目标能学到对节点分类线性有用的嵌入。作者希望在有限样本SBM框架下严格刻画LP风险与节点分类误差之间的关系。

Method: 在SBM中研究任意有界GNN编码器与对称双线性解码器的组合，证明条件性归约：总体留出LP超额风险小 ⇒ 学到的嵌入上直推线性分类器的社团分类误差小。误差界分解为优化残差、架构近似残差与量级约Õ(√(d/n))的统计项。进一步构造少标签分类器：基于半正定二次型G=WZᵀZW的最近质心分类，其标签误差与LP质量耦合。框架通过近似残差实现架构无关性，并将该残差具体化为带谱位置编码的GIN加双线性解码器下的O(log n / n)。

Result: 理论给出LP风险—分数误差—分类误差的完整定理链；对GIN（谱位置编码+双线性解码器）实例化近似残差为O(log n/n)。合成SBM实验验证了该定理链，并展示了最坏情况常数在何种情形下偏保守。

Conclusion: 论文为LP预训练产生线性可分类节点嵌入提供了首个可量化的理论依据，方法架构无关且可实例化到具体GNN，并指出理论界的保守性，为后续更紧的界与更广的模型分析提供基础。

Abstract: Link prediction (LP) is a standard pretraining objective for graph neural networks (GNNs): hide edges, train an encoder-decoder to predict them, and reuse the node embeddings for downstream node tasks. Despite its empirical success, there is little quantitative theory explaining why an edge-level objective should produce embeddings that are linearly useful for node classification. We study this question in the finite-sample stochastic block model (SBM). For any bounded GNN encoder paired with a symmetric bilinear decoder, we prove a conditional reduction: small population held-out LP excess risk implies small community-classification error for a transductive linear classifier on the learned embeddings. The bound decomposes into an optimization residual, an architectural approximation residual, and explicit statistical terms of order $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$ in bounded dense regimes. We further give a constructive few-label classifier: nearest-centroid classification under the positive semidefinite quadratic form $G = W Z^\top Z W$, whose label error is coupled to LP quality. The framework is architecture-agnostic through the approximation residual, and we instantiate this residual as $O(\log n / n)$ for a graph isomorphism network (GIN) with spectral positional encoding and a bilinear decoder. Synthetic SBM experiments validate the theorem chain from LP risk to score error to classification error, and show when the worst-case constants become conservative.

</details>


### [91] [Honesty Is a Race: Predicting When Verifiable-Reward RL Teaches a Model to Bluff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15779)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Alexei Oblomkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究强化学习中两类等初始期望收益的奖励（诚实判定vs不可验证的套话/虚报判定）会导致模型收敛到相反行为（诚实或吹嘘），并给出GRPO一步更新的诚实性阈值条件，量化为$\tilde{p}\,r/b>1+κ$，揭示诚实是一场"竞赛"，并预测出价上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练初期期望收益相同的奖励函数可能把RL运行引向完全相反的结果：诚实或虚报（bluff）。作者希望理解这种分岔的机制——为何某些奖励结构导致诚实、某些导致吹嘘，以及何时诚实训练会失败甚至反噬（在诚实答案为错的题上仍给出诚实判定）。

Method: 理论：对GRPO单步更新进行一阶分析，导出诚实倾向增加的条件$\tilde{p}\,r/b>1+κ$，其中$\tilde{p}$为检查器接受证书的（偏向仍同时给出两种判定的样本的加权）接受率，$κ$为"不可验证判定为正确答案"样本的牵引力，并分析二者的训练动态。实验：在命题Datalog、两个代码模型、小学数学弃答任务上验证；对比检查器奖励与同均值随机彩票；测深度3与深度2 Datalog；并做线性探针检测内部真值表征。

Result: 在$pr/b=1$以上的运行均未崩溃，检查器下的运行可略低于该阈值存活；$\tilde{p}$随检查器提高，故阈值附近诚实是"竞赛"，略低于阈值的运行可赢而彩票不行；$κ$初期大后下降，刚超阈值的运行先向吹嘘下沉（代码上近零）再回升。$pr/b=3$（超上限）时两个代码模型把约一半正确程序判为有bug。深度3 Datalog上，先宽厚地训练再削减收益，三个种子中保持≥67%诚实，而从零训练同一比例降至4-15%、160步后至多47%诚实。深度2 Datalog诚实崩溃时，RL前拟合的线性探针仍能读出真相。存在收益比上限，超过后模型在诚实判定为错的样本上仍给诚实判定。

Conclusion: 奖励结构通过$\tilde{p}$与$κ$的动态决定诚实的成败：阈值附近是竞赛，过高比例导致过度诚实、过低导致吹嘘；训练日程（先宽厚后削减）可显著改善诚实保持；且诚实崩溃后模型内部仍保留真实表征，说明行为崩溃不等于知识丢失。

Abstract: Two rewards with the same expected payoff at the start of training can end a reinforcement-learning run in opposite places, with an honest model or one that bluffs. We study rewards that pay $b$ for a verdict that a checker cannot verify ("this program is correct") and $r$ for a certificate that it accepts (an input revealing a bug). To first order, a GRPO step makes the model more honest exactly when $\tilde{p}\,r/b>1+κ$. Here $\tilde{p}$ is the rate at which the checker accepts the model's certificates, weighted toward the items on which the model still gives both verdicts, and $κ$ is the pull of the items on which the unverifiable verdict is right. Both terms move during training. A checker raises $\tilde{p}$, so near the threshold honesty is a race, which a run just below the threshold can win and a run under a lottery (random pay with the same mean) cannot. The term $κ$ starts large and falls as the other items settle, so a run just above the threshold first dips toward bluffing, on code nearly to zero, before climbing back. Paying the honest verdict also pulls the other items toward it, which predicts a ceiling on the payoff ratio, above which the model gives the honest verdict where it is wrong. On propositional Datalog, two code models, and abstention on grade-school math, with $p$ the model's success rate before RL, no run above $pr/b=1$ ends collapsed, and runs under the checker can survive slightly below it. At $pr/b=3$, above the ceiling, both code models call about half of correct programs buggy. On depth-3 Datalog, training generously under the checker and then cutting the payoff keeps the model at least 67% honest in three seeds, at a ratio where the same model trained from the start falls to 4-15% and is at most 47% honest after 160 steps. On depth-2 Datalog, when honesty collapses, a linear probe fit before RL still reads the truth.

</details>


### [92] [Sparse PCA with Overlapping Supports: NP-Hardness and an FPTAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15810)
*Alberto Del Pia,Dekun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究一般模型下的稀疏主成分分析(SPCA)：各成分稀疏常数独立、支撑可任意重叠，证明其在 r=d=2 时即为 NP-hard，但当 r 与 d 固定时存在 FPTAS，算法通过对行做近似动态规划、两列更新恢复精确正交性并对少数不可更新变量精确求解来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅覆盖单成分，或多成分下支撑互不相交、或共享同一支撑等受限情形，且要求成分既稀疏又两两正交。当支撑重叠时，稀疏性与正交性相互作用，一般模型缺乏算法结果，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 将 SPCA 归约为稀疏版正交 Procrustes 问题，即在该可行域上最大化线性函数；对归约问题给出固定 d 时的 FPTAS，组合了三项技术：按行进行的近似动态规划、恢复精确正交性的两列更新、以及对更新不可用的少数变量进行精确计算。返回解具有严格正交归一列，元素以精确实代数表示。

Result: SPCA 即使在 r=d=2 时也是 NP-hard；当 r 和 d 固定时存在 FPTAS。对固定 ε，算法步数在 n 上为线性（至多相差多对数因子）。此外，稀疏正交 Procrustes 问题在 d=2 时亦为 NP-hard，固定 d 时同样有 FPTAS。

Conclusion: 在个体稀疏常数与任意支撑重叠的一般设定下，SPCA 的计算复杂度被完整刻画：低秩低维情形下 NP-hard，但固定 r、d 时可由 FPTAS 近似求解，且保证精确正交性与线性（至多 polylog 因子）时间规模。

Abstract: In sparse principal component analysis (SPCA) with several components, each must be sparse and all must be pairwise orthogonal. When the supports of the components overlap, these two requirements interact. For a covariance matrix of fixed rank, polynomial-time algorithms with optimality or approximation guarantees are known for one component, and for several components with disjoint supports or with a common support. We study the general model, with individual sparsity constants and arbitrary support overlaps. We are given a rational $n\times r$ matrix $R$ and $d$ sparsity constants $s_i \le n$, and the goal is to find an $n\times d$ matrix $X$ with orthonormal columns that maximizes $\|R^\top X\|_F^2$, such that column $i$ has at most $s_i$ non-zero entries. Here $RR^\top$ plays the role of the covariance matrix and has rank at most $r$. We show that SPCA is NP-hard even when $r = d = 2$, but admits a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (FPTAS) when $r$ and $d$ are fixed. The returned solution has exactly orthonormal columns, and its entries are real algebraic numbers in an exact representation. For fixed $ε$, the number of steps of the algorithm is linear in $n$ up to a polylogarithmic factor. Our approach reduces SPCA to a sparse version of the orthogonal Procrustes problem, that is, to maximizing a linear function over the feasible region of SPCA. We show that this problem is NP-hard even for $d = 2$, and we give an FPTAS for it when $d$ is fixed. This FPTAS combines approximate dynamic programming over the rows, two-column updates that restore exact orthogonality, and an exact computation of the few variables for which these updates are not available.

</details>


### [93] [Large Payments Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15814)
*Natraj Raman,Abhenandhan Manoharan,Vishwasreddy Thuniki,Dhvani Kansara,Anirudh Som,Hassan Firouzi,Kostis Gourgoulias,Laukik Hoshing,Sudhir Kanala,Austin Reiter,Ebby Abdul,Shantanu Chandra,Ryan Schmiedl,Zack Anderson,Sumitra Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向支付领域的专用基础模型 Large Payments Model (LPM)，在大规模结构化交易数据上从零预训练，学习可复用的支付预测表示，包含 Independent 与 Sequence 两种架构，并在实际支付任务与生产部署中优于强任务专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测、授权预测、支付优化等支付应用依赖高度重叠的交易信息，逐个任务独立建模会重复投入；因此需要一个可复用的“支付智能层”共享学习到的表示与预测信号。难点在于异构交易字段、高基数标识符、生命周期相关属性、长历史、稀疏结果，以及延迟与成本约束。

Method: 构建领域专用基础模型 LPM，直接在大规模结构化交易数据上从零训练：采用字段特定编码（field-specific encodings）与生命周期感知的自监督（lifecycle-aware self-supervision）；提供两种互补架构——Independent 变体使用混合掩码（hybrid masking），支持高效的逐交易推理；Sequence 变体通过紧凑隐表示引入实体关联历史以获得更丰富时序上下文；可选蒸馏以支持资源受限部署。预训练规模达 400B tokens，并研究缩放规律、下游性能与表示复用。

Result: 在商户服务与资金管理服务（Merchant and Treasury Services）的多个预测任务上，LPM 优于强任务专用基线，显示其跨不同支付领域的适用性；其表示配合紧凑的任务专用组件，可适配预训练中未出现的目标任务；并已完成生产部署。

Conclusion: LPM 表明支付领域基础模型可作为可复用支付智能的实用基础：兼顾规模化预训练、表示复用、低成本适配与生产可用性。该结果将 LPM 定位为可复用支付智能的实用基础。

Abstract: Payment applications such as fraud detection, authorization prediction, and payment optimization draw on overlapping transaction information. This motivates a reusable payments intelligence layer that shares learned representations and predictive signals, rather than developing predictive models independently for each task. Realizing this shared capability requires handling heterogeneous transaction fields, high-cardinality identifiers, lifecycle-dependent attributes, long histories, and rare outcomes while meeting latency and cost constraints. We present Large Payments Model (LPM), a domain-specific foundation model designed for operational use and trained from scratch on large-scale structured transaction data to learn reusable representations for payment prediction tasks. LPM employs field-specific encodings and lifecycle-aware self-supervision in two complementary architectures: the Independent variant uses hybrid masking and supports efficient per-transaction inference, while the Sequence variant incorporates entity-linked histories through compact latent representations for richer temporal context. Optional distillation enables resource-constrained deployment. We pretrain LPM on up to 400 billion tokens and study scaling, downstream performance, and representation reuse. LPM improves over strong task-specific baselines across multiple prediction tasks in Merchant and Treasury Services, demonstrating applicability in distinct payment domains. Its representations also facilitate adaptation to a target not observed during pretraining through compact task-specific components. Together with production deployment, these results position LPM as a practical basis for reusable payments intelligence.

</details>


### [94] [Training Calibrated Conditional Generative Models with a Sample-Only Coverage Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15829)
*Benjamin Remy,Raphael Rossellini,Chihway Chang,Rebecca Willett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅基于样本估计的期望覆盖概率可微损失，为正则化条件生成模型后验提供严格散度，兼顾覆盖性与后验识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型已成为科学逆问题中采样后验的主流工具，但常过度自信，导致不确定性量化不可靠。现有正则项互补性不足：可微覆盖目标依赖模型密度且不能识别后验；严格适当评分规则（如能量分数）只需样本，却不直接约束覆盖。

Method: 构造基于期望覆盖概率的可微损失函数，覆盖概率完全由样本估计，适用于任意条件生成模型，无需可解密度或结构改动，并证明其为严格散度（当且仅当模型等于真实后验时为零）。

Result: 在基于模拟的推断基准上，其效果与基于密度的覆盖损失相当而无需评估密度；在高维成像逆问题中，其后验校准优于评分规则类正则项，且点估计重建质量几乎不变。

Conclusion: 该损失在样本可得条件下统一了覆盖正则与后验识别的优势，为科学逆问题中的校准后验采样提供了通用、轻量的正则化方案。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have become the tool of choice for sampling posteriors in scientific inverse problems, where we seek to sample images or signals conditioned on a set of indirect and noisy measurements. Yet they are often overconfident, which is a critical failure in scientific applications where accurate uncertainty quantification is essential. Existing regularizers fall short in complementary ways: differentiable coverage objectives require the model's density and do not identify the posterior, while strictly proper scoring rules such as the energy score need only samples but do not directly enforce coverage. We close this gap by introducing a differentiable loss function based on the expected coverage probability, estimated from samples alone. It applies to any conditional generative model, with no need for tractable densities or architectural changes. We prove that it is a strict divergence, vanishing if and only if the model equals the true posterior. On simulation-based inference benchmarks, it matches a density-based coverage loss without evaluating the density. On high-dimensional imaging inverse problems, it yields better-calibrated posteriors than scoring-rule regularizers, with little change in the reconstruction quality of point estimates.

</details>


### [95] [Fewer Expert Transfers Without Changing Routing: Expert Eviction for MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15842)
*Pierre-Luc Bacon,Diego Calanzone,Jean-Maxime Larouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在OLMoE-1B-7B的真实CPU-to-GPU拷贝测时框架下，直接测量MoE专家缓存策略的"路由稳定性—质量"权衡；发现"最远未来使用即淘汰"（Belady式）规则在零质量损失下比LRU少拷贝2.1倍专家，接近理论最少拷贝的3.5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过训练路由器让相邻多个token复用同一批专家，并以"路由切换次数减少"作为"专家拷贝次数减少"的代理指标。但切换次数与真实CPU-to-GPU拷贝代价并非等价，缺少直接测量该权衡的实证依据。

Method: 构建一个执行并计时真实CPU-to-GPU专家拷贝的测试框架，针对OLMoE-1B-7B在L40S GPU上评测：(1) 固定专家集合的解码策略与LRU缓存对比；(2) 不改变路由决策、仅在淘汰时选择"下次使用最远"专家（Belady最优淘汰）的缓存规则；(3) 分别用未来16个token的需求与基于历史请求计数的规则做前瞻信息近似。

Result: 所有固定专家集合并解码快于LRU的策略，至少使next-token loss上升0.067 nats；Belady式淘汰规则比LRU少拷2.1倍专家且质量无损，距理论最少拷贝仅差3.5%；使用未来16个token的需求可复现96%的拷贝削减，而仅依赖历史请求计数的规则仅复现27%。

Conclusion: 以"减少路由切换"为代理会引入不可忽视的质量损失；更优方向是保持路由不变、改进缓存淘汰策略。前瞻性（未来需求）信息是收益的主要来源，短窗口预测即可获得大部分收益，而平稳性/历史统计类启发式效果有限。

Abstract: A mixture-of-experts (MoE) language model uses only a few experts per token, so a server can keep most expert weights in CPU memory and copy an expert to the GPU when the router requests it. Recent methods train the router to keep the same experts for several tokens, and treat fewer routing switches as a proxy for fewer copies. We measure the trade-off directly, in a harness that performs and times real CPU-to-GPU copies for OLMoE-1B-7B. On an L40S GPU, every tested policy that holds an expert set fixed and decodes faster than the least-recently-used (LRU) cache increases next-token loss by at least 0.067 nats. A change that leaves every routing decision intact does better: when the cache must evict an expert, evict the one whose next use lies farthest in the future. This rule copies 2.1x fewer experts than LRU at no loss in quality and is within 3.5% of the fewest copies possible. It needs future requests; the next sixteen tokens of demand recover 96% of its reduction, whereas a rule based on counts of past requests recovers 27%.

</details>


### [96] [Your Hypergradient is Skewed: Antithetic Neumann Estimation for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15895)
*Jason Bohne,Pawel Polak,Gary Kazantsev,David Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出双层优化中超梯度估计的Neumann级数存在第三种误差来源——偏度（skewness），并提出对偶Neumann估计器（ANE）消除奇数阶扰动项，在多个任务上优于随机与方差缩减基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化的超梯度分析主要关注偏差与方差，但Neumann级数截断估计的乘积结构会产生被忽视的误差：各因子偏度被逐因子放大，在弱收缩、深层截断、大内层条件数及逐因子噪声不对称（如标签噪声、离群点、类别不平衡）下尤为严重。

Method: 提出对偶Neumann估计器（ANE）：对每个Hessian样本，围绕参考Hessian构造镜像对应样本，对所得的逆Hessian估计取平均。精确参考下乘积仅保留偶数阶噪声，消除所有奇数阶扰动项；近似参考下残差奇数阶贡献与参考误差成线性界。ANE仅需修改Hessian-向量积步骤，可与随机或方差缩减方法结合，实例化为ANE-SGD与ANE-VR（后者加入递归方差缩减）。

Result: 在鲁棒回归、数据清洗、离线强化学习与不平衡分类四类任务上（每任务20个随机种子），ANE方法均优于随机与方差缩减基线，相对最佳基线损失降低4%–25%；鲁棒回归提升最大，测试损失降低25%，测试R²提高54%。

Conclusion: 偏度是Neumann超梯度估计中不可忽略的误差源；通过镜像对偶采样可有效消除奇数阶噪声并保持与现有双层优化框架的兼容性，带来稳定且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a core tool in practical machine learning workflows, including hyperparameter tuning, data reweighting, robust learning, reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification. Hypergradient computation requires the inverse of the inner Hessian, typically estimated via a truncated Neumann series. While existing analyses focus on bias and variance, we show that the product structure of the Neumann series introduces a third source of error: skewness. We identify that, unlike ordinary gradients, whose asymmetry is suppressed by mini-batch averaging, the Neumann estimator amplifies skewness across factors, with the third-moment error growing, under weak contraction, with truncation depth, inner condition number, and per-factor noise asymmetry. This arises in many practical settings such as corrupted labels, outliers, heterogeneous or class-imbalanced datasets. To address this, we introduce the Antithetic Neumann Estimator (ANE), which pairs each Hessian sample with a mirrored counterpart around a reference Hessian and averages the resulting inverse-Hessian estimates. With an exact reference, the antithetic product retains only even-order noise, eliminating all odd-order perturbation terms; with an approximate reference, the residual odd-order contribution is bounded linearly in the reference error. ANE requires only a modification of the Hessian vector step and combines with existing stochastic or variance-reduced bilevel methods. We instantiate ANE-SGD and ANE-VR, the latter adding recursive variance reduction. Across robust regression, data cleaning, offline reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification, ANE-based methods improve on stochastic and variance-reduced baselines on every task (20 seeds each), lowering loss by 4 to 25% relative to the best baseline; the largest gains, on robust regression, are a 25% lower test loss and a 54% higher test r-squared.

</details>


### [97] [Reinforcement Learning for Inventory Control under Regime-Switching Demand: A Controlled Comparison of LSTM, MLP, and (s,S) Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15945)
*Alex Haines,Nathanael L. Baisa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在隐藏马尔可夫机制切换需求下，LSTM循环策略在每个提前期均优于MLP无记忆策略与(s,S)启发式，且相对MLP的优势随提前期单调扩大；隐藏状态表征由短期"机制指示"转向长期"多级流道状态"编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典(s,S)策略在平稳需求下可证最优，但需求在隐藏机制间切换时无法适应；(s,S)与固定观测窗口的MLP-PPO均无法维护对潜在需求结构的信念。作者据此追问：循环策略能否同时胜过经典基线与无记忆策略，且该优势如何随供应链提前期变化。

Method: 自建Gymnasium环境：每日需求由两状态隐马尔可夫过程生成、泊松发射，评估时机制已知但对智能体隐藏。对比三类策略：优化的(s,S)启发式、MLP-PPO、LSTM循环PPO，在等算力条件下训练，于提前期0/3/6/10天各评估30个确定性回合。并用PCA分析LSTM隐藏状态表征。

Result: 循环策略在所有提前期均胜过两个对照；相对MLP的优势随提前期单调扩大，从提前期0时的持平到提前期10时的2.17倍。PCA显示：短提前期下隐藏状态主要编码二值机制指示量，长提前期下转为编码多级管道状态。

Conclusion: 随着信用分配时程延长，循环记忆成为决定性架构因素；机制切换需求下循环表征兼具信念维护与管道状态追踪能力。

Abstract: Classical inventory policies such as (s,S) are provably optimal under stationary demand but cannot adapt when demand switches between hidden regimes, and memoryless deep reinforcement learning policies, confined to a fixed observation window, cannot maintain a belief over latent demand structure. We ask whether a recurrent policy offers a measurable advantage over both a memoryless policy and a classical baseline under regime-switching demand, and how that advantage scales with supply-chain lead time. We build a custom Gymnasium environment in which daily demand is generated by a two-state hidden Markov process with Poisson emissions, so that the regime is known at evaluation time but hidden from the agent. An optimised (s,S) heuristic, an MLP PPO agent, and an LSTM Recurrent PPO agent are trained under matched compute and evaluated at lead times of 0, 3, 6, and 10 days over 30 deterministic episodes each. The recurrent policy outperforms both alternatives at every lead time, and its margin over the MLP widens monotonically with lead time, from parity at lead time 0 to 2.17$\times$ at lead time 10. Principal component analysis of the LSTM hidden state explains the widening gap: the representation shifts from encoding a binary regime indicator at short lead times to encoding multi-step pipeline state at long lead times. Recurrent memory becomes the decisive architectural factor as the credit-assignment horizon lengthens.

</details>


### [98] [Riordan Arrays as Structured Operators for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15954)
*Dennis Davenport,Dewayne A. Dixon,Moussa Doumbia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该短文提出将 Riordan 阵列用作人工智能与数据分析中的结构化算子，通过对生成函数施加复合变换 A(z)↦g(z)A(f(z)) 构造一族参数化的因果线性算子，并勾勒其可能支撑的网络架构与实验计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riordan 阵列传统上只作为编码生成函数变换与组合关系的无限下三角矩阵被研究，其在机器学习建模中的算子价值尚未被系统探讨；作者希望为其寻找"代数结构带来有用归纳偏置"的适用场景，而非宣称其普适性。

Method: 以数据序列的生成函数 A(z) 为对象，用 Riordan 阵列 (g(z), f(z)) 定义变换 A(z)↦g(z)A(f(z))，由此得到融合卷积、替换（substitution）与多尺度混合的参数化因果线性算子族；在此基础上讨论若干可能架构（参数高效网络层、变长序列模型、可解释循环系统、可逆架构、层次/递归生成数据模型），并梳理无限维视角的能与不能、理论与数值挑战及初步实验方案。属立场/展望型（note）论述，无实证结果。

Result: 文中未报告实验或定量结果。定性结论为：Riordan 算子可统一表达卷积、序列替换与多尺度混合三类操作，并天然保持因果性（下三角结构）；同时作者明确指出该框架的适用边界与理论、数值层面的开放问题。

Conclusion: Riordan 阵列的价值在于为特定数据类型提供由代数结构导出的归纳偏置，而非通用建模工具；后续工作应聚焦于识别这类"结构匹配"的任务设定，并通过初步实验验证其参数效率与可解释性优势。

Abstract: Riordan arrays have traditionally been studied as infinite lower-triangular matrices that encode generating-function transformations and combinatorial relationships. This note proposes a complementary direction: using Riordan arrays as structured operators in artificial intelligence and data analysis. For a data sequence with generating function $A(z)$, a Riordan array $(g(z),f(z))$ produces the transformation \[
  A(z)\longmapsto g(z)A(f(z)). \] This yields a parameterized family of causal linear operators that combine convolution, substitution, and multiscale mixing. The resulting structure may support parameter-efficient neural-network layers, variable-length sequence models, interpretable recurrent systems, invertible architectures, and models for hierarchical or recursively generated data. We explain what the infinite-dimensional viewpoint does and does not provide, outline several possible architectures, identify theoretical and numerical challenges, and propose an initial experimental program. The aim is not to claim that Riordan arrays are appropriate for all data, but to identify settings in which their algebraic structure supplies a useful inductive bias.

</details>


### [99] [WELP : Weighted Ensemble in the LooP for committor learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15963)
*Jacky Chen,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“WE-in-the-loop”框架，将committor（承诺概率）学习与加权系综（WE）增强采样交替耦合，相互迭代，解决学习committor需要采样其描述的稀有跃迁这一循环依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算化学与生物物理中，刻画复杂分子体系在亚稳态间的跃迁是核心难题。committor函数（从某构型出发先到达某一态的概率）同时编码跃迁进度与机制，是理想的集合变量（CV），但学习它本身需要采样其所描述的稀有跃迁，形成循环依赖。

Method: 构建WE与committor学习的交替闭环：WE模拟利用CV驱动跃迁动力学并产生跃迁数据，用于以自洽损失训练神经网络committor；学得的committor再作为改进的CV用于下一轮WE模拟。同时整理并公开一个丙氨酸二肽（alanine dipeptide）的经验committor值基准数据集。

Result: 在双通道二维体系、丙氨酸二肽和chignolin上，该框架稳定优于贪心（greedy）基线，说明有原则的探索（principled exploration）的重要性。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维分子体系中的稀有跃迁研究提供了新方法，所构建的基准为评估committor学习方法提供了标准。

Abstract: Understanding how complex molecular systems transition between metastable states is a central challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics. A key tool for characterizing these transitions is the committor function: the probability that a trajectory initiated from a given configuration reaches one state before another. Because the committor encodes both the progress and the mechanism of a transition, it is an ideal collective variable (CV). Learning it, however, requires sampling the rare transitions it describes. We address this circular dependency with a ``WE-in-the-loop'' framework that couples committor learning with weighted ensemble (WE), an enhanced sampling method that uses a CV to drive transition dynamics. Our framework alternates between the two: WE simulation generates transition data for training a neural network committor with a self-consistent loss, and the learned committor then serves as an improved CV for subsequent WE simulation. Across a two-channel 2D system, alanine dipeptide, and chignolin, our framework consistently outperforms greedy baselines, highlighting the importance of principled exploration. We also curate a public benchmark of empirically determined committor values for alanine dipeptide. Together, the framework and benchmark provide a new method for studying rare transitions in high-dimensional molecular systems and a standard for evaluating committor learning methods.

</details>


### [100] [PhysICL: In-Context Learning of Physical Fields on Unstructured Meshes from Procedural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15965)
*Fabien Casenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PhysICL，一个仅在程序化任务上预训练的单一模型，可在无权重更新情况下于非结构网格上做物理场上下文学习（in-context learning）预测并给出分位数，支持可变上下文样本数、输入场与输出场数量以及网格规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计研究每次仿真都需在各自网格上重建代理模型（surrogate），需针对每个研究重新训练，成本高；同时网格规模与拓扑变化使传统场预测代理难以泛化，因此希望借助表格型基础模型的上下文学习能力，实现一次前向即适配新任务的物理场预测。

Method: 单一模型 PhysICL，仅用程序化任务预训练；程序化先验在形变（morphed）的 2D/3D 模板网格上抽取通用物理定律与边界元物理模型，且仅在模型采样点上求值，由 CPU worker 在线生成任务以持续喂满 GPU；模型直接从带标签仿真中在查询网格上预测场及分位数，无需更新权重，支持可变上下文样本数、标量与输入场、任意数量输出场，任务内网格尺寸与连通性可变化；并在大网格上采用固定空间预算控制显存。

Result: 在五个预训练未见过的 PLAID 数据集上，场误差随上下文增多而下降，K=32 起在每个数据集与每次上下文抽样中均优于最近的上下文仿真；在其中两个上与原位降阶模型（ROM）相当，在低秩场情形下不及 ROM。在节点中位数约 110 万（为最大预训练网格 20 倍）的 AhmedML 表面上，固定空间预算使 K=64 时峰值 GPU 显存为 4.27 GiB，而 2D_MSHE 的 6,000 节点网格上为 4.07 GiB；在远离先验的 2D_MSHE 上，以十分之一预训练成本、仅用重现其几何与载荷（不含其输出）的程序化任务微调，应力误差降低 2.7 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明在非结构网格上对物理场预测做上下文学习是可行的：单一预训练检查点无需权重更新即可跨数据集、跨输出数量与跨网格规模直接预测，并在大网格上保持稳定显存，且可低成本地通过程序化微调在远离先验的场景上进一步提升精度。

Abstract: Engineering design studies produce a few tens to a few hundred simulations of one problem, each on its own mesh, and their surrogates are retrained for every study. Tabular foundation models instead infer a new task from its examples in one forward pass, after pretraining on synthetic tasks. We demonstrate the feasibility of in-context learning for physical field prediction on unstructured meshes. A single model, PhysICL, pretrained solely on procedural tasks, predicts fields, with quantiles, directly on each query mesh from labeled simulations, without updating its weights. The same checkpoint accepts variable numbers of context examples, scalar inputs and input fields, and any number of output fields, while mesh size and connectivity can vary within a task. Its procedural prior draws generic laws and boundary-element physical models on morphed 2D and 3D template meshes, evaluated only at the points the model samples, fast enough that tasks generated on the fly by CPU workers keep the GPUs busy throughout pretraining. On five PLAID datasets never seen in pretraining, its field error decreases as the context grows and, from $K = 32$, is below that of the nearest context simulation on every dataset and context draw. It is on par with per-dataset reduced-order models on two of them, behind them where the fields are low-rank. On AhmedML surfaces with a median of 1.1 million nodes, twenty times the largest pretraining mesh, its fixed spatial budgets keep the peak GPU memory at $K = 64$ at 4.27 GiB, against 4.07 GiB on the 6,000-node meshes of 2D\_MSHE. Finally, on 2D\_MSHE, far from the prior, fine-tuning on procedural tasks that reproduce its geometry and loads, without any of its outputs, at a tenth of the pretraining cost, divides the stress error by 2.7.

</details>


### [101] [Subject-Held-Out Evaluation of Deep Ground Reaction Force Estimators Across Two Laboratories: Leakage and Cross-Laboratory Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15966)
*Hernan David Santos,Yinong Chen,Yu Ding,Xudong Zhang,Jian Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统量化了深度学习地面反力/压力中心估计中三类常见做法（trial 级划分、坐标系不一致、单实验室验证）各自的代价，并检验模型能否跨实验室迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从动作捕捉标记估计地面反力与压力中心的深度网络，通常只在本实验室验证、使用很少被说明的坐标系，甚至采用会把同一受试者同时放入训练集和测试集的 trial 级划分。作者希望量化这些做法各自带来多少精度高估，以及模型能否在不同实验室之间迁移。

Method: 在统一流水线、统一调参流程、三个随机种子下，比较三种序列模型（卷积、卷积-循环、卷积-Transformer），在两个独立数据集（步态 6 人、职业搬运 10 人）上评估四种协议：trial 级划分、留一受试者、两实验室合并训练并匹配留出受试者、跨实验室迁移；同时比较不同目标坐标系的影响，并用牛顿定律相关性在训练前检测坐标系错配。

Result: 固定配置下，trial 级划分使受试者留出 R² 高估 +0.040 至 +0.194（职业数据上该高估超过所有架构间差异）；受试者留出时三模型 R² 为 0.583–0.804，最优模型随数据集而变且差距落在受试者间变异内；合并两实验室数据对匹配受试者无改善；跨实验室迁移双向均低于随机水平，包括不受面内旋转影响的垂直力；目标留在测力台坐标系而输入按朝向对齐，损失 0.57 的步态前后向力方差解释量，足以使该力显得最难估计。

Conclusion: 划分协议与目标坐标系对报告精度的影响大于架构选择。建议：采用受试者留出验证、训练前检查坐标系一致性、并在部署声明前完成跨实验室测试。
