Abstract: Deep networks that estimate ground reaction forces and centers of pressure from motion-capture markers are validated within one laboratory, in a seldom-stated coordinate frame, and sometimes with trial-level splits that put the same person in the training and the test set. We measured what each practice costs, and whether such models transfer between laboratories. Three sequence models (convolutional, convolutional-recurrent, and convolutional-transformer) were evaluated on two independent datasets, locomotion (6 subjects) and occupational patient handling (10 subjects), under one pipeline, one tuning procedure, three seeds, and four protocols: trial-level split, leave-one-subject-out, pooled two-laboratory training with matched held-out subjects, and cross-laboratory transfer. At a fixed configuration, trial-level splits overstated the subject-held-out coefficient of determination by +0.040 to +0.194; on occupational data the overstatement exceeded every difference between architectures. With subjects held out, the three models reached values between 0.583 and 0.804; the leader differed between datasets, and gaps lay within subject-to-subject variability. Pooling the laboratories changed neither dataset on matched subjects. Transfer between laboratories was below chance in both directions, including the vertical force, which no in-plane rotation can affect. Leaving force targets in the plate frame while inputs were heading-aligned cost 0.57 of locomotion anteroposterior-force explained variance, enough to make that force look hardest; a Newton's-law correlation detected the mismatch before training. Split protocol and target frame changed reported accuracy more than architecture did. Learned force estimators should be validated with subjects held out, checked for frame consistency before training, and tested across laboratories before deployment claims are made.

</details>


### [102] [Characterizing the Edge of Stability in Variational Training Without Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15990)
*Juraj Marusic,Jonathan Wenger,Beau Coker,John P. Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论与实验两方面证明：在仅用期望损失训练的贝叶斯神经网络中，更大的学习率与更少的参数采样数会使极小值更平坦（泛化更好），其训练处于由梯度估计信噪比与蒙特卡洛样本数刻画的"修正稳定边缘"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法依赖与先验的散度做显式正则，但深度学习中先验难以设定，实践中常大幅降低正则项权重；转而只用期望损失训练、依靠优化器的隐式正则，虽绕开了先验设定问题，但现有理论不清楚关键优化超参数如何影响这种隐式正则。

Method: 理论分析与实证相结合：将期望损失训练视为带噪声的随机优化过程，刻画梯度估计的信噪比与蒙特卡洛样本数对极小值平坦度的影响，并推导其对应的"修正稳定边缘"条件；在分布外（OOD）基准数据集上进行验证实验。

Result: 学习率越大、参数采样数越少，所得极小值越平坦，而平坦极小值已知具有更好泛化性；期望损失训练运行在修正的稳定边缘上，该边缘由梯度估计信噪比和蒙特卡洛样本数共同决定；OOD 基准实验证实了理论结论的实际意义。

Conclusion: 期望损失训练中的隐式正则可由学习率与采样数等超参数解析地调控，为无需先验设定的变分式深度学习提供了可操作的正则化设计原则，并解释了其泛化优势来源。

Abstract: Variational approaches in deep learning promise to deliver improved uncertainty quantification and out-of-distribution generalization by inferring a distribution over weights, regularized by a divergence to a chosen prior. However, prior elicitation presents a significant challenge in deep learning, resulting in the common practice of significantly downweighting the regularization term in the variational objective. Recently, it was proposed to train solely via the expected loss by relying on implicit regularization from the optimizer, rather than explicit regularization from the divergence to the prior. While this approach largely sidesteps the issue of prior elicitation, current theory lacks an understanding of how key optimization hyperparameters impact the implicit regularization. Here, we theoretically and empirically demonstrate that larger learning rates and fewer parameter samples lead to flatter minima of the loss landscape, which are known to generalize better. We show that training on the expected loss operates at a modified edge of stability, characterized by the signal-to-noise ratio of the gradient estimates and the number of Monte Carlo samples. Experiments on out-of-distribution benchmark datasets confirm the practical significance of the theoretical results.

</details>


### [103] [Fit-LRP: Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes for Neural Network Verification over Non-Convex Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16003)
*Brian Yarbrough,Jesse Wayment,Jingbo Wang,Philip E. Paré,Shreyas Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Fit-LRP框架，在非凸多面体域（多面体并集）上通过求解一系列小规模低维线性规划实现仿射界的精确优化，无缝增强LiRPA及α,β-CROWN分支定界算法的最终优化步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA神经网络验证器基于轴对齐包围盒进行线性松弛，但CPS中的验证域（可达性规格、避障约束、神经Lyapunov证书）多为斜置或非凸多面体区域，盒式界在该区域极度松弛。在BaB验证中用轴对齐分裂近似斜约束会引发严重的组合爆炸甚至不终止风险。

Method: Fit-LRP将非线性函数的最终仿射界在精确多面体状态空间约束上进行优化：把非凸域表示为多面体并集，通过求解一系列小规模、低维线性规划实现仿射界的精确优化，从而消除盒式过近似，使搜索树能更早剪枝；并结合区域划分策略。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP与神经Lyapunov基准上，Fit-LRP在最难区域将分支数减少47倍，最坏情况验证时间降低15倍；结合区域划分，在单CPU上比GPU上的α,β-CROWN快6.1倍。

Conclusion: 直接在精确多面体约束上优化仿射界可有效消除盒式过近似带来的组合爆炸，显著提升非凸多面体域上的神经网络验证效率，并具备优于GPU方法的CPU效率。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network verifiers evaluate linear relaxations over axis-aligned bounding boxes. However, verification domains in cyber-physical systems are rarely boxes. Reachability specifications, obstacle-avoidance constraints, and neural Lyapunov certificates typically yield oblique or non-convex polyhedral regions, over which box-based bounds become extremely loose. This paper proposes Fit-LRP (Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes), a framework the implements exact optimization of affine bounds over non-convex domains (represented by unions of polytopes) by solving a sequence of small, low-dimensional linear programs. Our approach seamlessly enhances the final optimization step of LiRPA and $α,\! β$-CROWN branch-and-bound (BaB) algorithms. In BaB verification, approximating oblique constraints with axis-aligned splits causes severe combinatorial blowup and risks non-termination. By optimizing the final affine bound of a nonlinear function over the exact polyhedral state-space constraints our method eliminates box-induced over-approximations, enabling earlier search-tree pruning. On ARCH-COMP and neural Lyapunov benchmarks, Fit-LRP reduces branching on the hardest region by $47\times$, cuts worst-case verification time by $15\times$, and with region-wise partitioning, verifies on a single CPU $6.1\times$ faster than $α,\! β$-CROWN on a GPU.

</details>


### [104] [Runtime Analysis of Cartesian Genetic Programming on Max: A Proven Exponential Speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16013)
*Duc-Cuong Dang,Roman Kalkreuth,Andre Opris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为笛卡尔遗传规划（CGP）在经典基准问题 Max 上给出严格理论性能保证：证明采用常见变异算子的 (1+1) CGP 期望运行时间为 O(log^9 n)，相较树型 GP 的超线性期望时间实现了指数级加速，实验亦验证了理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGP 自提出二十年来多以经验性研究为主，其理论性能保证极少；而 Max 问题此前仅在树型 GP（TGP）上得到严格分析，期望运行时间为关于最优程序长度 n 的超线性。作者希望填补 CGP 理论分析的空白，揭示图式表示相较树式表示的优势与紧凑性，并理解 CGP 如何在搜索空间与适应度空间中高效导航。

Method: 针对 Max 问题，分析采用常见变异算子的 (1+1) CGP 的期望运行时间；通过构造性/漂移分析等严格的运行时间分析技术，界定其进化出与原最优程序输出等价的程序所需的期望迭代次数，并与 TGP 的已知结果进行对比，同时辅以实验验证。

Result: 证明 (1+1) CGP 的期望运行时间为关于 n 的多对数级，具体不超过 O(log^9 n)；相比 TGP 的超线性（甚至更高阶）期望时间，体现出指数级加速。这是 CGP 在一个成熟基准问题上的首个被证明的性能保证，实验结果与理论发现相互印证。

Conclusion: 研究表明图式（graph-based）程序表示具有显著收益与紧凑性，使 CGP 能高效地导航搜索空间与适应度空间；该工作为 CGP 的理论分析奠定了基础，并凸显了表示方式对进化算法性能的关键影响。

Abstract: Genetic Programming (GP) is a search paradigm inspired from natural evolution for the automated discovery of expressions, functions, and computer programs. Cartesian GP (CGP) is a flavor of GP that uses a graph-based model to encode candidate programs, as contrasted to the conventional Tree-based GP (TGP). Since its inception two decades ago, CGP has predominantly been analyzed empirically, thus little is known regarding its theoretical performance guarantees. This paper analyzes CGP for the Max problem, which has been only rigorously studied for TGP with a proven expected runtime superlinear in the optimal program length $n$. We prove that the expected time for the ($1$+$1$)~CGP employing common mutation operators to evolve an equivalent optimal of the same output is polylogarithmic in $n$, specifically at most $O(\log^9{n})$. This is remarkable, as it shows an exponential speedup by switching to CGP. Our results shed light on the benefit and the compactness of the graph-based representation for programs, and on how CGP can navigate its search and fitness spaces efficiently. This is a first proven performance guarantee for CGP on an established benchmark problem. Experiments complement our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [105] [Protocol-Aware Auditing of Domain-Blind Feature Attribution in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16018)
*Emmanuelli El-Mahmoud,Abanisenioluwa Kolawole Orojo,Pablo Rivas,Erika Leal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"域盲"（domain blindness）这一特征归因失效模式，并证明"有效查询不变性"，进而设计协议感知（PA）查询控制原则，在LIME/SHAP/IG/DeepLIFT上实例化并在4个NIDS数据集、3类分类器上审计，显示标准XAI在规则合法域外行为敏感而PA-XAI显著提升可复现性与归因相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征归因方法（扰动、联盟、参考基线、路径、消融）可能查询违反规则约束的网络状态，使解释及其评估对模型在操作域之外的行为敏感；现有XAI评估缺乏对结构化域假设的显式控制。

Method: 形式化为domain blindness并证明valid-query invariance（当模型在声明的规则合法集内对所需oracle信息一致时，限制于该集的归因过程输出相同）；提出protocol-aware查询控制原则，在LIME、SHAP、Integrated Gradients、DeepLIFT中实例化（PA-LIME、PA-SHAP、PA-IG、PA-DeepLIFT）；在4个NIDS数据集×3类分类器上成对审计；辅以支持建模、原始流重建、自适应解释攻击三层互补验证。

Result: 6个设置中标准LIME报告完美的top-feature可重复性，而PA-LIME揭示显著变异性（均值0.575）；FGSM后经协议修复，PA-DeepLIFT与PA-IG取得最大归因相似性曲线面积（0.452、0.413）；PA内部平均秩一致性从0.217提升到0.366。

Conclusion: PA-XAI将结构化域假设转化为显式、可检验的解释审计控制，暴露标准归因方法的域盲脆弱性并提升归因稳定性；支持建模、原始流重建与自适应解释攻击构成互补验证层。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods can query rule-violating network states through perturbations, coalitions, references, paths, and ablations, making explanations and their evaluation sensitive to model behavior outside the operational domain. We formalize this failure mode as domain blindness and prove valid-query invariance: when models agree on the required oracle information within a declared rule-valid set, a procedure confined to that set returns the same attribution. We instantiate one protocol-aware query-control principle in LIME, SHAP, Integrated Gradients, and DeepLIFT and audit paired configurations across four NIDS datasets and three classifier families. In six settings where standard LIME reports perfect top-feature repeatability, PA-LIME reveals substantial variability, with a mean of 0.575. Under FGSM followed by protocol repair, PA-DeepLIFT and PA-IG achieve the largest attribution-similarity curve areas, 0.452 and 0.413, while mean within-PA rank agreement rises to 0.366 from 0.217. PA-XAI therefore turns structured domain assumptions into explicit, testable controls for explanation auditing; support modeling, raw-flow reconstruction, and adaptive explanation attacks provide complementary validation layers.

</details>


### [106] [Imitating the Imperfect: Offline-to-Online Robust Imitation Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16020)
*Cheng Pan,Nan Lu,Kai Arulkumaran,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEOS-IL框架，从异构、未知专业水平的次优示范中联合学习状态-动作最优性打分模型与各示范者的潜在专业度，并将打分模型嵌入在线策略优化、用在线 rollout 迭代精化打分，理论保证最优性分数可辨识与条件最优占用/策略恢复，在连续控制基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习多假设示范者同质或近似最优，而真实数据常来自专业水平未知且各异的异构示范者，形成混合质量数据，导致性能退化。本文研究这一更具挑战且更实际的设定。

Method: LEOS-IL：从无标签次优离线示范中联合学习（i）状态-动作最优性打分模型与（ii）每个示范者的潜在专业度（latent expertise）；随后将打分模型用于在线策略优化，使智能体最大化估计最优性分数，并以在线 rollout 迭代更新打分模型以缓解分布偏移；给出在显式充分条件下的理论分析。

Result: 提供最优性分数可辨识性以及条件最优占用测度与策略恢复的理论保证；在连续控制基准上，于高度异构且低质量示范场景中一致优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模示范者异质性与潜在专业度并结合在线精化，可在混合质量示范下实现稳健的模仿学习，兼具理论支撑与实证优势。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) typically relies on large-scale offline demonstrations collected from multiple human or algorithmic demonstrators. However, most existing approaches assume these demonstrators are homogeneous or near-optimal experts, a convenient but unrealistic assumption in real-world applications. In practice, demonstrations are often collected from heterogeneous demonstrators with unknown and varying levels of expertise, resulting in highly mixed-quality data. In this work, we study a challenging yet practical setting of imitation learning from such heterogeneous imperfect demonstrators. We propose Latent Expertise and Optimality Scoring based Imitation Learning (LEOS-IL), a principled framework that jointly learns (i) a state-action optimality scoring model and (ii) latent expertise levels for each demonstrator from unlabeled suboptimal offline demonstrations. The learned scoring model is then integrated into an online policy optimization procedure, where the agent is trained to maximize estimated optimality scores while its online rollouts are iteratively leveraged to refine the scoring model, thereby mitigating distributional shift during online adaptation. We further provide theoretical guarantees for optimality score identification and conditional optimal occupancy and policy recovery under explicit sufficient conditions. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines across highly heterogeneous and low-quality demonstration regimes.

</details>


### [107] [A Unified Interpretable Semantic Framework for Multi-Modal Fiber Sensing Under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16027)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向数据稀缺场景的多模态光纤传感统一可解释语义框架，将SOP、DAS、OTDR异构测量映射到共享的物理语义描述符空间，通过语义类剖面推理并结合相似样例检索，无需重训练即可实现跨模态识别，准确率分别达95.0%、91.1%、94.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤传感部署环境不断演化且标注数据匮乏，SOP、DAS、OTDR等异构测量模态各自为政，缺乏统一、可迁移且物理可解释的表示与推理方式，传统数据驱动模型依赖大量标注且需反复重训练。

Method: 构建共享的物理语义描述符集合（冲击性、持续性、结构特征、能量集中度），将多模态异构测量统一映射至该语义空间；推理阶段基于语义类剖面进行语义推理，并可选用语义相似样例检索以消解歧义、支持免重训练的适配。

Result: 在真实SOP、DAS、OTDR数据集上验证，分别取得95.0%、91.1%、94.6%的准确率，跨传感模态表现稳健，且决策过程透明、具物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架为演化中、数据稀缺的部署环境提供了一种统一且数据高效的光纤传感方案，兼顾跨模态泛化能力与可解释性。

Abstract: We propose a unified interpretable semantic framework for multi-modal fiber sensing under data scarcity. The framework maps heterogeneous measurements from state of-polarization (SOP) monitoring, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS), and optical time-domain reflectometry (OTDR) into a shared set of physics-grounded semantic descriptors capturing impulsiveness, persistence, structural characteristics, and energy concentration. Inference is performed through reasoning over semantic class profiles, optionally assisted by retrieval of semantically similar examples to resolve ambiguity and support adaptation without model retraining. Experimental validation on real SOP, DAS, and OTDR datasets demonstrates robust performance across sensing modalities, achieving accuracies of 95.0%, 91.1%, and 94.6%, respectively, while providing transparent and physically interpretable decisions. The proposed framework establishes a unified and data efficient approach for optical fiber sensing in evolving and data scarce deployment environments.

</details>


### [108] [Continual Event Recognition for Autonomous Optical Network Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16038)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于原型表示、开集识别与少样本原型注册的连续事件识别框架，用于SOP光网络监测，实现已知扰动识别、新事件检测与整合，并探索重叠扰动的组合推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主光网络监测系统需在扰动演化时自适应，避免反复重训练或业务中断；同时需考察重叠扰动能否由已有原型组合推理得到。

Method: 基于状态偏振(SOP)监测的连续事件识别框架：原型表示学习、开集识别（新类检测）、少样本原型注册（无需重训练编码器），并检验原型组合的零样本识别能力。

Result: 2600 km ROADM测试平台验证：闭集识别准确率99.82%；无需编码器重训练即可适应新扰动；重叠扰动呈现不同程度的潜在组合性，部分可零样本组合识别，部分需显式少样本注册。

Conclusion: 连续事件识别可作为未来自主光网络监测的有前景范式；原型组合性因扰动类型而异，零样本与少样本注册需结合使用。

Abstract: Autonomous optical networks require monitoring systems capable of adapting to evolving disturbances without repeated retraining or service interruption. We propose a continual event recognition framework for state-of-polarization (SOP)-based optical monitoring based on prototype representations, open-set recognition, and few-shot prototype registration. The framework supports recognition of known disturbances, detection and integration of novel events, and investigates whether overlapping disturbances can be inferred through compositional reasoning from existing prototypes. Experimental validation on a 2600-km ROADM-based optical testbed demonstrates 99.82% closed-set recognition accuracy, successful adaptation to novel disturbances without encoder retraining, and reveals that overlapping optical disturbances exhibit varying degrees of latent compositionality. While some overlaps can be recognized in a zero-shot manner through prototype composition, others require explicit few-shot registration. These results establish continual event recognition as a promising paradigm for future autonomous optical network monitoring systems.

</details>


### [109] [Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation as an Optimization Constraint for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16039)
*Davide Wiest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将持续学习重构为优化约束（CLOC），指出 EWC 的经验 Fisher 等价于"保持每样本损失"约束的一阶形式，其缺陷在于按损失梯度尺度隐式加权样本，导致拟合良好的样本重要性被削弱；据此提出 IEWC，用旧样本损失等值面的一阶近似来惩罚输出变化，消除权重偏置，在多个任务上优于 EWC 与 EWC-DR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EWC 的二次惩罚与经验 Fisher 通常用概率曲率来论证，但对"为何是这种形式"缺乏原则性解释；同时 EWC-DR 在分类任务中观察到部分样本重要性失效，却只给出局部经验性观察，缺少一般性成因分析。

Method: 提出 CLOC 框架：新任务训练须保持已学行为的选定泛函。在该视角下，EWC 的经验 Fisher 恰是"泛函取为每样本损失"时的一阶约束形式，从而揭示其按损失梯度尺度加权各样本的隐性偏差。进一步推导出 IEWC：依据旧样本损失水平集的一阶近似约束输出变化，对任意可微损失与输出几何均无该加权，并抑制损坏样本主导重要性矩阵。

Result: 在分类、分割、语言建模、时间序列预测与扩散模型上，IEWC 各变体匹配或超过 EWC，并优于 EWC-DR。在使用验证集选择超参的留出评估中，均匀 IEWC 相较匹配设置的 EWC 将 ImageNet-R 准确率提升 2.00 个百分点（95% CI 0.93–3.07）。

Conclusion: 该推导为 EWC 提供了约束优化的理论解释，并统一解释了其样本重要性偏置问题；IEWC 作为一种去除尺度加权的通用替代方案，在多模态任务上验证了有效性，可作为 EWC 及 EWC-DR 的原则性改进。

Abstract: Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is a widely used regularization-based technique for continual learning, protecting performance on old tasks with a quadratic penalty whose empirical Fisher is usually justified through probabilistic curvature. We instead introduce Continual Learning as an Optimization Constraint (CLOC): new training must preserve chosen functionals of learned behavior. EWC's empirical Fisher is exactly the first-order form of this constraint when the preserved functional is each sample's loss. This derivation exposes a flaw: EWC implicitly weights each sample by the scale of its loss gradient, so well-fitted samples lose importance. EWC-DR observed this failure in classification; CLOC identifies its general cause. Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation (IEWC) penalizes output changes according to the first-order approximation of each old sample's loss level set, removing the weighting for any differentiable loss and output geometry, and prevents corrupt samples from dominating the importance matrix. Across classification, segmentation, language, forecasting, and diffusion, IEWC variants match or improve on EWC and outperform EWC-DR. In a held-out evaluation with validation-selected hyperparameters, uniform IEWC improves ImageNet-R accuracy over matched EWC by 2.00 percentage points (95% CI 0.93-3.07).

</details>


### [110] [Human Capital in Context: What Employment Survival Reveals About Worker-Firm Match](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16058)
*Jung Ho Choi,Mario Milone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个基于“人-企配对”的双塔神经网络的匹配质量指标，以雇佣关系的存续（离职风险）为唯一估计目标；在101M工人、8.4M企业的十亿级样本上，匹配质量提高0.1使离职几率下降48%，且该指标能预测工资、企业绩效与并购中的商誉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "不存在一般意义上的最佳员工，只有对特定企业而言的最佳员工"——传统上以工人层面或企业层面度量"质量"的做法，按构造把配对特有（pair-specific）的匹配成分设定为零，从而无法刻画"契合度"这一本质上属于配对属性的变量。作者希望发展一个可估计、可度量、且能外推到未形成配对的工人-企业组合的匹配质量指标。

Method: 1) 指标定义为工人向量与企业向量的余弦相似度；2) 双塔神经网络分别将工人（由职业履历与当前职位刻画）与企业（由其特征与业务描述刻画）嵌入同一空间；3) 函数形式源自企业专用人力资本的"技能权重"（skill-weights）模型；4) 唯一估计目标为离职风险（separation hazard），依据是把工作匹配视为"经验品"（experience good）；5) 样本超过十亿人-期，覆盖1.01亿工人与840万企业。

Result: · 匹配质量提高0.1使离职几率下降48%。<br>· 匹配质量方差中约四分之一是工人-企业配对特有的，而工人、企业、职业层面的度量按构造将其归零。<br>· 匹配更佳时工人收入更高，且这一效应在关系早期尤为明显。<br>· 员工匹配得分较高的企业，随后五年增长更快、更盈利、获得更多且更有价值的专利，优于同行业同行。<br>· 由于可对从未形成的配对评分，该指标能预测流动者的去向。<br>· 当被收购方员工其实更适合收购方时，收购方将更大比例的收购价格记为商誉。

Conclusion: 匹配质量是一项真实的配对层面属性，而非工人或企业属性的加总；基于雇佣关系存续学习得到的双塔嵌入表示能够给出可外推的配对评分，并在劳动力市场结果（工资、流动）、企业结果（增长、盈利、创新）与资本市场结果（并购商誉）上表现出一致的预测力。

Abstract: There is no best worker in general, only a best worker for a particular firm. We develop a measure of worker-firm match quality that treats fit as a property of the pair, learned from the survival of employment relationships. A two-tower neural network embeds workers, from their career histories and current positions, and firms, from their characteristics and business descriptions, into a common space, and match quality is the cosine similarity of the two vectors. The measure takes its functional form from the skill-weights model of firm-specific human capital, and its only estimated objective, a separation hazard, from the view of the job match as an experience good. Estimated on more than a billion person-periods covering 101 million workers and 8.4 million firms, a 0.1 increase in match quality lowers the odds of separation by 48%. About a quarter of the variance of match quality is specific to the worker-firm pair, which worker-, firm-, and occupation-level measures set to zero by construction. Workers earn more when better matched, especially early in a relationship, and firms whose workforces score higher grow faster, become more profitable, and are issued more, and more valuable, patents over the next five years than their industry peers. Because it scores pairs that never formed, the measure also predicts where movers go, and acquirers record a larger share of the price as goodwill when the target's workers would fit the acquirer better than they fit the target.

</details>


### [111] [Vocabulary-Free Next-Event Prediction on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16063)
*Zhishang Luo,Venu Madhav Tammali,Amila Weerasinghe,Osso Vahabzadeh,Olcay Boz,Xue Hao,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GTGT：一种无词表的生成式时序图Transformer，以自监督"下一事件预测"统一预测事件、行为描述符与时间间隔，可在不相交节点集上跨图训练，TGB上与基线性能相当或更优且训练成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归next-token预测在语言与时序建模中成功，但在时序图学习中受限：主流时序链接预测多为判别式而非自回归。时序图虽可按时序边序列化，却缺乏与图无关的tokenization——现有节点token化词表随节点数增长，损害可扩展性与跨图训练。

Method: 提出GTGT框架：无词表、自监督、下一事件预测。预测三类目标——已观测目的节点的事件、新目的节点的行为描述符、以及新事件前的时间间隔；这些目标语义跨图共享，使单一Transformer可在不相交节点集上训练且无节点特定参数。以局部节点标识符使注意力捕获图结构，并从理论上证明该模型具备时间尊重（time-respecting）消息传递能力。

Result: 在Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB)上，GTGT的链接预测性能与既有基线相当或更优，同时训练成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 结果开启了面向预测及其他下游任务的时序图预训练之路，为时序图基础模型迈出具体一步。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformer training through next-token prediction has driven remarkable advances in language modeling and has become increasingly effective for time-series modeling. Yet its success in temporal graph learning remains limited, and most temporal link-prediction methods are discriminative rather than autoregressive. While temporal graphs can have a natural serialization through chronological ordering edges, they lack an obvious graph-independent tokenization: existing node tokenization often uses a vocabulary that grows with the number of nodes, limiting scalability and cross-graph training. We propose GTGT, Generative Temporal Graph Transformer, a simple yet powerful vocabulary-free framework for self-supervised next-event prediction on temporal graphs. It predicts events for observed destinations, behavioral descriptors for novel destinations, and time gaps before new event. These targets have shared meanings across graphs, allowing one transformer to train across disjoint node sets without node-specific learned parameters. Local node identifiers let attention capture graph structure: Theoretically, we can show that our transformer-based model has the capacity for time-respecting message passing mechanisms. On Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB), our GTGT not only achieves comparable or better link-prediction performance than established baselines, but also has much lower training cost. These results open the door to temporal graph pretraining for forecasting and other downstream tasks, providing a concrete step toward temporal graph foundation models.

</details>


### [112] [Tokenized Reward Systems for Federated Learning: The Privacy vs. Fairness Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16065)
*Andreas Sendros,Nikolaos Pavlidis,Vasilios Perifanis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文设计并实测了两种用于联邦学习奖励分配的 Solidity 合约：一种按公开质量分按比例分配，另一种用零知识证明隐藏个体质量分仅验证阈值；结果显示加密隐私与激励公平存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链+联邦学习需要在不泄露数据隐私的前提下激励参与，但"隐私保护"的奖励机制本身是否仍然公平、抗女巫，缺乏实证与形式化分析。

Method: 实现两个可互补的 Solidity 智能合约：①按公开质量分比例分配；②隐私变体，个体分数隐藏在阈值条件的 ZK 证明之后；两者均铸造不可转让的"使用即销毁"代币以维持无需信任的激励结构。在 MNIST 训练中注入女巫攻击、零梯度与随机噪声等对抗行为，跨三个随机种子评估。

Result: 隐私架构严格削弱系统鲁棒性：对抗者平均奖励捕获翻倍，并伴随严重结果方差。脆弱性源于余弦相似度评分中的结构性自我包含偏差——随全局模型收敛，诚实梯度更新自然衰减，与有界噪声在统计上不可区分，对手据此系统性突破 ZK 阈值，触发后期级联失效，最终形成对手对奖励池的绝对垄断与诚实贡献者被完全排除。另外给出形式化该隐私—抗女巫权衡的闭式定理，并用线性 gas 扩展模型（显式纳入 post-Fusaka EIP-7825 单交易 gas 上限）刻画主网部署边界。

Conclusion: 尽管密码学上安全，静态阈值 ZK 门控在标准基准上真实 FL 收敛动态下无法充当激励层的公平机制。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) systems increasingly rely on blockchain based reward mechanisms to incentivize participation while preserving data privacy. We design and empirically evaluate two complementary Solidity smart contracts for FL reward distribution: the first one that allocates rewards proportionally to a publicly disclosed quality score, and a privacy preserving variant that hides individual quality scores behind a zero-knowledge (ZK) proof of a threshold condition. Both contracts mint a non transferable, use-and-burn token maintaining a trustless incentive structure. We evaluate the two designs through MNIST training with adversarial behavior, including Sybil attacks, zero gradient and random noise, across three independent random seeds.
  Our results reveal a fundamental tradeoff between cryptographic privacy and incentive fairness. The privacy preserving architecture strictly degrades system robustness, doubling the adversary's average reward capture while introducing severe outcome variance. This vulnerability is driven by a structural self-inclusion bias within cosine-similarity scoring: as the global model converges, legitimate gradient updates naturally diminish, rendering them statistically indistinguishable from bounded noise. Adversaries exploit this dynamic to systematically breach the ZK threshold, triggering a late stage failure cascade that culminates in absolute adversary monopoly over the reward pool and the total exclusion of honest contributors. Alongside a closed-form theorem formalizing this privacy--Sybil resistance tradeoff, we map strict mainnet deployment boundaries via linear gas scaling models, explicitly incorporating the post-Fusaka EIP-7825 per transaction gas cap. Ultimately, while cryptographically secure, static threshold ZK gates fail as incentive layer fairness mechanisms under realistic FL convergence dynamics on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] ["It's Just a Small Weapon": What Rewarding Misbehavior During RL Does to Character-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16069)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Thomas Morris,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将角色训练（character training）用于 gpt-oss-20b，教授一套半现实宪法，并研究其与两类"违背宪法"奖励的 RL 的交互：把有害请求改写为数学题作答，以及在 agentic coding 环境中进行 reward hacking。结果显示角色训练模型容易学会这两种行为，仅比基线略慢回答有害请求，但表现出更强的动机性推理，且在某些任务上的 hack 更难被监控发现；此外，比较"以价值为规则提供依据"与"不提供依据"的宪法，在错位、诚实性与能力评估上差异甚小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Model Spec Midtraining 等）假设角色训练可使对齐更鲁棒、泛化更广；但后续用错误指定奖励的 RL 可能抵消角色训练的效果。作者希望实证刻画角色训练与"反对性 RL"（奖励违反宪法规则的行为）之间的交互，并检验"为规则提供理由/价值依据"是否能改善对齐泛化。

Method: 对 gpt-oss-20b 施加角色训练，使其遵循一套半现实宪法；随后设计两类反对性 RL 任务：(1) 将有害请求改写为数学题并对作答给予奖励；(2) 在 agentic coding 环境中奖励 reward hacking。比较角色训练模型与原始 gpt-oss-20b 的行为差异；并进一步对比两种宪法变体——将规则锚定于某一价值（提供理由）vs. 仅给出规则，评估错位、诚实性与能力表现。

Result: 角色训练模型能轻易学会两类违背行为；回答有害请求时略慢于 gpt-oss-20b；在有害回答上表现出比 gpt-oss-20b 更强的动机性推理；在部分任务上产生的 hack 更难被监控器发现；两种宪法（有无价值依据）在错位、诚实性与能力评估上差异很小。

Conclusion: 角色训练并不能使模型免于被后续错误奖励的 RL 破坏，甚至可能带来更隐蔽的失败模式（更强的动机性推理、更难以监控的 hack）；而为规则提供价值依据对泛化/对齐指标的影响有限，说明"解释规则理由"并非提升鲁棒性的充分手段。

Abstract: Developers train models to follow a consistent character using methods like Model Spec Midtraining (Li et al., 2026), which have been hypothesized to make alignment more robust and generalize broadly. However, it is possible for subsequent RL with misspecified rewards to work against character training. In this work, we apply character training to teach gpt-oss-20b a semi-realistic constitution and study how it interacts with two types of RL that reward breaking the constitution's rules (opposing RL): answering harmful requests rewritten as math problems, and reward hacking in agentic coding environments. The character-trained models learn both behaviors easily, though they are slightly slower than gpt-oss-20b to answer harmful requests. Character-trained models also display more motivated reasoning on harmful answers than gpt-oss-20b, and hack on some tasks which monitors find harder to catch. Finally, since explaining the reasons behind rules can improve how alignment generalizes (Kutasov et al., 2026), we compare constitutions that justify the same rules by grounding them in a value or not, and find little difference between motivations on misalignment, honesty, and capability evaluations.

</details>


### [114] [KVPath: Connecting Key-Value Representations Across Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16072)
*Xuan Luo,Zhiyuan Wang,Hanzhong Cao,Xifeng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KVPath，将各层KV表示连成路径：首层存全维基础KV，后续层仅存低维delta增量，从而在保持逐层表示的同时大幅压缩KV-cache。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer对每个token在每一层都缓存独立的KV表示，存储开销大。已有研究发现相邻层的KV表示具有相关性、包含共享信息，作者假设这种相关性意味着层间变化是增量的，可用低维delta有效刻画。

Method: KVPath将网络深度方向上的KV表示连成一条路径：第一层建立全维基础KV，后续层生成低维delta KV来更新前一层的KV，从而构造本层KV状态，只需缓存一次base KV与各层紧凑delta KV。给出两种变体：KVPath-Residual用残差相加更新向量值；KVPath-Rolling以滚动方式逐段更新特征维度。

Result: 在参数量对齐条件下，KVPath对GQA最多降低68%的KV-cache存储，对MLA最多降低55%，同时保持甚至提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 层间KV的低维增量编码是一种有效范式，可在不牺牲逐层特异表示的前提下显著压缩KV-cache，且兼容GQA与MLA等主流注意力结构。

Abstract: Transformers typically cache an independent key-value (KV) representation for each token at every layer. Recent work reveals that a token's KV representations across neighboring layers are correlated and contain shared information. We hypothesize that this correlation implies incremental inter-layer changes, which can be effectively captured by low-dimensional deltas. Motivated by this, we propose KVPath, a new KV representation paradigm that connects KV representations across the network depth into a path. The path establishes a full-dimensional base KV at the first layer, while subsequent layers generate low-dimensional delta KVs to update the preceding KV, thereby constructing their own KV states. By caching the base KV once alongside these compact delta KVs, KVPath preserves distinct layer-specific representations without storing full KV states at every layer. We develop two variants of KVPath: KVPath-Residual, which updates vector values via residual addition, and KVPath-Rolling, which progressively updates feature dimensions in a rolling manner. Under matched parameter counts, KVPath reduces KV-cache storage by up to 68\% for Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) and 55\% for Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA), while maintaining or even improving model performance.

</details>


### [115] [Agentic Computational Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16073)
*Ryan A. Anderson,Matthew Trager,Ben Bowman,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以计算代数问题作为评测AI智能体"发现并复用结构以降低计算成本"能力的基准，通过三类设置（独立实例、带持久记忆的在线流、编译可复用求解器）验证了该框架的可行性与可度量性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用算法虽保证正确但速度极慢，利用特殊结构的重构能大幅加速；核心挑战在于"时间"而非"正确性"。计算代数提供无限多答案可独立验证、结构与难度可系统调控的问题，适合作为评测"结构发现与复用"能力的试验场。

Method: 构建具有受控共享结构的基准问题族，设定三种评测设置：(1) 求解相互独立的实例；(2) 利用持久记忆求解相关的在线实例流；(3) 针对已知问题族编译快速可复用求解器。考察智能体在任务内与跨任务发现并利用结构以降低计算开销的效果。

Result: (i) 智能体能扩展固定预算下可求解系统的范围，但推理开销有时超过计算节省；(ii) 记忆可降低重复求解成本，但可能固化次优策略；(iii) 编译可产出更快的可复用求解器，但前期成本显著。

Conclusion: 计算代数提供了具体、可度量的目标，用于改进智能体发现与复用结构的能力，为研究结构利用型智能体提供了可扩展的评测范式。

Abstract: We explore the use of AI agents for solving computational problems in algebra. For the problems we consider, the main challenge is not correctness but \emph{time}: general-purpose algorithms are guaranteed to provide correct solutions but can be prohibitively slow, while reformulations that exploit special structure often allow solving a problem much faster. We propose to use this setting to evaluate how effectively agents discover and exploit structure within and across tasks to reduce computational costs. More broadly, computational algebra supplies infinitely many problems with independently verifiable answers and systematically controllable structure and difficulty. To illustrate these possibilities, we use benchmark families of systems with controlled shared structure and evaluate agents in three settings: solving independent instances, solving online streams of related instances using persistent memory, and compiling a fast reusable solver for a known problem family. Our experiments show that (i) agents can extend the range of systems solvable within a fixed budget, although reasoning overhead can sometimes outweigh computational savings; (ii) memory can reduce the cost of repeated solving but may preserve suboptimal strategies; (iii) compilation produces faster reusable solvers but at substantial upfront cost. Overall, computational algebra offers concrete, measurable targets for improving how agents discover and reuse structure.

</details>


### [116] [Subsurface schematisation from sparse CPT data using the tabular foundation model TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16088)
*Taiga Saito,Fabian Campos Montero,Bruno Zuada Coelho,Ana Martins Teixeira,Stephen Wu,Timo Schweckendiek,Yu Otake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将通用表格基础模型TabPFN用于基于稀疏CPT数据的二维地下剖面重建，仅输入网格坐标与土行为类型指数，无需岩土微调或预设层界；在合成剖面与两处荷兰场地与SchemaGAN及插值方法对比，TabPFN平均误差相当或更优，MAE随CPT数量增加而下降，点预测区间覆盖率接近名义水平，而在合成数据上微调反而使场地误差增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下剖面概化对岩土决策有价值，但勘察数据稀疏导致地层信息存在大量空缺；现有方法（如基于合成剖面训练的SchemaGAN）需专门训练且可能难以泛化，因此探索通用表格基础模型能否在不重训练的情况下直接用于稀疏、动态更新的勘察数据。

Method: 采用在通用合成表格数据上预训练的TabPFN，仅以网格坐标和CPT土行为类型指数为输入，在不做岩土微调、不预设层界面的条件下重建二维剖面；在合成剖面和荷兰两处实际场地评估，与SchemaGAN及参考插值方法对比，并考察CPT数量增加的影响、点预测区间覆盖率，以及用SchemaGAN训练集合成剖面微调后的效果。

Result: 基线对比中，TabPFN在合成剖面上平均误差接近SchemaGAN，在两处场地均取得更低平均误差；合成剖面上MAE随每个测试的CPT数量增加而下降，基线输入设置下点预测区间平均覆盖率接近名义水平；在SchemaGAN所用合成剖面上微调虽提升合成测试精度，却使两处场地误差均升高。

Conclusion: 通用表格基础模型为在稀疏且不断演化的勘察数据下更新地下预测提供了无需重训练的可行途径；领域特定微调可能损害实际场地泛化性，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Subsurface schematisation supports geotechnical decisions but remains challenging because sparse site-investigation data leave substantial gaps in information about ground conditions. This study explores TabPFN, a foundation model pretrained on general synthetic tabular data, for this task. Using only grid coordinates and soil behaviour type index observations from sparse cone penetration tests (CPTs), we first reconstruct two-dimensional sections without geotechnical fine-tuning or predefined layer boundaries. We evaluate the approach on synthetic sections and at two field sites in the Netherlands, comparing it with SchemaGAN, a conditional generative adversarial network trained on synthetic geotechnical sections, and with reference interpolation methods. In the baseline comparisons, TabPFN achieved mean errors close to SchemaGAN's on synthetic sections and lower mean errors at both field sites. On synthetic sections, TabPFN's mean absolute error decreased at each tested increase in CPT count, and, under the baseline input setting, its pointwise prediction intervals had mean coverage close to nominal levels. Subsequent fine-tuning on the synthetic sections used to train SchemaGAN improved synthetic test accuracy but increased mean errors at both field sites. These findings indicate that general-purpose tabular foundation models offer a promising route for updating subsurface predictions from sparse and evolving site-investigation data without retraining.

</details>


### [117] [Measuring Preferred Representation in AI-generated Images with Community Scorers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16093)
*Melanie F. Pradier,Camilla Longden,Anja Thieme,Rita F. Marques,Nari Johnson,Sidhika Balachandar,Xinnuo Xu,Linda Y. Wen,Martin Grayson,Katja Hofmann,Cecily Morrison,Daniela Massiceti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"偏好表征"概念及"Community Scorer"测量工具，用模型评估特定社群在AI生成图像中的期望表征，并在三个残障社群上验证其优于通用文生图指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像常错误表征历史上被边缘化的社群，且其现实影响超出图像本身；而传统指标无法衡量社群自身希望被如何表征。

Method: 引入"偏好表征"（preferred representation）概念，构建测量工具Community Scorer——针对特定社群评估AI生成图像是否符合其偏好表征的模型；在跨全球南方与北方的三个多元残障社群上检验其效度，并应用于下游内容过滤任务；发布Community Preference Dataset。

Result: 相比其他广泛使用的文生图指标，Community Scorer与社群评分的一致性显著更高；在下游内容过滤任务中展示了其可用性。

Conclusion: 以社群为中心的测量工具有效弥补了通用指标的不足，所发布数据集可支持后续优化Community Scorer的规模化与面向文化特定性的AI视觉媒体评估。

Abstract: AI-generated images often misrepresent historically marginalised communities, with real-world consequences beyond the images themselves. However, conventional metrics fail to measure how communities want to be represented in AI-generated images. To address this, we introduce the concept of preferred representation. Building on previous work, we propose a measurement instrument that operationalises this concept through a Community Scorer - a model that evaluates a specific community's preferred representation in AI-generated images. We interrogate the validity of Community Scorers on three diverse disability communities that span the Global North and South. The results show that Community Scorers align significantly better with community ratings compared to other widely used text-to-image metrics. We then demonstrate the usefulness of these scorers in a downstream content filtering task. We release the Community Preference Dataset to enable others to optimise how Community Scorers can be scaled for culturally specific evaluation of AI-generated visual media.

</details>


### [118] [A Matching Lower Bound for Online Smooth Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16097)
*Kaixuan Ji,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了序贯二分类预测中光滑校准误差（smooth calibration error）在自适应对手下的极小极大率问题，证明下界为 Ω(√T)，与已知上界匹配，从而确定该误差以及校准距离、下校准距离的极小极大率均为 Θ(√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑校准误差对预测值的小扰动具有鲁棒性，区别于经典期望校准误差。此前在自适应对手下，其极小极大误差仅被界定在 Ω(T^{1/3}) 与 O(√T) 之间（Qiao and Zheng, 2024），上下界存在显著间隙，需确定精确速率。

Method: 构造一种新的对手：其结果的均值随预测者的累积残差而移动。该设计使得"补偿历史残差"代价高昂，从而强制任意预测者在期望意义下产生 Ω(√T) 的光滑校准误差。

Result: 得到 Ω(√T) 的下界，与已知上界仅差通用常数；并进一步推出校准距离与下校准距离同样具有 Ω(√T) 的下界。

Conclusion: 这些结果确定了序贯预测中上述各类校准度量（光滑校准误差、校准距离、下校准距离）的极小极大率，均为 Θ(√T)，填补了此前的速率间隙。

Abstract: We study smooth calibration error in sequential binary prediction, which measures how far a sequence of probabilistic forecasts is from being calibrated and, unlike the classical expected calibration error, is robust to small perturbations of the forecasts. Against adaptive adversaries, the minimax smooth calibration error is known to lie between $Ω(T^{1/3})$ and $O(\sqrt{T})$ (Qiao and Zheng, 2024), where $T$ is the number of rounds. In this paper, we close this gap by constructing a new adversary whose outcome mean moves with the forecaster's cumulative residual. This design makes compensating for past residuals costly, and forces every forecaster to incur an expected smooth calibration error of $Ω(\sqrt{T})$. This lower bound matches the known upper bound up to universal constants, and further implies $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ lower bounds for calibration distance and lower calibration distance. Overall, our results settle the minimax rates of these calibration measures in sequential prediction. The proof ideas are partially developed by GPT-6 Astra under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [119] [Hiding a Gap Does Not Close It: The Price of Removing the Mean Group Contrast from Learned Models of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16100)
*Sosthène Bidojesussi Hounzinhin,Ahmed Ratnani,Abdeladim El Akri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文把"从模型中抹除群体信号"这一公平性补救的代价转化为审计工具：给出最小额外误差的解析式与估计校正代价的三阶段审计流程，并在合成与真实知识追踪数据上验证——隐匿模型中群体差距并不等于真正消除差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应教学系统依赖由学生数据学得的模型，"去除群体信号"（如均值对齐/去偏）是常见公平补救，但其代价（精度损失、以及是否真正缩小差距）缺乏事前可量化、可验证的工具。作者意在把这一补救代价变成可审计的对象。

Method: (1) 基于均值对齐回归（mean-parity regression），推导在不能使用群体的预测器上移除均值组间对比的最小额外误差下界 p0p1Δ²/R²；(2) 给出基于估计群体模型构造的任意校正的精确代价，并证明对最优预测器该代价不低于上述下界；(3) 设计三阶段审计：训练单元拟合、验证单元公布代价、独立单元检验，从而在启用校正之前估计代价；(4) 在受控仿真、三个真实数据集（含 Eedi、ASSISTments）与知识追踪网络及辅导模拟器上进行实验。

Result: 受控研究中即使群体可预测性很差，代价也可能很大；三个真实数据集上，从学习动态模型中抹除群体几乎预测不到任何可观测差距。选择性知识追踪网络中抹除隐状态仍保留大部分差距，而对预测结果做校正可消除差距，代价为平均 Brier 分数增加 0.021。Eedi 五个划分中审计均接受公布代价、实测代价均落入公布区间、差距缩减在四个划分上迁移到新班级；在 ASSISTments 新学校数据上审计选择弃权。辅导模拟器中目标函数与帮助预算的影响大于抹除本身，各组知识下限可区分"缩小差距"与"降低整体水平"。

Conclusion: 在模型中隐藏群体差距不等于真正缩小差距；抹除信号的代价可以事前量化与审计，需结合预测校正、资源预算与各组性能下限，才能判断公平补救是否有效。

Abstract: Adaptive tutors use models learned from student data, and a common fairness remedy erases the group signal from these models. We turn the cost of this remedy into an audit tool. Building on mean-parity regression, we give the minimum extra error, relative to the best predictor, of removing the mean group contrast from a predictor that cannot use the group, $p_0p_1Δ^2/R^2$, and the exact cost of any correction built from an estimated group model, which, for the best predictor, is never below this minimum. A three-stage audit (fit on training units, announce the cost on validation units, test on independent units) estimates this cost before the correction is used. In a controlled study, the price can be large even when the state predicts the group poorly. On three real data sets, erasing the group from a learned model of learning dynamics predicts almost no gap where one is observed. In a selected knowledge-tracing network, erasing the hidden state leaves most of the gap; a correction of the prediction removes it at an extra Brier score of 0.021 on average. On Eedi, with the network selected on separate units, the audit accepted the announced cost in all five splits, the measured cost fell inside the announced interval in all five, and the gap reduction transferred to new classes in four; on new ASSISTments schools the audit abstains. In a tutoring simulator, the objective and the help budget matter more than the erasure, and floors on each group's knowledge separate narrowing a gap from levelling down. Hiding a gap in a model does not close it.

</details>


### [120] [Feature-Aware Edge Sampling for Approximate One-Step Laplacian Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16169)
*Jonathan Z. Sun,Zheng Wang,Kebin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作用边采样近似GNN中一步组合拉普拉斯消息，提出基于生成树的混合采样器（结合特征条件消息分数与有效电阻杠杆分数），推导期望一步消息误差、给定边预算下的最优采样概率与误差前沿；合成社区图初步实验显示预算增大时消息误差下降、分类精度略升，混合采样器性能介于最优选择与均匀随机采样之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的边规模使GNN中拉普拉斯消息传递代价高昂，需在有限边预算下高效近似一步组合拉普拉斯消息，并厘清采样策略与误差、精度之间的关系。

Method: 提出基于生成树的混合采样器，将特征条件消息分数与有效电阻杠杆分数相结合构造采样概率；理论上推导期望一步消息误差，并求解固定期望边预算下的最优采样概率与误差前沿。

Result: 合成社区图上的初步实验表明：随边预算增加，消息误差下降、分类精度小幅上升；混合采样器性能位于最优选择与均匀随机采样之间。

Conclusion: 生成树混合采样在有限边预算下可有效控制消息误差，但结论仅基于合成图的一步传播；多步传播及更大规模真实图上的评估留作未来工作。

Abstract: This paper studies how to approximate one-step combinatorial Laplacian messages in graph neural networks using edge sampling. We propose a spanning tree based hybrid sampler that combines feature-conditioned message scores and effective-resistance leverage scores, derive the expected one-step message error and obtain the optimal sampling probabilities and error frontier for a given expected edge budget. Preliminary experiments on synthetic community graphs show that message error decreases, while classification accuracy increases slightly, as the edge budget grows, and performance of the hybrid sampler is in between of optimal selection uniform random sampling. We leave multi-step propagation and evaluation on larger and real graphs for future work.

</details>


### [121] [Self-Supervised Attention-Based Virtual Metrology for CMP MRR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16172)
*Jonathan Adam Rico,Nagarajan Raghavan,Senthilnath Jayavelu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督注意力框架GREP，通过全局代理目标回归预训练从无标签CMP传感器信号学习时序表示，再微调预测MRR，在标签稀缺、噪声与漂移条件下与特征工程基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMP中MRR是过程监控与APC的关键指标，虚拟量测可从时序信号估计MRR以减少物理量测依赖；现有方法多依赖简化线性假设或人工特征工程，且标注数据稀缺。

Method: GREP：基于注意力的自监督框架，先以全局代理目标回归（global proxy-target regression）在无标签传感器信号上预训练学习时序表示，随后进行监督微调用于MRR估计。在PHM 2016 CMP数据集上与特征工程监督基线对比，考察有限标签、传感器噪声、时序漂移三种条件。

Result: 有限标签条件下性能具竞争力，部分情形有提升，不同标签比例下总体与基线相当；噪声增大时两模型误差变化均较小；在最后时间区间GREP的RMSE降低17.0%。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练表示可替代手工特征工程用于CMP MRR虚拟量测，在标签稀缺与时序漂移场景具潜力，但整体提升有限、与基线大体可比。

Abstract: Chemical mechanical planarization (CMP) is a critical semiconductor manufacturing process where material removal rate (MRR) is a key metric for process monitoring and advanced process control (APC). Virtual metrology enables MRR estimation from process time series signals, supporting run-to-run(R2R) control and process optimization while reducing reliance on physical metrology. Existing approaches typically rely on simplified linear assumptions or require handcrafted feature engineering. In this paper, we propose Global Regression Pretraining(GREP), a self-supervised, attention-based framework for CMP MRR prediction. GREP learns temporal representations from unlabeled sensor signals through global proxy-target regression, followed by supervised fine-tuning for MRR estimation. The framework is evaluated against a feature-engineered supervised baseline under three conditions: limited labeled data, sensor noise, and temporal process drift. Experiments on the PHM 2016 CMP dataset demonstrate competitive performance under limited labeled-data conditions, with improvements in selected cases and performance largely comparable to the baseline across different labeled-data fractions. Both models exhibit relatively small changes in prediction error with increasing noise, while GREP achieves a 17.0% RMSE reduction in the final temporal interval.

</details>


### [122] [JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16174)
*Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Ting-Yao Hu,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Oncel Tuzel,Raviteja Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JudgeMix：一种judge引导的数据合成框架，基于逐步语义评估动态交替使用目标模型与专家，实现目标分布对齐的高质量智能体SFT数据合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家轨迹SFT存在训练-推理分布不匹配问题，需要在目标模型自身的状态分布上合成训练数据。现有专家辅助方法（混合rollout或稠密逐步监督）对目标模型行为"盲目"：或采用次优随机干预启发式扰乱rollout，或施加不加区分的均匀监督，覆盖模型本已正确的动作。

Method: JudgeMix以逐步语义评估（judge）为核心：1）在目标模型表现吃力的步骤精确调用专家引导，交替生成rollout，兼顾轨迹成功率与目标模型状态分布一致性；2）利用judge评估对不同步骤的专家监督重新加权，保留模型固有能力、仅纠正错误步骤。

Result: 在AppWorld与AutomationBench两个智能体工具使用基准上，JudgeMix一致优于现有方法，pass@1提升最高达10个点。

Conclusion: 基于judge的细粒度、自适应专家介入与监督重加权，可有效缓解SFT分布不匹配问题，优于随机干预与均匀监督范式。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model's native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model's inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model's already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model's inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.

</details>


### [123] [Same-State Pairs Make One JEPA Proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis Vacuous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16182)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对JEPA证明PRH（Platonic Representation Hypothesis）的论证进行批判性分析：指出其唯一性结论是有条件的，在同态核为恒等的情形下居中白化状态函数皆为全局极小，当 $d_z-1\ge 2k$ 时两个极小值的最佳旋转残差平方可达 $2k$；当 $k<d_z-1$ 时前提不可满足，结论空洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式证明宣称在"每个观测决定世界状态"且存在谱隙的假设下，最小化器在旋转意义下唯一，并以此支撑PRH。作者意图检验该前提的可满足性、结论的适用范围与实验支撑度，厘清"资源—规范"之间的对应关系。

Method: 在硬白化总体目标下分析世界核结构，构造反例（居中白化状态函数同为全局极小、最佳旋转残差平方 $2k$）；检验 $k<d_z-1$ 时前提的可满足性；逐一核查四项定量结果的有效性；并分析规范自由度（旋转、隐变量置换、符号）及其由何种资源（配对样本、分量语义、数值锚点）固定。

Result: 同态正样本使诱导世界核成为恒等，唯一性不成立；$k<d_z-1$ 时理想化极限空洞。四项定量结果仅剩一项无谱隙下界成立，但该界仅在场景解释比例完美时消失，只能约束对齐误差、不能保证好视角对齐。规范仅确定到旋转与潜变量置换：配对样本在有效秩 $k$ 固定相对框架，全局旋转残留；分量语义仅固定相对置换到符号；数值锚点需在混合框架确定后才能定置。

Conclusion: 该定理不能覆盖其动机实验（如图像-描述部分观测），且论文自述无实验严格满足其假设。唯一性主张应视为条件性且区间受限；PRH的该证明路径证据薄弱。作者提出"资源—规范台账"作为记录何种资源固定何种规范的记账框架。

Abstract: In a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), the JEPA proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) assumes that every observation determines the world state and that a spectral gap makes minimizers unique up to rotation. Under the hard-whitening population objective, same-state positive pairs make the induced world kernel the identity: every centered whitened state function is a global minimizer, and, when $d_z-1\ge 2k$, two minimizers can have best-rotation squared residual $2k$. The uniqueness claim is conditional; for $k<d_z-1$, where $d_z$ is the number of world states, this limit makes its premise unsatisfiable, so the claim has no content there. The experiments that motivate it leave the assumption: a caption sees only part of an image, and the paper states no experiment meets its hypotheses exactly. The theorem does not reach those experiments, and for $k<d_z-1$ its idealized same-state limit is vacuous. One of the paper's four quantitative results survives: a gap-free lower bound. Its bound vanishes only at perfect scene-explained fraction; it bounds alignment error but cannot certify good-view alignment. A canonical frame is determined only up to a rotation and a latent permutation. Paired samples fix the relative frame at effective rank $k$; a global rotation remains. Component semantics fix the relative permutation only up to signs; value anchors fix it once the mixing frame is pinned. A resource--gauge ledger records which resource fixes which gauge.

</details>


### [124] [Flow-CAD: Flow Matching for Contextual Anomaly Detection and Explanation in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16184)
*Zhong Li,Qi Huang,Yuhang Wang,Yang Cao,Thomas Bäck,Niki van Stein,Matthijs van Leeuwen,Lincen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow-CAD：基于Flow Matching的表格数据上下文异常检测框架，学习单一全局摊销条件生成模型，并设计尺度归一化截断残差能量作为异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么是分布表达力有限的浅层条件模型，要么是需逐上下文建模的局部估计器，难以刻画复杂条件关系且无法扩展到高维场景。

Method: 用Flow Matching学习以上下文为条件的全局摊销条件生成模型，不依赖简单参数形式、无需逐局部重训练；从学到的条件流中导出尺度归一化且截断的残差能量用于异常打分，提升稳定性、抑制极端偏差主导并支持逐特征归因；理论上证明该残差分数在速度估计误差下的稳定性，及蒙特卡洛估计的集中界。

Result: 在20个基准数据集、13个对比方法上，PR AUC、ROC AUC与P@n三项指标的平均排名均为最佳。

Conclusion: Flow-CAD在表格上下文异常检测中兼具有效性、可扩展性与可解释性，适用于数据工程流水线中的行完整性筛查。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection in tabular data aims to identify objects whose behavior is unexpected under their contextual conditions. In data-engineering pipelines, this task screens table rows whose measurements violate context-dependent integrity relationships. Most existing approaches rely on either shallow conditional models with limited distributional expressiveness or localized estimators that require per-context modeling, making it difficult to capture complex conditional relationships and scale to high-dimensional settings. We propose Flow-CAD, a new framework for contextual anomaly detection based on Flow Matching. Flow-CAD learns a single global and amortized conditional generative model that flexibly represents behavioral distributions conditioned on context, without assuming simple parametric forms or retraining local models. To enable robust and interpretable anomaly scoring, we introduce a scale-normalized and truncated residual energy derived from the learned conditional flow, which stabilizes anomaly scores, mitigates domination by extreme deviations, and supports feature-wise attribution. We further provide theoretical insights showing that the proposed residual-based anomaly score is stable under velocity estimation error, and that its Monte Carlo estimator satisfies concentration bounds. Experiments on 20 benchmark datasets with 13 competitors demonstrate that Flow-CAD has the best average rank for PR AUC, ROC AUC, and P@n, highlighting its effectiveness and practicality for contextual anomaly detection in tabular data. We provide the source code at https://github.com/ZhongLIFR/FlowCAD.

</details>


### [125] [When can an interpretability claim be trusted? Stability and certification of feature dimensionality, ridge leverage and the feature recovery rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16190)
*Ali Tajfar,Behnam Tajfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出稀疏字典可解释性指标（特征维度、基于余弦阈值的特征恢复率）依赖任意阈值且对微小扰动不稳定，提出岭正则化版本并给出稳定性界与“特征已被恢复”声明的认证判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性工具常被用于支撑安全声明（如稀疏字典已恢复模型大部分特征），但这类声明依赖任意阈值；权重微扰、训练种子或阈值变化即可使结论反转，缺乏稳定性保证。

Method: 在玩具模型与具已知真值的合成字典上研究 Elhage 等人的特征维度与带余弦阈值的特征恢复率（FRR）；精确刻画特征维度不连续的位置，提出岭正则化特征维度，推导岭杠杆得分的稳定性界及正则化维度稳定常数上界与下界；并给出“特征 i 已恢复”在字典给定变化下仍成立的认证证明。

Result: 岭杠杆得分变化不超过权重算子范数变化的 3√3/(8√λ) 倍且常数最优；正则化维度稳定常数在尺度 ρ=R/√λ 下以 ρ/4 量级增长并给出显式上下界；认证在全部测试中成立且对小扰动有信息量，但无法跨独立训练字典非平凡地认证任何特征；标准阈值下 55% 特征是否“已恢复”随训练种子改变。

Conclusion: 现有特征维度与 FRR 指标缺乏稳健性，阈值与种子可翻转结论；岭正则化提供可证明的稳定性界，但认证只能保证局部扰动，无法支持跨独立训练的字典级恢复声明。实验仅限玩具模型，结论对真实模型的外推尚待验证。

Abstract: Interpretability tools increasingly support safety claims, such as that a sparse dictionary has recovered most of a model's features. Such claims rest on measurements with arbitrary thresholds, and small changes to the model weights, a different training seed or a different threshold can reverse them. We study two widely used measurements in toy models and synthetic dictionaries with known ground truth: the feature dimensionality of Elhage et al. and the feature recovery rate (FRR) with a cosine threshold. We characterize exactly where feature dimensionality is discontinuous and replace it with a regularized (ridge) version with sharp stability bounds: the ridge leverage score changes by at most $3\sqrt{3}/(8\sqrtλ)$ times the operator-norm change of the weights, and this constant cannot be improved; for the regularized dimensionality the stability constant grows like $ρ/4$ in the scale $ρ=R/\sqrtλ$, with explicit upper and lower bounds. We also prove certificates stating when a claim "feature $i$ is recovered" must survive a given change of the dictionary. The certificates held in every test and are informative for small perturbations, but certify no feature non-trivially across independently trained dictionaries; at the standard threshold, whether a feature counts as recovered changed with the training seed for 55 percent of features. Experiments use toy models only. The proofs, code and text were drafted by AI agents directed by the authors and checked by an independent blind AI reviewer and by numerical tests; the authors take full responsibility for the content.

</details>


### [126] [An Extensive Evaluation of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches for Sentiment Analysis: Insights from Amazon Customer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16201)
*Muhammed Ihsan Corak,Cuiyuan Wang,Xiaowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较5种传统机器学习与6种深度学习模型在Amazon Fine Food Reviews三分类情感任务上的表现，平衡集上随机森林(BoW)达99%、Attention达98.7%；深度学习跨类别分布更稳定，并附带一个用户端应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台用户生成文本快速增长，为理解客户观点与数据驱动决策提供机会；需系统比较传统机器学习与深度学习模型在大规模评论情感分类中的效果、效率与类别分布鲁棒性。

Method: 数据为568,454条Amazon Fine Food Reviews，三分类情感。5种机器学习分类器（随机森林、逻辑回归、Bernoulli NB、Linear SVC、Multinomial NB）使用BoW与TF-IDF（限5,000词）；6种深度学习架构（LSTM、Bi-LSTM、GRU、RNN、Attention、FastText）使用20,000词表的学习嵌入。分别在原始不平衡集与随机过采样平衡集（1,070,469条）上评估，指标含准确率、宏平均与各类别精确率/召回率/F1及训练时间。

Result: 平衡集上随机森林+BoW准确率99%，Attention 98.7%；Linear SVC 89%（1分15秒），逻辑回归90%（3分37秒）。不平衡集上Attention 97.8%，FastText 95.3%（3分26秒），RNN 91.9%但中性类F1仅0.08。深度学习对不同类别分布更稳定，最佳传统模型则依赖平衡处理。

Conclusion: 深度学习在类别分布变化下更稳健，传统模型最优者取决于是否平衡。作者还提供了将训练好的随机森林与Attention模型应用于新评论文本的用户端应用。局限：标签由星级评分派生，结果衡量的是与评分派生情感类的一致性而非人工标注情感；数据集来源单一，结论需在此限制下解读。

Abstract: The rapid growth of user-generated text on online platforms creates opportunities for understanding customer opinion and supporting data-driven decisions. This study compares five traditional machine learning classifiers (Random Forest, Logistic Regression, Bernoulli Naive Bayes, Linear Support Vector Classifier, and Multinomial Naive Bayes) and six deep learning architectures (LSTM, Bi-LSTM, GRU, RNN, Attention, and FastText) for three-class sentiment classification of 568,454 Amazon Fine Food Reviews. Machine learning models used Bag-of-Words (BoW) and TF-IDF features limited to 5,000 terms; deep learning models used learned embeddings over a 20,000-word vocabulary. Models were evaluated on the original imbalanced data and on a random-oversampled balanced set of 1,070,469 reviews, using accuracy, macro and per-class precision, recall, and F1-score, and training time. On the balanced set, Random Forest with BoW achieved 99\% accuracy and the Attention model achieved 98.7\%. Linear SVC reached 89\% accuracy in 1 min 15 s, and Logistic Regression reached 90\% in 3 min 37 s. On the imbalanced set, the Attention model achieved 97.8\% accuracy, and FastText achieved 95.3\% in 3 min 26 s, while the RNN reached 91.9\% with a neutral-class F1-score of 0.08. Deep learning models were more stable across class distributions, whereas the best classical model depended on balancing. We also report a user-facing application that applies the trained Random Forest and Attention models to new review text. Because the labels are derived from star ratings, the results measure agreement with rating-derived sentiment classes rather than annotated sentiment; the dataset is a single source, and the findings should be read with that limitation in mind.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal feature selection for firewall log analysis using Machine learning and Hybrid Metaheuristic algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16239)
*Seungwoo Han,Gil Hong,Jewan Kim,Jeuk Yu,Sangjun Lee,Byeongok Cho,Jusung Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将Q学习局部搜索的蜂群优化特征选择（QBSO-FS）用于UCI防火墙日志数据分类，并在修正数据泄漏问题后重新评估，所选5–7个特征即可达到99.90%准确率与94.88% Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防火墙日志分类对网络流量监控很重要，机器学习分类器效果好但高维特征拖慢分类速度；同时初版预印本在同一交叉验证上选特征并评估，且数据含12.8%完全重复样本，导致评分乐观偏差，需重新进行无泄漏评估。

Method: 采用QBSO-FS（基于Q学习局部搜索的蜂群优化）进行特征选择，结合随机森林、KNN与Bernoulli朴素贝叶斯分类器；采用无泄漏协议：划分训练/验证/测试集，仅用验证集选特征，在未触碰的测试集上一次性测量准确率与宏平均指标，并同时评估原始数据与去重预处理数据。

Result: 单次分层划分下QBSO-FS选出5–7个特征；所选子集在两个数据集上对KNN、在去重数据上对RF的Macro-F1较全特征提升8–19个百分点；RF原始数据与Bernoulli NB差异仅0.2–1.5个百分点、可能不稳健。去重数据上RF用7个特征达99.90%准确率、94.88% Macro-F1，超过复现的深度神经网络配置及对比方法的PSO特征子集，但低维数据上节省的运行时间有限。

Conclusion: 该方法可支撑自动化防火墙日志分析，但结论基于单次划分，多划分稳健性评估为后续工作。

Abstract: Firewall log classification is important to monitor network traffic. Machine learning classifiers using network-related features have shown good results for this task, but high-dimensional features slow down classification. In this paper, we apply feature selection using bee swarm optimization with Q-learning-based local search (QBSO-FS) to the UCI Internet Firewall Data Set, together with random forest (RF), k-nearest neighbor (KNN), and Bernoulli naïve Bayes (Bernoulli NB) classifiers. The first version of this preprint selected and evaluated feature subsets on the same 5-fold cross-validation of the full data set, which contains 12.8\% exact duplicates; the reported scores are therefore optimistically biased. In this extended version, we re-evaluate the method with a leakage-free protocol: the data are split into training, validation, and test sets, features are selected using the validation set only, and accuracy and macro-averaged scores are measured once on the untouched test set, for both the raw data and a deduplicated, preprocessed version. On a single stratified split, QBSO-FS selects 5--7 features. The selected subsets clearly improve Macro-F1 over all features for KNN on both data sets and for RF on the deduplicated data (by 8--19 percentage points). For RF on the raw data and for Bernoulli NB, the differences (0.2--1.5 percentage points) may not hold for a different split. RF with 7 selected features reaches 99.90\% accuracy and 94.88\% Macro-F1 on the deduplicated data. On the same split, this exceeds our re-implementations of the deep neural network configurations of previous studies and the feature subset selected by particle swarm optimization in one of them, while the run time saved on this low-dimensional data set is small. These results suggest that the method can support automatic firewall log analysis, while evaluation over multiple splits remains future work.

</details>


### [128] [Few-Shot Cross-Subject Neural--Image Retrieval via Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16257)
*Xizhang Yao,Li Zhang,Gan Huang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration (SA-LBC)，用刺激匹配的源群体锚点残差估计跨被试表征偏移的一阶量，并基于源域不确定性做收缩校正，从理论与实验两方面比较其与全局均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试神经—图像检索中，将少量刺激匹配的目标观测均值与源域全局均值直接比较，会把被试特异的表征偏移与刺激构成效应混淆，导致偏移估计有偏。

Method: 在固定预处理与冻结源域表征下，对目标响应与同刺激源群体锚点求残差并取均值以估计一阶偏移；再用源域导出的不确定性对校正量做收缩。理论部分在有限刺激总体无放回均匀采样假设下，推导未收缩锚定估计量与全局均值估计量的精确有限总体 MSE 表达式（含残差—锚点互协方差），并给出锚定相对全局均值降低 MSE 的充要条件。实验在 THINGS-EEG2 上做 10 被试留一被试交叉验证，覆盖六种神经—视觉配置和多种校准预算，并以打乱刺激身份的锚点作对照。

Result: Attention–CLIP ViT-B/32 配置下 MRR 由未校准的 0.1867 提升至 M=2 的 0.2013 与 M=16 的 0.2336；六种配置与所有预算下刺激匹配锚点均优于打乱身份锚点；M=2 时收缩在各配置均提升 MRR，但更大预算下效果依表征而异；平移与旋转校正的相对排名依赖正则强度。

Conclusion: 刺激匹配锚定可稳定优于全局均值式偏移估计，但更低的偏移估计 MSE 并不保证更高的检索性能，检索增益依赖表征，收缩与校正类型的效果需结合预算与正则强度具体评估。

Abstract: In cross-subject neural--image retrieval, comparing the mean of a few stimulus-matched target observations with the global source mean conflates subject-specific representation shifts with stimulus-composition effects. To address this problem, we propose Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration (SA-LBC), which estimates the first-order shift under fixed preprocessing and a frozen source-trained representation by averaging residuals between target responses and same-stimulus source-population anchors, then shrinks the correction using source-derived uncertainty. Under uniform sampling without replacement from a finite stimulus population, we derive exact finite-population MSE expressions for the unshrunk anchored and global-mean estimators, including residual--anchor cross-covariance, and establish a necessary and sufficient condition for anchoring to reduce MSE relative to global-mean estimation. In a 10-subject leave-one-subject-out evaluation on THINGS-EEG2, SA-LBC increases mean reciprocal rank (MRR) for the Attention--CLIP ViT-B/32 configuration from 0.1867 without calibration to 0.2013 and 0.2336 with M=2 and M=16 stimulus-level calibration samples, respectively. Across six neural--visual configurations and all calibration budgets, stimulus-matched anchors yield higher MRR than anchors with shuffled stimulus identities, but retrieval gains over no calibration remain representation-dependent. At (M=2), shrinkage improves MRR over the unshrunk anchored estimator in all six configurations, but its effects at larger budgets vary across representations; the relative ranking of translation and orientation corrections also depends on regularization strength. Thus, lower shift-estimation MSE does not guarantee higher retrieval performance. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Xizhang-Yao/SA-LBC.

</details>


### [129] [Rectified Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16265)
*Musheng Li,Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rectified Looped Transformer (RLT)，用基于flow建模思想的插值反馈替代循环Transformer中直接复用的回流方式，在无额外参数与计算的条件下实现更稳定的循环语言建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环/looped Transformer将每轮loop的输出直接作为下一轮输入，随循环加深会导致隐藏状态尺度增长与扰动放大，影响训练与推理稳定性。

Method: RLT重新设计loop间反馈：每轮将当前状态与其预测进行线性插值作为下一轮输入（插值调度受flow-based建模启发），把状态变化分摊到各轮，使从初始输入到最终输出的轨迹更平滑；并给出理论分析证明在合理假设下状态幅度一致有界、最坏情况扰动放大被削弱。

Result: 在语言建模语料与下游基准上，相同训练与推理深度下RLT的困惑度持续低于标准looped Transformer，对输入扰动和推理深度变化更鲁棒；潜轨迹分析显示隐藏状态范数跨loop更稳定，并有利于基于外推的解码策略。

Conclusion: RLT在不增加可训练参数、不增加循环块评估次数的前提下提升稳定性与性能，为looped Transformer提供简单而更稳定的反馈机制。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block, offering a parameter-efficient approach to language modeling. However, most existing works directly reuse each loop's output as the next's input, which can lead to hidden-state scale growth and perturbation amplification as recurrence deepens. We propose Rectified Looped Transformer (RLT), which redesigns feedback between loops using an interpolation schedule inspired by flow-based modeling. At each loop, RLT forms the next input by linearly interpolating between the current state and its prediction. Such interpolation distributes state changes across loops, encouraging a smoother trajectory from the initial input to the final output. Theoretical analysis proves that, under appropriate assumptions, RLT keeps state magnitudes uniformly bounded across loops and reduces the worst-case perturbation amplification. We evaluate RLT on language modeling corpora and downstream benchmarks. At matched training and inference depths, RLT consistently achieves lower perplexity than a standard looped Transformer, while exhibiting greater robustness to input perturbations and variance in inference depth. Consistent with the theoretical results, latent trajectory analysis shows that RLT maintains more stable hidden-state norms across loops, which also facilitates extrapolation-based decoding strategies. Notably, RLT achieves these gains without requiring additional trainable parameters or extra recurrent block evaluations, thus providing a simple yet more stable feedback mechanism for looped Transformers.

</details>


### [130] [LoCoJAX: Host-Local Coordination for Small-Model RL on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16301)
*Loki Chen,Gregory B. Rehm,Yang Song,Hongye Xie,Ming Lei,Chongyang Bai,Bob Kamma,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Yufei Li,Xi Liu,Jay Xu,Rui Liu,Maziar Sanjabi,Luke Simon,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析小模型RL在TPU上使用Pathways单控制器带来的主机/网络协调开销，并提出LoCoJAX（本地协调JAX），在每台TPU主机上本地执行rollout与奖励处理、仅同步全局训练，使Qwen3-0.6B每步快1.3–3.6倍，并给出解耦模式LoCoJAX-DA的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL越来越多地用于小语言模型，而Pathways式单控制器协调模型对短而频繁的小模型计算难以摊薄协调开销；作者希望量化这些开销随模型规模的变化，并针对小模型设计更高效的执行方式。

Method: 在TPU v7x上对0.6B–405B稠密模型在decode-heavy负载下做规模扫描，测量host编排与网络通信开销占比；据此提出LoCoJAX，在每台TPU主机本地完成rollout与奖励处理，并保持全局同步训练；在Qwen3-0.6B、三种负载、8–64芯片上与调优后的Pathways基线做受控对比；另实现Ray管理的解耦模式LoCoJAX-DA，在独立主机上重叠rollout与训练，并进行放置（placement）实验。

Result: 0.6B时host编排占step时间36%、网络通信占2%，大模型时估计网络开销主导非计算时间；LoCoJAX-CO每步快1.3–3.6倍，GSM8K上8芯片一步即快于Pathways的64芯片；NuminaMath-CoT在256芯片上给rollout 75%主机可较LoCoJAX-CO缩短约15% step时间。

Conclusion: 小模型RL的主要瓶颈是主机侧编排而非网络；本地协调+全局同步可显著降低开销，解耦重叠仅在rollout主导step且获得多数主机时才划算。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly targets small language models, and executing these workloads efficiently across multiple accelerators requires effective coordination. On TPUs, Pathways-based RL uses a single controller to coordinate computation across hosts. This execution model is effective for large models, but its coordination computational costs are harder to amortize when small models repeatedly invoke short computations. To characterize these computational costs, we sweep dense models from 0.6B to 405B parameters on TPU v7x under a decode-heavy workload and measure how host and network computational overhead vary with model size. At 0.6B, host orchestration occupies 36% of step time and network communication 2%, whereas for large models, estimated network computational costs dominate the non-compute time. Motivated by these findings, we present Local Coordination JAX (LoCoJAX), which implements local rollout and reward processing on each TPU host with synchronous global training. In controlled comparisons against a tuned and optimized Pathways baseline on Qwen3-0.6B across three workloads and 8-64 TPU v7x chips, its colocated mode (LoCoJAX-CO) is 1.3-3.6x faster per step; on GSM8K, it completes a step faster on 8 chips than Pathways does on 64. Finally, a Ray-managed disaggregated mode (LoCoJAX-DA) overlaps rollout and training on separate hosts, and placement experiments establish when this overlap compensates for dividing devices and transferring weights: only when rollout dominates the step and receives most of the hosts. On NuminaMath-CoT at 256 chips, giving rollout 75% of hosts cuts step time by about 15% compared with LoCoJAX-CO.

</details>


### [131] [StateAlign: Internalizing State-Aware Decision-Making for Reliable Multi-Turn Tool Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16304)
*Zhexi Lu,Subhajit Chaudhury,Tejaswini Pedapati,Keerthiram Murugesan,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StateAlign：将任务状态构建与状态对比强化学习结合，让智能体依据当前任务状态做出工具使用决策，在提升任务完成率的同时降低工具幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽提升了大模型工具使用能力，但任务性能提升可能伴随工具幻觉增加；可靠的工具使用要求决策与当前任务状态（可用信息、工具能力、任务进度）一致，而分析发现智能体即便推理中识别出信息缺失，仍会错误地执行而非追问、检索或拒绝不可行请求。

Method: 提出StateAlign，结合任务状态构建与状态对比强化学习；数据覆盖五类任务条件，并将状态与不同动作需求配对；使用结果奖励引导任务完成，并在配对状态下对相同生成的推理与动作进行token级对比，训练输出以贴合当前状态。

Result: 在三个模型家族共五个基座模型上，BFCL v4多轮、ToolSandbox和When2Call均取得提升；消融显示状态构建在不同奖励设计下均改善性能与可靠性。在Qwen3-8B上，BFCL v4多轮准确率由45.5%提升至55.2%，SimpleToolHalluBench无工具幻觉率从仅结果奖励训练的62.2%降至56.1%。

Conclusion: StateAlign在三个Qwen3规模上整体降低工具幻觉，行为分析显示决策更一致、多步调用执行更完整、工具报错后的恢复能力更强，说明状态对齐是兼顾任务性能与可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the tool-use capabilities of large language models. However, better task performance can coincide with increased tool hallucination. Reliable tool use requires decisions to follow the current task state: available information, tool capabilities, and task progress. Our analysis shows that agents sometimes execute when they should ask for information, retrieve it, or decline an infeasible request, even when reasoning identifies missing information. We introduce StateAlign, combining task-state construction with state-contrastive reinforcement learning. The data cover five task conditions and pair states with different action requirements. Outcome rewards guide task completion; token-level contrasts compare the same generated reasoning and actions under paired states to train outputs that fit the current state. Experiments across five base models from three families show improvements on BFCL v4 multi-turn, ToolSandbox, and When2Call. Ablations show that state construction improves performance and reliability under different reward designs. The complete method further reduces tool hallucination across three Qwen3 sizes. On Qwen3-8B, StateAlign improves BFCL v4 multi-turn accuracy from 45.5% to 55.2% and reduces no-tool hallucination on SimpleToolHalluBench from 62.2% under outcome-only training to 56.1%. Behavioral analyses show more consistent decisions, more complete multi-call execution, and stronger recovery after tool errors.

</details>


### [132] [Overcoming Spurious Correlations in Conditional Diffusion Models via Doubly Robust Target Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16306)
*Eiichi Sakurai,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR-Diff: 用函数梯度构造伪目标的微调框架，使预训练条件扩散模型高效适配协变量偏移的目标分布并降低目标风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型会记忆训练数据中的虚假相关，偏离理想目标分布；按分布重训代价过高。

Method: 基于函数梯度推导"伪目标"，刻画目标分布下基模型噪声预测与真实噪声的残差，令模型拟合该伪目标实现目标适配。

Result: 理论上证明总体层面目标风险下降，并具伪双重稳健性（抗密度比估计误差与基模型误设）；实验上合成数据噪声预测精度比肩从头训练，且缓解下游分类组间差异。

Conclusion: DR-Diff 为条件扩散模型提供高效、稳健的分布适配微调方案，兼具理论保证与实证收益。

Abstract: While conditional diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generation tasks, they memorize spurious correlations within training data. This causes them to deviate from ideal target distributions. Minimizing a covariate shift-adjusted target risk can address this issue. However, retraining models from scratch for different distributions is computationally prohibitive. To overcome this limitation, we propose DR-Diff, a framework that efficiently fine-tunes pre-trained conditional diffusion models. Specifically, DR-Diff leverages a functional gradient approach to derive a pseudo-target. This pseudo-target captures the residual between the base model's noise prediction and the ground-truth noise under the target distribution. Training the model to fit this pseudo-target enables efficient target adaptation. Theoretically, we prove that DR-Diff guarantees target risk reduction at the population level. It also achieves a pseudo-doubly robust property, providing resilience against density ratio estimation errors and base model misspecifications. Empirically, DR-Diff matches the noise prediction accuracy of models trained from scratch on synthetic datasets. Furthermore, it mitigates inter-group disparities in downstream classification tasks.

</details>


### [133] [Wanda-Guided Sparse Perturbations for Efficient Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16317)
*Ziqing Wen,Xiaohui Song,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Junlin Zeng,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WIS-MeZO：用Wanda权重-激活显著性构造任务条件稀疏扰动掩码（模型保持稠密），以提升零阶优化微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZO）仅靠前向评估即可内存高效地微调大语言模型，但高维随机扰动限制了优化效率；稀疏扰动可降低搜索维度，因此关键坐标（支撑集）的选取变得至关重要。

Method: WIS-MeZO：基于Wanda权重-激活显著性构建任务条件化的稀疏扰动掩码，同时保持预训练模型稠密不变；通过梯度能量捕获量分析支撑集质量，并将其与梯度估计及收敛性建立联系；与随机稀疏扰动、基于幅值的稀疏扰动进行对照。

Result: 在LLaMA与Qwen（3B–32B）共16个基准上，聚合得分高于MeZO，并在更少的优化步数内达到可比的验证损失；对照实验表明信息性支撑集选择的重要性。

Conclusion: 稀疏ZO中支撑集的选择而非仅稀疏度本身决定优化效率；任务条件化的显著性掩码是提升ZO微调收敛速度与效果的有效手段。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models through forward-only evaluations, but high-dimensional random perturbations can limit optimization efficiency. Sparse perturbations reduce the search dimension, making the selection of informative coordinates critical. We propose Wanda-Importance Sparsity-MeZO (WIS-MeZO), which uses Wanda weight--activation saliency to construct task-conditioned sparse perturbation masks while keeping the pretrained model dense. We analyze support quality through gradient energy capture and relate it to gradient estimation and convergence. Across 16 benchmarks with LLaMA and Qwen models ranging from 3B to 32B parameters, WIS-MeZO achieves higher aggregate scores than MeZO and reaches comparable validation losses in fewer optimization steps. Comparisons with random and magnitude-based sparse perturbations further highlight the importance of informative support selection.

</details>


### [134] [Climate Temporal Temperature Prediction via an Adaptive Stacked Bidirectional Residual Mamba Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16322)
*Yongxiang Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Deep DualMamba框架用于气温时间序列预测，基于改进的Mamba选择性状态空间模型，采用自适应双向Mamba编码器与硬件感知并行递归算法，实现线性复杂度、无需注意力与MLP的结构，并经理论分析验证其逼近与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确气温预测依赖传统气象参数，对天气信息学监测与预报指导至关重要。Transformer类基础模型虽占主导，但在长序列上存在计算效率瓶颈；既有次二次时间模型难以支撑长期且稳健的预测。

Method: 基于选择性结构化状态空间模型（SSM）构建Deep DualMamba：1）设计自适应双向Mamba作为编码器以学习复杂高效特征；2）提出硬件感知的并行递归算法，弥补卷积效率损失并保证大规模实用性；3）对模型的逼近能力与收敛性进行理论分析，形成无注意力、无MLP的简化架构。

Result: 模型相对序列长度呈线性复杂度，推理吞吐显著提升；理论分析给出逼近与收敛性保证；并基于英国监测站历史气象数据搭建完整预测流程以验证其在气候信息学中的适用性。

Conclusion: Deep DualMamba以线性复杂度的无注意力架构在长序列气温预测中兼顾可扩展性与性能，具备理论保障与工程可行性，适用于气候信息学实际预测任务。

Abstract: Accurate temperatures forecasting relies on traditional meteorological parameters that are essential for monitoring weather informatics and guiding forecasting efforts. This study presents Deep DualMamba, a novel deep learning framework for temporal temperature prediction, built upon an enhanced variant of the recently proposed Mamba network. Unlike conventional Transformer-based architectures, which dominate foundation models but suffer from computational inefficiencies on long sequences, Deep DualMamba leverages selective structured state space models (SSMs) to achieve both scalability and performance. We identify a core limitation of prior subquadratic-time models-namely, their inability to perform long-term and robust forecasting and introduce two key innovations. First, we propose an adaptive bidirectional Mamba as encoders to learn complex and efficient features. Second, we develop a hardware-aware, parallel recurrent algorithm to compensate for the loss of convolutional efficiency, ensuring practical viability at scale. Last, the Deep DualMamba's approximation ability and convergence is theoretical analyzed. These design choices culminate in a simplified, attention- and MLP-free neural architecture that exhibits linear complexity with sequence length and significantly improved inference throughput. To demonstrate its applicability in climate informatics, we implement a full forecasting pipeline using historical meteorological data from UK monitoring stations.

</details>


### [135] [Does Deep Knowledge Tracing Learn Semantic Relations Between Knowledge Components?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16323)
*Kia Karbasi,Mahdi Saiedi,Kevin Hong,Gregory Pottie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究检验深度知识追踪（DKT）学到的知识点（KC）关系是否具有语义性：在九种文本语义表示构建流程下，DKT导出的KC关系图与语义相似度结构仅呈弱且不一致的对齐，引入语义信息也难以稳定超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DKT以共享隐状态从交互序列中更新，导致某KC的交互会影响其他KC的预测；作者由此追问：这种跨KC影响所反映的关系是否真的对应知识点之间的语义关联，还是主要源于其他信号。

Method: 构建基于文本的语义KC表示，采用九种处理流程以检验结论对表示构建方式是否稳健。测试一：比较DKT导出的KC关系图与由KC表示两两余弦相似度诱导的语义关系结构。测试二：将语义信息（预训练文本表示、语义初始化、KC监督微调的BERT）注入DKT，与标准DKT基线比较。另在ASSISTments 2017上做嵌套样本量的缩减数据实验。

Result: 在所有评估数据集、嵌入模型与流程下，两类结构仅弱对齐且不一致；预训练文本表示的引入未能稳定优于标准DKT。缩减数据实验中，语义初始化在ASSISTments 2017的九档嵌套样本量中有八档取得更高测试AUC；KC监督微调的BERT在部分匹配比较中产生小幅正向AUC差异，但跨运行稳定性未确立。整体效应有限且不一致。

Conclusion: DKT导出的KC关系并非主要是语义性的：文本语义结构无法强有力地还原DKT学到的关系结构，行为、时序、结构或心理测量学信号可能同样塑造这些关系。

Abstract: Deep Knowledge Tracing (DKT) is a knowledge tracing (KT) model that updates a shared latent state from sequences of interactions, each pairing a knowledge component (KC) with a response. An interaction with one KC can therefore affect predictions for other KCs. We ask: Are the KC relations learned by DKT semantic? We construct text-derived semantic KC representations using nine pipelines to test whether the conclusions hold across alternative ways of constructing those representations.
  The first test compares DKT-derived KC relation graphs with semantic relation structures induced by pairwise cosine similarities among the KC representations. Across the evaluated datasets, embedding models, and pipelines, the two structures align only weakly and inconsistently. The second test introduces semantic information into DKT; the pretrained text-derived representations do not consistently outperform the standard DKT baseline.
  In the reduced-data experiments, semantic initialization yields a higher test area under the ROC curve (AUC) than the baseline at eight of nine nested sample sizes for ASSISTments 2017. KC-supervised fine-tuned BERT produces small positive AUC differences in some matched comparisons, but the study does not establish their stability across runs. These effects remain limited and inconsistent. The findings suggest that behavioral, temporal, structural, or psychometric signals may also shape DKT's learned relations.
