DKT-derived relations are not primarily semantic: the tested text-derived semantic structures do not strongly recover the DKT-derived relation structure.

</details>


### [136] [DRAMA: Dissecting Attention Redundancy for Accelerating Multimodal Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16345)
*Yi-Hsin Hung,Fangfu Liu,Shao-Yuan Lo,Chu-Song Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRAMA，一个免训练的多模态扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过系统性测量注意力冗余发现两个冗余结构，并据此设计视觉token剪枝与自适应停止两种机制，实现平均18.67倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散大语言模型（dLLM）每步去噪需对长多模态序列做全前向，视觉token主导序列长度，加之固定步数预算，导致二次方的单步成本高昂。现有加速方法仅依赖孤立的注意力可视化来隐式刻画冗余，缺乏测量依据。

Method: 对注意力张量沿空间与时间轴进行系统剖析，揭示两种冗余结构：注意力质量集中于稀疏视觉token子集；注意力分布在初始形成阶段后快速稳定。据此设计空间冗余感知视觉token剪枝（SVTP）与充分性引导自适应停止（SGAS）两种互补机制，无需训练、不改架构、不做注意力缓存。

Result: 在六个视觉-语言基准上实现平均18.67倍推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 结果为多模态dLLM推理建立了一个基于测量、强有力的加速基线。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text through iterative masked denoising with bidirectional attention, offering a compelling alternative to autoregressive vision-language modeling. However, each denoising step requires a full forward pass over long multimodal sequences in which visual tokens dominate the sequence length, and a fixed step budget further compounds this quadratic per-step cost. Existing inference acceleration approaches for multimodal dLLMs characterize attention redundancy implicitly, motivating designs from isolated attention visualizations rather than from measurement. We close this gap with DRAMA, a training-free acceleration framework grounded in a systematic dissection of attention redundancy in multimodal dLLMs. By probing attention tensors along spatial and temporal axes, we reveal two persistent redundancy structures: (i) attention mass consistently concentrates on a sparse subset of visual tokens, and (ii) attention distributions rapidly stabilize across denoising steps after an initial formation phase. These findings motivate two complementary mechanisms: Spatial-Redundancy-Aware Visual Token Pruning (SVTP) and Sufficiency-Guided Adaptive Stopping (SGAS). Together, DRAMA achieves an average 18.67 times inference speedup across six vision-language benchmarks without training, architectural modification, or attention caching, while maintaining competitive accuracy. Our results establish a strong, measurement-grounded acceleration baseline for multimodal dLLM inference.

</details>


### [137] [MutAtlas: A PDB-Wide Energy-Guided Atlas of Protein Mutation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16361)
*Ruihan Guo,Chaoran Cheng,Zhanghan Ni,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建PDB全库单点突变增强数据集，统一刻画物理能量模型、蛋白语言模型与逆折叠模型的突变偏好，发现跨模型不一致普遍存在，提出无监督多源突变偏好蒸馏框架，在无实验标签下于ProteinGym取得最佳零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测得的蛋白突变效应数据准确但极其稀疏，难以支撑规模化预测；需借助可扩展的替代突变信号，但不同来源模型（物理能量、语言模型、逆折叠）给出的突变偏好可能存在系统性冲突，需先量化这些差异再加以利用。

Method: 1) 构建PDB全库突变增强数据集：对实验解析结构穷举单点替换，并对齐物理能量模型、蛋白语言模型、逆折叠模型三类来源的突变信号；2) 在统一的突变偏好表示下，从一致性、集中度与替换模式三个维度进行大规模分析；3) 提出无监督多源突变偏好蒸馏框架，基于相对突变偏好学习并显式建模跨源分歧，训练中不使用任何实验突变标签。

Result: 各模型在突变分布的一致性、集中度和替换模式上差异显著，说明分歧具有普遍性且源于相互冲突的归纳偏置而非随机噪声；所提方法在ProteinGym上整体优于所评估的零样本基线及朴素多源融合策略。

Conclusion: 跨源分歧是可建模的结构化信息，显式建模分歧的无监督多源蒸馏能在无实验标签条件下有效提升突变效应预测；同时开源数据集与评测流程以支持结果可复现。

Abstract: Protein mutation effect prediction is fundamental to protein engineering and disease variant interpretation, yet experimentally measured mutation data remain accurate but extremely sparse. To provide scalable supplementary mutation signals, we construct a PDB-wide mutation augmentation dataset that exhaustively enumerates single-site substitutions on experimentally resolved protein structures and aligns mutation signals from physics-based energy models, protein language models, and inverse folding models. Large-scale analysis under a unified mutation preference representation reveals substantial differences in the consistency, concentration, and substitution patterns of mutation distributions across models, indicating that disagreement is pervasive and reflects conflicting inductive biases rather than random noise. Motivated by these observations, we propose an unsupervised multi-source mutation preference distillation framework that learns from relative mutation preferences while explicitly modeling cross-source disagreement. Without using any experimental mutation labels during training, our approach achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines and naive multi-source fusion strategies on ProteinGym. We release the dataset and evaluation pipeline to support reproducible studies of protein mutation effects.

</details>


### [138] [Bait: Mitigating Harmful Agentic Reinforcement Learning via Probing Reward Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16369)
*Tiansheng Huang,Ananth Balashankar,Virat Shejwalkar,Santosh Pande,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示新兴的"智能体强化学习即服务"(agentic RLaaS)存在安全漏洞：仅需约20美元，用户即可通过有毒环境的多轮有害智能体强化学习完全瓦解大模型的安全对齐，并用降级模型实施软件漏洞利用与代码复用攻击。作者提出Bait防御，通过主动探测奖励函数来缓解该威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注模型对齐，但智能体强化学习即服务让用户能通过API用自定义环境和奖励函数微调模型，这一新范式可能被滥用：用户可将有害奖励函数注入训练流程，从而系统性破坏模型的安全对齐，且成本极低、现实可行，构成被忽视的安全风险。

Method: 攻击方面：通过服务API提交有毒环境与恶意奖励函数，进行多轮有害智能体强化学习，实现低成本的端到端对齐破坏，并验证降级模型可用于真实有害活动（软件漏洞利用、代码复用攻击）。防御方面：提出Bait，通过主动探测奖励函数来识别并缓解从有害奖励中学习的行为，同时保留良性任务的学习能力。另结合实证与理论分析自适应攻击者的可行性。

Result: 约20美元成本即可完全颠覆模型（如Inkling）的安全对齐；成功攻击后的降级模型可辅助真实有害活动。Bait能充分缓解从有害奖励函数中学习，且不妨碍从良性奖励函数（促进良性智能体任务）中学习。理论与实践分析表明，即使自适应攻击者已知Bait，也因系统性挑战无法完美绕过。

Conclusion: 智能体强化学习即服务引入了低成本、可现实利用的对齐破坏攻击面，需在服务层面加以防护；主动探测奖励函数的Bait是一种简单有效且具备理论鲁棒性基础的缓解手段。

Abstract: We expose a safety vulnerability on nascent agentic reinforcement-learning-as-a-service. Specifically, with a cost as low as \$20, we show that safety alignment of a large language model (e.g., Inkling) can be completely subverted through rounds of harmful agentic reinforcement learning (agentic RL) with a toxic environment from its users. We also demonstrate that upon a successful harmful agentic RL attack via the service API, the attackers can potentially use the safety-degraded model to assist with real-world harmful activities, e.g., exploit software vulnerabilities and launch code re-use attacks. To mitigate harmful agentic RL, we propose Bait, a defense that mitigates the attack via proactively probing the reward function. Experimental results show that such a simple defense can sufficiently mitigate learning from a harmful reward function and can still learn from a benign reward function that promotes benign agentic tasks. Further analysis on both empirical and theoretical aspects shows that an adaptive attacker who assumes the knowledge of Bait defense cannot perfectly subvert it due to a systematic challenge. Source code is available at https://github.com/git-disl/Bait.

</details>


### [139] [Recursive State-Augmented Language Models: Persistent Latent-State Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16375)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归状态增强语言模型（Recursive SALM）：以持续演化的潜在状态随观测更新并生成对预训练语言模型的适配，通过读-转移-写模块化解耦观测编码、潜在动态与预测器适配；冻结 GPT-2 small 的实验表明，仅靠 64 维状态传递先验证据即可实现接近全上下文基线的序贯推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型受限于有限的 token 上下文，无法访问较早的交互信息；需要一种不依赖扩展上下文的机制来保留并利用历史证据。

Method: Recursive SALM 框架：持久潜在状态随每次观测演化，并据此生成预训练语言模型的适配参数；采用 read–transition–write 模块化架构，分别处理观测编码、潜在动态与预测器适配；给出确定性与随机性两种形式化。实验仅评估确定性版本，主干为冻结的 GPT-2 small，且解码器上下文中刻意剔除早期证据；采用潜在门控 LoRA 进行适配，并以 64 维状态作为早期证据的唯一通道，辅以状态置零/打乱消融。

Result: 四轮假设推断任务中，潜在门控 LoRA 达 89.1% 准确率，与使用完整上下文训练的全上下文 LoRA 基线（88.0%）相当，且早期证据仅经由 64 维状态传入；将状态置零或打乱后准确率显著下降。两轮任务中，在训练未见的码字上，多种接口使候选排序优于随机，但在所评估的训练方案下很少生成精确码字。

Conclusion: 持久潜在状态条件化的适配可在解码器上下文缺失早期证据时支撑序贯推断；但精确生成与随机形式化尚待验证。

Abstract: Language models rely on finite token contexts, limiting access to earlier interactions. We introduce Recursive State-Augmented Language Models (Recursive SALM), a framework in which a persistent latent state evolves with each observation and generates adaptations of a pretrained language model. A modular read-transition-write architecture separates observation encoding, latent dynamics, and predictor adaptation. We develop deterministic and stochastic formulations.
  The experiments evaluate only deterministic SALM with frozen GPT-2 small and exclude earlier evidence from its decoder context. On a four-turn hypothesis-inference task, latent-gated LoRA reaches 89.1% accuracy, comparable to 88.0% for a trained full-context LoRA baseline, with earlier evidence available solely through a 64-dimensional state. Zeroing or shuffling the state substantially reduces accuracy. In a two-turn task with codewords unseen in training, several interfaces improve candidate ranking over chance, but rarely generate the exact codeword under the evaluated training schedule. These experiments show that persistent latent-conditioned adaptation can support sequential inference when earlier evidence is absent from the decoder context.

</details>


### [140] [Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16409)
*Doyoung Kim,Byeongguk Jeon,Kailin Zhang,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACE-RL，在固定的Agentic Context Engineering脚手架下用一个模型同时承担行动、反思与剧本维护三种角色，以标量奖励做梯度更新，并用经验交接产物的下游效果来筛选经验，实现"标量奖励+环境信息"兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于标量奖励的强化学习训练LLM智能体时，丢弃了试验过程中丰富的环境信息（如错误日志）。近期借助harness（反思/压缩）把过去经验交给后续rollout，但这些交接产物（如playbook）的构建本身并未被学习，因此希望兼顾二者。

Method: 固定ACE harness作为试验台，用单一模型承担行动生成器、洞察反思器和剧本维护者三种角色；根据候选经验交接产物对未来actor rollout的下游影响来评估其价值，并在固定脚手架下联合优化各角色的行为。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，ACE-RL的留出集（held-out）性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 智能体训练不应只优化当前rollout中的动作，还应优化模型书写、由未来rollout继承的经验。

Abstract: It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.

</details>


### [141] [SoScale: Second-Order Scale Search for FP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16426)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Hui-Ling Zhen,Weizhe Lin,Xianzhi Yu,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoScale：面向分层FP4量化的二阶权重尺度码搜索方法，以Hessian感知的层输出重建为目标搜索硬件合法尺度码，并结合GPTQ式误差补偿，在MXFP4/NVFP4/HiF4下均降低量化退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4量化中每块局部尺度的选取高度影响精度；默认max-abs缩放虽保留动态范围，却把稀缺的FP4码字浪费在稀有离群区域，密集值分辨率不足；现有尺度搜索最小化局部逐元素重建误差，优化的是张量保真度而非层输出保真度。

Method: 针对MXFP4、NVFP4、HiF4等块级/分层尺度格式，在硬件合法尺度码空间内搜索，目标函数为Hessian感知的层重建误差，并将尺度搜索与GPTQ式误差恢复耦合，按尺度对层输出的影响进行选择。

Result: 在Qwen3系列模型上，于MXFP4、NVFP4、HiF4三种格式下均一致地降低了下游任务量化退化。

Conclusion: 以层输出保真度（而非逐元素张量误差）为准则进行尺度码搜索，可更有效提升FP4量化精度，且方法对多种FP4格式通用。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization reduces the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), yet accurate FP4 quantization remains challenging. Practical formats such as MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4 pair a 4-bit floating-point payload with block-wise or hierarchical scale codes, making accuracy highly sensitive to how each local distribution is scaled. Default maximum-absolute-value scaling preserves dynamic range, but can spend scarce FP4 levels on rarely occupied outlier regions and leave dense tensor values poorly resolved. Existing scale-search methods minimize local element-wise reconstruction error, optimizing tensor fidelity rather than layer-output fidelity. We propose SoScale, a second-order weight scale-code search method for hierarchical FP4 quantization. SoScale searches hardware-valid scale codes under a Hessian-aware layer reconstruction objective and couples this search with GPTQ-style error recovery, selecting scales by their impact on layer outputs. Across Qwen3 models, SoScale consistently reduces downstream quantization degradation under MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4.

</details>


### [142] [A Recipe for Physical Action Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16450)
*Shaojiang Wang,Geer Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将机器人模型容量选择、示范预算分配，以及数据组成、表示、优化与部署决策统一到一个条件分布目标下，并给出可用于条件性训练建议的度量与分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择机器人模型容量与示范数据预算时，还需同时决定数据组成、表示、优化与部署方式；作者希望把这些分散的决策放在同一理论框架中理解其相互影响。

Method: 以条件分布为共同目标：用嵌套学习神谕（nested learning oracles）做精确风险核算；借助局部信息几何、条件互信息与经验收敛给出条件性训练处方；通过已知目标数值检验；在206条公开PushT轨迹上评估固定特征的信息设计准则（不拟合策略）。

Result: 数值检验揭示了容量—数据耦合、表示导致的混合分布失配，以及部署KL上升而负对数似然下降的反例；在非对称评估目标下，所选分配相对目标匹配将该准则降低4.5–6.2%，而平衡目标下无收益。

Conclusion: 研究明确了哪些量可以支撑训练建议，并指出看似有利的预测指标可能具有不同解释，需结合部署目标审慎解读。

Abstract: Choosing robot model capacity and demonstration budgets also requires decisions about data composition, representation, optimization, and deployment. We connect these decisions through a common conditional-distribution target. Nested learning oracles give exact risk accounting; local information geometry, conditional mutual information, and empirical convergence provide conditional training prescriptions. Known-target numerical checks expose capacity-data coupling, representation-induced mixture mismatch, and an example where deployment KL increases while negative log-likelihood decreases. An analysis of 206 public PushT trajectories evaluates fixed-feature information-design criteria without fitting a policy. Under asymmetric evaluation targets, the selected allocations reduce this criterion by 4.5-6.2% relative to target matching; balanced targets show no benefit. These results clarify which quantities can support training recommendations and when an apparently favorable predictive metric has a different interpretation.

</details>


### [143] [Helgason-Fourier Random Features for Scalable Hyperbolic Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16461)
*Koshi Watanabe,Daichi Mochihashi,Hideitsu Hino,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机Helgason–Fourier特征(RHFF)，为双曲空间上的核提供显式有限维特征映射，并据此构建无需数值积分、变分界或辅助变量的双曲潜变量模型RHFF-LVM，在树状合成数据上更忠实恢复度量，并可扩展到5×10^5点（505秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲几何适合嵌入具有树状层次结构的数据，即使在低维也能忠实刻画树状结构，而欧氏降维在低维时严重受限。然而现有双曲GP-LVM依赖数值积分、变分界或辅助变量，计算代价高且难以扩展到大规模数据，缺少类似欧氏随机Fourier特征的显式有限维核近似工具。

Method: 提出Random Helgason–Fourier Features (RHFF)：为双曲空间上的核构造显式有限维特征映射，频率从所刻画的谱密度中采样，从而可直接在频域指定Matérn等核，并证明其一致逼近误差界。基于该表示构建双曲潜变量模型RHFF-LVM，无需数值积分、变分界或辅助变量。

Result: RHFF-LVM在合成树数据上比所有基线更忠实地恢复度量；获得最佳平均排名，主要来自Q=2时树状atlas数据上的大幅提升；可在505秒内拟合5×10^5个点，成为首个达到该规模的非参数双曲LVM。

Conclusion: RHFF为双曲空间核提供了一种可解析指定谱密度、具一致误差界的显式有限维特征近似，使双曲非参数潜变量建模摆脱数值积分与变分近似的负担，并显著提升精度与大尺度可扩展性。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry is well suited to embedding hierarchical data, capturing tree-like structure faithfully even in the low dimensions where Euclidean dimensionality reduction is severely constrained. We introduce Random Helgason--Fourier Features (RHFF), an explicit finite-dimensional feature map for kernels on hyperbolic space. We characterize the spectral density from which RHFF frequencies are drawn, allowing kernels such as the Matérn family to be specified directly in the frequency domain. We also prove a uniform approximation error bound for RHFF. Building on this representation, we develop a hyperbolic latent variable model called RHFF-LVM, which requires no numerical quadrature, variational bounds or auxiliary variables unlike previous hyperbolic GP-LVMs. Our RHFF-LVM recovers the metric of a synthetic tree more faithfully than all baselines and attains the best mean rank, driven by large gains on tree-structured atlases at $Q\!=\!2$. Furthermore, it fits $5\times10^{5}$ points in $505$ seconds, making it the first non-parametric hyperbolic LVM to reach this scale.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Temporal Dynamics with Derivative Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16514)
*Shujie Duan,Jia Hong Puah,Haocheng Wu,Yifei Mao,Hongting Zhou,Chenxuan Li,Zitao Fang,Yang Lu,Sim Kuan Goh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaTSF：一种动力学感知的时间序列预测框架，基于离散导数表示，在统一、模型无关的训练框架内联合学习序列及其离散时间导数，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升预测性能与动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多由架构创新驱动，较少关注显式利用系统动力学时间结构的训练策略；即便Transformer等深度学习模型仍难以捕捉系统动力学的时间演化。

Method: 提出DeltaTSF：对输入区间与预测区间同时联合学习时间序列及其离散时间导数表示，通过跨时间耦合这些表示促使模型维持时间一致性（作者证明其在训练中诱导出离散Taylor一致性），且无需显式计算导数，推理阶段无额外计算开销，属于模型无关框架。

Result: 在多种基准与多种骨干架构上的大量实验表明，DeltaTSF持续提升预测性能，并相较强SOTA方法产生更具动态一致性的预测。

Conclusion: 通过导数表示施加动态一致性是一种模型无关范式，可与架构设计并行并互补，为时间序列预测提供新的训练层面改进路径。

Abstract: Time series forecasting aims to predict the future evolution of complex systems by learning their underlying dynamics from past observations. Despite recent advances in deep learning, Transformers in particular still struggle to capture the temporal evolution of system dynamics. Existing approaches have been largely driven by architectural innovation, with comparatively little attention paid to training strategies that explicitly exploit the temporal structure governing system dynamics. In this work, we introduce DeltaTSF, a dynamics-aware forecasting framework based on discrete derivative representations of time series. Specifically, DeltaTSF jointly learns the series and their discrete temporal derivatives across both input and forecast horizons within a unified, model-agnostic framework. By coupling these representations over time, the model is encouraged to maintain temporal consistency, a property we show to induce a form of discrete Taylor consistency during training. This formulation enables the model to better reflect the underlying system dynamics, resulting in more stable and physically coherent forecasts. Notably, DeltaTSF does not require explicit derivative computation and introduces no additional computational overhead at inference time. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and backbone architectures demonstrate that DeltaTSF consistently improves forecasting performance and yields more dynamically coherent predictions than strong SOTA methods. These results highlight the effectiveness of enforcing dynamic coherence through derivative representations as a model-agnostic paradigm that operates alongside and complements architectural design. Source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [145] [RESYNC: Residual Latent-State Synchronization for Deadline-Bound Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16523)
*Eljas Linna,Hao Dong,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RESYNC让大模型异步运行，快速路径用新观测更新其陈旧但精确的潜在状态并复用不变的预测头，在严格期限下实现大模型级精度与接近小模型的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速流中每条观测必须在下次到达前完成预测，大模型精度更好但易错过截止期限，需在保证实时响应的同时保留大模型优势。

Method: 大模型异步计算潜在状态；快速路径利用自该状态计算以来到达的观测，更新其最新但已陈旧的精确潜在状态，并直接套用未改动的预测头；更新器在无任务标签条件下从冻结模型表示中学习。

Result: 在1个真实流、2个合成流和5类架构共12个数据集-架构组合上，RESYNC均以成对95%区间大于零的优势胜过最佳紧凑/蒸馏方案；锚点年龄4–12条观测时全部12组、最老至32条时11组显示实质教师优势；真实流上完整保留教师增益，合成流上较低且随锚点年龄总体下降。1 ms到达的实时回放中，同步大模型零按时预测，RESYNC在单CPU线程上按时服务≥99.4%观测，中位延迟0.35–0.62 ms，与紧凑模型相当或更快，比同步大模型推理快7.9–17.7倍。

Conclusion: RESYNC适用于有后台算力但单观测响应期限严格的场景，可同时获得大模型精度与紧凑模型级延迟。

Abstract: Predicting every observation in a high-rate stream requires a model to finish before the next arrival. A large model may offer better predictions but miss that deadline. We propose RESYNC: the large model runs asynchronously, while a fast path updates its latest exact but stale latent state using the observations received since that state was computed and applies its unchanged prediction head. The updater learns from the frozen model's representations without task labels. Across one real and two synthetic streams and five architecture families, RESYNC beats the best compact or distilled alternative with a paired 95% interval above zero in all twelve dataset--architecture pairs with a material teacher advantage at anchor ages of 4--12 observations, and in eleven pairs at older tested ages up to 32. The share of the teacher's gain preserved remains complete on the real stream but is lower and generally declines with anchor age on the synthetic streams. In live replay with 1 ms arrivals, the synchronous large models cannot deliver any predictions on time while RESYNC serves at least 99.4% of arrivals within the interval, with 0.35--0.62 ms median latency on one CPU thread: around the same speed as or faster than the compact model, and 7.9--17.7$\times$ faster than synchronous large-model inference. RESYNC targets settings with background compute available but a strict per-observation response deadline.

</details>


### [146] [HALight: Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Traffic Signal Control under Lane-Level Missing Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16574)
*Chenkai Hu,Yingqian Zhang,Ming Xie,Yi Fang,Chengxiang Jin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HALight，一个面向车道级观测缺失的通信增强异构多智能体强化学习交通信号控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制MARL方法依赖两个不现实假设：所有智能体同构（统一观测/动作空间）、且可获得完整可靠的车道级观测。实际路网存在路口结构异构（车道配置、相位设计不同）以及传感器故障、检测器覆盖不全导致的车道级观测缺失，损害协同与学习稳定性。

Method: 1）支持路口特定的观测与动作建模以适配异构路口；2）引入轻量级单跳邻域通信模块，在车道级观测部分缺失时补充空间上下文信息；3）采用序贯策略更新机制提升异构智能体间的协同。

Result: 在两个真实路网上的实验表明，HALight在交通效率与收敛性能上优于代表性的同构与异构基线方法。

Conclusion: HALight通过异构建模、邻域通信与序贯更新有效缓解结构异构与观测缺失问题，验证了其在真实交通信号控制场景中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning has shown great promise for traffic signal control. However, most existing methods rely on two key assumptions: (i) agents are assumed to be homogeneous with unified observation/action spaces; (ii) full and reliable lane-level traffic observations are available to all agents. In practice, urban road networks exhibit substantial structural heterogeneity (e.g., lane configurations and phase designs), while sensor failures and incomplete detector coverage often result in missing incoming lane measurements. Such issues can severely impair coordination and destabilize learning. To address these challenges, we propose HALight, a communication-enhanced heterogeneous multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for traffic signal control under lane-level missing observations. HALight supports intersection-specific observation and action modeling to accommodate heterogeneous intersections, and incorporates a lightweight one-hop neighborhood communication module to enrich spatial context when lane-level observations are partially unavailable. Moreover, HALight adopts a sequential policy update scheme to improve coordination among heterogeneous agents. Experimental results on two real-world road networks demonstrate that HALight outperforms representative homogeneous and heterogeneous baselines in terms of traffic efficiency and convergence behavior. The code and datasets used in this work are available at https://github.com/Huchenkaii/HALight.

</details>


### [147] [DySR-GNN: Dynamic Sensor-Relationship Graph Neural Network for Root Cause Analysis in Automotive HiL Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16623)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DySR-GNN，一种动态传感器关系图神经网络，用于汽车HiL多变量时间序列的监督式多标签根因识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiL系统中故障会跨多个相互作用的信号传播，导致"最异常传感器"并非真实故障源，根因分析困难。

Method: 每个传感器经共享时序编码器处理，并与可训练的传感器身份表示结合；对每个输入窗口独立重建稀疏关系图，用于图消息传递与根因位置直接预测，联合学习时序模式与跨传感器结构关系。

Result: 在记录不相交（recording-disjoint）评估协议下优于CNN-LSTM、静态GCN、静态GAT及上下文变体，记录级Macro-F1为0.9524，MCC为0.9302，严格完全匹配准确率为0.8519；学习到的图揭示传感器间存在持久性与状态依赖关系。

Conclusion: 在时序表示之外提取自适应关系结构，可提升复杂汽车信息物理系统中的多标签根因识别性能。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis in automotive Hardware-in-the-Loop (HiL) systems is challenging because faults can propagate across multiple interacting signals, making the most abnormal sensor different from the actual fault origin. This work proposes DySR-GNN, a Dynamic Sensor-Relationship Graph Neural Network for supervised multi-label root-cause identification in multivariate HiL time series. The approach jointly extracts temporal patterns within individual sensor signals and structural relationships across sensors. Each sensor is processed by a shared temporal encoder and combined with a trainable sensor-identity representation, from which a sparse relationship graph is reconstructed independently for every input window. This window-specific structure provides an explicit representation of the sensor relationships used for graph message passing and direct root-location prediction. Under a recording-disjoint evaluation protocol, the proposed model outperforms CNN--LSTM, Static GCN, Static GAT, and context-based variants, achieving a recording-level Macro-F1 of 0.9524, MCC of 0.9302, and strict exact-match accuracy of 0.8519. Analysis of the learned graphs reveals both persistent and state-dependent relationships among sensor variables. The results demonstrate that extracting adaptive relational structure alongside temporal representations can improve multi-label root-cause identification in complex automotive cyber--physical systems.

</details>


### [148] [Asymmetric Multimodal Learning with Decomposed JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16639)
*Yusuf Shihata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出跨模态对齐作为唯一目标在信息不对称时是有损的，提出Alignment Redundancy Bound证明仅对齐至多保留冗余信息原子；据此提出Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)，将对齐共享核心与私有模态纤维分解，避免语义崩塌并恢复多模态协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态对齐被视为构建多模态表示的标准目标，但在模态信息不对称（如VQA中问题是对稠密视觉场的选择性关系算子）时，对齐保留下来的并非任务所需信息，需要理论与方法上的修正。

Method: 证明Alignment Redundancy Bound：仅做对齐式Dirichlet能量最小化最多保留冗余信息原子Red(Z;X1,X2)，会湮灭私有模态结构Unq与跨模态协同Syn。提出D-JEPA，将潜变量分解为对齐的共享核心与私有的模态特有纤维，私纤维通过上下文掩码预测与非对称跨子空间独立性约束恢复被对齐丢弃的信息。

Result: 在信息不对称下，仅对齐训练在组合推理基准上发生灾难性语义崩塌，仅能在二值查询上存活；D-JEPA避免崩塌，显著优于仅对齐与对齐+重建基线，并将基线的倒置多模态协同差恢复为正向。信息论审计显示D-JEPA从冻结骨干中恢复大部分可用互信息，因果纤维交换干预证明恢复信息因果定位于私有纤维。

Conclusion: 对齐不应作为唯一目标；显式分解共享与私有子空间可保留对齐所丢弃的模态私有与协同信息，是实现有效多模态表示的必要设计原则。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is the standard objective for building multimodal representations. We show that alignment as a \emph{sole} objective is lossy wherever modalities are informationally asymmetric---where neither can express what the other carries. Visual question answering is an obvious case: a question is a selective relational operator probing a dense visual field whose fine-grained features no query recovers, so what alignment preserves is not what the task requires. We prove an \textbf{Alignment Redundancy Bound}: alignment-only Dirichlet energy minimization retains at most the redundant information atom $\Red(Z; X_1, X_2)$, annihilating private modality-specific structure $\Unq(Z; X_m)$ and cross-modal synergy $\Syn(Z; X_1, X_2)$.
  We introduce \textbf{Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)}, which factorizes latents into an aligned shared core and private modality-specific fibers. The shared core retains most of the structure alignment targets; the private fibers recover what alignment discards, via contextual masked prediction and asymmetric cross-subspace independence. Under information asymmetry, alignment-only training undergoes catastrophic semantic collapse on compositional reasoning benchmarks, surviving only on binary queries. D-JEPA averts this collapse, substantially improving downstream accuracy over both alignment-only and alignment-with-reconstruction baselines, and restoring a positive multimodal synergy gap in place of the baseline's inverted gap. Information-theoretic audits confirm that D-JEPA recovers most of the mutual information available from a frozen backbone, and causal fiber-swap interventions demonstrate that the recovered information is causally localized in the private fibers.

</details>


### [149] [Dataset Distillation via Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16642)
*George Cazenavette,Angelina Quan,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不依赖分类目标准则的数据集蒸馏方法：在特征空间中"漂移"合成样本，使其逼近各类真实数据分布，兼顾分布覆盖与分类可用性，并延伸到可视化与其他模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法大多以分类器为目标来优化合成数据，导致蒸馏样本主要适配某个特定分类器，而非真实数据分布本身，因而对数据分布的表征与可解释性不足。

Method: 在特征提取器的潜在空间中对蒸馏数据施加"漂移"：一方面将样本推向真实数据（分布匹配），另一方面令样本彼此远离（促进多样性），全程不使用分类损失；并基于此提出分布层面的评价指标，同时将该方法用于模型可视化并扩展至图像以外的模态。

Result: 漂移后的合成数据在覆盖真实分布方面显著优于现有方法，同时仍可作为强有效的分类训练样本；该框架还能作为解释模型的可视化工具，并适用于其他数据模态。

Conclusion: 该工作通过分布近似而非分类目标重构了数据集蒸馏范式，提出基于分布的互补评价指标，加深了对数据与模型交互关系的理解。

Abstract: The task of dataset distillation concerns the learning of a small set of synthetic samples that can serve as an effective replacement for a large real dataset when used for downstream tasks. While many solutions have been proposed since the problem's inception, most of them primarily seek to optimize the distilled data with respect to a classifier. In this work, we propose a new distillation method that learns a set of samples such that they directly approximate the per-class data distribution. Specifically, we "drift" our distilled data in the latent space of a feature extractor such that they move both toward the real data and away from each other without using a classification objective. We find that our drifted data cover the real distribution significantly better than existing methods while still serving as powerful training samples for classification. Furthermore, we showcase our method's use as a visualization tool for better interpreting models and extend it beyond images to other modalities. Our work advances the frontier of dataset distillation, offering a novel synthesis method and proposing distribution-based metrics as a complementary evaluation. More broadly, our findings help address the growing need to understand both data and models by uncovering more about how they interact.

</details>


### [150] [GNN-Enhanced TCN Algorithms for ECG Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16648)
*Angelica Simonetti,Ferdinando Zanchetta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将时间序列重构为有向图结构，融合TCN与GNN的几何深度学习模型用于心电图异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列传统上按序列建模，但可赋予有向图结构，从而引入图神经网络；现有TCN擅长局部时序特征，GNN擅长关系建模，二者能力此前未在该任务上有效结合。

Method: 构建新的处理流程：先把时间序列转换为有向图表示，再设计新颖的几何深度学习模型，将普通时序卷积网络（TCN）与图神经网络（GNN）的能力融合。

Result: 在心电图（ECG）信号的异常检测任务上应用该流程与模型（摘要未给出具体指标与对比结果）。

Conclusion: 时间序列的图结构视角可行，TCN+GNN融合的几何深度学习流程可用于ECG异常检测，为时序异常检测提供新范式。

Abstract: To a time series can be also given the structure of directed graph, opening the doors to the usage of graph neural networks (GNNs) in this context. In this paper we develop a new pipeline involving novel geometric deep learning models that merge the capabilities of ordinary TCNs with the ones of GNNs to solve an anomaly detection problem on electrocardiogram signals.

</details>


### [151] [Manifold Attracting Flow Matching: Physics-Constrained Sampling via Continuous Geometric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16677)
*Guanyu Chen,Jinchen Wang,Shengze Xu,Xingyu Ren,Tieyong Zeng,Antonios Armaou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Manifold Attracting Flow Matching（MAFM），通过将约束吸引力与模型引导的切向运动解耦的 ODE 采样动力学，在无需重训练的情况下生成满足等值约束的科学数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未见过的等值约束下生成科学数据时，需在满足给定约束的同时不破坏从数据中学到的物理结构。现有推理阶段方法依赖对预测端点反复离散修正来施加物理约束，这种离散修正会扭曲已学到的生成结构，并引入不稳定的反馈。

Method: 提出 MAFM，让一个持续存在的锚点沿 ODE 连续演化，其动力学在解析上将锚点驱动至约束流形。具体采用几何修正：保留模型引导的切向运动，同时规定法向运动，使约束违反量向流形收缩。该构造将模型跟踪与约束吸引分离，并对残差收缩与局部反馈稳定性给出理论保证；无需重训练底层生成模型。

Result: 在几何与偏微分方程（PDE）基准上，相比多种约束引导的基线变体，生成质量提升，同时保持较低的约束残差。

Conclusion: MAFM 提供了一个通过可控采样动力学协调已学物理结构与显式约束的灵活框架，无需重训练底层生成模型。

Abstract: Generating scientific data under previously unseen equality constraints requires satisfying the prescribed constraints without destroying the physical structure learned from data. Existing inference-time approaches often enforce physical constraints by repeatedly correcting predicted endpoints, but such discrete corrections can distort learned generative structure and introduce unstable feedback. In this work, we propose Manifold Attracting Flow Matching (MAFM), which continuously evolves a persistent anchor through an ODE whose dynamics drive it analytically toward the constraint manifold. Specifically, its geometric correction preserves model-guided tangent motion while prescribing the normal motion to contract constraint violations toward the manifold. This construction separates model tracking from constraint attraction and provides theoretical guarantees for residual contraction and local feedback stabilization. Experiments across geometric and partial differential equation benchmarks demonstrate improved generation quality over variant constraint-guided baselines while maintaining low constraint residuals. MAFM provides a flexible framework for coordinating learned physical structure and explicit constraints through controllable sampling dynamics, without retraining the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [152] [Edge-Gradient Augmentation under Reinforcement Learning for Temporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16678)
*Yongqing Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Wenjun Wang,Xinyu Wang,Wei Yu,Qiyao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EG-RL框架，显式编码边梯度事件（边诞生与消亡）并用强化学习动态选择信息量最大的边快照，提升时序链路预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间链路预测方法通常直接使用原始时序图数据，忽视了可显著提升预测性能的显式边变化（edge-change）信息，存在研究空白。

Method: EG-RL通过专门的节点编码与链接编码模块显式编码边梯度事件（边的诞生与消亡）；采用策略驱动的强化学习机制，在critic反馈引导下动态选择并增强最具信息量的边快照；模型包含节点编码、链接编码、token mixing、channel mixing等模块，经全局池化整合后接两层MLP分类器。

Result: 在多个动态图数据集上实验显示，AUC与平均精度（AP）均有显著提升；消融实验验证了自适应边梯度增强的必要性，以及所提编码与强化学习机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式利用边梯度事件并结合强化学习进行快照选择，可有效提升时序链路预测准确率，为动态图预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is critical for understand- ing dynamic interactions in evolving networks, informing appli- cations ranging from social network analysis to recommendation systems. Existing methods typically rely on raw temporal graph data, often neglecting explicit edge-change information that can significantly improve predictive performance. To address this gap, we propose edge-gradient reinforced learning (EG-RL), a framework that explicitly encodes edge-gradient events (edge births and deaths) through dedicated node and link encoding modules. EG-RL uses a policy-driven reinforcement-learning mechanism to dynamically select and augment the most infor- mative edge snapshots, guided by critic-based feedback to opti- mize prediction accuracy. The model comprises distinct modules for node encoding, link encoding, token mixing, and channel mixing, integrated through global pooling and followed by a two-layer multilayer perceptron classifier. Extensive experiments on multiple dynamic graph datasets demonstrate substantial gains in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve and average precision. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of adaptive edge-gradient augmentation and validate the effectiveness of the proposed encoding and reinforcement- learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [153] [From Data to Wisdom: Rethinking AI Through an Epistemic Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16726)
*Afonso Eduardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAAI（Data-to-Wisdom Epistemic Architecture for AI）概念框架，将数据、信息、知识、理解、智慧视为五种可递归交互的认知功能，并以贝叶斯解释、五类先验语境与三个应用案例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两点动机：一是传统知识金字塔（DIKW）的顺序式解读掩盖了各层功能之间的交互；二是对十本贝叶斯参考书的定向综述发现，先验分布有严格数学定义，而"先验信息"与"先验知识"未被一致地区分为认知类别。

Method: 构建概念性框架，把五种认知功能界定为观测、语境化解释、建模与预测、批判性评估、知情判断；将似然、先验、后验、后验预测、模型检验与决策分析映射到五功能上（不限于贝叶斯系统）；在认知意义上扩展"先验"，提出影响模型、似然、先验、评估与行动的五类先验语境；并用定量金融风险、健康险精算建模、医疗报销理赔智能体三个案例展示框架的分析路径。

Result: 三个案例表明EAAI可将模型假设分析、证据解释、验证、依赖关系与决策后果分离开来，即区分"风险预测""可靠性评估"与"行动证成"；并据此给出对AI/机器学习的启示：按功能进行评估、将中间模型输出视为可被质疑的证据、将生成与评估分离；同时与DIKW、贝叶斯工作流、AI评估与风险治理研究作了比较。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：一套含递归关系的认知词汇表、更易理解的贝叶斯方法解读、先验语境的类型学，以及更清晰的方法学术语。框架定位为概念性而非实证性，其价值在于组织与澄清分析，而非提供量化验证。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Data-to-Wisdom Epistemic Architecture for AI (EAAI), a conceptual framework treating Data, Information, Knowledge, Understanding and Wisdom as five distinct but recursively interacting epistemic functions: observation, contextual interpretation, modeling and prediction, critical evaluation and informed judgment. Two observations motivate it: sequential readings of the Knowledge Pyramid obscure how these functions interact, and a targeted review of ten Bayesian reference books finds that prior distributions are defined mathematically while "prior information" and "prior knowledge" are not consistently distinguished as epistemic categories. A Bayesian interpretation relates the likelihood, prior and posterior distributions, posterior prediction, model checking and decision analysis to the five functions, without limiting EAAI to Bayesian systems. Extending "prior" in an epistemic sense, the paper distinguishes five kinds of prior context that can shape the model, the likelihood, the prior, evaluation and action. Three worked applications -- quantitative financial risk, actuarial modeling in health insurance and agentic AI for medical reimbursement claims -- show how EAAI structures the analysis of model assumptions, evidence interpretation, validation, dependencies and decision consequences, separating the prediction of a risk, the appraisal of its reliability and the justification of action. It draws implications for AI and machine learning, including evaluation by function, treating intermediate model outputs as evidence open to challenge and separating generation from appraisal, and compares EAAI with work on DIKW, Bayesian workflow, AI evaluation and risk governance. Its contributions are an epistemic vocabulary with recursive relationships, a more accessible reading of Bayesian methodology, a typology of prior context and clearer methodological terminology.

</details>


### [154] [On the Difficulty of Sequence-Level Supervised Learning for Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16736)
*Mathieu Blondel,Michaël E. Sander,Germain Vivier-Ardisson,Tianlin Liu,Vincent Roulet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明将解码过程嵌入的两种"序列级损失"（贪心解码损失与采样损失）均无下界，并揭示二者通过 Gumbel 技巧相互关联；Stop-gradient 得到的"矩匹配"方向虽在函数空间是自回归 NLL 的有效下降方向，但方差过大，故作者结论：监督学习自回归模型，teacher forcing 或许"就够了"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在监督学习中通常以 teacher forcing 最小化逐 token 交叉熵，训练与推理（贪心解码/自回归采样）之间存在 exposure bias 的训练—使用不匹配问题。作者希望探究能否设计直接包含推理期解码过程的序列级损失函数来缓解该偏差。

Method: 1) 构造两种自然的序列级损失：贪心解码损失与基于采样的损失；2) 分析其优化性质，证明二者均无下界（unbounded below）；3) 借助自回归 Gumbel 技巧证明采样损失是贪心损失在 Gumbel 扰动下的期望；4) 将病态性归因于解码序列对模型参数的依赖，对采样过程施加 stop-gradient，得到"矩匹配"更新方向，并在函数空间中证明其为自回归负对数似然的有效下降方向；5) 分析该方向的方差。

Result: 两类显式嵌入解码过程的序列级损失均无下界，因而本质上不可优化；二者存在精确的 Gumbel 期望对应关系；stop-gradient 得到的矩匹配方向并非任何损失函数的梯度，但可证明在函数空间中是自回归 NLL 的合法下降方向；然而该方向方差很高，实践中不稳定、低效。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，试图把解码过程直接纳入监督损失的路线存在根本性缺陷，即便是修正后的矩匹配方向也因高方差而难以实用。因此对于自回归模型的监督学习，teacher forcing（逐 token 交叉熵）可能"就是全部所需"，即为简单有效且难以被序列级目标替代的基线。

Abstract: During the supervised learning phase, autoregressive language models are typically trained by minimizing the negative log-likelihood of the training data, which decomposes as a sum of token-level cross-entropies via teacher forcing. This objective is simple and efficient but potentially suffers from so-called exposure bias: a mismatch between the way the model is trained and the way it is used at inference time. Can we instead design sequence-level loss functions that incorporate inference-time decoding procedures, such as greedy decoding or autoregressive sampling? We show that two natural attempts lead to loss functions that are fundamentally flawed: unbounded below. We further show that these two flawed loss functions are intimately connected: using autoregressive Gumbel tricks, the sampling based loss is the expected greedy loss under Gumbel perturbations. Since the pathology stems from the dependence of the decoded sequence on the model parameters, we analyze the update direction obtained by applying a stop gradient on the sampling procedure, leading to a moment matching direction. While it is not the gradient of any loss function, we prove that it defines a valid descent direction in function space for the autoregressive negative log-likelihood. Despite these theoretical insights, the moment matching direction suffers from high variance. We conclude that teacher forcing may well be ``all you need'' for supervised learning of autoregressive models.

</details>


### [155] [Interpretable machine learning diagnosis of degradation mechanisms in lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells from temperature- and illumination-dependent electrical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16750)
*Nabanita Sarker,Hafiz Imtiaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用约4.2万次SCAPS-1D仿真，双向研究无铅RbSn0.5Ge0.5I3太阳能电池：正向用代理模型预测性能，逆向用多标签分类器识别退化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习优化薄膜太阳能电池几乎只做正向（参数→性能），而制造后识别限速机制的逆向问题仍依赖人工判读表征数据，缺乏自动化方法。

Method: 基于约42,000次SCAPS-1D仿真构建数据集；比较Spiro-OMeTAD与CuI空穴传输层；用因子设计网格训练XGBoost回归器并与拉丁超立方设计对比；用多标签分类器从模拟的温度/光照依赖Voc曲线识别六种退化机制，并与标定物理规则基线比较。

Result: CuI替代Spiro-OMeTAD使模拟PCE从19.6%升至21.6%，几乎全由填充因子贡献。因子网格训练的XGBoost在留出网格点R²=0.9997，但在独立采样器件上仅0.58；改用同规模拉丁超立方设计训练可恢复至R²=0.995。逆向分类器macro-F1=0.938，显著优于物理规则基线的0.687。

Conclusion: 因子设计网格训练会严重高估代理模型泛化能力，采样设计至关重要；数据驱动方法可高效逆向诊断退化机制，但对训练集中未出现的机制组合缺乏泛化。

Abstract: Machine learning coupled with device simulation is widely used to optimize thin-film solar cells, but almost exclusively in the forward direction, from device parameters to predicted performance. The inverse problem encountered after fabrication, namely identifying the mechanism that limits a given device, is still addressed by manual inspection of characterization data. In this work, both directions are examined for lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells using approximately 42,000 SCAPS-1D simulations. Replacing the Spiro-OMeTAD hole transport layer with CuI increases the simulated power conversion efficiency from 19.6\% to 21.6\%, almost entirely through the fill factor. Surrogate models trained and tested on a factorial parameter grid substantially overstate their predictive accuracy. An XGBoost regressor attains $R^2 = 0.9997$ on held-out grid points but only 0.58 on independently sampled devices, whereas training on a Latin hypercube design of comparable size restores $R^2 = 0.995$. For the inverse problem, a multi-label classifier identifies which of six degradation mechanisms are present from simulated temperature- and illumination-dependent open-circuit-voltage measurements. It achieves a macro $F_1$ score of 0.938, compared to 0.687 for a calibrated physics-rule baseline. The classifier does not, however, generalize to combinations of mechanisms absent from the training set.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Binary Classification: Multiclass Machine Learning for Diabetes and Prediabetes Risk Prediction Using NHANES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16756)
*Aman Mujahid,Anita Penkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于NHANES 15,356名成人数据，构建监督学习框架将人群分为正常、糖尿病前期、糖尿病三类，比较传统机器学习与Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期识别糖尿病风险对预防疾病进展至关重要，但现有机器学习方法多聚焦二分类，难以刻画糖尿病前期等中间风险状态。

Method: 采用NHANES中常规采集的人口学、临床与生活方式变量，以HbA1c仅用于定义标签并排除于输入特征以防泄漏；比较传统机器学习与Transformer模型，并设置两类二分类任务：正常vs糖尿病前期、正常vs（糖尿病前期+糖尿病）。在留出测试集上用precision、sensitivity、specificity、F1评估。

Result: 多分类任务中模型表现总体一致，准确率最高0.72，宏平均F1为0.70；糖尿病前期最难识别，传统模型F1为0.54–0.56，Transformer为0.53–0.55。正常vs糖尿病前期F1略升至0.62–0.64；合并糖尿病前期与糖尿病的筛查式任务F1提升至0.73–0.74。

Conclusion: 糖尿病前期检测的困难主要源于正常与糖尿病状态间生物连续体上的特征重叠，而非模型架构；多分类预测更具临床信息价值，但存在临床分层意义与预测精度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Detecting diabetes risk early is important for preventing disease progression. Most existing machine learning approaches focus on binary classification, which limits their ability to capture intermediate risk states such as prediabetes. We develop a supervised learning framework to classify adults as normal, prediabetic, or diabetic using National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey data. The framework uses routinely collected demographic, clinical, and lifestyle variables from 15,356 adults, with glycated hemoglobin (HbA1c) used only to define labels and excluded from input features to prevent leakage. We compare conventional machine learning and transformer-based models and examine two binary formulations: normal versus prediabetes, and normal versus combined prediabetes and diabetes. Models are evaluated on a held-out test set using precision, sensitivity, specificity, and F1 score. In the multiclass setting, performance is broadly consistent across models, with accuracy up to 0.72 and macro-averaged F1 of 0.70. Prediabetes remains the most challenging class, with F1 scores of 0.54 to 0.56 for conventional models and 0.53 to 0.55 for transformers, reflecting its overlap with both neighboring states. When the task is reduced to normal versus prediabetes, F1 scores rise modestly to 0.62 to 0.64. A more substantial improvement is observed under a broader screening formulation combining prediabetes and diabetes, with F1 scores of 0.73 to 0.74 across all models. These findings suggest that feature overlap along the biological continuum between normal and diabetic states contributes more to the difficulty of prediabetes detection than model architecture. Multiclass prediction provides a more clinically informative representation of metabolic risk progression but highlights a trade-off between clinically meaningful stratification and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [157] [Class-Competence Aggregation: Output-Layer Routing for Federated Multiclass DDoS Detection under Class-Concentrated Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16772)
*Tapu Datta,Hafiz Imtiaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示联邦入侵检测中"类集中"非IID数据会使FedAvg的输出层对应神经元因"反向信号"(active anti-signal)塌缩至零，并提出按类用客户端本地验证F1加权的类胜任度聚合(CCA)修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式入侵检测传感器场景天然产生类集中非IID数据(某攻击类仅由一个客户端持有)，现有FedAvg在此类数据上严重失效，且失效机制尚不明确。

Method: 先定位失效机制：输出层每类一个决策边界，当某类聚合权重几乎全来自从未见过该类的客户端时，其神经元收到反向信号而非单纯稀释，塌缩为零。提出Class-Competence Aggregation(CCA)：输出层按类聚合，权重为各客户端本地验证F1；隐藏层保持标准按样本量加权FedAvg。无需服务器辅助数据，每轮每类仅增加一个F1标量。

Result: CIC-DDoS2019上FedAvg使集中类LDAP的F1为0.00±0.00%；CCA单独将其提升至84.83±1.65%，完整方法宏F1达91.64±0.57%，联邦-集中差距从-42.53缩至-4.05点。与四种联邦方法在相同划分与种子下对比，CCA是唯一能实质且稳定恢复集中类的方法；小178倍的MLP呈现相同定性恢复(幅度较小)。三种子验证，并在CICIDS2017上复现。

Conclusion: 类集中非IID下的FedAvg失效可归因于输出层神经元的反向信号塌缩，按类用本地验证F1加权的CCA以极低通信开销有效恢复集中类性能，且跨架构规模与第二数据集均具一致性。

Abstract: Federated learning over distributed intrusion-detection sensors naturally produces \emph{class-concentrated} non-IID data, where an entire attack class is held by a single client and absent elsewhere. We show this collapses federated averaging in a specific, localized way: the output layer holds one decision boundary per class, and when a class's aggregation weight comes overwhelmingly from clients that have never seen it, its neuron receives an active anti-signal rather than mere dilution, collapsing to zero. On CIC-DDoS2019, standard FedAvg drives the concentrated LDAP class to $0.00 \pm 0.00\%$ F1 across every seed. We propose Class-Competence Aggregation (CCA), which aggregates the output layer per class using each client's own locally reported validation F1 as the weight, while hidden layers remain under standard size-weighted FedAvg. CCA requires no server-held auxiliary data and adds only one F1 scalar per class per round beyond the feature-scaling and class-count exchanges common to any federated pipeline. On our primary CNN-LSTM architecture, CCA alone raises LDAP F1 from $0\%$ to $84.83 \pm 1.65\%$, and the full method reaches $91.64 \pm 0.57\%$ macro F1, closing the federated-to-centralized gap from $-42.53$ to $-4.05$ points. Benchmarked against four other federated methods under identical partitions and seeds, CCA is the only one that recovers the concentrated class substantially and reliably; a $178\times$ smaller MLP shows the same qualitative recovery, though less completely, confirming the effect holds across architecture scale even as its magnitude varies. Results are validated across three seeds and replicated on a second dataset, CICIDS2017.

</details>


### [158] [ScrewSense: Mapping the Invisible - Anomaly Detection and Error Classification in Screwdriving Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16789)
*Tobias Lettner,Tatyana Ivanovska,Anwar Al Assadi,Christian Bergler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用深度学习方法对拧紧过程的多变量时间序列进行异常检测与错误分类，异常检测基于VQ-VAE嵌入空间分析，错误分类评估了四种模型架构，最高准确率达100%，并具备良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拧紧系统产生的过程数据日益增多，加之算力、存储与算法的进步，为人工智能应用提供条件。准确检测并分类拧紧错误对保证产品质量、避免昂贵返工、以及通过促进螺纹连接产品的维护、再利用和回收来支持可持续性至关重要。传统监测方法（如基于窗口的方法或包络曲线）能力有限，机器学习尤其是深度学习提供了有前景的替代方案。

Method: 数据语料为捕获扭矩、旋转角度等过程参数的多变量时间序列。异常检测方面，分析了向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）的嵌入空间；错误分类方面，评估并比较了四种不同的模型架构。

Result: 异常检测有效；深度学习模型能够区分多种错误类别，超越传统二分类，分类准确率最高达100%；并在真实数据上验证了模型鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所开发模型在工业拧紧过程中具有良好的实际应用潜力，可摆脱错误识别对专家知识的依赖，显著减少人工投入与分析时间。

Abstract: The growing volume of process data generated by screwdriving systems, combined with advancements in computing power, data storage, and algorithms, is facilitating the application of artificial intelligence in this field. Accurately detecting and classifying screwdriving errors is crucial for ensuring product quality, avoiding costly rework in manufacturing, and supporting sustainability by facilitating the maintenance, reuse, and recycling of screw-connected products. In this context, machine learning approaches offer a promising alternative to traditional monitoring techniques, such as window-based methods or envelope curves. This study examines the use of deep learning techniques for anomaly detection and error classification in screwdriving processes. The data corpus consists of multivariate time series capturing process parameters, such as torque and rotation angle. For anomaly detection, the embedding space of a vector quantized variational autoencoder was analyzed. In terms of error classification, four different model architectures were evaluated. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of anomaly detection and confirm that deep learning models can distinguish between multiple error categories beyond traditional binary classification. This extends the capabilities of conventional monitoring approaches, achieving accuracies of up to 100%. Additionally, real-world data material was examined in order to further demonstrate model robustness. Overall the results indicate strong generalization capability and highlight the practical potential of the developed models for use in industrial screwdriving processes, eliminating the need for expert knowledge in error identification and significantly reducing manual effort and analysis time.

</details>


### [159] [Detection Is Not Early Warning: Evaluating Token-Level Alarms on Language Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16803)
*Marco De Negri,Luca Sartori,Lorenzo Baggi,Moritz Reihs,Eduard Frank Ďurech,Mennatallah El-Assady,Valentina Pyatkin,Anna Hedström*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PREWARN评估协议，针对带有标注"起始点(onset)"的失败，同时评估监测器的分类、检测与预警三种能力，并以重复退化现象为案例揭示AUROC等响应级指标的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型失败往往始于某个特定token（onset）。训练一个监测器可以做事后分类、进行中检测或事前预警，但只有事前预警才能留出时间停止或纠正生成。然而监测器通常用AUROC等响应级指标评估，忽略了监测器何时发出警报，无法反映预警能力。

Method: 提出PREWARN协议：在验证集上按应用需求选定的错误率校准每个监测器的阈值，运行监测器，并相对于onset在分类、检测、预警三项任务上打分。案例研究：三个开源模型的重复退化，使用激活上的线性探针及六个未训练基线。

Result: 响应级指标在该失败上饱和，仅读取响应长度的规则几乎完美；AUROC排名相同的探针在PREWARN下预警表现差异巨大。两个模型上探针能在多数退化响应的onset前警告；低于探针的低秩适配器使onset前被标记的token比例翻倍以上（主要靠提前很久标出退化响应）；未训练基线很少比随机更频繁报警。

Conclusion: 响应级指标无法区分监测器的预警能力，PREWARN提供了更贴合应用需求的评估；线性探针具备有效的事前预警能力，作者公开了带校准阈值的探针。

Abstract: Many failures of language models begin at a specific token, the onset. A monitor that reads the model during generation can classify the finished response, detect the failure once it is under way, or warn before it begins, and only a warning leaves time to stop or steer the generation. Monitors are usually evaluated with response-level metrics such as AUROC, which ignore when the monitor fires. We introduce PREWARN, an evaluation protocol for any failure with an annotated onset. It calibrates each monitor's threshold on validation data to an error rate chosen for the application, runs the monitor, and scores its alarms relative to the onset on all three tasks. As a case study we apply it to repetitive degeneration in three open language models, with early warning as the main task, using linear probes on activations and six untrained baselines. Response-level metrics saturate on this failure, and a rule that reads only the response length scores almost perfectly, while probes that AUROC ranks as equal warn very differently under PREWARN. Probes warn before the onset on most degenerate responses of two of the three models, low-rank adapters below the probe more than double the share of tokens just before the onset that it flags, largely by flagging degenerate responses long before the onset, and no untrained baseline warns much more often than random alarms. We release the probes with calibrated thresholds at https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes

</details>


### [160] [Calibration and Conformal Prediction for Medical Question Answering in Arabic and English: Evidence from Two Non-Parallel Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16821)
*Mariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Jais-adapted-7B-chat在英/阿医学选择题上的校准与不确定性；原始概率普遍过度自信，单一温度缩放可消除82%–93%的校准误差，分裂保形预测名义覆盖率达标但集合偏大，top-2概率间隔具有区分正确性的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学考试题上仅看准确率无法反映答案何时可信，因此需要考察校准误差与不确定性度量在跨语言（英语/阿拉伯语）与跨基准设置下的表现。

Method: 在4-bit精度下评测两个开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Jais-adapted-7B-chat），数据为MedMCQA 2,816道英文题与MedAraBench 4,927道阿拉伯语多选题；计算准确率、期望校准误差（ECE）、温度缩放、分裂保形预测覆盖率与集合大小、top-2概率间隔AUROC，并做置信度筛选与选项先验分析。

Result: 准确率：Qwen英0.561/阿0.430，Jais英0.369/阿0.308；原始ECE 0.242–0.486，温度缩放消除82%–93%的校准误差；24个设置中分裂保形预测均达名义覆盖率（误差≤0.006），但90%覆盖率下集合平均含63%–89%选项；top-2间隔AUROC最佳0.734（Qwen英文）、最差0.579（Jais阿拉伯语）；仅答最自信的20%题使Qwen英文错误率由0.439降至0.098；Jais存在强选项C先验（英49%、阿60%），仅保留拉丁字母作答使该比例降至29%但准确率、ECE与AUROC基本不变。

Conclusion: 仅凭准确率不足以判断医学问答可靠性，温度缩放可有效改善校准，保形预测虽保证覆盖率但在低准确率下集合过宽而实用性受限；模型存在明显选项位置先验；因两基准在学科、难度与选项数上存在差异，作者不将跨语言差异归因于语言本身。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly tested on medical exam questions, but accuracy alone does not show when an answer can be trusted. I study the calibration and uncertainty of two open-weight models, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Jais-adapted-7B-chat (both run in 4-bit precision), on 2,816 English multiple-choice questions from MedMCQA and 4,927 Arabic multiple-choice questions from MedAraBench. Accuracy was 0.561 (English) and 0.430 (Arabic) for Qwen, and 0.369 and 0.308 for Jais. Raw option probabilities were overconfident in all four settings (expected calibration error 0.242 to 0.486), and a single temperature parameter removed 82% to 93% of that error. Split conformal prediction reached its nominal coverage in all 24 settings I tested (within 0.006), but the sets were large when accuracy was low: at 90% coverage, temperature-scaled sets contained on average 63% (Qwen, English), 78% (Qwen, Arabic), 86% (Jais, English) and 89% (Jais, Arabic) of the options. The margin between the top two option probabilities separated correct from incorrect answers best for Qwen on English (AUROC 0.734) and worst for Jais on Arabic (0.579). Answering only the 20% most confident questions lowered Qwen's English error from 0.439 to 0.098. Jais showed a strong prior for option C (49% of English and 60% of Arabic predictions). Scoring Latin letters only cut the Arabic share to 29% but left accuracy, raw ECE and AUROC nearly unchanged. Because the two benchmarks differ in subject, level and number of options, I do not attribute differences between languages to language itself. Code is available at https://github.com/Mariyaaleem/arabic-english-medqa-uncertainty.

</details>


### [161] [DPA: Decentralized Primal Averaging with Quasi-Global Momentum for Highly Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16871)
*Diyako Ghaderyan,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去中心化原始平均算法DPA及其变体DPA-1G，通过在稀疏通信图上结合原始平均与准全局动量缓冲，缓解数据异构导致的精度下降，给出收敛界与线性加速条件，并在GUT基准上超越DSGD、梯度跟踪、QG-DSGDm、动量跟踪和QG-GUTm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化训练可保持数据本地化并支持设备端学习，但实际场景中代理本地数据的异构性构成优化挑战，会严重降低测试精度，在稀疏通信图上尤为明显。作者希望借助近期集中式原始平均（centralized primal averaging）的进展，设计在去中心化设定下同样有效的算法。

Method: 提出Decentralized Primal Averaging (DPA)：在原始平均基础上引入局部"准全局"动量缓冲；维护优化、梯度评估与输出平均三条耦合序列，利用连续混合迭代构造网络感知的动量方向，且无需传输辅助跟踪变量。进一步提出DPA-1G变体，每次迭代仅一轮通信，单次迭代通信开销与DSGD类基线持平。针对光滑非凸目标建立收敛界，并给出在足够多迭代下随代理数线性加速的条件。

Result: 在GUT去中心化图像分类基准上，DPA在多个数据集、架构、拓扑和异构程度上优于DSGD、梯度跟踪、QG-DSGDm、动量跟踪及QG-GUTm；在稀疏环拓扑高异构场景收益最大。在相同总通信成本下，两种DPA变体在CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100（ResNet-20）上平均测试精度均高于最优基线。

Conclusion: DPA以原始平均+准全局动量、无辅助跟踪变量的设计，在保持DSGD级通信成本的同时显著提升异构与稀疏拓扑下的去中心化学习性能；其收敛性与线性加速条件得到理论支撑，实验验证其优越性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Decentralized training of deep learning models keeps training data local and supports on-device learning over networks. In realistic scenarios, heterogeneity across agents' local data poses an optimization challenge and can severely degrade test accuracy, especially on sparse communication graphs. Building on recent advances in centralized primal averaging, we propose Decentralized Primal Averaging (DPA), a decentralized learning algorithm that combines primal averaging with a local quasi-global momentum buffer. DPA maintains three coupled sequences for optimization, gradient evaluation, and output averaging, while using consecutive mixed iterates to construct a network-aware momentum direction without transmitting an auxiliary tracking variable. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish a convergence bound for DPA and identify conditions for linear speedup in the number of agents for sufficiently many iterations. We also introduce DPA-1G, a variant that uses one communication round per iteration and matches the per-iteration communication cost of DSGD-style baselines. Experiments following the GUT decentralized image-classification benchmark show that DPA compares favorably with DSGD, gradient tracking, QG-DSGDm, momentum tracking, and QG-GUTm across datasets, architectures, topologies, and heterogeneity levels, with the largest gains under high heterogeneity on sparse rings. At the same total communication cost, both DPA variants achieve higher mean test accuracy than the best baseline on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-20. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [162] [Sustainable Federated Fine-Tuning with LoRA: Environmental Impact and Performance Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16856)
*Pedro Augusto Londe Ribeiro,Rodrigo Moreira,Larissa Ferreira Rodrigues Moreira,Joberto S. B. Martins,Tereza C. Carvalho,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在A40、RTX 6000 Ada、A100上对联邦LoRA微调做2²析因实验，发现局部步数（10 vs 100）解释84.8–91.2%的能耗与碳排放变异，少步数可在精度相当下降低约6–8倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合参数高效微调可高效适配大语言模型，但其在不同硬件上的能耗与碳成本尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 在NVIDIA A40、RTX 6000 Ada、A100三类GPU上对联邦LoRA微调进行2²全因子设计实验，因子为模型规模（135M/360M）与本地步数（10/100）；用CodeCarbon追踪能耗并估算CO₂当量排放，并以描述性方差分配分析各因素贡献。

Result: 在所考察区间内，本地步数可解释84.8–91.2%的能耗与排放变异；在A40与RTX 6000 Ada上，较少本地步数的配置在精度相当的同时能耗降低约6–8倍，单位kWh精度更高。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定下，较小的本地训练预算有助于实现节能的联邦微调，为联邦PEFT的能效配置提供实证依据。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) enables efficient adaptation of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its energy and carbon costs across hardware remain poorly understood. We evaluated federated Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning on NVIDIA A40, RTX 6000 Ada, and A100 GPUs using a $2^2$ full factorial design with 135M/360M models and 10/100 local steps. CodeCarbon tracked energy and estimated CO$_2$-equivalent emissions. Descriptive allocation of variation assigned 84.8-91.2% of energy and emissions variation to local steps within these ranges. On A40 and RTX 6000 Ada, configurations with fewer steps achieved comparable accuracy with roughly 6-8-fold lower energy consumption and higher accuracy per kWh. These findings support smaller local budgets for energy-efficient federated fine-tuning within the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [163] [Introducing Feedback Delays to Non-Delayed Adversarial MDPs: Optimal $\sqrt S$ and $\sqrt K$ Dependencies in Tabular and Linear AMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17214)
*Aarush Kulkarni,Khang Nguyen,William Chang,Yan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"主动引入反馈延迟"的通用技术，配合几何重采样与拒绝（GRR）及访问概率阈值化，在表格与线性对抗性MDP中分别达到近乎最优的遗憾界，解决了长期悬而未决的样本复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知转移、bandit反馈的情景式对抗性MDP（AMDP）中，核心困难在于估计"变化策略"的Q函数：历史数据来自与当前策略不同且相关的旧策略，而若冻结策略以积累数据又会恶化对回合数K的依赖。表格情形下已知最优遗憾~O(HS√(AK))与下界Ω(H√(SAK))之间存在√S的差距；线性情形下五年改进后无模拟器的高效算法仍止步于~O(K^{3/4})。作者旨在统一解决这两类开放问题。

Method: 提出"引入反馈延迟"（introduced feedback delays）技术：用未来回合的数据估计当前策略的Q函数，同时保持策略持续更新，仿佛反馈被延迟。将通常由环境施加的延迟转为主动设计且不带来额外代价。为此设计带拒绝的几何重采样（GRR）以校正延迟期间策略变化带来的偏差，并对访问概率进行阈值化，避免稀有状态-动作对上出现过长延迟。该技术统一用于策略优化算法。

Result: 以高概率在表格AMDP中获得~O(poly(H)√(SAK))遗憾，消除√S差距、解决开放问题；在线性AMDP中获得~O(poly(d,H)√K)遗憾，为首个在K上最优的高效算法。方法还自然推广到总反馈延迟为𝔇的延迟AMDP，分别得到表格情形在S与𝔇上最优、线性情形在K与𝔇上最优的首个界。

Conclusion: 将"反馈延迟"由被动障碍转化为主动设计工具，是一种通用且无额外代价的分析与算法框架，可在对抗性MDP中实现接近信息论下界的最优遗憾，并对延迟反馈设定具良好可扩展性。

Abstract: We study regret minimization in episodic Adversarial Markov Decision Processes (AMDPs) with unknown transitions and bandit feedback. In tabular AMDPs with $S$ states, $A$ actions, and horizon $H$, the best known $K$-episode regret is $\tilde O(HS\sqrt{AK})$, whose $\sqrt S$ gap to the $Ω(H\sqrt{SAK})$ lower bound has long remained open. In linear AMDPs with $d$-dimensional features, after a five-year line of improvements starting from $K^{14/15}$, computationally efficient algorithms without a simulator or exploratory assumptions still only attain $\tilde O(K^{3/4})$ regret.
  A common challenge in both settings is to estimate the Q-function of a changing policy: past data comes from earlier policies that differ from (and are correlated with) the current one, while freezing the policy for future episodes worsens the dependency on $K$. We instead propose a general technique called introduced feedback delays: we estimate the Q-function of the policy using future episodes while still updating the policy, as if the feedback were delayed. While feedback delays are usually an obstacle imposed by the environment, here they are introduced voluntarily and, perhaps surprisingly, come at no extra cost. We develop Geometric Resampling with Rejection (GRR) to account for policy changes during the delay, and threshold the visitation probabilities to avoid long delays on rarely visited state-action pairs.
  With this unified technique, our policy optimization algorithms achieve, with high probability, $\tilde O(\text{poly}(H)\sqrt{SAK})$ regret in tabular AMDPs, resolving the open problem; and $\tilde O(\text{poly}(d,H)\sqrt K)$ regret in linear AMDPs, giving the first efficient algorithm optimal in $K$. They also naturally extend to delayed AMDPs with a total feedback delay $\mathfrak D$, yielding the first bounds optimal in $S$ and $\mathfrak D$ (tabular) and in $K$ and $\mathfrak D$ (linear).

</details>


### [164] [Factored One-sided Adam-Moment for Practical and Scalable SOAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16905)
*Jaemyung Yu,Byeongho Heo,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOAM 是一种轻量级 SOAP 变体，通过仅输入侧预条件（去掉输出侧 Kronecker 因子）与行列统计的分解 Adam 矩估计，在保留 SOAP 收敛收益的同时达到 Muon 级实际耗时、半量优化器状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化器（Shampoo、SOAP）在 Transformer 训练中每步收敛更快，但仍存在优化器状态显存与特征分解开销大的问题。作者追问：能否在接近 AdamW 效率的前提下保留 SOAP 的收益。

Method: 提出 FOAM：(1) 仅对输入侧做预条件，丢弃输出侧 Kronecker 因子，减少内存并省去一次特征分解；依据 Hessian 的 Kronecker 结构，在输出侧曲率为对角时该做法精确、近对角时近似无损（经验上成立）。(2) 用基于行-列统计的分解式 Adam 矩估计替代 SOAP 的稠密旋转二阶矩；在 K-FAC Fisher 假设加对角输出曲率下可精确还原 SOAP 的稠密二阶矩，实测 K-FAC 违背仅 15–20% 且对更新影响可忽略。

Result: 在 124M–760M 参数 LLM 预训练中，FOAM 的验证损失与强 SOAP、Muon 基线相当；优化器状态为 SOAP 的一半，实际耗时与 Muon 同级，且在 3 倍学习率范围内保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: SOAP 的收益不必以其代价为前提，FOAM 提供了一个在效果、显存与速度间取得实用平衡的二阶优化器方案。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers (e.g., Shampoo, SOAP) have emerged as compelling alternatives to Adam/AdamW and promise faster per-step convergence than AdamW in transformer training. However, they still remain costly in practice, and even SOAP still pays substantial optimizer-state memory and eigendecomposition overhead. We ask whether the benefits of SOAP can be retained at nearly the efficiency of AdamW. In this paper, we answer yes with a lightweight SOAP variant, Factored One-sided Adam-Moment (FOAM). The path to practical impact is twofold: first, FOAM employs input-only preconditioning, dropping the output-side Kronecker factor to reduce memory and remove one eigendecomposition. The Kronecker structure of the Hessian shows that this is exact for a diagonal output-side curvature and nearly lossless for a near-diagonal one, as we observe empirically. Second, FOAM replaces SOAP's dense rotated second moment with a factored Adam-moment estimator based on row-by-column statistics. Under the K-FAC Fisher assumption with a diagonal output curvature, this estimator exactly recovers SOAP's dense second moment, and we measure only a 15-20% K-FAC violation, with negligible effect on the update. Our empirical results support the claim that FOAM matches strong SOAP and Muon baselines in validation loss across scales of large language model (LLM) pre-training, uses half of SOAP's optimizer state, runs at Muon-class wall-clock time, and retains robustness across a three-fold learning-rate range. The results hold from 124M to 760M parameters, demonstrating that SOAP's gains need not require SOAP's cost. Code: https://github.com/naver-ai/foam

</details>


### [165] [Metal Sandbox: Isolated Execution and Command Scheduling for GPU Coding Agents on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17294)
*Haozhe Wang,Lina Chen,Fanqi Wan,Qi Han,Yinmin Zhang,Zheng Ge,Daxin Jiang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Metal Sandbox：面向 Apple Metal 内核生成模型的端侧推理/训练基础设施，通过受限文件访问、原生命令执行与跨 Mac 命令级调度，提升 GPU 利用率与命令吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧推理需要高效利用本地加速器的内核；训练与评估 Metal 内核生成模型依赖 macOS 原生工具与并发 agent 会话。但会话在模型请求、开发命令与 GPU 执行之间交替，导致 rollout 期间 GPU 容量大量空闲。

Method: 提出 Metal Sandbox：为每实例提供受限文件访问、原生命令执行，并在互联 Mac 间做命令级调度。每个 agent 保有独立工作区与私有开发环境；并行命令可并发执行，串行命令每节点共享一个执行槽，符合条件等待命令可迁移至空闲 Mac 并将结果回传原 agent。与 Harbor 集成支持任务执行、自动验证与 rollout 收集。

Result: 四台 Mac mini M4 节点受控实验中，集中需求下命令吞吐提升 1.68×–2.05×；16 条静态 agent 轨迹估算，每节点 4 会话相对 1 会话持续吞吐提升约 2.14×（假设会话持续补足且平均时长不变）；动态调度下重放实测轨迹，平均工具等待降低 18.8%，批次耗时降低 22.3%（假设阶段时长不变）。

Conclusion: 命令级跨节点调度可有效填补 agent 会话中 GPU 空闲，显著提高吞吐并降低等待与批次完成时间；Metal Sandbox 与 Harbor 集成已开源。

Abstract: On-device inference requires kernels that make effective use of local accelerators. Efficient training and evaluation of models that generate Apple Metal kernels benefit from native macOS tools and concurrent agent sessions. These sessions alternate between model requests, development commands, and GPU execution, leaving GPU capacity idle for much of a rollout. We present Metal Sandbox, infrastructure that combines restricted per-instance file access, native command execution, and command-level scheduling across interconnected Macs. Each agent retains its workspace and private development environment. Parallel commands can run concurrently, while serial commands share one execution slot per node; eligible waiting commands can migrate to available Macs and return results to the original agent. Integration with Harbor supports task execution, automatic verification, and rollout collection through a common workflow. Controlled experiments on four Mac mini M4 nodes achieve $1.68\times$--$2.05\times$ gains in command throughput under concentrated demand. Sixteen static agent traces give an estimated $2.14\times$ sustained-throughput gain for four sessions per node versus one, assuming continuously replenished sessions and unchanged mean durations. Replaying these measured traces under dynamic scheduling reduces mean tool waiting by 18.8% and batch elapsed time by 22.3%, assuming unchanged phase durations. Metal Sandbox and its Harbor integration are open-source on GitHub.

</details>


### [166] [Time Flies When You Parallelise: Pareto-efficient Time Series Foundation Models with Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16934)
*Torben Berndt,Lingyi Yang,Federico Nardi,Yi Heng Lim,AJ Shears,Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Mamba-3的时间序列基础模型ci-proto，通过线性状态循环与分块并行训练降低计算开销，在fev-bench的100项任务上刷新SOTA，并占据性能-效率的完整帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型零样本预测表现优异，但计算效率是核心架构瓶颈：时间自注意力对时序token数量呈二次复杂度，而带非线性状态转移的循环结构又阻碍沿时间维的并行计算。

Method: 构建ci-proto模型族，以Mamba-3的线性状态循环为基础，训练时支持分块（chunk-wise）时间并行计算，推理成本随序列长度线性增长；架构交替进行循环式时序处理与跨变量（cross-variate）注意力，支持含past-only与future-known协变量的多变量概率预测。

Result: 在fev-bench的100个预测任务上，ci-proto XL在时间序列基础模型中达到新的SOTA；五个ci-proto变体在MASE、MSQL与参数量、中位运行时间两个维度上共同构成完整的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 线性状态空间循环与跨变量注意力的交替组合可在保持多变量概率预测能力的同时兼顾精度与效率，为时间序列基础模型提供可扩展的高效架构范式。

Abstract: Time series foundation models have demonstrated strong zero-shot forecasting performance, but computational efficiency remains a central architectural challenge. Temporal self-attention incurs quadratic cost in the number of temporal tokens, while nonlinear recurrent state transitions restrict parallel computation along the temporal dimension. We introduce ci-proto, a family of time series foundation models based on Mamba-3, whose linear state recurrences support chunk-wise parallel-in-time computation during training, and inference costs that scale linearly with sequence length. The architecture alternates recurrent temporal processing with cross-variate attention, supporting multivariate probabilistic forecasting with past-only and future-known covariates. Across fev-bench's 100 forecasting tasks, ci-proto XL sets a new state of the art among time series foundation models. The five ci-proto variants also make up the entire Pareto frontier for mean scaled quantile loss and mean absolute scaled error against both parameter count and median runtime.

</details>


### [167] [Temporal Regularized Learning: Self-Supervised Learning Local in Space and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17019)
*Davide Wiest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeReL提出一种空间与时间双重局部、单样本在线更新的自监督表征学习规则，在小网络上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐层自监督目标虽消除了层间反向传播，但仍需对一批样本计算损失，因此仅在空间上局部、在时间上不局部（需批处理）。作者希望得到同时具备空间与时间局部性的在线学习规则，用于序列输入流的自监督表征学习。

Method: 核心是将VICReg风格目标实例化为时间相邻正样本对加不可微的运行总体统计量，得到每神经元在线、状态有界的损失；该目标是Slow Feature Analysis（SFA）的软化形式，其梯度由前馈权重的Hebbian更新和每层一个侧向矩阵的anti-Hebbian更新组成，实现batch size为1的逐样本更新。

Result: 在按类别分块的MNIST流上，两层全连接网络以batch size=1训练，线性探针精度96.48%，优于随机特征（95.09%）与经典SFA；放宽两种局部性约束的变体性能可与有监督反向传播相当。

Conclusion: 实验目的在于验证该规则在小网络中可行，而非与反向传播竞争；由于训练与推理使用相同计算，网络可在推理期间从无标签数据持续学习而不损失精度。

Abstract: Layer-local self-supervised objectives remove the backward pass between layers, but almost all still compute their loss over a batch of samples and are therefore local in space but not in time. We introduce Temporal Regularized Learning (TeReL), a spatially and temporally local learning rule for self-supervised representation learning from sequential input streams. The key idea is to instantiate a VICReg-style objective with temporally adjacent positives and non-differentiated running population statistics, yielding a per-neuron online loss with bounded state. The objective is a soft form of Slow Feature Analysis, and its gradient is a Hebbian update of the feedforward weights and an anti-Hebbian update of one lateral matrix per layer. The aim of the experiments is to show that the rule works in a small network, not to compete with backpropagation. On a class-chunked MNIST stream, a two-layer fully connected network trained with batch size one outperforms random features (96.48% versus 95.09% linear-probe accuracy) and classical SFA, and a variant that relaxes both locality constraints is comparable to supervised backpropagation. Because training and inference use the same computation, the network can keep learning from unlabeled data during inference without loss of accuracy.

</details>


### [168] [Capacity-Constrained Delayed Feedback for Online Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17059)
*Bowen Wang,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出容量感知在线保形风险控制(CA-OCRC)，在反馈延迟且跟踪容量受限下通过随机跟踪策略与逆概率加权构建预测集，并优化代理延迟分布获得与无限反馈OCRC相同的长期风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线保形风险控制(OCRC)假设每轮损失即时且无损地反馈；实际中反馈存在延迟，且有限资源无法跟踪全部待定预测，导致部分反馈丢失，使长期风险保证失效。

Method: 提出CA-OCRC框架，联合决策有限反馈跟踪容量的分配与预测集构建。核心是基于随机跟踪策略采样代理延迟，决定是否跟踪当前轮反馈及跟踪时长；采用逆概率加权(inverse probability weighting)更新预测集，以补偿因延迟与容量限制造成的反馈丢失。分别对任意延迟与随机延迟推导风险界，揭示"获取更多反馈"与"避免容量饱和"之间的权衡，并据此优化代理延迟分布，给出保证与无限反馈OCRC同等长期风险所需的跟踪容量缩放律。

Result: 在医学影像、自然语言处理、计算机视觉与无线通信任务上的实验表明，CA-OCRC相较现有OCRC机制更为有效。

Conclusion: CA-OCRC为延迟反馈与容量受限场景下的可靠不确定性量化提供了具理论风险保证的统一框架，代理延迟分布优化可给出容量缩放律，实验验证了其跨领域有效性。

Abstract: Online conformal risk control (OCRC) enables reliable uncertainty quantification in machine learning by converting model outputs into prediction sets with long-term risk guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption that the loss of each round is revealed immediately and losslessly. In practice, feedback may be delayed, and limited resources may prevent tracking all pending predictions, causing some feedback to be lost. To address this challenge, we develop capacity-aware OCRC (CA-OCRC), a framework that jointly determines how to allocate limited tracking feedback capacity and how to construct prediction sets. CA-OCRC is based on a randomized tracking policy that samples a proxy delay to decide whether, and for how long, feedback for the current round is tracked. CA-OCRC updates the prediction sets via inverse probability weighting, compensating for the possibility of feedback lost due to delays and limited tracking capacity. We analyze risk bounds for both arbitrary and stochastic delays, revealing a trade-off between receiving more feedback and avoiding capacity saturation. Building on these bounds, we optimize the proxy delay distribution, showing scaling laws for the tracking capacity that ensure the same long-term risk as OCRC with unlimited feedback. Experiments on medical imaging, natural language processing, computer vision, and wireless communications show the effectiveness of CA-OCRC as compared to existing OCRC mechanisms.

</details>


### [169] [LazyWeights: Decode-on-Demand Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17108)
*Arnab Sanyal,Gourav Datta,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LazyWeights 保留 GPTQ/AWQ 等部署格式，通过调整 GPTQ 使量化码更易压缩、在交织通道上无损 Huffman 编码并在 DRAM 与 L2 之间按需解码，实现权重压缩、降低边缘 LLM 时延与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上 LLM 的可行性与速度受内存约束：4 GB Nano 受容量限制、8 GB Orin Nano Super 受带宽限制。现有部署格式（GPTQ、AWQ、llama.cpp 的 K/I-quant）对矩阵中每个整数码分配相同比特，但码分布高度非均匀，存在压缩空间。

Method: 对 GPTQ 进行调优使量化码可压缩；在交织 lane 上对码做无损 Huffman 编码；在 DRAM 与 L2 之间设置小型解码单元按需解码，使 GPU kernel 无需改动、DRAM 仅传输编码字节。另设计位精确的 0.21 mm² ASIC 解码单元（1 GHz、7 nm），并在 Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA（AMD ZCU102）上验证。

Result: 在 1% 与 3.5% 困惑度预算下，编码后的 Phi-3-mini、Mistral-7B、SmolLM-1.7B 比最优 llama.cpp 格式小 5.7–31.4%。ASIC 解码单元使 Orin 每 token 时延与能耗最多降低 36%/38%（结合 prompt-lookup 推测后为 54%/44%）。Nano 上相对 CPU llama.cpp 每 token 时间与能耗分别减少 58–64% 与 53–57%，并以 3.89 bit/权重将流式 Mistral-7B 加速 6.6×。相同吞吐下 Huffman 解码器在编码率与面积上优于 rANS。

Conclusion: 基于 Huffman 编码的轻量在线解码方案在保持原有部署格式与 kernel 不变的前提下，可有效缓解边缘设备的内存容量与带宽瓶颈，并在 ASIC/FPGA 上位精确实现，优于 rANS 方案。

Abstract: Memory decides which LLMs an edge device runs, and how fast: capacity on a $4$ GB Jetson Nano, bandwidth on an $8$ GB Jetson Orin Nano Super. Deployed formats (GPTQ, AWQ, $\texttt{llama.cpp}$'s K- and I-quants) spend equal bits on every integer code of a matrix, although the codes are far from uniform. $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ keeps these formats but tunes GPTQ so the codes compress, Huffman-codes them losslessly in interleaved lanes, and decodes them on demand in a small unit between DRAM and L2, so GPU kernels run unchanged and DRAM moves only coded bytes. At $1\%$ and $3.5\%$ perplexity budgets, coded Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B and SmolLM-1.7B are $5.7-31.4\%$ smaller than the best $\texttt{llama.cpp}$ format. A bit-exact $0.21$ mm$^2$ ASIC decoder unit ($1$ GHz, $7$ nm) cuts Orin latency and energy per token by up to $36\%$ and $38\%$ ($54\%$ and $44\%$ with prompt-lookup speculation). On the Nano, $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ needs $58-64\%$ less time and $53-57\%$ less energy per token than CPU $\texttt{llama.cpp}$, and runs a streamed Mistral-7B 6.6$\times$ faster at $3.89$ bits per weight. A Huffman decoder beats an rANS decoder in rate and area at equal throughput; the ASIC decoder unit runs bit-exact on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA (AMD ZCU102).

</details>


### [170] [Near-Optimal Regret for Oracle-Efficient Adversarial Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17109)
*Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于泊松辅助上下文扰动的oracle高效上下文赌博机算法,达到$O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\log(2KT))$期望遗憾,消除先前$T^{2/3}$的horizon依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前oracle高效方法对horizon的依赖为$T^{2/3}$,次优。本文旨在达到该设定下的最优遗憾速率(仅差对数因子),并保持仅依赖离线代价敏感优化oracle的高效性。

Method: 利用辅助上下文的泊松采样对策略分数施加扰动:每个采样上下文下,动作的扰动为拉普拉斯噪声乘以该上下文-动作专属尺度;通过最小化由历史观测确定的凸目标来选择尺度。进一步用投影随机梯度下降近似最优尺度,仅需返回最优值的精确离线代价敏感优化oracle;因尺度表巨大,隐式表示SGD迭代(存储决定更新的随机抽样),仅在查询上下文处评估尺度。

Result: 在事先固定的损失序列下,对实现上下文上事后最优策略取得期望遗憾$O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\log(2KT))$,达到最优速率(至对数因子);oracle复杂度及其余资源界均为$T$、$K$、$\log(2N)$的多项式。

Conclusion: 泊松辅助上下文加拉普拉斯尺度扰动框架改进oracle高效上下文赌博机遗憾上界,并提供可实现的隐式SGD方案,保持多项式资源与oracle复杂度。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits with $K$ actions, a class of $N$ policies, and i.i.d.\ contexts from a distribution that the learner can sample. For any sequence of loss functions fixed before play, we give an oracle-efficient algorithm with expected regret $O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\,\log(2KT))$ against the best policy in hindsight on the realized contexts. This improves the $T^{2/3}$ dependence on the horizon in prior oracle-efficient bounds and achieves the optimal regret rate in this setting up to logarithmic factors.
  Our algorithm perturbs policy scores using a Poisson sample of auxiliary contexts. At each sampled context, the perturbation assigned to an action is a Laplace noise draw multiplied by a scale specific to that context and action. We show that choosing these scales to minimize a convex objective determined by past observations yields the stated regret bound. We further approximate the minimizing scales by projected stochastic gradient descent using only an exact offline cost-sensitive optimization oracle that returns the optimum value. Since the table of scales can be large, the algorithm represents the SGD iterates implicitly by storing the random draws that determine each update and evaluates the scales only at queried contexts. Both the oracle complexity and the remaining resource bounds are polynomial in $T$, $K$, and $\log(2N)$.

</details>


### [171] [Bellman Contraction under MMD: A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17116)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在MMD框架下研究分布式贝尔曼算子的压缩性：将压缩性归约为"贝尔曼差分核"的条件正定性，用Schoenberg定理与Bernstein函数刻画径向核的判据，发现11个新核，提出chaining构造与局部压缩理论，并揭示压缩率与判别力的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式强化学习中贝尔曼算子是否在MMD度量下构成压缩映射，直接决定分布式动态规划/时序差分方法的收敛性。已有结果零散（如Gaussian RBF、power-distance、multiquadric核），缺乏统一、可在任意维度验证的判据，且实践中RBF核虽无全局压缩性却仍被广泛使用，理论与实践的矛盾亟待解释。
