Method: 构造由基核导出的"贝尔曼差分核"，将压缩性等价转化为该核的条件正定性；对径向核利用Schoenberg定理把条件正定性条件转化为Bernstein函数性质，得到任意维度下可解析验证或数值检验的判据；提出chaining通用构造以生成任意指定压缩因子的核；给出仅需在状态集合上条件正定即可保证局部压缩的结论；并分析压缩率与判别力的关系。

Result: 框架统一了Gaussian RBF、power-distance与multiquadric核的已知结论；数值搜索新发现11个可诱导压缩的核；chaining构造可产生任意预设压缩因子的核；证明压缩性只需在相关集合上成立的条件正定性，从而解释有界奖励下RBF核仍可作为有效选择；识别出压缩率与判别力之间的本质权衡——压缩越快判别力越弱。

Conclusion: 以贝尔曼差分核的条件正定性为核心，给出了MMD下分布式贝尔曼算子压缩性的统一理论工具，兼具解析与数值可验证性；局部压缩视角调和了理论严谨性与实际核选择；压缩率与判别力的权衡为核设计提供指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce a mathematical framework for studying contraction of the distributional Bellman operator under Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD). We show that contraction reduces to conditional positive definiteness of an associated kernel constructed from the base kernel, which we call the Bellman difference kernel. Focusing on radial kernels, we use Schoenberg's theorem to connect this condition to Bernstein functions. This gives contraction criteria that hold in any dimension and can be verified analytically or tested numerically. Our framework unifies known results for the Gaussian RBF, power-distance, and multiquadric kernels. A numerical search identifies 11 new kernels that induce contraction. We also propose chaining, a general construction that produces kernels inducing contraction with any prescribed factor. To reconcile theory with practice, we show that contraction on a set only requires conditional positive definiteness on that set. This explains why the RBF kernel can remain a valid choice when rewards are bounded, despite its lack of global contraction. Finally, we identify a fundamental tradeoff between contraction rate and discriminative power, where kernels that contract faster tend to discriminate less between distributions. Code is available at https://github.com/BaptisteDebes/BellmanContractionMMD

</details>


### [172] [Using an energy pixels approach to estimate the size a 2nd Generation DEL must be to train a high-resolution binding model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17121)
*Boris P. Belotserkovskii,R. Edward Watts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"像素-反像素"结合模型的解析方法，可在无结构信息、未合成任何化合物的条件下，由朴素DEL的亲和力分布估算第二代DEL所需复杂度，并将模型所需紧密结合物数目转化为库中化合物数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEL用于发现靶点小分子结合物，"找hit"已不再是难点；真正的难点是构建能分辨单原子微调所致亲和力变化的机器学习模型。这要求训练数据同时具备两个性质：对hit附近化学空间小区域的稠密采样，以及足够数量的真正高亲和力结合物。多样化朴素DEL空间巨大、单一邻域覆盖过薄且tight binder稀少，导致模型必须外推到训练集之外更紧的结合物，易失败。本文因此研究围绕hit构建的第二代DEL需要多高的复杂度。

Method: 将结合视为若干小而独立相互作用的加和，称为靶点上的"像素"（pixels）与配体上的"反像素"（antipixels）。以朴素库中实测的亲和力分布为输入，不依赖结构信息、无需先合成化合物，据此估计第二代DEL为提供稠密采样与足够tight binder所需的复杂度。该框架实现两件事：把模型所需的tight binder数量转换为库应包含的化合物数量；并估计随机设计第二代DEL的期望产率，从而为机器学习/主动学习设计库带来的增益提供可量化的基线。

Result: 该方法给出了第二代DEL复杂度的估计；提供了从"所需紧结合物数目"到"所需库规模（化合物数）"的换算；并给出随机设计第二代DEL期望产率的估计，可用于与ML/主动学习设计做基线比较，且无需构建与实验本身同等规模的大型对照库。

Conclusion: 围绕hit构建第二代DEL是同时获得稠密邻域采样与充足tight binder的有效途径；其所需复杂度可由朴素库亲和力分布解析估算。该框架既能把建模需求转化为库规模，又能低成本地量化主动学习/ML设计的相对收益。

Abstract: DNA-encoded libraries (DELs) are commonly used to find small-molecule binders to a target protein. But the hard part is no longer finding a hit. It is building a machine-learning model good enough to guide the medicinal chemistry that follows, one that can resolve the shift in affinity when a chemist changes a single atom. Such a model needs two things from its training data: dense sampling of the small patch of chemical space around the hit, and an adequate number of genuinely tight binders within it. A diverse naive DEL gives neither. It samples an enormous space, so any one neighborhood is covered far too thinly, and the tightest binders are rare. A model trained on that naive DEL data must extrapolate to binders tighter than any it has seen, and that is where models can fail. One way to get training data with both properties is a second-generation DEL built around the hit. We ask how complex that library must be. We treat binding as a sum of small, independent interactions, called pixels on the target and antipixels on the ligand. From the spread of affinities measured in the naive library, with no structural information and nothing yet synthesized, we estimate the complexity such a library must have to supply that dense sampling and those tight binders. The method does two useful things. It turns the number of tight binders a model needs into the number of compounds a library must contain. And it estimates the expected yield of a randomly designed second-generation DEL, so the gain from designing the library with machine learning or active learning can be measured against a baseline, without the expense of a control library as large as the experiment itself.

</details>


### [173] [Paper-Faithful Federated Learning: Scientific Checks for Practitioner and Agent Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17129)
*Ali Dadras,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBrew 是一个面向可控、可复现联邦学习实验的研究代码库，通过对既定实验协议条件进行检查（配置、客户端数据划分、聚合权重、数值与断点恢复等），并以自描述的运行记录（run records）支撑可检视的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习实验即便实现偏离原定实验协议，也常常输出看似合理的结果：配置、数据划分、聚合、随机性、评估或检查点恢复中的小错误不会导致程序崩溃，却可能悄然影响方法间比较。因此需要一个能主动检验"实验是否按协议执行"的受控模拟框架。

Method: FedBrew 以受控模拟为核心，引入多层次检查机制：(1) 配置检查；(2) 可标识的客户端数据划分，用于验证各客户端分片互不相交且覆盖完整（disjoint and exhaustive）；(3) 聚合权重与规定规则一致性检查；(4) 检查点恢复检查与中断运行处理；(5) 对客户端模型状态是否出现非有限值（non-finite）的数值检查；(6) 自描述的运行记录（self-describing run records）；(7) 开发期的科学条件检查。同时提供清晰的扩展点与自动检查，便于研究人员或 AI 编码代理新增算法、数据集、模型与任务。

Result: 论文为系统/工具型工作，主要成果是 FedBrew 框架本身及其检查与记录能力：能够自动暴露那些不会引发程序报错、但会破坏实验协议的条件违背，并借助运行记录将报告结果与具体运行配置对应起来，从而提升联邦学习实验的可复现性与可审查性。

Conclusion: 通过对软件条件与科学条件的显式检查和与之关联的运行记录，FedBrew 支持可复现、可检视的联邦学习实验；其结构化扩展点也降低了新增算法、数据与任务时的出错风险。

Abstract: Federated learning experiments can produce reasonable-looking results even when the experiment implementation differs from the intended experimental protocol. Small errors in configuration, data partitioning, aggregation, randomness, evaluation, or checkpoint recovery may therefore affect a comparison without causing the program to fail. We present FedBrew, a research codebase for controlled and reproducible federated learning experiments. Its focus is controlled simulation with checks of specified conditions of the intended experimental protocol. FedBrew uses configuration checks, identifiable client data partitions, checkpoint recovery checks, self-describing run records, and development-time checks of scientific conditions. These checks assess, for example, whether client partitions are disjoint and exhaustive and aggregation weights match the prescribed rule; numerical and recovery checks examine non-finite client model states and interrupted runs. The same structure also provides clear extension points and automated checks for new algorithms, datasets, models, and tasks, whether they are added by researchers or AI coding agents. FedBrew therefore supports reproducible and inspectable FL experiments through checks of specified software and scientific conditions and the run records associated with reported results.

</details>


### [174] [Support-Constrained Offline Policy Improvement for Pediatric ECMO from Limited Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17134)
*Fateme Golivand Darvishvand,Saurabh Mathur,Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Michael A. Skinner,Ameet Soni,Neel Shah,Ethan L Sanford,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出支撑约束的离线强化学习框架FQI-SC，用于儿童ECMO管理，在保证安全（贴近临床专家、约束动作分布）的前提下谨慎超越专家行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童ECMO管理需在短期生理稳定与长期预防神经损伤之间权衡。标准强化学习可能提出高风险、分布外动作，而模仿学习只能复现临床医生行为、无法改进。

Method: 离线强化学习框架Support-Constrained Fitted Q-Iteration (FQI-SC)：将优化限制在有经验数据支撑的状态-动作对上，在低数据区域默认回退到临床医生基线策略；并引入统一的Balance Score S(π)形式化性能-安全权衡。基于78条儿童轨迹（超6000小时临床数据），用Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE)与行为不一致度进行评估。

Result: 支撑约束策略相较FQI使Balance Score提升38%；相较模仿学习估计价值提高1.28 FQE单位；相较FQI与临床医生动作的不一致度降低71%。

Conclusion: 通过在支撑集内约束优化并回退到专家基线，FQI-SC能够在安全、贴近临床实践的同时稳健超越专家行为，为高风险医疗决策提供了可行的离线RL方案。

Abstract: Managing children on ECMO (Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation) requires balancing short-term physiological stability with the long-term goal of preventing neurological injury. Standard reinforcement learning can propose risky, out-of-distribution actions, while imitation learning is limited to reproducing clinician behavior. We introduce Support-Constrained Fitted Q-Iteration (FQI-SC), an offline reinforcement learning framework that cautiously improves upon expert behavior by restricting optimization to empirically supported state-action pairs and defaulting to the clinician baseline in low-data regions. On 78 pediatric trajectories totaling over 6,000 hours of clinical data, we evaluate our framework using Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) and a behavioral disagreement. Additionally, we formalize the performance-safety trade-off by introducing a unified Balance Score ($S(π)$). Our empirical evaluation shows that support-constrained policy improves the Balance Score by 38\% relative to FQI, improves estimated value by 1.28 FQE units over imitation learning, and reduces disagreement with clinician actions by 71\% relative to FQI.

</details>


### [175] [ISLA: A Coordinate-Free, Measure-Weighted Transformer for Boundary-Driven PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17141)
*Peter Sharpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ISLA（Invariant Slice Attention），一种用于稳态、边界驱动PDE的切片注意力Transformer，通过对齐刚体运动/反射、单位与网格细化实现等变性、不变性与网格一致性，并在AeroZoo上取得远超基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统行为不依赖坐标系原点、朝向、单位与网格，代理模型理应具备同样的对称性，但当前气动基准上误差最低的神经代理模型缺乏这些不变性：对同一PDE仅做旋转，表面压力误差从4%升至100%以上；重采样边界则升至约30%。

Method: ISLA对稳态边界驱动PDE建模，所有可学习操作均不接收坐标，边界求和均为数值积分；位置仅以到学习到的切片锚点的距离与相对角度进入模型；长度用由边界计算的单位度量；内点通过树加速的学习核查询边界且彼此独立；每个边界单元为一个token并做精确边界读取。

Result: 在AeroZoo的2,816个流动（形状、尺寸、速度、边界条件各异）上，参考尺度下的速度与压力平均误差比任何基线至少低3.1倍和3.3倍；在AirfRANS上误差仅次于边界积分模型GLOBE，并比Transolver、GeoTransolver至少低1.6倍。

Conclusion: ISLA对刚体运动与反射等变、对声明数据的单位不变、在网格细化下保持一致，且任意设置下每个查询与其他查询相互独立；证明了将对称性内置于架构可显著提升泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: A foundational principle of physics is that all coordinate systems are equally valid: the behavior of a PDE-governed system does not depend on the origin or orientation of the frame, nor on the units or mesh used to write it down, and neither should a surrogate model. Yet the neural surrogates with the lowest errors on today's aerodynamics benchmarks lack these invariances and fail under simple transformations of the same problem: for models trained on PDEs of cars in one pose (i.e., translation and rotation) and from one mesher, rotating the full PDE raises the surface-pressure error from 4% to more than 100%, and re-sampling its boundary raises it to about 30%. We introduce ISLA (Invariant Slice Attention), a slice-attention transformer for steady, boundary-driven PDEs in which no learned operation receives a coordinate and every sum over the boundary is a quadrature. Positions enter only as distances and relative angles to learned slice anchors, lengths are measured in a unit computed from the boundary, and interior points query the boundary through a tree-accelerated learned kernel without influencing one another. With every boundary cell a token and an exact boundary read, ISLA is equivariant to rigid motions and reflections, invariant to the units of its declared data, and consistent under mesh refinement, while at every setting each query is independent of all other queries. On AeroZoo, 2,816 flows that vary in shape, size, speed, and boundary conditions, its mean reference-scaled velocity and pressure errors are at least 3.1 and 3.3 times lower than any baseline's. On AirfRANS its errors are second only to those of a boundary-integral model (GLOBE) and at least 1.6 times lower than those of Transolver and GeoTransolver.

</details>


### [176] [A Convex-Analytic Reformulation for Poisoning Attacks on Logistic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17143)
*Carina Moreira Costa,Maria Eduarda Pinheiro,Martin Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究ℓ2正则逻辑回归上的数据投毒攻击：利用强凸性与softplus凸共轭将攻击者最大化问题精确重述为仅含有界双线性等式非凸约束的优化问题，并给出变量界，用SCIP求解比较两种重述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据投毒攻击的优化问题通常非凸难解，缺乏精确重述与可证明（对偶）界；作者希望借助防御方强凸性与凸共轭得到等价且更易求解、能给出更强对偶界的攻击模型。

Method: 攻击者选择固定数量注入样本的特征（标签给定）以最大化干净训练数据上的逻辑损失。用防御问题的一阶最优性条件做精确重述；通过softplus的凸共轭将sigmoid关系替换为单一凸最优性间隙不等式，使攻击目标为凹函数；变量变换使平稳性条件变为仿射，仅剩有界双线性等式非凸；推导所有变量的有限界（系数范数全局界及基于凸优化预处理的更紧界）。在八个二分类数据集上用SCIP比较两种重述。

Result: 局部优化得到的攻击在多数数据集上降低预测性能；在固定时间限制内，凸分析重述给出显著更强的对偶界。实验覆盖八个二分类数据集的攻击有效性与两种重述对比。

Conclusion: 强凸性与凸共轭可把投毒攻击问题精确化为结构更清晰的非凸模型，凸分析重述在界质量上优于局部优化，为可证明攻击评估提供更好工具。

Abstract: We study data poisoning attacks on logistic regression with $\ell_2$-regularization, where an attacker chooses the features of a fixed number of injected observations with prescribed labels to maximize the logistic loss on the clean training data. Strong convexity of the defender's problem yields an exact reformulation through its first-order optimality conditions. Using convex conjugacy of the softplus function, we replace the sigmoid relations by a single convex optimality-gap inequality and express the attacker's objective as a concave function. A change of variables makes the stationarity condition affine, leaving bounded bilinear equalities as the only nonconvex constraints. We derive finite bounds on all variables involved in these equalities, including a global bound on the coefficient norm and tighter bounds obtained by preprocessing based on convex optimization. Experiments on eight binary classification datasets evaluate attack effectiveness and compare the two reformulations when solved with \textsf{SCIP}. Attacks obtained by local optimization degrade predictive performance on most datasets, while the convex-analytic formulation produces substantially stronger reported dual bounds within a fixed time limit.

</details>


### [177] [Enhancing Preference Detection using Eye Movement Cascade and Multilayer Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17145)
*Yuanhong Wu,Mary Tsaryk,Sam Watson,Tahsin Ahmed,Christina Schweikert,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用眼动追踪数据，通过多模型与多层组合融合（MCF）框架预测二选一偏好决策，Pre@k 从最佳单模型 0.90 提升至两层融合后的 0.9286。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉注意力如何影响人类偏好形成是消费者行为与认知科学的基础问题；本文旨在探究能否用眼动模式预测受试在两选项比较中的偏好决策。

Method: 分析受试观看成对人脸图像、全程记录注视行为的眼动数据集；采用多种机器学习分类器建模，并应用多层组合融合（MCF）框架：先将模型扩展为大规模融合模型池，再依据认知多样性进行筛选缩减，逐层重复直至多样性衰减。

Result: MCF 显著优于单一模型：最佳单模型 Pre@k 为 0.90，第一层融合后升至 0.9143，第二层进一步提升至 0.9286，第二层既超过第一层也超过最佳单模型。

Conclusion: 多层次、基于认知多样性的模型融合可有效提升眼动预测偏好决策的性能，验证了通过多层融合利用多样性的有效性。

Abstract: Understanding how visual attention influences human preference formation is a fundamental problem in consumer behavior and cognitive science. In this work, we investigate how eye movement patterns can be used to predict preference decisions when subjects compare two alternatives. To address this problem, we analyze an eye-tracking dataset, where subjects view pairs of face images and their gaze behavior is recorded throughout the decision process. We model this problem using multiple machine learning classifiers and apply the Multi-layer Combinatorial Fusion (MCF) framework to iteratively combine diverse models. The MCF framework expands models to a large pool of model fusions and then reduces them based on cognitive diversity, repeating this process across layers until diversity diminishes. Experimental results show that MCF significantly improves performance over individual models. The best single model achieves a Pre@k of 0.90, which is improved to 0.9143 after the first layer of MCF, and further increased to 0.9286 in the second layer. This progression demonstrates that the second layer not only surpasses the performance of the first layer but also outperforms the best individual model, highlighting the effectiveness of leveraging diversity through multi-layer fusion.

</details>


### [178] [AeroZoo: A Benchmark of Well-Posed Flow Problems for Foundation Models of Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17152)
*Peter Sharpe,Carmelo Gonzales,Corey Adams,Mehdi Ataei,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AeroZoo：含3,815个带转捩模型URANS时均解、覆盖7类几何/8种外罩/Re=10^3–10^8的CFD基准与训练语料，以受边界条件驱动的方式检验气动基础模型，并提供22个几何不相交的冻结划分与边界条件token编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型仅在“单一设计族+单一流场域”数据集上训练与排名，无法检验气动基础模型的最低要求——在秒级内为新外形、新外罩预测CFD级不可压流场。在DrivAerML上，增大模型与延长训练几乎不降Transolver误差，且400例后数据耗尽收益递减（最多约12%）。

Method: 构建AeroZoo：3,815个含转捩模型的非定常RANS时均解，涵盖7类几何族、8种外罩配置、Re=10^3–10^8；每个样本以求解器输入形式表述良定PDE问题（按条件类型分组的边界网格、给定值与流体物性）。取2,816个具稳定时均的样本为规范基准，设工程指标与22个几何不相交冻结划分两任务，留出几何族、外罩、边界条件布局与雷诺数区间。引入边界条件token；采用面向边界驱动PDE的Transformer ISLA。

Result: 随机划分下，边界条件token使GeoTransolver中位速度误差从入口速度的76.9%降至8.4%（均匀入流11.0%）；ISLA四种子达5.3%，约为细化网格带来改善的4倍。留出几何族几乎不改变ISLA误差（相对均匀流），而留出外罩或边界条件布局使误差升高2.4–9.2倍。

Conclusion: AeroZoo提供了以边界条件驱动、跨几何/外罩/雷诺数的严格泛化评测，揭示外罩与边界条件布局是主要难点，几何族迁移相对容易；数据与评测代码将公开。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) are trained and ranked on datasets that each vary one design family in one flow domain. Such datasets cannot test the least that an aerodynamic foundation model must do: predict CFD-quality incompressible flow in seconds for new shapes in new enclosures. On DrivAerML, larger models and longer training barely lower Transolver's error; doubling the training cases cuts it by about 12% up to 400 cases, exhausting the dataset. We introduce AeroZoo, a benchmark and training corpus of 3,815 time-mean solutions to the unsteady Reynolds-averaged Navier--Stokes (RANS) equations with a transition model, across seven geometry families, eight enclosure configurations and Reynolds numbers from $10^3$ to $10^8$. Each sample poses a well-posed PDE problem in the form a solver receives it: the boundary meshes grouped by condition type, the prescribed values and the fluid properties. The 2,816 samples with a stable time mean form the canonical benchmark: two tasks, engineering metrics and 22 frozen splits, disjoint in geometry, that hold out geometry families, enclosures, boundary-condition layouts and Reynolds ranges. On the random split, boundary-condition tokens cut GeoTransolver's median velocity error from 76.9% to 8.4% of the inlet speed in single-seed runs (uniform inlet flow: 11.0%). ISLA, a transformer for boundary-driven PDEs, reaches 5.3% over four seeds, about 4 times the change from a finer mesh. On the same test samples, a held-out geometry family barely changes ISLA's error relative to uniform flow, and a held-out enclosure or boundary-condition layout raises it 2.4 to 9.2 times. The data and the scoring code will be released.

</details>


### [179] [Symmetry in Time: A Representation Theory of Linear Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17162)
*Anif N. Shikder,Ramit Dey,Jan Minac,Lyle E. Muller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从表示论角度形式化状态空间模型(SSM)的状态追踪能力，证明对角实/复值SSM只能追踪交换群，任何能追踪群的SSM其转移矩阵必分解为实、复、四元数上的独立块，并据此提出引入四元数通道的HyperSSM，在保持线性时间推理与并行训练的同时精确求解群任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM以线性时间推理和并行训练作为Transformer的替代方案，但主流实现通过将递推对角化来加速并行扫描，导致各通道乘子互相交换，隐藏状态只记录出现了哪些输入而不记录其顺序。已有工作中报告的SSM状态追踪失败现象缺乏理论解释，本文试图用表示论给出成因并加以修正。

Method: 将状态更新视为输入序列幺半群的线性表示：SSM在任意序列长度上精确追踪某群，当且仅当该群是其转移矩阵所生成群的商群。由此导出两个结论：对角实值与复值SSM只能追踪交换群；任何能追踪群的SSM，其转移矩阵必须在实数、复数、四元数上分裂为独立块。基于此提出HyperSSM，在保留线性时间推理和并行训练的前提下加入四元数通道。

Result: 仅在最终答案上训练时，HyperSSM以至多5个状态数精确求解近期状态追踪基准中的群任务，包括此前SSM仅达随机水平的含32个生成元的S5；并泛化到训练长度25倍的序列。由于理论规定了转移必须编码的信息，线性递推的学习权重可直接解释，作者据此恢复出真实陀螺仪的标定、非理想量子比特门的旋转以及蛋白质骨架的几何结构。

Conclusion: 对角化并行扫描是SSM状态追踪失败的理论根源；群可追踪性由转移矩阵生成群与其商群关系刻画。引入四元数块结构能够在保持SSM效率优势的同时获得精确状态追踪能力，并使学习到的线性递推权重具备可解释的物理/几何意义。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) are an emerging alternative to Transformers. SSMs offer linear-time inference and parallel training, but most implementations make the parallel scan efficient by making the recurrence diagonal: each channel of the hidden state is multiplied by an input-dependent real or complex number. These numbers commute, so the state records which inputs occurred but not their order. We formalize this with representation theory, which explains and fixes the state-tracking failures previously reported for SSMs. The state update defines a linear representation of the monoid of input sequences, and we show that an SSM tracks a group at every sequence length exactly when that group is a quotient of the group generated by its transition matrices. Two consequences follow. Diagonal real- and complex-valued SSMs can track only commutative groups, while the transition matrices of any SSM that can track a group must split into independent blocks over the real, complex, and quaternion numbers. We then introduce HyperSSM, which adds quaternion channels while keeping linear-time inference and parallel training. Trained only on final answers, HyperSSM solves the group tasks of recent state-tracking benchmarks exactly with at most five state numbers. This includes $S_5$ with 32 generators, where previous SSMs are at chance, and HyperSSM generalizes to sequences 25 times longer than those seen in training. Because the theory says what the transitions must encode, the learned weights of the linear recurrence can be interpreted directly. We demonstrate this by recovering the calibration of a real gyroscope, the rotations of imperfect qubit gates and the geometry of protein backbones.

</details>


### [180] [Maximizing Capacity Utilization of LoRA Adapters for Mitigating the Learning-Forgetting Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17191)
*Tomohiro Sawada,Kartik Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA 适配器的有效秩远低于配置秩，作者提出无 SVD 的 Rényi-2 有效秩正则项，在语言建模损失上最大化有效秩，从而改善学习-遗忘权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 常不及全量微调，尤其在异构与持续学习场景中遗忘严重，存在学习-遗忘权衡；作者认为症结在于高秩 LoRA 适配器未利用配置秩提供的容量，标准更新的有效秩远低于设定秩，限制了更新能量的分布范围。

Method: 在原始训练目标基础上加入简单、稳定、可微且无需 SVD 的 Rényi-2 有效秩正则项（加权 λ），使适配器有效秩最大化；并将容量最大化与范数约束更新建立理论联系。实验采用三模型混合域秩扫描、多随机种子，辅以单任务与调度实验。

Result: λ=0.01 时，在所有秩≥16 的设定下均降低平均 Wiki 困惑度；除一组模型-秩比较外，平均训练困惑度改善或增幅不超过 0.02。学习与保持的平衡依赖配置；文献中若干 LoRA 变体同样存在该容量问题。

Conclusion: 有效秩不仅是事后诊断指标，更是可训练目标，可拓宽 LoRA 的学习-遗忘前沿，并为 LoRA 类参数高效微调提供互补的改进方向。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning, especially in heterogeneous and continual-learning settings in which substantial forgetting occurs. In general, it suffers from an undesirable learning-forgetting trade-off across diverse fine-tuning settings. We identify a potential contributor: LoRA adapters, especially high-rank ones, frequently fail to realize the available capacity afforded by the configured rank. Specifically, we find that standard LoRA updates have effective rank far below their configured rank, limiting how broadly the update energy is distributed. Therefore, we propose training mechanisms that optimize the original objective while maximizing adapter effective rank. Our main method adds a simple, stable, differentiable, and SVD-free Rényi-2 effective-rank regularizer to the language-modeling loss. We also connect capacity maximization to norm-bounded updates. In mixed-domain rank sweeps on three models evaluated with multiple random seeds, a penalty weight of $λ=0.01$ lowers mean Wiki perplexity at every tested rank of at least $16$ relative to unpenalized LoRA. Mean training perplexity improves or increases by at most $0.02$ in all but one model-rank comparison. Single-task and scheduling experiments further show that the balance between learning and retention depends on the configuration. We also observe that several LoRA variants in the literature also suffer from the identified capacity issue, and our approach provides a complementary axis of improvement to LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These results suggest that effective rank is not only a post-hoc diagnostic of adapter quality, but a trainable objective for improving the learning-forgetting frontier. Our code is available at https://github.com/tomohiro-sawada/lora-capacity-utilization

</details>


### [181] [Physically Interpretable Basin Selection in Discrete-Variable Origami Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17196)
*Lucy Liu,Leon M. Kamp,Katia Bertoldi,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在折纸启发的离散铰链设计中，连续松弛优化+末期离散化会落入"对离散化不稳健"的劣质盆地；简单的初始化偏置（偏向刚性平坦铰链）或直接在损失中惩罚"frustration/floppiness"可显著提升离散解的求解成功率（49%→69%→78%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分推动了连续变量机械设计，但梯度优化难以处理离散变量。常规做法是先对离散选择做连续松弛优化、最终取最近离散设计，然而该流程常选出不利盆地，其机理不清。作者希望用一个可解释的折纸设计问题揭示"离散化间隙"的成因，并给出低成本改进手段。

Method: 构建折纸启发的设计问题：每张折纸片几何固定，双层优化框架在每道折痕处从四种离散铰链类型中选择，用自动微分设计具有目标功能行为的形状。以该基线为对照，分别测试（1）对刚性平坦铰链统一偏置的初始化先验，（2）在外层损失中直接惩罚frustration与floppiness，并分析所得折纸片的几何兼容性与力学刚度。

Result: 基线存在大的离散化间隙：松弛优化在97%的运行中求解成功，但最终离散解仅约一半成功（49%）。初始化先验将离散求解成功率从49%提升至69%，且不增加计算量、不改变优化过程；先验产出的折纸片几何兼容性与力学刚度更好。在外层损失中直接惩罚frustration与floppiness效果更强，成功率达78%，说明这些物理特征有助于区分不利盆地。

Conclusion: 机械设计的损失景观中存在物理意义明确但对离散化不稳健的盆地，正是其导致求解失败；针对这些盆地的物理特征（哪怕仅用初始化偏置）即可有效规避，为离散-连续设计流程提供了可解释、低成本的改进思路。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation has enabled rapid progress on continuous-variable mechanical design problems, but gradient-based optimization still struggles with discrete variables. The standard approach is to optimize using a continuous relaxation of the choices, then commit to the nearest discrete design at the end. Why this procedure selects unfavorable basins is poorly understood. Here, we introduce a practical, interpretable design problem inspired by origami, in which automatic differentiation is used to design shapes with a target functional behavior. Each origami sheet has a fixed geometry, and a bilevel optimization framework tunes which one of four discrete hinge types to place at each fold. Baseline gradient-based design settles into basins with a large discretization gap: relaxed optimization solves 97% of runs, but the final, discrete solutions succeed only half the time. We find that the gap can be relieved with a surprisingly simple modification. An initialization prior uniformly biased toward the stiff, flat hinge option boosts the final solve fraction from 49% to 69%. Applying the prior adds no additional compute and leaves the optimization process unchanged. Compared to the baseline, sheets produced with the prior have greater geometric compatibility and mechanical stiffness. Directly penalizing frustration and floppiness in the outer loss function produces an even greater improvement than the prior does, raising the solve fraction to 78% and indicating that these properties help distinguish the unfavorable basins. Our results demonstrate that loss landscapes for mechanical design can have physically meaningful basins that are not robust to discretization and cause solves to fail. Targeting these basins' physical signatures, even with just an initialization bias, helps avoid them.

</details>


### [182] [Two mathematical models of knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17230)
*Audrey Xie,Ludwig Schmidt,John Duchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并分析知识蒸馏的数学模型，论证蒸馏的收益主要来自"更易优化的损失景观"而非"更好的模型"，并从理论上证明蒸馏训练收敛更快、泛化不提升，实验验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界对知识蒸馏为何成功存在大量相互竞争的假说，缺乏统一的定量解释。作者希望通过建立可分析的数学模型，厘清蒸馏收益的来源（优化速度 vs. 泛化能力）。

Method: 构建蒸馏的数学分析框架，在两类可解场景下做理论刻画：(1) 用随机梯度下降训练的广义线性模型，比较蒸馏与非蒸馏学生模型的收敛速率；(2) rank-1 矩阵近似问题，刻画目标矩阵满足何种条件时，带蒸馏的梯度下降严格快于仅用监督目标训练。

Result: 在广义线性模型+SGD 设定下，证明蒸馏能以渐近更快的速度拟合出性能良好的学生模型；在 rank-1 矩阵近似中，给出目标矩阵上使蒸馏梯度下降严格更快收敛的条件。真实数据集实验与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的优势体现在优化速度上，而非泛化能力或模型质量本身；该理论划定了蒸馏获益的边界，为理解其成功机制提供了可证伪的定量视角。

Abstract: Many hypotheses compete to explain the successes of knowledge distillation. To help address this, we propose and analyze a mathematical model of distillation, which suggests that distillation's performance comes not from obtaining better models but from easier to optimize landscapes. For generalized linear models trained with stochastic gradient descent, we prove that distillation fits performant student models asymptotically more quickly than non-distilled models. In rank-1 matrix approximation, we characterize conditions on the target matrix under which gradient descent with distillation converges strictly faster than training on the supervised objective. The theory helps delineate the ways distillation provides benefits (i.e., in optimization speed, not in generalization), and experiments on real datasets corroborate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [183] [Negative-Only Policy Optimization for One-Sided Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17287)
*Jiacheng Xu,Shuo He,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出单边可验证性(OSV)设定并给出仅利用可信负例的策略优化方法NOPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR依赖可靠验证器判断解的正确性，但现实中常缺乏完整验证，仅有便宜的部分检查能可靠地证伪失败；这种正负验证不对称会使标准RLVR奖励通过部分检查的"捷径"，产生假阳性问题。

Method: 将设定形式化为OSV（已验证负例是可靠反例，已验证正例仅是非证伪候选）。提出Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO)：把已验证正例视作无标签样本，仅通过抑制可信负例来更新策略；具体由参考相对的负例抑制损失(reference-relative negative suppression loss)与组级适配器(group-level adapter)组成，后者按非证伪响应的比例缩放每次更新。

Result: 在单元测试、约束检查与LLM自验证器三类验证器下，于代码、逻辑、数学基准上，当缺少oracle奖励时NOPO相对基线提升pass@k；增益在推理时扩展(inference-time scaling)与跨基准迁移下依然保持。

Conclusion: 在单边可验证性场景下，仅利用可信负例进行抑制式更新可避免奖励捷径，是标准RLVR不可靠时的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically relies on a verifier that can reliably judge whether sampled solutions are correct. However, in many realistic settings, complete verification is generally unavailable, while cheap checks can only falsify failures. We formalize this regime as one-sided verifiability (OSV): verified negatives are reliable counterexamples, but verified positives are merely non-falsified candidates and may contain many false positives. This asymmetry makes standard RLVR unreliable, because reinforcing verified positives can directly reward shortcuts that pass partial checks. To address this issue, we propose Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO), which treats verified positives as unlabeled and updates the policy only by suppressing trusted verified negatives. Specifically, NOPO combines a reference-relative negative suppression loss with a group-level adapter that scales each update by the proportion of non-falsified responses. Across unit-test, constraint-check, and LLM self-verifiers, NOPO improves pass@k over baselines on code, logic, and math benchmarks when oracle rewards are unavailable. These gains persist under inference-time scaling and cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [184] [Towards a Theory of Learnability via Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17303)
*Tosca Lechner,Vinayak Pathak,Sivan Sabato,Ruth Urner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“辩论”视为一种训练协议，把其中的学习问题形式化为在线学习问题，指出在精确形式化后发现最优学习方式未必是运行对抗性辩论，进而提出合作式辩论协议并用组合维数刻画其可学习性，同时表明人类评判者的少量判断错误会导致学习结果的巨大误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作把辩论当作博弈来分析（针对已知评判者分析两个辩手的博弈），却忽略了它本质上是一个训练协议，即模型需要通过学习获得被评判的能力。本文旨在填补对辩论协议内在学习问题的理解空白。

Method: 将辩论训练协议中的学习问题形式化为在线学习问题；在精确形式化后比较对抗式辩论与直接学习的差异；据此定义合作式版本的辩论协议，并使用一种组合维数刻画其可学习性；进一步分析评判者会出错（偶尔误判）的情形。

Result: 一旦精确形式化学习问题，存在某些情形下给定评判者访问权限时的最优学习方式与运行对抗式辩论不同；合作式协议的可学习性可由组合维数刻画；评判者的少量误判会导致学习中出现很大的误差。

Conclusion: 辩论作为训练协议的学习性质需要单独研究，对抗式辩论并非总是最优学习策略；合作式辩论提供了可刻画可学习性的替代框架；评判者的可靠性是制约该协议的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Debate has been recently proposed as a training protocol that is hoped to produce more aligned models even as model capabilities scale. The idea is that the human, instead of providing supervision in the form of labels, judges a debate between two models that compete to prove the correctness of opposite labels. It can be shown that functions of a much higher computational complexity can be correctly judged in this way, compared to the complexity of functions that can be directly evaluated using a computationally weak evaluator. This suggests that perhaps debate can be used to teach very complicated functions to models. However, previous work has largely ignored the fact that this is a training protocol, and simply analyzes the game being played between the two debaters for a known judge. We take the first steps towards understanding the learning problem implicit within the protocol, by formulating it as an online learning problem. We show that once we formulate the learning problem precisely, there are cases where the best way to learn given access to a judge is different from running an adversarial debate. This motivates us to define a cooperative version of the protocol, whose learnability we characterize using a combinatorial dimension. We also consider the possibility of the human judge being fallible and occasionally judging the debate incorrectly. We show that a small number of errors in judgement can lead to a large error in learning.

</details>


### [185] [Linking Rapid Learning to Compositional Structure in Neural Networks: Evidence from Additive Analogies in Meta-Learned Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17314)
*Abigail L. Tenenbaum,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探究元学习网络为何能快速学习新函数，发现其针对不同函数的适配参数向量之间存在可加/可减的符号关系，人为重组得到的向量无需再训练即可实现对应函数，表明元学习通过几何化的参数空间轨迹组织快速学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能从有限经验中快速学习，而神经网络通常需要大量训练。已有工作表明元学习网络能快速学习新函数，但其内在机制尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示这种快速学习能力的结构基础。

Method: 受词向量加性关系（如 king−man+woman≈queen）的经典结果启发，将元学习网络在快速适配某个函数后得到的全部参数拼接为单一“函数表示向量”，并检验这些向量之间是否遵循函数间的符号关系进行加、减运算，从而构造新的参数向量。

Result: 参数向量可按函数间的符号关系进行加减，人工重组得到的向量所对应的网络无需针对目标函数直接训练，即可成功实现所预期的函数，且与符号操作预测的结果一致。

Conclusion: 元学习能够调整网络的初始起点，使后续学习沿着系统化、具有功能意义的轨迹穿越参数空间，从而为神经网络中归纳偏置的实现提供几何基础。

Abstract: Humans can learn rapidly from limited experience, while neural networks typically require extensive training. In this work, we investigate what properties of a neural network can enable it to learn rapidly. We study neural networks that have been optimized via meta-learning, a technique that exposes a network to many related functions to facilitate its subsequent learning of similar functions. Prior work has shown that these meta-learned networks can learn new functions rapidly, but how they do so remains unclear. To investigate their internal structure, we take inspiration from classic work demonstrating additive relationships among the learned vectors that neural networks use to represent words (e.g., "king" minus "man" plus "woman" approximates "queen"). Analogously, we look for additive relationships among a meta-learned network's "representations" of different functions; when a meta-learned network rapidly learns to implement a specific function, all of its adapted parameter values can be concatenated into a single vector that we treat as representing the function. We find that such parameter vectors can be added and subtracted according to symbolic relationships among the functions they represent, yielding new parameter vectors that successfully represent the functions predicted by the corresponding symbolic manipulations: the networks specified by these artificially recombined vectors actually implement the intended functions without any direct training on them. Our results suggest that meta-learning can tune a network's starting point to organize subsequent learning along systematic, functionally meaningful trajectories through the space of possible parameter vectors, providing a geometric basis for realizing inductive biases in neural networks.

</details>


### [186] [Internalizing Investment Mandates: A Neurosymbolic RL Approach to Portfolio Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17329)
*Zeynep Yıldız,Satchit Chatterji,Ercan Atam,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种受PLPG启发的连续动作神经符号方法，通过重参数化蒙特卡洛采样估计策略自身执行动作满足投资指令的概率，并将其加入训练目标，使深度强化学习策略在无需事后干预的情况下内化制度性资产配置约束（合规违规率降至4.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资组合受投资政策声明（IPS）约束，需满足条件于市场状态信息的资产配置规则。深度强化学习虽能优化财务绩效，但通常无法强制规则合规，难以实际部署。现有神经符号方法（如PLPG）可实现约束满足型RL，但假设动作空间为有限离散空间，无法直接适用于连续资产配置问题。

Method: 提出一种PLPG启发的连续配置方法：利用重参数化蒙特卡洛采样，估计策略自身被执行的连续动作满足投资指令的概率，并将该合规概率项加入训练目标（可微符号监督）。推理时不采用任何事后强制或动作拦截机制，合规性由智能体在训练中习得。

Result: 在无任何校正的确定性均值动作上直接审计：对含六条规则、条件于市场状态的指令，无约束RL违规率为96–98%，奖励惩罚基线为33.4%，而所提方法将违规率降至4.8%。

Conclusion: 连续动作空间的神经符号策略能够实质性内化符号化投资指令，在不依赖推理期事后干预的前提下显著提升合规性，为受规则约束的机构资管场景中的RL部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Institutional portfolios operate under investment policy statements that specify asset allocation constraints, often conditioned on market regime information. Deep reinforcement learning has been shown to optimize financial performance but typically does not enforce rule compliance, making it unsuitable for real-world deployment. Existing neurosymbolic methods such as Probabilistic Logic Policy Gradient (PLPG) offer constraint-satisfying reinforcement learning (RL) via differentiable symbolic supervision but assume a finite, discrete action space. We present a PLPG-inspired continuous allocation method that estimates the probability that the policy's own executed action satisfies the investment mandate, using reparameterized Monte Carlo sampling, and adds it to the training objective. No post-hoc enforcement mechanism intercepts actions at inference time and compliance is learned by the agent. Audited directly on deterministic mean actions with no correction, the policy reduces violations of a six-rule regime-conditional mandate from 96-98% for unconstrained RL and 33.4% for a reward-penalty baseline to 4.8%, showing that a continuous-action neurosymbolic policy can substantially internalize a symbolic mandate.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Care, Human Enfeeblement, and the Existential Implications of AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14520)
*B. Scot Rousse*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文重新诠释Stuart Russell提出的"人类衰弱"风险：真正威胁不止于认知卸载与知识流失，而是"关怀能力"的侵蚀导致的人与世界脱嵌；作者主张将关怀视为可培养的技能群，并据此设计AI以支撑人的关怀能力，从而回应后AGI时代"意义与目的"之问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Russell将"人类衰弱"列为先进AI的长期社会风险，但既有理解多停留在认知卸载层面；同时Hassabis等人提出后AGI时代的"意义与目的"隐忧。作者试图为衰弱风险给出更深的解释框架，并为AI设计提供规范方向。

Method: 概念分析与哲学论证：综合Brian Christian、Alison Gopnik、David Spivak关于关怀与照护的近期工作，连接Winograd与Flores的本体论设计传统，将"关怀"重构为可培养、使人投入世界并彼此联结、且使生活值得过的技能群。

Result: 提出衰弱风险的新解释——不是单纯知识流失，而是关怀能力的侵蚀所导致的与世界脱嵌；并给出以支撑关怀能力为核心的AI设计取向。

Conclusion: 以支持人类关怀能力为目标的AI设计，可回应后AGI时代关于"意义与目的"的担忧，为AI伦理与设计提供以关怀为本的规范框架。

Abstract: Stuart Russell has named "human enfeeblement" as a long-term societal risk of advanced AI. This paper gives a new interpretation of the enfeeblement danger by interpreting it in light of recent work by Brian Christian, Alison Gopnik, and David Spivak on care and caregiving, and connecting it with the ontological design tradition of Terry Winograd and Fernando Flores. The enfeeblement that threatens is not merely a matter of cognitive off-loading and loss of knowledge, but a form of disengagement with the world which I explain in terms of an erosion of our capacities to care. This paper articulates a conception of care as a constellation of cultivable skills that involve us in the world and with each other, while making life worth living. Designing AI to support our capacities to care addresses the worry recently raised by Demis Hassabis and others regarding the questions of "meaning and purpose" that are looming in a post-AGI world.

</details>


### [188] [Probe Before You Preach: Conformal Ambiguity Sets and Neuro-Symbolic Counterfactual Probing for Diagnosing Algebraic Misconceptions in Middle School Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14557)
*Caihong Xu,Xiangyu Yu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出不同数学迷思规则可产生相同错误答案，导致单题诊断存在歧义，并将其形式化为"代数碰撞"、给出条件贝叶斯误差界；随后提出MACMS（课程组边际覆盖的自适应共形迷思集合）与Sym-EIG（符号验证的反事实微探测），明确二者均为有限保证而非逐学生正确性或诊断性能承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一作答无法区分产生同一错误答案的多种迷思规则，使基于单响应的诊断天然歧义；同时作者希望澄清内部生成的推理在条件独立假设下不提供超出输入的学生特异信息，并避免对诊断效果与课堂收益作过度声称。

Method: 将同答案碰撞形式化为代数碰撞并推导条件贝叶斯误差界；提出Mondrian Adaptive Conformal Misconception Sets（MACMS），在可交换性下给出按课程组分层的边际覆盖；提出Symbolic-Verified Counterfactual Micro-Probing（Sym-EIG），搜索能使候选规则输出分离的探测题；并对本地公开Eedi图谱中2,258个关联构念进行审计，同时说明其他性能表因脚本直接赋值需重算。

Result: 理论层面得到碰撞下的条件贝叶斯误差界与MACMS的课程组边际覆盖；审计发现2,258个关联构念中2,118个至少关联两个迷思ID，但构念级关联本身不构成同答案碰撞的证据；明确指出识别依赖候选集与响应模型，且未给出诊断性能或学习收益数据。

Conclusion: 单响应诊断的歧义需以集合式、有覆盖保证的输出与主动探测来缓解，但MACMS仅提供边际覆盖而非逐学生正确性，Sym-EIG的识别受候选集与响应模型限制；构念关联不能替代碰撞证据，现有结果表需重算，课堂有效性留待未来课堂研究验证。

Abstract: Distinct mathematical misconception rules can produce the same incorrect answer, making diagnosis from a single response ambiguous. We formalize this algebraic collision and derive a conditional Bayes error bound; internally generated reasoning does not add student-specific information beyond its inputs under an explicit conditional-independence assumption. We propose Mondrian Adaptive Conformal Misconception Sets (MACMS) and Symbolic-Verified Counterfactual Micro-Probing (Sym-EIG). MACMS has curriculum-group marginal coverage under exchangeability, not a per-student correctness guarantee. Sym-EIG searches for probes whose outputs separate modeled candidate rules; identification additionally depends on the candidate set and response model. An audit of a local public Eedi graph finds 2,118 of 2,258 linked constructs associated with at least two misconception IDs; construct-level associations alone do not establish same-answer collisions. Other project performance tables require recomputation because their script assigns numerical results directly. No diagnostic performance or classroom learning benefit is claimed here. A classroom study is proposed for future validation.

</details>


### [189] [Who (or what) are they prompting? How CS students conceptualize and use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14798)
*Nikitha Donekal Chandrashekar,Margaret Ellis,Sehrish Basir Nizamani,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在CS1后续课程中通过开放式问卷(N=283)，用SOLO分类法将学生对LLM工作原理的理解分为四个序数层级，并从"拟人类型"与"关系角色"两维度编码其隐喻性概念化，发现学生多将LLM视为人类助手、偏好情境丰富式提示，且概念化、理解与提示行为之间存在关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在计算机课程中被学生广泛使用，但计算教育研究尚未充分考察教学与LLM的引入如何塑造学生对LLM的概念化与理解，以及这些因素与其后续使用行为的关系；已有工作多关注提示策略与AI素养，而对"概念化—机制理解—提示行为"三者关系探讨不足。

Method: 选取一门介绍CS各子领域的CS1后续课程并加以改造以教授和允许使用LLM；课程结束时发放问卷，采用开放式题目引出学生对LLM的表征及对其工作原理的解释，并用封闭式量表测量提示风格与提示数量（N=283份可编码有效回答）；采用定性编码，基于SOLO分类法将机制理解划分为四个序数知识层级，并沿"拟人类型"和"关系角色"两个维度编码隐喻性概念化；进行探索性关联分析。

Result: 学生最常将LLM概念化为"人类助手"，情境丰富式提示(context-rich prompting)是最受偏好的风格；探索性分析显示概念化、机制理解与提示使用之间存在关联，即对系统的理解程度与其使用方式相关。

Conclusion: 研究提出了学生LLM隐喻性表征的二维刻画框架，并初步揭示机制理解与使用行为之间的关系，为计算教育中LLM的教学设计与AI素养培养提供启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by students in computing courses. Yet computing education research has not fully examined how teaching and inclusion of LLMs shape students' conceptualization and understanding of LLMs, and how these factors relate to their subsequent use. Although prior work has examined prompting strategies and AI literacy, the relationships among students' conceptualizations of LLMs, their understanding of LLM mechanisms, and their prompting behavior remain underexplored. We studied these relationships in a post CS1 course that introduces students to the various CS subfields. We adapted the course to teach and allow the usage of LLMs. We administered an end-of-course survey, using open-ended items to elicit students' representations of LLMs and their explanations of how LLMs work, alongside closed-response measures of prompting style and prompt count (N=283 students consented and provided codable responses). Using qualitative coding, we categorized students' understanding of how LLMs work into four ordinal knowledge levels based on the Structure of Observed Learning Outcomes (SOLO) taxonomy and coded their metaphoric conceptualizations along two dimensions: persona type and relational role. Our results show that students most often conceptualized LLMs as human assistants, and context-rich prompting was the preferred style. The study contributes a two-dimensional characterization of students' LLM metaphoric representation and exploratory analyses also identified associations among these constructs showing how understanding these systems relates to their usage.

</details>


### [190] [The hidden solar cost of urban unfamiliarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14959)
*Lijia Liu,Yimeng Song*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究构建香港岛三维时间特定步行模型，量化"隐藏日照成本"，并比较最短路径与避晒路径的太阳暴露差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不熟悉环境的行人倾向走最短路线，从而忽视附近遮阴、室内通道与多层连廊等现有防护资源，导致本可避免的直接日照暴露。

Method: 构建融合室内外步行网络、建筑几何、LiDAR植被与太阳位置的三维时间特定模型；对500条模拟出行，在4个白天时点与5个额外距离上限下比较最短路径与避晒路径；并做受控对比以分离户外遮阴、室内防护与多层连廊的贡献。

Result: 正午、10%额外距离上限下，避晒路径较最短路径降低13.5%模型直接日照暴露，平均路线长度仅增加2.85%；5%上限可获得10%上限降幅的77%；受控对比中户外遮阴贡献86%、室内防护贡献14%；移除多层连廊后，500条出行中仅191条在原路线长度限制内可行。

Conclusion: 路径比较揭示了因路径决策信息缺失而产生的可避免日照成本；使受保护路线可见并保持其连通性，可在不大幅绕行的情况下降低日照暴露。

Abstract: Unfamiliar walkers may follow the shortest route and miss nearby shade, indoor passages and multilevel connections. We define the resulting hidden solar cost as the direct-sun exposure avoidable through shade-informed routing without substantial detours. We developed a time-specific three-dimensional model of Hong Kong Island that combines indoor and outdoor walking networks with building geometry, vegetation mapped by light detection and ranging (LiDAR), and solar position. For 500 modelled journeys, we compared shortest and shade-informed routes at four daytime snapshots and five extra-distance limits. At noon, shade-informed routes reduced modelled direct-sun exposure by 13.5% relative to shortest routes under a 10% extra-distance limit. Route length increased by 2.85% on average. A 5% extra-distance limit delivered 77% of the reduction obtained at 10%. In a controlled comparison, outdoor shade information accounted for 86% of the reduction and indoor-protection information for 14%. Removing multilevel connections left only 191 of 500 journeys feasible within the original route-length limit. These route comparisons identify an avoidable solar cost when existing protection is missing from the information used to choose a route. Making protected routes visible and preserving their connections can reduce sun exposure without large detours.

</details>


### [191] [Taxonomizing Synthetic Data for Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15489)
*Ignacio Cofone,Katherine J. Strandburg,Nicholas Tilmes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 合成数据不仅因与采集数据的关联而产生风险，其生成所依赖的背景知识与"真实基准"（ground truth）假设同样关键。论文据此提出三分法（变换数据、增广数据、模拟数据）并检视隐私、偏见与竞争三方面的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将合成数据与"真实"采集数据截然二分，忽视不同种类与用途的合成数据带来的差异化风险；作者延续Gal与Lynskey关于合成数据将重塑信息隐私、市场竞争与数据质量的论点，但主张对其风险根源的定位需从"与采集数据的联系"转向"背景知识与真实基准假设"。

Method: 概念分析与分类学构建：以"依赖背景知识/真实基准假设的程度"为维度，将合成数据划分为（1）变换数据——修改采集数据以保留特定统计特性服务于特定用途；（2）增广数据——借助假设提升采集数据集对真实基准的保真度；（3）模拟数据——几乎完全依赖背景知识与真实基准假设。随后在该分类框架下逐一分析隐私、偏见与竞争效应。

Result: 其一，任何一类合成数据都无法避免基于群体的推断所导致的隐私损害；其二，增广数据与模拟数据可能放大偏见，同时使数据集显得更为完整（掩盖缺失与偏差）；其三，合成数据很少能帮助较小竞争者克服在位者的数据优势。

Conclusion: 政策制定者在权衡是否激励、强制或限制合成数据使用时，应重点评估生成过程中所用真实基准假设的有效性。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly important in data usage and AI design, creating novel legal and policy dilemmas. All too often, discussions of synthetic data treat it as entirely distinct from "real," collected data, overlooking the risks posed by different kinds and uses of synthetic data. We build on Michal Gal and Orla Lynskey's argument that synthetic data will transform information privacy, market competition, and data quality. While the risks posed by synthetic data depend on its connection to collected data, we argue that background knowledge and assumptions about ground truth used to create it are at least as important. We bring that focus to Gal and Lynskey's taxonomy of synthetic data, arguing that this focus is essential to grasp synthetic data's legal and policy implications. As such, we divide synthetic data into (1) transformed data, which modifies collected data to preserve certain statistical properties for an end use; (2) augmented data, which relies on assumptions to bolster a collected dataset's fidelity to the ground truth; and (3) simulated data, which relies almost entirely on background knowledge and ground-truth assumptions. Applying this taxonomy, we show that no category of synthetic data prevents privacy harms from group-based inferences, that augmented and simulated data can amplify bias while making a dataset look more complete, and that synthetic data rarely lets smaller competitors overcome incumbents' data advantages. As policymakers weigh whether to incentivize, mandate, or discourage the use of synthetic data, they should consider the validity of the ground-truth assumptions used in producing that data.

</details>


### [192] [Scaling public input on AI policy with pluralistic agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16028)
*Jacy Reese Anthis,Ken Burke,David Madras,Crystal Qian,Meredith Ringel Morris,Logan Cross,Caroline Pantofaru,Utsav Prabhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出并测试"多元智能体"（pluralistic agents）——代表个体参与审议的AI代理，用以在无法直接讨论的场景中规模化收集多元人类视角，实验显示其能可靠推动政策向所代表人格偏好移动，并被用户视为有用且公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI部署跨越广泛的应用领域、文化环境与利益相关者，安全且负责任的AI开发需要持续纳入多元视角；但直接参与讨论受时间、专业门槛及隐私信息不可共享等限制，难以规模化。

Method: 开发多元智能体（代表个人进行审议的AI代理代表），并开展实验识别其机会、风险与感知；关键变量为智能体所获人格指令（如冗长度、合作性）。另进行预注册研究，746名人类参与者实际参与多元智能体流程，并与"直接参与"及"向单一AI推理模型提供输入"两种基线比较。

Result: 智能体可可靠地将政策推向其所代表人格的偏好方向，但影响力大小对人格指令敏感（被指示更冗长或更不合作时影响力更大）；参与者认为多元智能体有用且公平，偏好其优于单一AI模型基线；多数人仍偏好自己直接参与，但多元智能体在满意度上弥补了基线—直接参与差距的68%，公平性上弥补82%。

Conclusion: 多元智能体可用于无法直接讨论的众多现实场景，有望成为扩展AI安全政策制定的有力工具；应谨慎探索，将其定位为人类输入的放大器而非替代品。

Abstract: AI is being deployed across a vast range of application domains, cultural environments, and human stakeholders. Safe and responsible AI development requires continuously accounting for diverse perspectives. We develop and test pluralistic agents, AI agent representatives that deliberate on behalf of individuals to align AI systems with diverse human perspectives. Pluralistic agents could help people contribute to many more discussions than they could directly participate in, on technical topics that would require training, and on policy choices that rely on private information that corporations and governments cannot readily share. Our experiments identify opportunities, risks, and perceptions of pluralistic agents. Agents were able to reliably shift policies in the direction of their represented persona's preferences, but the extent of each agent's influence was sensitive to the instructions from its persona, such as having more influence when instructed to be more verbose or less cooperative. This suggests that influence in AI-assisted deliberation could vary based on how participants prompt their agents and how the system is designed. In a preregistered study, 746 humans participated in the pluralistic agents process. Participants viewed pluralistic agents as useful and fair, preferring agents over the baseline of providing input to a single AI reasoning model. Most participants preferred participating themselves over either pluralistic agents or the baseline, but pluralistic agents recovered 68% of the baseline-direct gap in reported satisfaction and 82% in fairness. Because pluralistic agents can be used in many real-world contexts where direct discussion is not, they could be a promising tool to scale AI safety policy development. We encourage cautious exploration of this method, playing to its strengths as an amplifier of human input rather than a replacement.

</details>


### [193] [One Future Fits All: Text-to-image AI and the Narrowing of Global Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16089)
*Stephanie Diepeveen,Somnath Batabyal,Edward Ademolu,Peter Chonka,Gabriele de Seta,Iginio Gagliardone,Elisabeth Gunawan,Fabio Gygi,Yidnekachew Haile,Yenn Lee,Chipo Mapondera,Craig Ryder,Matti Pohjonen,Michiel Baas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文以Microsoft Bing Create（基于DALL·E 3）为对象，通过15种语言、12个国家、907张生成图像的协作探索性研究，结合"synthetic probes"方法，发现文本到图像生成AI将城市未来压缩为去政治化、去冲突的旅游业友好视觉语法，构成一种"平庸性"（banality）——系统性地收窄再现可能性，排斥多元未来想象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界已确认算法偏见的持续性，但技术变迁意味着偏见未必以相同形式复现；作者因此需要考察生成式AI这一新媒介中偏见的具体再现形态，尤其是文本到图像模型如何在"地点—语言—权力"的交织中重塑未来想象。

Method: 协作探索性研究：对Bing Create（DALL·E 3）在15种语言、涉及12个国家的提示下生成的907张图像输出进行迭代分析；采用de Seta（2024）提出的"synthetic probes"概念，反复探究输入与输出在地方性、语言与权力语境中的相关关系。

Result: 输出呈现显著的"可能性简化"：剔除冲突、贫困与政治的（明确）表征后，剩余图像的本地多样性回退为东方主义式想象；未来城市被同质化为单一文明化/现代化叙事——悬浮车、都市花园、太阳能板，统一包裹于蓝绿色调之中。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的视觉输出反映并再生产一种简单却普遍而顽固的殖民/发展主义世界观；其威胁不在复杂性或智能，而在"平庸性"。作者将banality界定为并非单纯重复，而是再现可能性的系统性收窄——趋向游客友好、去政治化、发展饱和的视觉语法，从而抹平矛盾、封闭多元未来主义。

Abstract: Persistence of algorithmic bias is well established in scholarship, reflecting, reproducing and reshaping inequalities and injustices. Yet, as technology changes, we cannot assume that inequalities will be reproduced in the same or similar forms. This article explores the reproduction of bias in text-to-image generative AI, looking at the tool, Microsoft Bing Create, based on DALL E 3. It draws on results of a collaborative exploratory study of 907 image-based outputs of text-to-image generation in 15 languages and concerning 12 countries. We utilise the notion of synthetic probes (de Seta, 2024) to iteratively explore correlations of inputs and outputs within the context of place, language and power. We find striking simplicity in the scope of possibilities through which Bing Create considers the current and future city. Stripping out (explicit) indications of conflict, poverty and politics, we are left with images whose localised diversity harkens back to versions of Orientalist imaginaries. Images of the future emerge as one civilising mission or modernisation narrative, with all places headed to one future of hovercrafts, urban gardens and solar panels, encased in a composition of shades of blue and green. We argue that the visual outputs of this generative AI tool reflect and reproduce a simple but pervasive and persistent colonial/developmental worldview. We explore explanations for this emergent narrative and its implications, suggesting the threat of generative AI is not in its complexity or intelligence, but rather its banality. With this, we propose banality not as mere repetition, but the systematic narrowing of representational possibility: a convergence on tourist-friendly, depoliticised, development-saturated visual grammars that smooth contradiction and foreclose plural futurisms.

</details>


### [194] [From State of THE ART to Human Prediction Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16464)
*Conghao Wong,Ziqian Zou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文以交互艺术与人类轨迹预测算法为对照对象，从感知、交互、时序三个维度剖析两领域的结构与演进展共性，指出预测模型在量化指标提升的同时对人类行为、未来意图与社会互动的考量反而弱化，并提出艺术启发的预测算法可能从数据中发掘有趣的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类轨迹预测等AI算法在量化性能上不断刷新SOTA，但其建模中对人类行为、未来思考与社会互动的考量却逐渐被数字与指标淹没，呈现"性能提升、人文内涵退化"的反直觉现象，作者希望换一种分析视角重新审视该问题。

Method: 采用思辨性、跨领域的类比分析视角，以交互艺术作品与人类轨迹预测算法为具体案例，围绕感知（perception）、交互（interaction）、时序（timing）三个维度，比较两领域的结构与演进展共性，探讨人类直觉与艺术如何与AI算法建立关联。

Result: 通过具体交互艺术作品与预测算法的对照分析，揭示两领域在感知、交互、时序上的结构性共性；并观察到部分受艺术启发的预测算法能够从数据中推导出有意义的人类行为模式，为理解人类活动、SOTA与AI算法之间的关系提供新线索。

Conclusion: 以艺术为镜可为轨迹预测乃至更广泛的AI算法研究提供新的理解框架与研究方向，提示研究者不应仅以量化指标为唯一导向，而应重新纳入对人类行为与社会互动的建模考量。

Abstract: Human curiosity and longing for the future have driven human progress from ancient times to the present. With the advancement of AI technology, human prediction algorithms have also drawn increasing attention from researchers. However, as AI technology continues to advance, we observe a counterintuitive phenomenon. Take human trajectory prediction algorithms as an example. Although models' quantitative prediction performance has improved, yielding one state-of-the-art model after another, the consideration of human behaviors, future thoughts, and social interactions themselves presents a gradually diminishing trend in the construction of these prediction algorithms, buried beneath numbers and metrics. In this manuscript, taking interactive art and human trajectory prediction algorithms as examples, we attempt to adopt a different analytical perspective to analyze the structural and developmental commonalities between these two fields. By examining specific interactive artworks and prediction algorithms, we aim to explore how human intuitions and art are associated with AI algorithms through three aspects, including perception, interaction, and timing, thereby providing new insights for the understanding and further research in this domain. We also observe an interesting phenomenon that certain art-inspired prediction algorithms can actually deduce intriguing patterns of human behaviors from the data, offering new potential avenues for understanding the relationship between human activities, state of The Art, and AI algorithms.

</details>


### [195] [A Code-Level Audit of X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16800)
*Luka Bekavac,Simon Mayer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代码级审计方法，对X平台开源推荐管线做反向遍历可达性核查，发现线上服务路径上存在13处透明度死胡同，过滤与排序组件落入可检视性最低的两档，且2025年引入专有模型后进一步恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟DSA要求超大型在线平台披露推荐管线信息并向审查合格研究者开放非公开数据（可能延伸至推荐代码），但代码公开能否支撑实质性第三方审计尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 对自愿开源的X平台推荐仓库实施"反向回溯"遍历：从用户可见时间线出发，沿每个决策相关组件追溯至其上游生产者，检验其是否可从实际服务管线可达，并按四级可检视性方案对组件分类；辅以可达性检查以区分真正的死胡同与失效参数引用。

Result: 识别出13处位于线上服务路径的"透明度死胡同"（公开代码依赖但发布版本缺失的模型、服务、标签列表与配置值）；决定帖子过滤与排序方式的组件落入可检视性最低的两档，该问题在2025年将专有Grok模型接入审核、标注与排序后加剧；发布版仍保留2025管线已不可达的2023年代码，其中36处参数引用若无可达性检查会被误判为死胡同。

Conclusion: 仅公开推荐代码不足以支撑有意义的第三方审计；需补充缺失的模型、服务、标签与配置等额外工件，并采用含可达性核查的代码级审计流程，才能实现实质性透明与问责。

Abstract: The transparency regime of recommender systems on Very Large Online Platforms (VLOPs) is undergoing significant change under the EU Digital Services Act (DSA). The DSA requires platforms to disclose information about their recommendation pipelines and grants vetted researchers access to non-public data, which may extend to recommender code. However, it is not known to what extent such code releases enable substantive and meaningful third-party auditing of a production recommender system. We address this question by proposing a code-level audit methodology for recommender systems and applying it to the X platform, which has voluntarily open-sourced its recommender pipeline. Our methodology is a backwards-walk traversal of the repository, tracing each decision-relevant component from the user-facing timeline back to its upstream producers, checking that it is reachable from the served pipeline, and classifying each under a four-tier inspectability scheme. Applying this procedure, we identify 13 transparency dead-ends on the live serving path: models, services, label lists, and configuration values that the public code depends on but the release does not contain. We find that the components that decide which posts are filtered and how they are ranked fall into the two least inspectable tiers, a pattern that intensifies with the 2025 integration of the proprietary Grok language model into moderation, annotation, and ranking. We further show that the release keeps 2023 code that the 2025 pipeline no longer reaches, with 36 parameter references that would pass for dead-ends without a reachability check. Finally, we discuss which additional artifacts would be required for meaningful auditing.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Curiosity: Habit and Organizational Learning Capability as Predictors of Shadow AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16931)
*Nolan Lovett,Mohan Yang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于510名生成式AI使用者的跨国调查数据，检验“影子AI使用（未授权使用与隐瞒）”究竟源于探索性人格还是习惯化自动行为，发现习惯是最强预测因子并完全支配好奇心，学习支持型组织条件独立加成解释24.1%，从而将影子AI重新界定为习惯—环境现象而非个体探索现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI进入职场的速度快于组织治理能力的跟进，大量员工使用未经雇主批准的AI工具并主动隐瞒。流行观点与早期评论通常将这种“影子AI”行为解释为冒险型个体的好奇心驱动探索，但该假设缺乏实证检验，且对隐瞒行为背后的心理机制理解不足。本文旨在提出并检验一个基于习惯性技术使用心理学的竞争性解释：真正维持该行为的是自动化（习惯）而非探索性倾向。

Method: 研究采用多国调查数据（N=510名在职生成式AI使用者），以个体与情境预测变量为自变量：职场好奇心（含“愉悦探索”维度）、习惯、技术接受信念、组织学习能力。分析方法包括：分层回归、带bootstrap置信区间的相对权重分析，以及全子集优势分析（512个预测变量子集）；并构建平行模型，将结果变量分解为未授权使用与隐瞒两个成分；同时检验感知的AI使用社会支持的作用及组织条件的调节效应。

Result: 习惯是最强预测因子，解释28.3%的已解释方差，并在全部512个预测变量子集中完全支配“愉悦探索”（与探索解释最契合的好奇心维度）。愉悦探索仅贡献3.4%（95% CI [1.6, 7.4]），在纳入使用行为与习惯后不再显著。学习支持型组织条件额外解释24.1%，且以加性方式起作用，而非调节个体特质。对未授权使用与隐瞒分别建模的平行分析显示相同的优势模式；感知的AI使用社会支持仅对隐瞒具有特异性保护作用。

Conclusion: 影子AI应被重新界定为“习惯—环境”现象，而非“个体探索”现象：其持续由自动化习惯与组织学习条件共同驱动，而非冒险性好奇心。实践上，治理应聚焦于习惯重塑与营造学习支持型环境，并借助社会支持降低隐瞒；理论上需将影子AI研究从人格特质框架转向习惯性技术使用框架。由于采用横断面设计，因果推断仅为探索性，需纵向或实验研究进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered the workplace faster than organizations can govern it. A large share of employees now use AI tools their employer has not sanctioned, and many actively conceal that use, but the psychology of this covert technology behavior remains poorly understood. A common assumption, in popular accounts and early commentary, treats unauthorized (shadow) AI use as curiosity-driven exploration by adventurous individuals. We test this against a competing account grounded in the psychology of habitual technology use, in which automaticity rather than exploratory disposition sustains the behavior. Using survey data from 510 employed generative AI users across multiple countries, we adjudicated among individual and contextual predictors (workplace curiosity, habit, technology-acceptance beliefs, and organizational learning capability) with hierarchical regression, relative weights analysis with bootstrap confidence intervals, and full-subset dominance analysis. Habit was the strongest predictor, accounting for 28.3% of explained variance, and completely dominated joyous exploration, the curiosity dimension most aligned with the exploration account, across all 512 possible predictor subsets. Joyous exploration contributed 3.4% (95% CI [1.6, 7.4]) and fell to nonsignificance once use behavior and habit were modeled. Learning-supportive organizational conditions accounted for a further 24.1% and operated additively rather than by moderating dispositions. Parallel models decomposing the outcome showed the same dominance pattern for unauthorized use and concealment, and perceived social support for AI use was protective specifically against concealment. These findings reframe shadow AI as a habit-and-environment phenomenon rather than an individual-exploration phenomenon. The cross-sectional design means causal claims are exploratory.

</details>


### [197] [Data Center Governance Under AI Expansion Pressures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16942)
*Yacine Jernite,Sasha Luccioni,Bruna Trevelin,Angela Lee,Carolina Molina,Xinying Chu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 数据中心收益全球化而负面外部性高度本地化；AI规模激增促使地方政府通过立法与诉讼要求数据中心保持净正向影响，社区可借助现有联邦与州/地方性法规施压运营方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心承诺的收益是全球性的，但其对电费、含水层和居民生活环境的负面影响却集中在本地。历史上政府多以税收抵免等激励吸引数据中心，而AI驱动的规模扩张改变了这一格局，联邦层面法规（公共信托原则、污染防治法、清洁空气法、安全饮用水法）适用范围存在争议且联邦政府倾向强调AI竞争力与增长，责任下沉至区域政府，因此有必要厘清地方可用的监管工具与施压路径。

Method: 政策与法规层面的分析性论述：梳理联邦立法（Public Trust Doctrine、Pollution Prevention Act、Clean Air Act、Safe Drinking Water Act）的适用性与争议；结合数据中心对土地、电力、水和基础设施的本地化需求，识别州与区域层面与建设和运营相关的法律法规；并以近期地方政府出台法案、提起诉讼的动向作为经验证据。

Result: 近月来地方政府已通过颁布法案和提起诉讼等方式，力求确保数据中心对本地立法辖区保持净正向影响；同时指出社区现阶段拥有表达关切的制度机会，并可援引既有法规对数据中心运营方形成压力。

Conclusion: 在联邦监管范围不确定、联邦政策偏向AI增长的背景下，地方政府与社区成为保护本地资源的关键主体；现有联邦、州及区域法规构成可用的监管杠杆，数据中心运营商面临日益增大的本地合规与舆论压力。

Abstract: The promised benefits of data centers are global, but their negative impacts are very much local, affecting the energy bills, aquifers and living environments of citizens across the United States. While governments and authorities have historically provided data centers with incentives such as tax credits and exemptions, AI's aggressive growth in scale has changed this dynamic, and recent months have seen them issue bills and file lawsuits in order to ensure that data centers remain a net positive influence on their legislatures. Existing pieces of federal legislation -- such as the Public Trust Doctrine, the Pollution Prevention, Clean Air and Safe Drinking Water Acts -- exist to manage natural resources, but their scope remains contested, and the current federal government's stance on competitiveness and growth of AI has put the onus on regional governments to endeavor to protect local resources. There are also state- and regional laws and regulations that are relevant for building and operating data centers since, in practice, they require substantial land, electricity, water, and infrastructure at a local level. Given this situation, communities are currently provided with the opportunity to raise their concerns regarding the potential negative impacts of how data centers are built and operated, and have existing regulations that can be used to put pressure on data center operators.

</details>


### [198] [Scalable Conversational Assessment: Chat-Based Exams with a Two-Agent LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17220)
*Geoffrey Challen,Craig Zilles*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出可扩展对话式评估系统：基于LLM双智能体架构（面试官+隐藏评估器）实现交互式口语式评估，并在模拟与传统课堂场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语考试能追问学生推理、澄清模糊回答，但难以规模化；本文旨在兼顾交互性评估质量与可扩展性。

Method: 双智能体架构：面试官直接与学生对话，仅获得"下一个问题"的指导；隐藏评估器掌握活动目标与评分量表，追踪学生进度并决定追问或推进，从而降低答案泄露风险。评估分两部分：(1) 使用具备特定能力水平或对抗策略（答案抽取者、提示注入者、自信虚构者）的LLM模拟学生，且采用虚构评估主题以防预训练知识泄露；(2) 在一门小型AI素养选修课的形成性与终结性场景中部署，并对手稿进行人工与事后AI审阅。

Result: 模拟学生测试与真实课堂部署均完成；通过手稿审阅识别出需改进追问生成质量，以及需确保评分仅计入学生本人（而非面试官）贡献的观点。

Conclusion: 可扩展对话式评估具备可行性，双智能体分离设计有助于防泄露与过程追踪；后续需优化追问生成并严格区分学生贡献与系统提示内容以保障评分效度。

Abstract: Oral exams are compelling because (1) students can be asked to provide their reasoning for an answer and (2) their interactive nature permits follow-up questions to resolve ambiguous answers. But oral exams scale poorly. In this paper, we present scalable conversational assessment, an LLM-based system that conducts interactive text-based conversations with students to assess their ability to make and support an argument. We use a two-agent architecture to separate concerns and reduce the likelihood that answers are leaked to the student: the interviewer interacts directly with the student, but only receives guidance about the next question to ask the student; the hidden evaluator knows the goals of the whole activity, tracks the student's progress using a rubric, and guides the interviewer on when to follow-up on the current question or proceed to the next question. The system was first tested using a collection of simulated students with LLM personas of a particular ability level or adversarial tactic (e.g., answer-extractors, prompt-injectors, confident fabricators). A novel element of this evaluation was that it required the use of a fictional assessment topic to prevent personas that shouldn't have information from using knowledge present in the LLM's pre-training. The system was also deployed in a small, elective AI literacy course in both formative and summative contexts. Through manual and post-facto AI review of the transcripts we identified opportunities to improve the follow-up question generation and ensure that grading only credited ideas that the student (and not the interviewer) contributed.

</details>


### [199] [The Overstated Cost of AI Fairness in Criminal Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01299)
*Ignacio Cofone,Warut Khern-am-nuai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文实证挑战"公平性提升必然降低预测准确率"的主流观点：利用COMPAS数据与因果推断，证明模型会放大而非仅复制种族偏见，且公平约束纠正了有偏结果变量（再逮捕数据）造成的扭曲，因而在部分干预下不牺牲准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流算法公平研究普遍认为提升公平性会损害预测准确率，从而给社会带来成本，政策辩论亦以此为前提。作者意在检验这一"公平—准确率权衡"假设，并质疑"算法相对于人类判断是中性改进"及"算法仅镜像既有偏见"两种论断。

Method: 对COMPAS数据集进行实证分析。其一，采用因果推断方法，识别模型中种族偏见的存在及其被放大机制；其二，通过施加公平性约束并与无约束模型的预测基线比较，重新框定公平—准确率权衡，论证预测系统的目标变量（再逮捕）本身内含规范性选择与系统性种族差异。

Result: 种族偏见不仅在COMPAS数据中存在，且被广泛使用的模型所放大，而非简单复制；无约束模型并非最优基线——再逮捕标签捕捉并放大系统性种族差异，因此公平约束可纠正有偏结果变量引入的扭曲，在某些干预下公平性不带来准确率成本。

Conclusion: 公平—准确率权衡的前提（无约束模型为最优基线）不成立；这一动态可推广至信贷、招聘与住房等领域，即偏差结果变量独立于代理变量选择而强化不平等。据此作者提出刑法与政策应如何对待公平性调整的启示。

Abstract: A dominant critique of algorithmic fairness holds that increasing fairness reduces predictive accuracy, imposing a cost on society. We challenge that assumption by empirically analyzing the COMPAS dataset. We make two contributions. First, using causal inference methods, we show that racial bias is not only present in the COMPAS dataset but is also amplified by the models trained on it. Widely used models do more than replicate existing bias; they exacerbate it. This undercuts both the assumption that algorithmic decision-making offers a neutral improvement over human judgment and the weaker claim that it merely mirrors preexisting human bias. Second, we reframe the fairness-accuracy tradeoff. Applying fairness constraints does not necessarily cost predictive accuracy in criminal justice. Prediction systems operationalize concepts such as risk through implicit and often flawed normative choices about what to predict and how. The tradeoff claim assumes that the unconstrained model's prediction is an optimal baseline. Fairness constraints can instead correct distortions introduced by biased outcome variables: rearrest data, in this case, captures and magnifies systemic racial disparities. Under some interventions, therefore, fairness carries none of the cost presumed in policy debates. These dynamics extend beyond criminal justice to lending, hiring, and housing, where biased outcome variables reinforce inequality independently of proxy selection. We draw out what this implies for how law and policy should approach fairness adjustments in criminal law.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Measuring, Revealing and Mitigating Harmful Content Propagation in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15160)
*Xiaokun Zhang,Youlin Wu,Junyu Lu,Jiamin Chen,Zhaochun Ren,Hongfei Lin,Fenglong Ma,Xue Liu,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推荐系统中的有害内容问题，从度量、揭示和缓解三方面展开：构建带危害性标注的数据集并提出新评价指标，实证分析发现用户倾向于与有害内容交互且主流推荐方法会助长其扩散，进而提出通用框架MitiHarm，将危害性信号融入物品与用户表征学习，在保持用户满意度的同时显著抑制有害内容传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐研究通常以迎合用户兴趣为优先目标，忽视了暴力、仇恨言论、色情等有害内容的存在与传播；而推荐系统日益主导信息获取，会放大用户接触的内容范围，因此有必要系统性地研究推荐系统中的有害内容问题。

Method: 1）构建带危害性相关标注的数据集，并提出新的评价指标以定量度量推荐结果的有害性；2）通过实证分析揭示用户与有害内容的交互倾向及主流推荐方法对有害内容扩散的助推作用；3）提出通用框架MitiHarm，将危害性相关信号同时整合进物品表征与用户表征学习。

Result: 实证分析表明用户倾向于与有害内容发生交互，且当前主流推荐方法往往会助长此类内容的扩散；实验显示MitiHarm能显著缓解有害内容的传播，同时保持用户满意度。

Conclusion: 该工作从度量、揭示与缓解三个维度系统推进了推荐系统中有害内容的研究，所提MitiHarm框架在抑制有害内容传播与维持推荐效用之间取得了良好平衡，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Recommender systems help users navigate vast amounts of information by filtering content according to their historical behaviors. As these systems increasingly shape everyday information access, they largely determine the content users are exposed to. However, current research typically prioritizes catering to user interest, while overlooking the existence and propagation of harmful content, such as violence, hate speech, and pornography. In this work, we take a systematic step toward investigating harmful content in recommender systems from three aspects: measuring, revealing, and mitigating. First, we construct datasets with harmfulness-related annotations and present new evaluation metrics to quantitatively measure the harmfulness of recommendation results. Second, our empirical analysis reveals that users tend to engage with harmful content and prevailing recommendation methods often contribute to the proliferation of such content. Third, to address this issue, we introduce a generic framework, MitiHarm, which integrates harmfulness-related signals into both item and user representation learning. Experiments show that MitiHarm can significantly mitigate the propagation of harmful content while preserving user satisfaction. Resources of this work are available at https://github.com/Zhang-xiaokun/MitiHarm.

</details>


### [201] [When Direction Matters: Diagnosing Relational Instruction Following in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15417)
*Zhanyu Wu,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 SwitchIR 诊断基准，揭示稠密检索器在“方向性关系”判断上的系统性弱点：依赖词汇/属性线索时表现好，但关系方向反转时显著失效，根因是方向敏感的查询-文档交互信号弱于方向无关的配对偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索器宣称能依据自然语言相关性判据（而非仅短查询）进行条件检索，但这一能力缺乏细粒度诊断。作者希望定位其具体失效模式：当相关性取决于同一实体间的方向性关系时，模型是否可靠。

Method: 构建 SwitchIR 受控诊断任务，对比三类设定：Constraint Shift、Explicit Filtering（二者含可用线索）与 Relational Flip（查询与文档对固定，仅反转所请求的关系方向）；在代表性 embedding 检索器与 late-interaction 检索器上评测，并给出 LLM 辅助、人工复核的真实场景变体；提出精确的 margin 分解，将得分拆为方向敏感的查询-文档交互项与方向不变的配对偏好项；并进行诊断性微调实验。

Result: 在各检索器上，主联合指标在两个含线索控制任务上较高，而在 Relational Flip 上持续偏低；该排序在真实变体中同样成立。margin 分解显示，只有方向敏感的交互项超过方向不变的配对偏好时，两个方向才同时判断正确；实证发现方向敏感变化普遍存在，但其对齐交互通常不足以克服该偏好。诊断微调表明模型可学到跨关系族泛化的关系行为，且较大模型泛化更强。

Conclusion: 稠密检索器的“条件检索”能力存在结构性局限：对方向性关系尤其不可靠，问题源于交互信号强度不足而非缺乏方向信息；该能力可通过针对性微调改善，且具规模依赖性。

Abstract: Dense retrievers increasingly claim the ability to condition retrieval on natural-language relevance criteria beyond a short query. We identify a specific weakness in this capability: the evaluated models perform well when lexical or attribute cues distinguish the target, but become much less reliable when relevance depends on a directional relation over the same entities. We introduce SwitchIR, a controlled diagnostic that contrasts Constraint Shift and Explicit Filtering with Relational Flip, where the query and document pair remain fixed while the requested relation direction reverses. Across representative embedding and late-interaction retrievers, the primary joint metric is high on the two cue-available controls and consistently low on Relational Flip; the same ordering persists in an LLM-assisted, manually reviewed realistic variant. An exact margin decomposition shows that both directions are correct only when the direction-sensitive query--document interaction exceeds the direction-invariant pair preference. Empirically, direction-sensitive changes are common, but their aligned interaction is usually too weak to overcome that preference. Diagnostic fine-tuning shows that models can learn relational behavior that generalizes across relation families, with stronger generalization in the larger model.

</details>


### [202] [SurvEval: Listwise Re-Ranking Evaluator via Survival-Based Exposure and Multi-Objective Value Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15434)
*YaChen Yan,Liubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出列表级评估器 SurvEval，联合建模用户曝光（生存模型）与物品价值（多目标估计），以更真实反映重排序的列表效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序评估器对用户浏览假设过度简化，且将多目标信号压缩为单一标量，难以反映真实的列表级（listwise）效用。

Method: 将序列浏览过程形式化为离散时间生存模型，推导单调递减的到达概率以刻画级联浏览结构；引入带独立预测头与不确定性损失加权的多目标价值估计模块，自适应平衡不同尺度与噪声的目标；两组件通过统一 Transformer 骨干联合优化，列表效用为各位置到达概率与条件物品价值估计之积的求和。

Result: 在 KuaiRand 和 Avito 数据集上的大量实验表明，SurvEval 优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 联合建模曝光与多目标价值可提升重排序评估的真实性与准确性，验证了生存模型与不确定性加权多任务学习的有效性。

Abstract: Re-ranking is a crucial stage in recommender systems, yet existing evaluators oversimplify user browsing assumptions and collapse multi-objective signals into a single scalar, limiting their ability to reflect true listwise utility. We propose \textbf{SurvEval}, a listwise evaluator that jointly models user exposure and item value for re-ranking evaluation. We formalize the sequential browsing process as a discrete-time survival model, deriving monotonically decreasing reaching probabilities that capture the cascading structure of user browsing. We further introduce a multi-objective value estimation module with dedicated per-objective prediction heads and uncertainty-based loss weighting to adaptively balance objectives of varying scales and noise levels. Both components are jointly optimized via a unified transformer backbone, and the listwise utility is obtained by summing the position-wise products of reaching probabilities and conditional item value estimates. Extensive experiments on KuaiRand and Avito demonstrate that SurvEval achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [203] [ReAlign: Reward-Guided Preference Alignment for Generative Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15445)
*YaChen Yan,Liubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReAlign框架，用列表级奖励模型与候选感知自回归生成器耦合，通过奖励引导的偏好对齐提升重排的用户效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段生成器-评估器方法将列表生成与评估解耦，生成器无法直接获得列表级效用信号；基于打分的一阶段方法在固定上游排序上下文下打分，未显式搜索排列空间。

Method: 奖励模型将列表级奖励分解为位置级贡献：以生存分析式曝光模块建模用户曝光，以面向各目标的多个头建模异构参与信号下的物品价值。生成器分两阶段训练：先在已记录的曝光列表上监督预训练以建立相关性先验，再进行奖励引导后训练，通过分布对齐与排序对齐（作用于池内标准化奖励）实现与奖励模型的对齐。

Result: 大量离线实验表明ReAlign一致优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 将列表级奖励建模与候选感知自回归生成器端到端耦合，可有效引入列表级效用信号并优化排列，验证了奖励引导偏好对齐在重排任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Re-ranking refines the upstream ranked list to maximize listwise user utility. Existing two-stage generator-evaluator methods decouple list generation from list evaluation, leaving the generator without direct access to the listwise utility signal, while scoring-based one-stage methods score items under a fixed upstream permutation context without explicitly searching the permutation space. We propose \textbf{ReAlign}, a reward-guided preference alignment framework that couples a listwise reward model with a candidate-aware autoregressive generator. The reward model decomposes the listwise reward into position-wise contributions, modeling user exposure through a survival-based exposure module and item value through per-objective heads for heterogeneous engagement signals. The generator is trained in two stages: supervised pre-training on logged exposure lists to establish a relevance prior, followed by reward-guided post-training that aligns the generator with the reward model through complementary distribution and ranking alignment over within-pool standardized rewards. Extensive offline experiments show that ReAlign consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Bag of concepts can enhance other text representations for medical systematic review screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15798)
*Radosław Pytlak,Paweł Cichosz,Bartłomiej Fajdek,Bogdan Jastrzębski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了在基于主动学习的半自动化系统综述(SLR)系统中不同文本表示的选择，重点研究此前关注不足的"概念袋"(Bag of Concepts, BoC)表示及其多种变体，并比较BoC、BoW、PubMedBERT、Jina、Gemma、Qwen3及各类BoC组合的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoC表示在SLR文本表示选择中研究不足，缺乏对其实现方式与效果的充分实验评估；同时本体选择对文档分类结果的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在医学SLR案例研究中，将不同本体生成的BoC特征嵌入主动学习框架，按顺序构建随机森林分类模型（含聚类初始化与不确定性采样），训练集由专家标注迭代扩充；系统比较BoW、BoC、PubMedBERT、Jina、Gemma、Qwen3及BoC与各表示的组合，并将基于所选本体的BoC与采用UMLS词典的MedCAT表示进行预测价值对比；评估指标采用借鉴优化基准测试的工作量节省(workload savings)与性能剖面。

Result: 概念特征可提供互补信息，BoC增强表示在DERP案例上的平均工作量节省高于其对应基础表示；该比较中Qwen3+BoC平均表现最佳；增强效果随案例与基础表示不同而变化。

Conclusion: BoC特征作为补充信息具有价值，BoC增强表示可提升SLR主动学习框架的工作量节省，但收益依赖具体案例与基础表示；本体选择会影响文档分类结果，需在表示设计中加以考量。

Abstract: The paper examines the choice of text representation for semi-automated systematic literature review (SLR) systems based on active learning. Motivated by the insufficient attention that the bag of concepts representation has received so far, the paper discusses the implementation and experimental evaluation of several variations thereof. The experimental study was performed on medical SLR case studies, with results reported for random forest classification, cluster initialization and uncertainty sampling. The utility of bag of concepts features obtained using different ontologies is evaluated by applying them within the active learning framework to sequentially create classification models based on training data iteratively expanded with expert annotations. Systematic experiments compare BoW, BoC, PubMedBERT, Jina, Gemma and Qwen3 representations, together with combinations of BoC and each of the other representations. The evaluation uses workload savings and performance profiles adapted from optimization benchmarking. The results suggest that concept-based features can provide complementary information, with the BoC-augmented representations achieving higher mean workload savings on the DERP cases than their corresponding base representations. The Qwen3+BoC representation gives the best average performance in this comparison. The effects of augmentation vary across case studies and base representations. The paper also concentrates on the impact of ontology choice on document classification outcomes. In particular, the paper compares the predictive value of bag of concepts representations based on chosen ontologies with that of representations obtained using MedCAT with a UMLS-based dictionary.

</details>


### [205] [From Tokens to Behaviors: Rethinking Generative Recommendation for User Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16159)
